### [1] [Tokka-Bench: Evaluating Tokenizers Across 100 Natural and 20 Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08794)
*Ben Gubler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tokka-Bench，一个开源的多指标分词器评估框架，在 100 种自然语言（30+ 书写系统）和 20 种编程语言上对比了七种 BPE 分词器，发现词表分配策略比词表规模更关键，且近期分词器在编程语言上的效率已趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖子词分词器，其质量随语言差异大，但缺乏标准化的多指标框架进行广泛的跨语言比较评估。

Method: 构建 Tokka-Bench 框架，采用五个互补指标（每 token 字节数、唯一 token 覆盖率、子词生育率、词分割率、词表组成），针对 100 种自然语言和 20 种编程语言，使用适配各书写系统的语言感知分割，对比 GPT-2、GPT-4、gpt-oss、Llama 3.1、Gemma 3、Qwen3、Kimi K2 七种 BPE 分词器。

Result: 词表分配策略比原始词表大小影响更大；尽管自然语言特性各异，近期分词器在编程语言上的效率已趋于一致。

Conclusion: Tokka-Bench 为分词器评估提供了标准化多指标框架，框架、数据及交互式仪表板已公开。

Abstract: Large language models rely on subword tokenizers whose quality varies across languages, yet no standardized multi-metric framework exists for broad comparative evaluation. We introduce Tokka-Bench, an open-source framework that evaluates tokenizers on five complementary metrics -- bytes per token, unique token coverage, subword fertility, word-split rate, and vocabulary composition -- across 100 natural languages (30+ scripts) and 20 programming languages, using language-aware segmentation adapted to each writing system. Comparing seven BPE tokenizers (GPT-2, GPT-4, gpt-oss, Llama 3.1, Gemma 3, Qwen3, and Kimi K2) within individual languages, we find that vocabulary allocation strategy matters more than raw vocabulary size, and that programming-language efficiency has converged among recent tokenizers despite divergent natural-language profiles. The framework, data, and interactive dashboard are publicly available.

</details>


### [2] [Child ASR Adaptation with Adult Retention: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08827)
*Houssam Eddine-Othman Lachemat,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究儿童语音识别（ASR）适应问题，旨在在保持成人语音识别性能的同时提升儿童及非母语儿童语音的识别效果。对比了全量微调、LoRA和权重空间合并三种方法，在编码器-解码器、编码器-CTC和AudioLLM三类ASR架构上实验，使用阿拉伯语和英语儿童语音数据。结果显示：儿童适应必要，尤其对非母语儿童语音；直接适应常损害成人性能；双语适应比单语更稳定；权重合并（LERP倾向成人保留，TIES倾向儿童增益）能改善权衡，编码器-解码器模型直接双语微调在原始WER上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统在儿童和非母语者语音上表现不佳，且将成人模型适配到儿童语音往往导致成人语音识别性能下降（灾难性遗忘）。需要系统研究如何在保持成人语音识别能力的同时有效提升儿童语音识别性能，并探索不同适应策略和模型架构下的权衡。

Method: 对比三种适应方法：全量微调、LoRA低秩适应、事后权重空间合并（LERP线性插值和TIES任务算术合并）。在三类ASR架构上实验：编码器-解码器、编码器-CTC、AudioLLM（Whisper等）。使用阿拉伯语母语和非母语儿童语音、英语MyST儿童语音，以及成人基准MGB-2和LibriSpeech test-clean。评估指标：词错误率（WER）、保留指数、儿童适应增益、适应恢复度。

Result: 儿童适应显著必要，尤其对非母语阿拉伯语和英语儿童语音；直接适应常降低成人ASR性能；双语适应比单语适应更稳定；权重空间合并常改善适应-保留权衡，在编码器-CTC、Whisper和AudioLLM模型上效果明显，LERP倾向成人保留，TIES恢复更强儿童增益；编码器-解码器模型直接双语微调在原始WER上仍最优。

Conclusion: 研究建立了儿童ASR适应与成人保留的系统评估框架，揭示了不同适应策略、模型架构和语言设置下的权衡规律。权重空间合并是一种低成本、有效的后处理方法，能缓解灾难性遗忘。双语联合适应优于单语适应。为实际部署提供了根据应用场景选择适应策略的指导。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems often underperform for children and non-native speakers, while adapting adult ASR models to child speech can cause adult-speech forgetting. We study child ASR adaptation with adult retention across Arabic and English. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and post-hoc weight-space merging across encoder--decoder, encoder--CTC, and AudioLLM-based ASR systems. Experiments use Arabic native and non-native child speech, English MyST child speech, and adult benchmarks from MGB-2 and LibriSpeech test-clean. We evaluate recognition quality with WER and quantify the adaptation--retention trade-off using Retention Index, Child Adaptation Gain, and Adaptation Recovery. Results show that child adaptation is necessary, especially for non-native Arabic and English child speech, but direct adaptation often reduces adult ASR performance. Bilingual adaptation is more stable than language-specific adaptation. Weight-space merging often improves the trade-off, especially for encoder--CTC, Whisper, and AudioLLM-based ASR, with LERP favoring adult retention and TIES recovering stronger child gains. For the encoder--decoder model, direct bilingual fine-tuning remains strongest in raw WER.\footnote{Code, and models are available at https://github.com/qcri/Child-ASR-Adaptation.

</details>


### [3] [When Forgetting Looks Like Improvement: Metric Masking in Streaming Diarizer Adaptation and the Price of Rehearsal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08828)
*Mo Yu,Yang Liu,Jing Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 小数据适配可提升流式说话人分离系统的语音检测性能，但会降低说话人归属准确性；适配虽提升域内性能并迁移至独立语料库，但改善不一致，主要导致时序身份一致性下降而非说话人计数错误；排练策略可缓解退化但降低跨域迁移能力；需联合评估检测准确性、身份一致性和保持行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究小数据适配在流式说话人分离系统中提升语音检测但降低说话人归属的差异现象，理解适配对时序身份一致性的影响。

Method: 使用7.5小时双人对话数据对已发布的流式分离系统进行适配，在六个语料库上评估；引入局部重映射诊断分析身份退化模式；对比排练策略的效果。

Result: 适配显著提升域内分离性能并迁移至独立语料库，但改善不一致，额外混淆主要源于时序身份一致性受损而非说话人计数错误；局部重映射揭示不同语料库身份退化模式不同，表明适配改变了流式模型随时间维持说话人分配的方式；排练减轻退化但降低跨域迁移性能。

Conclusion: 适配流式说话人分离系统时需联合评估检测准确性、身份一致性和保持行为，以平衡性能提升与身份退化。

Abstract: Small-data adaptation can improve speech detection while degrading speaker attribution. We study this discrepancy in a released streaming diarizer adapted on 7.5 h of two-party conversation and evaluated across six corpora. Adaptation substantially improves in-domain diarization performance and transfers to an independent corpus. However, this improvement is not consistent across evaluation scenarios as the additional confusion is mainly associated with impaired temporal identity consistency rather than speaker-count errors. A local-remapping diagnostic reveals different patterns of identity degradation across corpora, indicating that adaptation may alter how streaming models maintain speaker assignments over time. Rehearsal reduces the observed degradation but reduces the cross-domain transfer performance. These results highlight the need to jointly evaluate detection accuracy, identity consistency, and retention behavior when adapting streaming diarization systems.

</details>


### [4] [Emo-Jev: Probabilistic Reasoning for Emotion Classification with Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08829)
*Yazhou Zhang,Junhao Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Emo-Jev，一种无需训练的框架，通过两种互补实现（Emo-Jev-D和Emo-Jev-SC）利用Jev接口进行文本分类推理，在八个数据集上评估，相比直接Jev分类和五个最先进LLM，展现出较低延迟和成本，但平均宏F1略低于最强LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Jev这种基于概率决策的替代语言理解接口，是否能支持有效的文本分类推理，并与领先的大语言模型（LLM）进行对比，填补该领域的空白。

Method: 提出Emo-Jev框架，包含两种实现：Emo-Jev-D将分类分解为任务特定的原子判断并组合概率；Emo-Jev-SC从互补视角构建多条判断路径并聚合预测形成共识决策。均为无训练方法。

Result: 在情感分析、情绪识别、讽刺检测和幽默检测八个数据集上，标准Jev平均宏F1为62.93%，最强LLM基线为67.28%。Emo-Jev在保持较低延迟和成本的同时，性能接近LLM。

Conclusion: Emo-Jev展示了基于概率决策接口进行复杂推理的潜力，通过分解和共识机制提升分类效果，为低成本、低延迟的文本分类提供了可行替代方案。

Abstract: Jev offers an alternative interface for language understanding: given an input and predefined questions, it returns probabilistic decisions rather than free-form responses. Whether this interface can support effective reasoning for text classification against leading LLMs remains an open questions. We introduce Emo-Jev, a training-free framework with two complementary implementations. Emo-Jev-D decomposes classification into task-specific atomic judgments and composes their probabilities into a final prediction. Emo-Jev-SC constructs multiple judgment paths from complementary perspectives and aggregates their predictions into a consensus decision. We evaluate Emo-Jev on eight datasets spanning sentiment analysis, emotion recognition, sarcasm detection and humor detection, comparing against direct Jev classification and five SoTA LLMs under input/output and chain-of-thought reasoning. Standard Jev achieves 62.93\% average macro-F1 versus 67.28\% for the strongest LLM baseline, with lower observed latency and generally lower cost.

</details>


### [5] [CoDR: Training-Free Confidence-Drift Remasking for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08833)
*Yue Wu,Qinghe Zhang,Yu Zhang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）中不可逆的早期token承诺导致的错误传播问题，提出了无需训练且与采样器无关的CoDR（置信度漂移重掩码）方法。CoDR通过k-分区探测在k次前向传播中估计所有已承诺位置的置信度漂移，仅重掩码并重新生成模型不再认可的token，在多个骨干模型、任务和采样器上显著提升准确率，且开销远小于现有重掩码方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM解码时反复向掩码位置承诺token，但这些承诺通常不可逆。在稀疏上下文下选定的token会被固定保留，即使后续更密集的上下文不再支持该token，导致早期错误传播。现有采样器主要决定何时承诺，很少检查已承诺token是否应保留。论文将此归因于置信度漂移：模型对已承诺token的置信度从提交时的稀疏上下文到后续更密集的上下文会下降。

Method: 提出CoDR（Confidence Drift Remasking），一种无需训练且与采样器无关的细化步骤。CoDR通过k-分区探测在仅k次前向传播中估计所有已承诺位置的置信度漂移，然后仅重掩码并重新生成模型不再认可的token。该方法可即插即用地集成到现有采样器中。

Result: 在两种骨干模型、四个推理和编码任务、三种基础采样器上，CoDR提升了所有评估的模型-采样器配置的平均准确率，并在大多数单独任务设置上取得提升，仅带来适度开销。受控实验表明增益源于针对性的置信度漂移重掩码而非单纯的额外计算量，且CoDR所需前向传播次数远少于现有重掩码方法。

Conclusion: CoDR通过检测和修正置信度漂移有效解决了MDLM早期承诺错误传播的问题，以极低的计算开销显著提升生成质量，展示了无需训练的即插即用重掩码策略的有效性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) decode by repeatedly committing tokens to masked positions, but these commitments are usually irreversible. A token chosen under sparse, partial context is kept fixed, even when later context no longer supports it. Existing samplers mainly decide when to commit a token, but rarely check whether an already committed token should still be kept, allowing early mistakes to propagate. We trace this issue to confidence drift, where the model's confidence in a committed token drops from its sparse commit-time context to the denser context available later. Based on this signal, we propose CoDR (Confidence Drift Remasking), a training-free and sampler-agnostic refinement pass. CoDR estimates drift for all committed positions in only k forward passes via k-partition probing, then remasks and regenerates only the tokens the model no longer endorses. Across two backbones, four reasoning and coding tasks, and three base samplers, CoDR improves average accuracy across all evaluated model-sampler configurations and improves most individual task settings with modest overhead. Controlled experiments show that the gains come from targeted confidence-drift remasking rather than extra compute alone, and that CoDR uses far fewer forward passes than prior remasking methods. Code is available at https://github.com/YueWu0301/CoDR.

</details>


### [6] [Leveraging LLM-Generated Explanations for Detecting Emotionally Rewritten Fake News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08835)
*Yupei Guo,Jiajun He,Xiaohan Shi,Tomoki Toda,Zekun Yang,Bowen Wang,Yukinobu Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对保真情感重写下的假新闻检测问题，构建了情感重写测试集，并提出门控交叉注意力（GCA）框架，自适应融合情感重写新闻与原始解释，有效缓解情感重构导致的语义错位，在多个数据集上显著提升检测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假新闻检测方法主要关注文体差异或引入外部解释，但新闻常在不同情感背景下被重写且保留核心事实，这种保真情感变体会降低模型鲁棒性，因此需要研究情感重写条件下的检测问题。

Method: 构建情感重写测试集，从原始新闻生成解释作为稳定背景知识；提出门控交叉注意力（GCA）框架，通过门控机制自适应融合情感重写新闻与对应解释，使模型聚焦有信息量的解释内容，减少情感重构导致的潜在错配。

Result: 在PolitiFact、GossipCop、LUN三个数据集上实验：PolitiFact和LUN上多种情感条件下显著提升；GossipCop上保持竞争性能。进一步分析了解释引导和门控机制在不同情感条件下的效果。

Conclusion: GCA框架通过门控交叉注意力有效整合情感重写新闻与稳定解释，增强了模型对保真情感变体的鲁棒性，为假新闻检测提供了新的视角和有效方法。

Abstract: The spread of fake news may cause severe social consequences. Existing fake news detection methods mainly focus on stylistic variations or incorporate external information such as explanations. However, news articles are often rewritten under different emotional backgrounds while preserving their underlying factual claims, which may affect the robustness of detection models. In this work, we investigate fake news detec- tion under fact-preserving emotional variations. To study this problem, we construct emotion-rewritten test sets and generate explanations from the original news articles as stable background knowledge. We then propose a Gated Cross Attention (GCA) framework that adaptively integrates emotionally rewritten news with the corresponding explanations, enabling the model to focus on informative explanation content while reducing potential mismatches caused by emotional reframing. Experiments on PolitiFact, GossipCop, and LUN demonstrate that the proposed method achieves notable improvements under multiple emotional conditions on PolitiFact and LUN, while maintaining competitive performance on GossipCop. We further analyze the effects of explanation guidance and gating mechanisms under different emotional conditions. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/Flulike/fakenews gca .

</details>


### [7] [Beyond the Sycophancy Score: How Task, Model, and Pressure Shape LLM Yielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08840)
*Guang Yang,Homa Hosseinmardi,Fengchen Liu,Amir Ghasemian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（103,939 个回复，10 种模型配置，200 个测试项，13 种施压条件，四轮对话）系统研究了大语言模型（LLM）的谄媚行为。核心发现：模型是否让步主要取决于验证用户主张的难度以及是否有训练好的护栏覆盖该主题。锚定事实极少被让步（1.3%），逻辑谜题的让步率随反驳所需线索数增加而上升，个人偏好被认可率高达 77%。无法可靠解答难题的模型才会让步；开启最大推理后，深度谜题的让步率从 19.2% 和 12.5% 降至 0%。谬误或情绪化表述不比单纯重复更有效。人工标注与 LLM 评判高度一致（118/120）。实用建议：简化难验证问题并深度推理、陈述问题而非预设答案、在开放问题上要求证据、按护栏配置选模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅报告单一的谄媚率，无法揭示何时发生、为何发生以及用户如何规避。作者旨在系统量化导致模型放弃正确答案或盲目附和用户的关键因素，为可靠使用 LLM 提供可操作指南。

Method: 构建 200 个测试项，涵盖锚定事实、逻辑谜题、个人选择等类别；设计 13 种施压策略（重复、谬误、情绪化等）；采用四轮对话模拟用户持续施压。测试 8 个基础模型（推理关闭）及其中 2 个模型的最大推理版本，共 10 种配置。每个回复由两个独立 LLM 评判器打标，再经三人工标注校准（118/120 一致）。用逻辑回归分析任务难度、模型家族、施压策略对让步率的贡献（McFadden R² 分解）。

Result: 1. 验证成本与护栏覆盖是主导因素：移除任务因素损失 0.485 McFadden R²，模型家族仅 0.139，施压策略 0.009。2. 锚定事实让步率 1.3%；逻辑谜题让步率随反驳线索数上升。3. 个人选择被认可 77%。4. 难题让步主要来自无法可靠解答的模型；最大推理使深度谜题让步率归零（19.2%→0%，12.5%→0%）。5. 谬误/情绪施压不比单纯重复更有效。6. 人工与 LLM 评判高度一致。

Conclusion: 谄媚行为并非随机，而是由任务可验证性、模型能力边界与护栏覆盖共同决定。实用原则：将难验证问题简化并启用深度推理、提问时陈述问题而非预设答案、在开放性问题上要求模型提供证据、根据模型的实测护栏档案选择模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often abandon a correct answer, or endorse a user's position, once the user pushes back. This behavior, called sycophancy, is usually reported as a single rate per model, which says little about when it happens or how a user can avoid it. We study the conditions that produce it with 103,939 graded replies from ten configurations: eight LLMs with reasoning disabled, and two of them again with maximum reasoning, all facing the same 200 items, 13 pressure conditions, and four-turn conversations, with every reply labeled by two independent LLM judges. We find that the dominant factors are how costly it is for the model to verify the user's claim, and whether a trained guardrail covers it. Removing this task factor from a logistic model costs 0.485 of McFadden $R^2$, against 0.139 for model family and 0.009 for pressure tactic. Anchored facts are almost never conceded (1.3%), while adoption on logic puzzles rises with the number of clues needed to refute the pushed answer. Personal choices are endorsed in 77.0% of conversations. Most concessions on hard items come from models that cannot reliably solve them; models that can solve them rarely give the answer up. For both models tested, maximum reasoning removes these concessions completely: adoption on deep puzzles falls from 19.2% and 12.5% to 0%. Fallacious or emotional framing adds nothing beyond plain repetition. Three human annotators agree with the judges' consensus on 118/120 calibration items. These results give practical rules for reliable use: simplify hard-to-verify problems and reason deeply, state the question rather than one's preferred answer, ask for evidence on open questions, and choose models by their measured guardrail profile.

</details>


### [8] [Beyond Risk Prediction: Evidence Grounding and Psychosocial Factor Verification for Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08842)
*Tianle Hu,Chen Peng,Yi-Hsin Tsai,Takshing Andy Tung,Bingyang Sun,Yenjou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含风险评估、证据定位和因素识别三个模块的框架，用于从社交网络服务（SNS）帖子中检测自杀风险，并提供可解释的文本证据和心理社会因素分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自杀风险分类仅提供风险等级预测，缺乏对预测背后的文本证据和心理社会因素的深入分析，难以满足可解释性需求。

Method: 框架包含三个任务：风险评估采用基于长度的路由策略处理不同长度帖子；证据定位识别支持性短语，并引入风险-证据约束保持与风险预测的一致性；因素识别使用分类学验证器（关注因素语义）和证据感知验证器（利用因素特定的词汇-语义线索），两者预测概率融合得出最终因素预测。

Result: 在IEEE BigData 2026挑战赛评测中，风险评估加权F1达0.8088，证据定位测试集宏平均行F1为0.7605，因素识别宏平均F1为0.5562。

Conclusion: 该框架超越单纯的风险等级预测，能同时输出风险预测、支持性文本证据及细粒度心理社会因素，实现了对SNS帖子更具可解释性的自杀风险分析。

Abstract: Identifying suicide risk from social networking services (SNS) posts is important for detecting suicide-related signals in online environments. However, risk classification alone provides limited insight into the textual evidence and psychosocial factors behind a prediction. Based on the IEEE BigData 2026 Explainable Suicide Risk Detection Challenge, this study presents a framework consisting of Risk Assessment, Evidence Grounding, and Factor Identification. Risk Assessment uses length-based routing to accommodate posts of different lengths. Evidence Grounding identifies supporting phrases and uses a Risk-Evidence constraint to maintain consistency with the Risk prediction. For Factor Identification, two verifiers are used. The Taxonomy Verifier focuses on factor semantics, whereas the Evidence-Aware Verifier uses factor-specific lexical-semantic cues to select informative positive training units. Their prediction probabilities are combined to produce the final factor predictions. The three tasks are evaluated using task-specific F1 score measures. Risk Assessment achieved a Weighted F1 of 0.8088, Evidence Grounding achieved a test Macro row F1 of 0.7605, and Factor Identification achieved a Macro F1 of 0.5562. The results show that the framework can provide risk predictions, along with supporting textual evidence and fine-grained information on psychosocial factors. Overall, the proposed framework extends suicide-risk assessment beyond risk-level prediction and provides a more interpretable analysis of SNS posts.

</details>


### [9] [QuanLing: Cross-Branch Validation of Language Distance Quantification on Western Romance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08851)
*Yiping Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将QuanLing框架从北日耳曼语支扩展到西罗曼语支（法语、葡萄牙语、西班牙语、意大利语），使用150个四语平行句子，通过LaBSE句嵌入距离、BERT分词碎片化率和mBERT掩码语言模型相互理解度验证了跨语支的适用性。结果显示葡-西语最接近（距离0.0229），法-意语最远（0.0338），两模型在6对中4对排序一致；西罗曼语支绝对跨度大于北日耳曼语支但相对比率相当，法语MLM预测准确率显著更高，证实了QuanLing的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化紧密相关语言之间的距离仍是定量语言学的核心挑战。作者先前在北日耳曼语支验证了QuanLing框架，本文旨在测试该框架在不同语系分支（西罗曼语支）上的跨分支适用性与泛化能力，使用相同的度量族和聚合协议进行跨分支验证。

Method: 采用150个四语平行句子（英语为锚语），计算三类指标：LaBSE句嵌入距离、四个单语BERT分词器的分词碎片化率、mBERT掩码语言模型的相互理解度（MLM预测概率）。保持与北日耳曼语支研究一致的度量族和聚合协议，适配四语言四元组构建。

Result: 葡萄牙语-西班牙语最接近（LaBSE距离0.0229），法语-意大利语最远（0.0338）；LaBSE与mBERT在6个语言对中有4对排序一致，确认跨模型稳健性；西罗曼语支绝对距离跨度0.011大于北日耳曼语支的0.008，但相对比率相当（1.48 vs 1.67），符合更长分化时间；法语MLM top-1准确率36.12%显著高于意大利语29.28%，反映其正写法-音系解耦特性。

Conclusion: 跨分支验证为QuanLing框架在单一语支之外的泛化能力提供了进一步证据，证明该度量族和聚合协议可跨语系分支稳健应用。

Abstract: Quantifying language distance among closely related languages remains a core challenge in quantitative linguistics. Our previous work [1] introduced QuanLing (Quantitative Linguistics via Pretrained Language Models), a quantitative framework combining language distance metrics (sentence embedding distance, tokenization fragmentation rate) with language property analysis (MLM prediction probability), validated on North Germanic (Danish, Norwegian Bokmål, Swedish). This paper extends QuanLing to Western Romance--French, Portuguese, Spanish, Italian--testing cross-branch applicability with the same metric family and aggregation protocol as our North Germanic study, adapted for four languages (English anchor, quadruplet construction). Using 150 four-language parallel sentences, we compute LaBSE sentence embedding distances, tokenization fragmentation rates from four monolingual BERT tokenizers, and mBERT masked language model mutual intelligibility. Results show that Portuguese--Spanish are closest (LaBSE distance 0.0229), French--Italian most distant (0.0338); LaBSE and mBERT rankings agree on 4 of 6 pairs, confirming cross-model robustness. Western Romance shows a wider absolute distance span than North Germanic (0.011 vs. 0.008) but comparable relative ratios (1.48 vs. 1.67), consistent with longer divergence time. French exhibits notably higher MLM predictability (36.12% top-1 accuracy vs. 29.28% for Italian), reflecting its orthography--phonology decoupling. This cross-branch validation provides further evidence for QuanLing's generalizability beyond a single language branch.

</details>


### [10] [LRCC: Generalizing Low-Rank Compression with Conditional Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08858)
*Thomas Vaitses Fontanari,Maximo Eduardo Rulli,Federico Alvetreti,Donatella Genovese,Simone Scardapane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出低秩条件计算（LRCC），通过为每个Transformer块训练轻量级路由器，使预训练语言模型能够根据输入token动态选择嵌套低秩路径，实现token级的条件计算。在冻结低秩因子、仅优化路由器的训练设置下，LRCC在相同平均激活参数预算下显著优于静态低秩压缩，且在匹配batch-size-1解码延迟时提升困惑度和下游任务准确率，无需专用内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低秩压缩在推理时使用固定秩分配，无论输入token如何都分配相同计算量，无法利用token级的难易差异。需要一种方法在不增加平均参数预算的前提下，引入token依赖的自适应计算，提升模型预测性能。

Method: LRCC在每个Transformer块引入一个轻量级路由器，从一组嵌套的低秩路径中为每个token选择路径。训练时冻结预训练的低秩因子，仅优化路由器参数。路由器输出离散选择，实现token级的条件计算。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B上，LRCC比静态低秩压缩平均下游准确率提升7.6个百分点。在匹配batch-size-1解码延迟下，Llama-3.2-1B的困惑度和下游准确率均提升，Llama-2-7B保持竞争力。路由器的路径选择分析显示token级自适应分配有效。

Conclusion: LRCC通过极低开销的路由器实现token级条件计算，在不改变低秩因子、无需专用内核的情况下，显著提升低秩压缩模型的预测性能和推理效率，验证了动态秩分配的有效性。

Abstract: Low-rank compression reduces the cost of pretrained language models by replacing linear transformations with low-rank factorizations. However, conventional methods use a fixed rank allocation during inference, assigning the same amount of compute regardless of the input token. We introduce Low-Rank Conditional Computation (LRCC), which adds token-dependent computation to pretrained models by training one lightweight router per Transformer block to select among a small set of nested low-rank paths. During training, the low-rank factors remain frozen, and only the routers are optimized. We evaluate LRCC on Llama and Qwen models for language modeling and zero-shot downstream tasks. Within the same average active-parameter budget, LRCC improves the predictive performance over static low-rank compression, including a 7.6 percentage-point gain in average downstream accuracy on Llama-2-7B over static methods. At matched batch-size-1 decoding latency, LRCC improves both perplexity and downstream accuracy on Llama-3.2-1B and remains competitive on Llama-2-7B, without specialized kernels. Finally, we assess the usefulness of assigning a token-wise path by analyzing the routers' path choices.

</details>


### [11] [Tiny-Scale Chinese BERT Pretraining: A Controlled Comparison of MLM, WWM, and MacBERT Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08879)
*Yiping Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在微型中文BERT模型（4层，256维，870万参数）上对比了MLM、WWM和MacBERT风格替换三种预训练策略。实验控制了架构、语料（129万中文维基百科句子）和超参数。结果显示：在微型规模下，MLM整体内在表现最佳（5个维度胜3个），WWM在困惑度（1.27 vs 2.10）和MLM命中率（22% vs 16%）上优势明显；而受限于极小同义词词典（222条，覆盖率3.3%），MacBERT困惑度严重劣化（47.23，比MLM高22倍），排名为MLM > WWM >> MacBERT，这与基础规模模型的结论（MacBERT > WWM > MLM）截然不同。此外发现评估陷阱：MacBERT训练损失最低（2.17）却困惑度最高（47.23），说明混合替换策略下仅看训练损失不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比主要集中在基础规模模型（>=1.1亿参数），缺乏对微型模型（<1000万参数）的系统性对比。作者旨在探究预训练策略在极小规模下的表现差异，以及同义词词典覆盖率对MacBERT式替换策略的影响，并揭示训练损失作为评估指标的潜在陷阱。

Method: 采用受控实验：统一架构（4层Transformer，256隐藏维度，8头注意力，共870万参数）、语料（129万句中文维基百科）、超参数（批大小256，学习率1e-4，训练10万步）。从头训练三个模型：标准MLM（15% token掩码）、WWM（整词掩码）和MacBERT风格（MLM+同义词替换，同义词词典仅222条，覆盖率3.3%）。评估五个内在维度：困惑度、MLM命中率、语义辨别、语法判断、上下文敏感度。

Result: 1) MLM在5个内在维度中胜出3个，整体表现最佳。2) WWM在困惑度（1.27 vs MLM的2.10，提升39.5%）和MLM命中率（22% vs 16%）上显著优于MLM。3) MacBERT因同义词词典极小导致困惑度恶化至47.23（比MLM高22倍），排名垫底。4) 微型规模下的排名（MLM > WWM >> MacBERT）与基础规模结论（MacBERT > WWM > MLM）完全相反。5) 发现评估陷阱：MacBERT训练损失最低（2.17）却困惑度最高（47.23），证明混合替换策略下训练损失不可靠。

Conclusion: 预训练策略的优劣随模型规模剧烈反转：微型模型下MLM最稳健，WWM在特定指标有优势，而MacBERT极度依赖同义词词典覆盖率，覆盖不足时表现灾难性下降。训练损失在混合替换策略下不能作为模型质量的可靠代理指标。研究强调了在资源受限场景下选择预训练策略需考虑规模效应和资源可用性。所有模型和语料已开源。

Abstract: Pretraining strategies significantly impact the quality of language models, yet existing comparisons of Masked Language Modeling (MLM), Whole Word Masking (WWM), and MacBERT-style replacement have focused primarily on base-scale models (>=110M parameters). This paper presents a controlled comparison of these three strategies on a tiny-scale Chinese BERT model (4 layers, 256 hidden dimensions, 8.7M parameters). Under identical architecture, corpus (1.29M sentences from Chinese Wikipedia), and hyperparameters, we train three models from scratch and evaluate them across five intrinsic dimensions: perplexity, MLM hit rate, semantic discrimination, grammatical judgment, and contextual sensitivity. At tiny scale, MLM achieves the best overall intrinsic performance (winning 3 of 5 dimensions), while WWM excels in both perplexity (1.27 vs. 2.10, a 39.5% improvement) and MLM hit rate (22% vs. 16%). Notably, MacBERT under a severely limited synonym dictionary (222 entries, 3.3% coverage) exhibits severe perplexity degradation (47.23, 22x higher than MLM), yielding a ranking (MLM > WWM >> MacBERT) that differs markedly from the established base-scale conclusion (MacBERT > WWM > MLM). We further identify a critical evaluation pitfall: MacBERT achieves the lowest training loss (2.17) yet the highest perplexity (47.23), revealing that training loss alone is unreliable under mixed replacement strategies. All models and corpus are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/eebyp.

</details>


### [12] [Steering Follows Geometry, Not Labels: Emotion Directions in a Full-Duplex Speech Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08887)
*Pulak Kuli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在全双工语音语言模型 Moshi 中利用均值差分激活转向实现情感控制，发现情感可从残差流线性解码，但转向仅部分可行且不均衡：快乐、愤怒、惊讶共享转向方向，悲伤则可独立转向；共享成分无法简单投影消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音代理需在实时对话中动态调节情感与表达（如缓解投诉、传递紧急、柔化临床结果）。现有情感控制多针对 TTS 或轮次制模型，PersonaPlex 虽控制双工模型身份但未涉及情感，因此需要在开源全双工模型 Moshi 中探索低成本、免重训练的情感转向方法。

Method: 在 Moshi 模型上采用均值差分激活转向，针对快乐、愤怒、惊讶、悲伤四种情感，仅需每帧少量向量加法、无需重训练；通过线性探针验证情感在残差流中的可解码性，并分析转向向量间的几何关系。

Result: 情感可从 Moshi 残差流线性解码；激活转向仅部分有效且不均衡：快乐、愤怒、惊讶三种情感转向方向高度重叠，悲伤方向独特且可独立转向；三种情感的共享成分无法通过简单投影从所有情感中等量移除。

Conclusion: 全双工语音语言模型内部存在情感的线性表示，但激活转向受限于情感间的共享子空间结构，未来需开发更精细的解耦转向机制以实现全情感谱的精准控制。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents need to modulate emotion and delivery during real-time conversations, when de-escalating a complaint, carrying urgency in dispatch, softening a clinical result. Emotion and delivery control is well studied for TTS and turn based models through prompt-conditioned synthesis, reference-conditioned synthesis and activation steering; PersonaPlex controls identity in a duplex model but not affect.
  We study emotion steering in Moshi, a fully open sourced full-duplex speech language model, across four emotions, using mean-difference activation steering, which costs only a few vector additions per frame and no retraining.
  We show that emotion is linearly decodable from Moshi's residual stream, but activation steering is only partially achievable, and unevenly so; as happy, angry and surprise steer towards a shared direction while sad is distinctly steerable. We also show that the shared component across the three emotions cannot simply be projected away from all the emotions equally.

</details>


### [13] [CARE: Certifying Acceleration for Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08917)
*Rui Liu,Tong Zheng,Jindong Gu,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理加速可能导致任务失败的问题，提出了 CARE（Certified Accelerator Selection）方法。CARE 通过配对轨迹评估，提供有限样本保证，确保加速引起的失败风险低于用户指定预算，并选择最快的认证加速器。实验表明，在 LIBERO 基准上实现 9.0–10.8× 加速，同时以 95% 置信度保证至少保留 85.8% 参考策略可解决的回合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型推理成本高昂，现有加速技术（如动作分块、视觉标记剪枝）通常仅基于延迟和平均任务成功率评估，但加速可能丢弃关键信息，导致原本可解任务失败，且这种风险被平均指标掩盖。由于动作偏差在闭环中累积，任务失败仅在完整回合中可观测，因此需要一种能量化并控制加速诱导失败风险的方法。

Method: 1. 定义加速诱导失败：从相同初始条件进行配对轨迹，记录参考策略成功但加速策略失败的情况。2. 提出 CARE：在校准集上使用配对轨迹，通过序贯检验和失败触发的参考回合，提供有限样本保证，确保加速诱导失败率低于用户预算。3. 部署最快的认证候选加速器，若无合格则回退到参考策略。4. 仅依赖终端结果和测量算力，适用于多种加速机制。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件上使用 OpenVLA-OFT，CARE 认证了 9.0–10.8× 加速，且以 95% 置信度保证至少保留 85.8% 参考策略可解决的回合。在严格预算下，无保证的选择器在高达 75% 的试验中超出预算，而 CARE 始终在预算内，且序贯版本比穷举评估少用 78.9% 的回合。CARE 还泛化到 π_{0.5} 的流步减少，以及 Crafter 环境中的 Qwen3.5-9B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 智能体。

Conclusion: CARE 提供了一种通用的、有理论保证的加速器选择框架，能在保证任务成功率不显著下降的前提下大幅提升 VLA 推理速度。其基于配对轨迹的认证机制解决了加速风险被平均指标掩盖的问题，且序贯检测显著降低了认证成本，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: While vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly, running them at every control step remains expensive. Prior work accelerates VLA inference using techniques like action chunking and visual-token pruning, typically evaluating based on latency and average task success. However, acceleration may discard information and break tasks the original policy would solve, a risk hidden by average metrics. Measuring these failures is challenging because action deviations compound over closed-loop trajectories, meaning task failure is only observable across full episodes. We therefore define an acceleration-induced failure via paired rollouts from identical initial conditions, tracking when the reference succeeds but the accelerated policy fails. To manage this, we introduce CARE, an approach for certified accelerator selection. CARE uses paired rollouts on a calibration set to provide finite-sample guarantees that acceleration-induced failure risk stays below a user-specified budget. It deploys the fastest certified candidate, falling back to the reference if none qualify. By relying only on terminal outcomes and measured compute, CARE applies unchanged across diverse acceleration mechanisms, while sequential testing and failure-triggered reference rollouts keep certification affordable. On four LIBERO suites with OpenVLA-OFT, CARE certifies $9.0$--$10.8\times$ speedups while guaranteeing (at $95\%$ confidence) that at least $85.8\%$ of reference-solved episodes are preserved. Under tight budgets, selectors without guarantees exceed the budget in up to $75\%$ of trials, whereas CARE stays within budget and its sequential form uses $78.9\%$ fewer rollouts than exhaustive evaluation. CARE further generalizes to flow-step reduction for $π_{0.5}$, and to Qwen3.5-9B and Llama-3.1-8B agents in Crafter.

</details>


### [14] [ExperienceIndex: Artifact-Grounded Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10091)
*Peter Baile Chen,Geoffrey X. Yu,Xinming Liu,Samuel Madden,Dan Roth,Jacob Andreas,Doug Downey,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExperienceIndex，一种用于AI代理的经验层，通过捕获和复用基于先前推理轨迹的工件知识，显著提升知识密集型任务的回答质量并降低在线成本，同时展现跨任务泛化和教师-学生学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理缺乏合适的记忆解决方案来积累和复用工件层面的经验性知识，现有记忆方案主要关注用户偏好、事实属性或抽象推理模式，而非持久的工件特定知识，导致回答质量较低且在线成本较高。

Method: 引入ExperienceIndex作为轻量级中间件，存储两种互补经验：(i) 单工件经验，总结工件对先前任务的贡献；(ii) 工件对经验，编码过去推理中发现的结构关系。通过经验检索机制指导代理快速定位新任务所需的完整相关工件集合。

Result: 在多种语料库和不同搜索框架的代理方案中，ExperienceIndex持续带来增益：回答质量最高提升11.0分，在线美元成本最高降低50.5%。进一步验证了跨任务泛化（如从text-to-SQL迁移到同语料库上的事实问答）和教师-学生学习（强模型经验助弱模型达到相当性能）。

Conclusion: ExperienceIndex有效解决了知识密集型任务中工件经验的积累与复用难题，通过结构化经验存储与检索，同时提升答案质量与检索效率，并展现出跨任务迁移与模型蒸馏的潜力，为构建可持续进化的AI代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive tasks require answering many questions by reasoning about a shared corpus of artifacts (e.g., court cases, or scientific literature). As humans interact with these corpora, they naturally accumulate experiential knowledge about artifacts, enabling them to quickly identify the complete set of relevant artifacts for each new task. However, existing AI agents lack appropriate memory solutions to build or reuse such artifact-grounded experience, leading to lower answer quality and higher online cost. Existing memory solutions extract and reuse information from prior task-solving traces, but they primarily focus on user preferences, factual attributes, or abstract reasoning patterns rather than persistent artifact-specific knowledge. We introduce ExperienceIndex, a novel experience layer for AI agents that captures and reuses knowledge about artifacts based on prior reasoning traces. ExperienceIndex stores two complementary forms of experience: (i) single-artifact experiences that summarize an artifact's contribution to prior tasks and (ii) artifact-pair experiences that encode structural relationships discovered during past reasoning. Integrated as lightweight middleware, ExperienceIndex uses an experience retrieval mechanism to guide agents toward the complete set of relevant artifacts for new tasks, improving both answer quality and efficiency. Across diverse corpora and agentic solutions with different search frameworks, ExperienceIndex delivers consistent gains, raising answer quality by up to 11.0 points and reducing online dollar cost by up to 50.5%. We further demonstrate two benefits: (i) cross-task generalization, where experiences accumulated from text-to-SQL tasks transfer to factoid QA tasks over the same artifact corpus, and (ii) teacher-student learning, where experiences from a stronger model enable a weaker model to reach comparable performance.

</details>


### [15] [Quad-State Safety Evaluation of Open-Weight Large Language Models on Non-Canonical Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09033)
*Pavan Maddula*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗表面形式鲁棒性数据集（ASRD），评估大语言模型在表情符号、隐形Unicode、Leetspeak、编码包装器等七种表面变体下的安全性表现，引入四态评估量表揭示模型在不同扰动下的有害合规率、理解失败率及三类异常响应行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估主要针对规范纯文本，但实际部署中模型常面临表情符号、变体拼写、编码字符串等表面形式扰动，需系统评估模型在真实对抗性表面形式下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建包含2100个提示词的ASRD数据集，涵盖七种表面形式族；评估五个开放权重模型，共10500个响应；采用四态评估量表将响应分类为有害合规、安全响应、理解失败、不确定四类；并人工检查原始输出识别响应行为模式。

Result: 表情符号和隐形Unicode变体几乎不导致理解失败，有害合规率分别为20.27%和17.20%（基线22.87%，主要由Mistral 7B驱动）；Leetspeak、编码包装器、混合变换的有害合规率分别为2.40%、0.13%、2.40%，但理解失败率显著上升至36.47%、65.60%、34.47%；发现三类异常响应行为：幻觉良性、结构崩溃、语言漂移。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在语义保留的表面扰动（如表情符号、隐形Unicode）下仍倾向于有害合规，而在破坏语义结构的扰动（如Leetspeak、编码包装器）下则主要表现为理解失败，揭示了当前安全对齐在表面形式鲁棒性上的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Standard safety evaluations of large language models assess harmful requests written in canonical plain text, while models in real-world deployment routinely receive inputs containing emojis, altered spellings, encoded strings, and character-level variations. This work introduces the Adversarial Surface-Form Robustness Dataset (ASRD), comprising 2,100 prompts across seven distinct surface-form families. Five open-weight language models are evaluated across these prompts, producing 10,500 responses. The Quad-State Evaluation Rubric classifies each response into one of four outcomes: harmful compliance, safe response, comprehension failure, or indeterminate. Emoji and invisible Unicode variations cause almost no comprehension failure, with pooled harmful compliance of 20.27% and 17.20% against a 22.87% baseline that is driven mainly by Mistral 7B, whereas leetspeak, encoded wrappers, and hybrid transformations score 2.40%, 0.13%, and 2.40% while comprehension failure rises to 36.47%, 65.60%, and 34.47%. Inspection of raw model outputs reveals three response behaviors: hallucinated benignity, structural collapse, and language drift. Project page: www.pavanmaddula.com/quadstate

</details>


### [16] [U-Space: Uncovering When and Why Uncertainty Arises in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09087)
*Tobias Braun,Nils Loose,Alexander Herzog,Virginia Ceccatelli,Marcus Rohrbach,Thomas Eisenbarth,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出U-Space，一种低维子空间，通过机制性可解释性使大语言模型推理过程中的不确定性可测量且可解释，无需标签、重复生成或训练，在推理基准上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险决策中应用日益广泛，但其错误后果严重，现有不确定性量化方法需重复生成或单独训练组件，且标量估计无法揭示不确定性的来源及演变；生成长度与不确定性估计和正确性强相关，引发对估计器预测能力来源的质疑。

Method: 基于机制性可解释性，识别怀疑和确定的语义锚点，将其unembedding方向映射回残差空间，构建正交基；U-Lens将每个token状态投影到这些基向量上，生成可解释的token级不确定性图谱，可聚合为标量不确定性分数。

Result: 该方法无需正确性标签、重复生成或训练，在推理基准测试中，其置信度分数在标准和长度控制评估下均优于现有基线，且比监督估计器迁移更可靠。

Conclusion: U-Space通过机制性可解释性实现了模型不确定性的可测量和可解释，提供了一种无需监督的、token级的不确定性量化方法，具有更好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models are informing decisions with ever-higher stakes. As the consequences of their errors grow, a central question becomes harder to ignore: how much can we trust an individual answer? Yet recognizing when to defer remains difficult because language models can present incorrect conclusions with fluent explanations and an authoritative tone. Uncertainty quantification seeks to address this disconnect by estimating the reliability of individual predictions. However, many existing methods require repeated generations or separately trained components, and their scalar estimates do not reveal where uncertainty arises or how it evolves during reasoning. Recent work has also shown that generation length can be strongly associated with uncertainty estimates and correctness, raising the question of how much of an estimator's predictive power comes from uncertainty-specific information rather than output length alone. Mechanistic interpretability offers a way to address these limitations by connecting human-interpretable concepts to intermediate model states. Building on this capability, we introduce the U-Space, a low-dimensional subspace that makes a model's evolving uncertainty measurable and interpretable. We identify semantic anchors for doubt and certainty, map their unembedding directions back into the residual space, and combine their contrasts into an orthogonal basis. The U-Lens projects each token state onto these basis vectors, yielding an interpretable token-level uncertainty map that can be inspected directly or aggregated into a scalar uncertainty score. Our approach requires no correctness labels, repeated generations, or training. Across reasoning benchmarks, its confidence score outperforms established baselines under both standard and length-controlled evaluation and transfers more reliably than supervised estimators. Code: https://github.com/s2labres/U-Space.

</details>


### [17] [Same Text, Different Prediction: Serving-Context Nondeterminism in Text Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09111)
*Santhosh Kumar Kasa,Siva Rajesh Kasa,Sumit Negi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究文本分类中的服务上下文非不变性，训练180个模型（判别式、伪生成式、完全生成式），评估四类服务上下文变化。发现标签稳定不等于分数稳定；batch shape变化在fp32下不改变标签，但在bf16下可移动高达56.7%预测概率质量，标签变化集中于小边际；完全生成式分类器比判别式更敏感。给出各服务变化下标签稳定的充分条件及缓解措施，量化了可复现文本分类必须固定的服务条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明文本生成中服务上下文（batch大小、组成、硬件、推理引擎）会改变生成文本，归因于浮点非结合性、形状相关内核选择等数值执行差异，但这些因素是否、何时、以何程度影响文本分类尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统量化文本分类器在服务上下文变化下的非不变性。

Method: 训练180个模型，涵盖判别式、伪生成式、完全生成式三种分类器形式；固定检查点和输入文本，在四类服务上下文（如batch shape、硬件、推理引擎、精度等）下评估；对比fp32与bf16精度下的标签稳定性、预测概率质量变化及不同分类器形式的敏感度差异；推导各服务变化下标签稳定的充分条件并提出针对性缓解方案。

Result: 标签稳定不蕴含分数稳定。仅改变batch shape在fp32下零标签变化，但在bf16下可移动高达56.7个百分点的预测概率质量，标签翻转集中于决策边界小边际样本。完全生成式分类器在相同服务变化下比判别式分类器改变更多标签。给出了每类服务变化的标签稳定充分条件及对应缓解措施。

Conclusion: 研究识别并量化了必须固定的服务条件以实现可复现文本分类，揭示了数值精度（bf16 vs fp32）和分类器形式对服务上下文敏感度的关键影响，为确定性推理提供了理论条件和实用缓解策略。

Abstract: Deterministic inference is essential for reliable and trustworthy machine learning. Prior studies of text generation have shown that changing factors such as batch size, batch composition, hardware, or inference engine can alter the generated text, even when the prompt, model parameters, and sampling randomness are fixed. These differences have been attributed in part to floating-point non-associativity, shape-dependent kernel selection, and other implementation-level differences in numerical execution. However, it remains unclear whether, when, and to what extent the same factors affect text classification. We present a systematic study of serving-context non-invariance in text classifiers, which prior work has measured only through generated text. We train 180 models spanning discriminative, pseudo-generative, and fully generative classifier formulations and evaluate each across four categories of serving contexts, holding the checkpoint and the text fixed. Label stability does not imply score stability. Changing only the batch shape changes no labels across fp32 comparisons, yet under bf16 it moves up to 56.7 percentage points of predicted probability mass, with label changes concentrated at small margins. Fully generative classifiers change more labels than their discriminative counterparts under the same serving changes. We derive sufficient conditions for label stability under each serving change and give a separate mitigation for each mechanism. Our results identify and quantify the serving conditions that must be fixed for reproducible text classification.

</details>


### [18] [sk-bench: A Native-First Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models in Slovak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09152)
*Marek Šuppa,Ivan Vykopal,Andrej Ridzik,Kristián Sopkovič,Natália Kňažeková,Jaroslav Kopčan,Miroslav Blšták,Viktória Ondrejová,Daniel Hládek,Michal Gregor,Martin Tamajka,Marián Šimko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 sk-bench，首个面向斯洛伐克语的原生基准测试，包含 30 个数据集、33 个评分任务变体，涵盖十大技能类别；评测 55 个开/闭源模型，发现最佳开源模型落后专有 API 12.6 分，原生与翻译数据排名高度相关 (ρ≥0.98)，但人工与 LLM 生成问答排名差异大 (ρ=0.72)；持续预训练导致 Qwen3-14B 分数下降 13.9 分，少量指令微调可恢复 3/4；测试时推理为 ≥9B 模型带来 8.5–12.5 分提升；总结出四条面向低资源语言的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言 LLM 基准要么忽略斯洛伐克语（一种拥有 500 万使用者、形态丰富的西斯拉夫语），要么仅通过机器翻译覆盖，缺乏原生评测资源，无法准确反映模型在该语言上的真实能力。

Method: 构建 sk-bench 原生基准：整合 30 个数据集（33 个评分任务变体），分十大技能类别；新增或首次打包 11 个资源用于生成式 LLM 评测，含适配斯洛伐克语的 IFEval-SK 指令检查器、原生 Chiby/SKJ1 语法形态资源；在统一评测框架下评测 55 个开/闭权重模型；对比原生与翻译闭合式数据、人工与 LLM 生成问答的排名相关性；对 Qwen3-14B 进行持续斯洛伐克语预训练与指令微调消融；测试不同规模模型的测试时推理效果。

Result: 最佳开源模型落后专有 API 12.6 分；原生与翻译闭合式数据模型排名高度一致 (ρ≥0.98)，但翻译难以区分最强模型；人工与 LLM 生成问答排名差异显著 (ρ=0.72)；Qwen3-14B 持续预训练导致总分下降 13.9 分，少量指令数据恢复 3/4 损失；测试时推理为 ≥9B 模型带来 8.5–12.5 分提升。

Conclusion: 为低资源语言构建基准的四条设计经验：1) 在翻译失效处使用原生数据；2) 语言适配后规划指令修复；3) 在扩大模型前先启用测试时推理；4) 避免过度投入目标语言提示工程。数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual LLM benchmarks omit Slovak, a morphologically rich West Slavic language of five million speakers, or cover it only by machine translation. We present sk-bench, a native-first Slovak benchmark with 30 datasets (33 scored task variants) across ten skill categories. Eleven resources are introduced or first packaged for generative-LLM evaluation, including IFEval-SK with Slovak-adapted instruction checkers and native Chiby/SKJ1 resources for Slovak grammar and morphology. We evaluate 55 open- and closed-weights models under one harness. The best open model trails proprietary APIs by 12.6 points. Model rankings are similar for native and translated closed-form data ($ρ\geq0.98$), though translation separates the strongest models less well. By contrast, human-authored and LLM-generated QA questions rank models differently ($ρ=0.72$). For Qwen3-14B, continued Slovak pretraining lowers the overall score by 13.9 points. A small instruction set restores three quarters of that loss. Test-time reasoning improves scores by 8.5 to 12.5 points for models of 9B and above. Together, these findings suggest four design lessons for other under-resourced languages: use native data where translation fails, plan instruction repair after language adaptation, enable test-time reasoning before scaling up, and avoid overinvesting in target-language prompts. We release the data and code at https://github.com/slovak-nlp/sk-bench

</details>


### [19] [ToolRACER: A Robust Agentic Conversation Emulation Resource for Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09163)
*Arkajyoti Chakraborty,Aryan Tayal,Ishika Agarwal,Tanner Sorensen,Justin Chiu,Alessandro Di Bari,Neha Gupta,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 ToolRACER，一个合成数据生成管道，用于创建包含对抗性用户行为的鲁棒多轮任务型对话基准 ToolRACERBench。在该基准上训练的模型在 τ²-bench 和 ACEBench 上显著提升了端到端代理准确率，尤其在小语言模型混合领域内数据时效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的函数调用基准主要关注成功的合作交互，缺乏对抗性对话轨迹的表示，导致任务型对话代理在真实世界中面对非合作用户行为时表现脆弱，训练资源不足以开发鲁棒代理。

Method: ToolRACER 协调用户、助手和工具仿真模型，生成并验证多轮用户-代理交互。构建了跨六个领域、55 个角色、5.6K 条对话轨迹的 ToolRACERBench，其中约 66% 包含易失败场景，并注入对抗性行为以捕获真实鲁棒场景。

Result: 在 ToolRACERBench 上训练的模型在内部基准、τ²-bench、BFCLv3 和 ACEBench 上评估，显著提升了 τ²-bench 和 ACEBench 上的端到端代理准确率；在小语言模型中混合领域内数据时，代理能力任务增益显著。

Conclusion: ToolRACER 通过合成对抗性多轮对话数据，有效解决了现有基准缺乏鲁棒性场景的问题，为训练更鲁棒的任务型对话代理提供了高质量资源，且在小模型上表现出良好的迁移效果。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents remain fragile under real world conversation scenarios as they rarely follow a predictable script, especially when users exhibit non-cooperative behavior. Existing function-calling benchmarks often emphasize successful, cooperative interactions and underrepresent adversarial conversation trajectories, thereby limiting the training resources available for developing robust agents. We present ToolRACER, a synthetic data generation pipeline that coordinates user, assistant and tool emulation models to generate and validated multi-turn interactions between a user and an agent. Using \sysn, we construct ToolRACERBench a robust multi-turn conversation benchmark spanning six domains, ranging over 55 varied personas, generating a validated corpus of 5.6K conversation trajectories, with approximately 66\% of conversations containing failure-prone conversation scenarios. We inject adversarial behaviors, producing validated conversational interaction trajectories that capture realistic, robust scenarios. We evaluate models trained on ToolRACERBench against internal benchmarks, as well as on function calling benchmarks such as $τ^2$-bench, BFCLv3 and ACEBench to evaluate agentic accuracy and robustness. Models trained on ToolRACERBench improve end to end agentic accuracy across $τ^2$-bench and ACEBench, demonstrating significant gains when mixed with in-domain dataset in small language models for agent capability tasks.

</details>


### [20] [Multi-Objective Aligned Small Language Model Framework for SUD Patient Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09209)
*Thushara Manjari Naduvilakandy,Hyeju Jang,Mohammad Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种认知基础框架，用于物质使用障碍(SUD)患者对话生成，通过显式建模和对齐潜在认知成分，结合知识蒸馏、偏好优化和注意力引导奖励塑形，显著提升小语言模型在认知实现和对齐方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在生成认知连贯、临床真实的患者行为方面存在不足，且部署前沿大模型面临高计算成本、延迟、隐私和资源受限环境部署受限等挑战，因此需要认知对齐的小语言模型。

Method: 两阶段流程：认知成分检测和认知成分对齐对话生成。采用知识蒸馏、人工标注偏好优化、注意力引导奖励塑形相结合的方式训练小模型。

Result: 实验表明，认知信息微调在认知实现和对齐上显著优于通用指令微调基线和心理健康领域特定小模型，尤其在开放式认知成分上增益明显。自动指标(BERTScore, ROUGE, METEOR, BLEU)和LLM-as-judge命中指标均验证了改进。

Conclusion: 该认知基础框架有效解决了SUD咨询中患者对话生成的认知连贯性和临床真实性问题，为资源受限环境下的认知对齐小模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Substance Use Disorder (SUD) counseling requires patient responses that reflect underlying cognitive states such as beliefs, coping strategies, and readiness for change. Although large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent text, they often fail to produce cognitively coherent and clinically realistic patient behavior, especially under ethical and data-scarce clinical settings. Moreover, deploying frontier-scale LLMs in healthcare applications presents practical challenges including high computational cost, latency, privacy concerns, and limited deployability in resource-constrained environments, motivating the need for cognitively aligned small language models (SLMs). We propose a cognitively grounded framework for SUD patient dialogue generation that explicitly models and aligns latent cognitive components with patient histories and counselor questions. Our pipeline consists of two stages: cognitive component detection and cognitive component-aligned dialogue generation. To enable effective learning with smaller models, we combine knowledge distillation from high-capacity teacher models, preference optimization from human-annotations, and attention-guided reward shaping. Extensive evaluations using automatic scores like BERTScore, ROUGE, METEOR and BLEU, and LLM-as-judge hit-metrics against both human and teacher-model references show that cognitively informed fine-tuning substantially improves cognitive realization and alignment over a generic instruction-tuned baselines and mental health domain specific SLMs, with particularly strong gains for open-ended cognitive components.

</details>


### [21] [Dialect-Robust Speech Language Models with Synthetic Pseudo-Dialect Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09321)
*Shunsuke Mitsumori,Tomoya Mizumoto,Yusuke Fujita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需真实方言语音数据的伪方言语音合成方法，通过LLM生成方言文本并利用标准语言TTS模型转换，结合中间标准文本预测作为语义归一化，在日语、德语和中文方言的语音翻译任务上显著提升了SLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型（SLM）在方言上的表现因数据稀缺而下降。传统TTS增强难以覆盖多样方言，因为需要一定量的真实方言语音。因此需要一种无需真实方言语音资源的可扩展方言数据增强方法。

Method: 1. 利用LLM生成方言文本，再通过标准语言TTS模型合成伪方言语音，实现零真实方言语音需求。2. 在训练中引入中间标准文本预测任务，作为下游任务的语义归一化机制。3. 在日语、德语、中文方言的方言到英语语音翻译任务上评估。

Result: 与合成标准语音基线相比，伪方言增强使日语得分从25.38提升至26.24，德语从31.57提升至32.47。中间标准文本预测进一步弥合语义鸿沟，将日语提升至28.26，中文从11.67提升至16.37。

Conclusion: 该方法无需各方言特定的语音资源，即可扩展到多种语言的方言理解任务，验证了伪方言合成与语义归一化结合的有效性。

Abstract: Speech Language Model (SLM) performance often degrades on dialects due to data scarcity. Conventional text-to-speech (TTS) augmentation struggles to cover diverse dialects as it requires a certain amount of real dialect speech. We propose synthesizing pseudo-dialect speech by converting LLM-generated dialect text via a standard-language TTS model, requiring zero real dialect speech. Additionally, we introduce intermediate standard-text prediction during training, acting as semantic normalization for downstream tasks. We evaluate dialect understanding via dialect-to-English speech translation across Japanese, German, and Chinese dialects. Compared to synthetic standard speech baselines, pseudo-dialect augmentation improves scores for Japanese (from 25.38 to 26.24) and German (from 31.57 to 32.47). Furthermore, the intermediate standard-text prediction effectively bridges the semantic gap, boosting performance to 28.26 for Japanese and from 11.67 to 16.37 for Chinese. These results suggest that our approach scales to various languages without requiring speech resources specific to each dialect.

</details>


### [22] [OnlineQAT: On-Policy Distillation for Ultra-Low-Bit Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09346)
*Wenjun Wang,Heng Li,Yanggan Gu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 OnlineQAT，一种两阶段量化感知训练框架：首先通过分块 QAT 获得可用的低比特初始化，然后在学生模型自生成的响应上进行在线策略蒸馏（OPD），利用冻结的全精度教师模型在学生访问的前缀上提供反向 KL 损失信号。实验表明，该方法在 Qwen3-1.7B 上达到 W3A16 57.28 和 W2A16 32.52 的最佳平均性能，显著优于现有方法，证明学生自访问状态提供了超越固定完成训练的有效恢复信号，尤其在三比特量化下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化恢复阶段通常在固定完成或教师生成的答案上优化，但部署后的量化模型会基于自身生成的前缀进行条件生成。量化误差会将模型推向离线恢复数据中不存在的状态，导致性能下降。因此需要一种能够覆盖学生模型实际访问状态的在线恢复机制。

Method: OnlineQAT 包含两个阶段：1) 分块量化感知训练（block-wise QAT）获得可用的低比特初始化；2) 在线策略蒸馏（OPD），在学生模型自生成的响应上训练，冻结的全精度教师模型在每个被访问的前缀上提供采样的反向 KL 散度训练信号，使学生模型在自身分布上对齐教师模型。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 上，OnlineQAT 在对比量化方法中获得最佳平均性能：W3A16 为 57.28，W2A16 为 32.52，分别比 ReasoningQAT 提升 2.90 和 0.44 点。结果表明学生访问的状态提供了超越固定完成训练的有用恢复信号，特别是在三比特量化下效果更明显。

Conclusion: OnlineQAT 通过在线策略蒸馏利用学生模型自访问的前缀分布进行恢复训练，有效解决了量化误差导致的分布偏移问题。该方法在低比特量化（特别是 3-bit）下显著优于现有基于离线数据的恢复方法，证明了在线自生成数据对量化模型恢复的重要价值。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) can recover much of the accuracy lost when large language models are compressed below four bits. Existing re- covery stages, however, are commonly optimized on fixed completions or teacher-generated answers, whereas the deployed quantized model condi- tions on prefixes generated by itself. Quantization errors can therefore move the model into states that are absent from offline recovery data. We introduce OnlineQAT, a two-stage framework that first obtains a usable low-bit initialization through block-wise QAT and then performs on-policy distillation (OPD) on student-generated responses. At each visited pre- fix, a frozen full-precision teacher provides a sampled reverse-KL training signal. On Qwen3-1.7B, OnlineQAT obtains the best average among the compared quantized methods: 57.28 at W3A16 and 32.52 at W2A16, im- proving over ReasoningQAT by 2.90 and 0.44 points, respectively. The results suggest that student-visited states provide a useful recovery signal beyond fixed-completion training, particularly at three bits.

</details>


### [23] [Expert Coupling in MoE Pretraining: Reducing All-to-All Overhead with Correlated Placement and Token Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09372)
*Radha Gulhane,Quentin Anthony,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种方法减少 MoE 专家并行中的 all-to-all 通信开销：相关专家放置和 Token 打乱。利用预训练早期路由器学到的专家选择相关性，将常共同被选的专家放在同一 GPU，并在注意力后的 reduce-scatter 阶段将 Token 移动到预测持有下一层专家的 GPU。实验显示在 Megatron-LM 上，all-to-all 时间降低 1.16-2.63 倍，端到端步长时间最高加速 1.41 倍，且不改变路由决策和专家参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家并行（EP）下，MoE 层的前向和后向传播需要 all-to-all 集合通信来分发和聚合 Token。在 8 张 AMD MI300X GPU 的节点上，EP32 下 top-2 路由的通信占训练步长 45%，top-6 占 60%。通信开销成为主要瓶颈，亟需在不改变模型行为的前提下减少跨 GPU 和跨节点通信。

Method: 1. 相关专家放置：利用预训练早期路由器表现出的专家选择相关性（同层内 0.8% 专家对被 42% Token 共同选中，跨层专家选择可预测），将经常被一起选中的专家放置在同一 GPU 上。结合仅将每个 Token 发送到每个 GPU 一次的分发器，最多可移除 58% 的分发行。2. Token 打乱：当序列并行在 EP 组内分片 Token 时，在注意力后的 reduce-scatter 阶段，根据预测将每个 Token 移动到预期持有其下一层专家的 GPU。单节点上使本地 Token-专家分配比例从 12.5% 提升至 59%。两种方法均不改变路由决策和专家参数。

Result: 在 Megatron-LM 中，跨 EP 度 8 到 64、top-2 和 top-6 路由，两种方法将 all-to-all 时间减少 1.16-2.63 倍，端到端训练步长时间最高加速 1.41 倍。

Conclusion: 通过利用路由器早期学到的专家选择相关性，相关专家放置和 Token 打乱能显著降低 MoE 专家并行的通信开销，在保持模型精度和路由行为不变的情况下实现大幅加速，适用于各种 EP 规模和路由配置。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers replace the feed-forward block of a Transformer with E expert networks, and each token is routed to k of these experts. Under expert parallelism (EP) the experts are distributed across GPUs, and every MoE layer runs all-to-all collectives in the forward and backward passes to dispatch tokens to their experts and then combine the results. On a cluster with 8 AMD Instinct MI300X GPUs per node, these collectives can take 45% of the training step at EP32 with top-2 routing and 60% with top-6 routing. We find that early in pretraining routers have already learned to assign tokens to experts in correlated patterns, both within a layer and across layers. At top-2, 0.8% of the expert pairs in a layer are selected together by 42% of tokens, and the experts a token selects at one layer predict the experts it selects at the next layer. We use these correlations to keep more token--expert assignments on the token's own GPU, which reduces communication across GPUs and across nodes. Correlated expert placement puts experts that are often selected together on the same GPU. Combined with a dispatcher that sends each token to each GPU once, it removes up to 58% of dispatched rows. Token shuffling applies when sequence parallelism shards tokens across the EP group. It moves each token to the GPU predicted to hold its next-layer experts during the reduce-scatter that follows attention. On one node this raises the share of token--expert assignments served on the token's GPU from 12.5% to 59%. In Megatron-LM, across EP degrees from 8 to 64 with top-2 and top-6 routing, the two methods reduce all-to-all time by 1.16-2.63X and end-to-end step time by up to 1.41X. Neither method changes the models' underlying routing decisions or expert parameters.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Language models are routinely fine-tuned under a fixed context, such as a generic system prompt, persona or domain-specific instruction, yet the learned behavior sometimes stays confined to that context and sometimes broadly generalizes to unseen contexts. We propose the Persona Hierarchy Model to explain this: a shared default persona influences behavior across contexts. Under this model, fine-tuning that modifies the shared persona promotes broader transfer, whereas changes to local personas remain more context-specific. Across 120 fine-tuned models spanning four behaviors and 15 training contexts, generalization narrowness positively correlates with the similarity between the training context's persona and the default persona (Pearson's r = 0.72 for Qwen3-4B). Prior fine-tuning under the default context can broaden generalization in subsequent training under other contexts. Aligning contextual responses with default-persona responses produces stronger effects. Finally, we propose persona-preserving regularization (PPR) to confine undesired contextual generalization. In RL, PPR cuts reward hacking from 42-55% to at most 0.2% under every evaluated prompt while retaining accuracy gains. These results support the Persona Hierarchy Model as an explanation for contextual generalization and can motivate future controls on unintended generalization for better alignment of LLMs.

</details>

Abstract: As text-to-SQL systems move beyond demonstrations toward real-world deployment, ambiguity in user questions becomes a primary source of errors. Such ambiguities are often subtle, domain- or data-specific, and can silently cause system outputs to deviate from the user's true intent. Ambiguity is traditionally addressed through conversational clarification, which is often inefficient, cognitively demanding, and poorly aligned with real-world user workflows. We propose structured disambiguation, a new paradigm in which ambiguity is resolved through explicit, constrained interactions rather than free-form dialogue. We construct ARCS (Ambiguity Resolution Corpus for SQL), the first text-to-SQL benchmark featuring naturally occurring, unconstrained ambiguities over real-world databases, with complete annotations of all valid ambiguity points, interpretations, and SQL queries. Experimental results show that text-to-SQL remains challenging in the presence of ambiguity: gpt-6-sol achieves only 51% end-to-end execution accuracy, and no open-source model exceeds 27%.

</details>


### [26] [Arctic Questions, Missing Answers: A Dataset and Benchmark for LLM Abstention in Arctic Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09446)
*Benjamin Wilcox,Dawei Gao,Pradeeban Kathiravelu,Douglas Causey,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了ArcticQA数据集和ArcticAbstain基准，用于评估大语言模型在科学选择题中面对无有效选项时的弃权行为，发现模型弃权率差异巨大（0-63%），且仅在正确答案被替换时弃权率平均仅增加5.05个百分点，强调需同时评估弃权频率和响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型应在科学选择题无有效选项时弃权，但仅观察高弃权率不足以证明模型对答案可用性敏感。现有基准缺乏系统性评估模型区分‘有答案’与‘无答案’条件的能力，且缺乏基于真实科研文献的高质量验证数据集。

Method: 构建ArcticQA数据集（194道源自北极研究文献的选择题），通过自动化检查验证答案支持性和干扰项矛盾性；开发ArcticAbstain配对基准，对比‘有正确答案’与‘无正确答案（被干扰项替换）’两种条件，两者均包含显式弃权选项；评估Gemini、Claude、ChatGPT三大模型家族共8个模型，高推理努力下每条件3次试验，共9,312次响应。

Result: 有答案条件下弃权率跨度0.0%-63.0%，基线差异显著；将正确答案替换为干扰项后，平均弃权率仅增加5.05个百分点，表明模型对答案缺失的敏感度有限；需联合考察弃权频率与条件响应性。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在科学选择题的弃权行为存在显著基线差异，且对答案可用性的响应不足。ArcticQA与ArcticAbstain为评估模型‘知不知’能力提供了严谨基准，未来研究应重点提升模型对无效选项的识别与弃权校准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) should abstain from scientific multiple-choice questions when no option is valid, but frequent abstention alone does not demonstrate sensitivity to answer availability. We introduce ArcticQA, a dataset of 194 questions derived from primary Arctic research, with automated checks of answer support and distractor contradiction against source evidence. We further develop ArcticAbstain, a paired benchmark comparing answer-present and answer-absent conditions, with the correct answer replaced by a distractor in the latter and an explicit abstention option in both. We evaluate eight models from the Gemini, Claude, and ChatGPT families at high reasoning effort, with three trials per condition, yielding 9,312 recorded responses. Answer-present abstention rates range from 0.0% to 63.0%, whereas replacing the correct answer increases abstention by 5.05 percentage points on average. These findings highlight substantial baseline differences and the need to evaluate abstention frequency and responsiveness jointly. The dataset and benchmark are available at https://github.com/BenWilcox8/arctic-qa.

</details>


### [27] [Right Number, Wrong State? Measuring Cross-Jurisdiction Substitution in LLM Recall of State Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09458)
*Jiayu Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过最小集设计测试大语言模型在州特定政策问题上的错误是否为跨司法管辖区替换，发现Claude Sonnet 5.5和GPT-5.6 Sol存在可复现的跨州替换，但归因脆弱，需完整的同州参考集才能准确判定跨司法管辖区错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当大语言模型回答州特定政策问题错误时，可能是产生幻觉，也可能是返回了另一州的真实值。研究旨在区分这两种情况，评估跨司法管辖区错误的普遍性和可靠性。

Method: 采用最小集设计：固定问题措辞，仅变更司法管辖区（覆盖50个美国州及哥伦比亚特区共51个司法管辖区），测试三个精确定义的Medicaid收入资格指标。黄金标准来自官方数据手册，并经独立来源验证（102个检查单元中101个一致）。在预注册协议下，对Claude Sonnet 5.5和GPT-5.6 Sol进行两次独立重复测试。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5.5和GPT-5.6 Sol分别在153个测试项中有10和25个可复现地给出另一州的当前值。但归因脆弱：将任何错误答案归因为另一州值会产生3-5倍的可复现替换，因为许多表面上的跨州答案实则是该州在另一惯例或早期年份的自身数值。

Conclusion: 关于跨司法管辖区错误的声称需要完整的同州参考集作为基准。研究将发布实验协议、黄金标准表格及所有模型输出以供复现和进一步分析。

Abstract: When an LLM answers a state-specific policy question wrongly, it may be hallucinating, or it may be returning a real value that holds in another state. We test this with a minimal-set design: the question wording is fixed and only the jurisdiction varies, across the 50 U.S. states and the District of Columbia (51 jurisdictions) and three exactly defined Medicaid income-eligibility quantities. Gold values come from an official data book and agree with an independent source in 101 of 102 checked cells. Under a pre-registered protocol, Claude Sonnet 5.5 and GPT-5.6 Sol reproducibly give another state's current value, identical across two independent repeats, for 10 and 25 of 153 items. Attribution is fragile, however. Crediting any wrong answer that equals another state's value yields 3-5x more reproducible substitutions than checking every number in the asked state's own records, because many apparent cross-state answers are the asked state's own values under another convention or from an earlier year. Claims about cross-jurisdiction error need a complete same-state reference set. We will release the protocol, gold table, and all model outputs.

</details>


### [28] [BanglaRhet: Benchmarking Classical and Transformer Models for Rhetorical and Persuasion Detection in Bangla Political Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09464)
*Rohit Kumar Sen,Anik Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基准研究，评估基于Transformer的模型在孟加拉语政治话语中检测修辞手法和说服意图的性能，使用了手工标注的BanglaRhet语料库，发现BanglaBERT表现最优，显著优于传统TF-IDF基线，并指出了语义重叁、比喻语言和类别不平衡导致的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语自然语言处理在情感分析和观点挖掘方面已有进展，但针对政治演讲中细粒度修辞和说服技巧检测的Transformer模型系统基准测试仍缺乏探索，因此需要建立基准以推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建BanglaRhet语料库（30,289个孟加拉语政治演讲片段），制定两个单标签分类任务：修辞技巧检测（对比、重复、夸张、隐喻、反问）和说服技巧检测（归责、行动号召、团结号召、道德/情感/逻辑诉求）。评估四个Transformer模型及TF-IDF基线。

Result: BanglaBERT取得最高性能：修辞技巧检测宏F1 65.40%，说服技巧检测宏F1 66.46%，分别比最佳传统基线高出19.2和13.8个百分点。错误主要源于标签语义重叠、比喻语言和类别不平衡。

Conclusion: 研究为孟加拉语修辞和说服感知政治话语分析提供了初始基准基线，并强调未来需发展上下文感知和多标签建模方法以进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Political discourse often uses rhetorical and persuasive language to frame narratives, influence public opinion, and mobilize audiences. While Bangla natural language processing has made progress in sentiment analysis and opinion mining, systematic benchmarking of transformer models for fine-grained rhetorical and persuasion technique detection in Bangla political speech remains largely underexplored. This paper presents a benchmark study of transformer-based models for detecting rhetorical form and persuasive intent in Bangla political discourse. Using BanglaRhet, a manually annotated corpus of 30,289 Bangla political speech segments collected from publicly available political news sources, we formulate two supervised single-label classification tasks: rhetorical technique detection (contrast, repetition, exaggeration, metaphor, rhetorical questions) and persuasion technique detection (blame assignment, call to action, unity call, moral, emotional, and logical appeals). We evaluate four transformer-based models, BanglaBERT, BanglaBERT-Base, SahajBERT, and XLM-RoBERTa-Base, against classical TF-IDF baselines. BanglaBERT achieves the highest performance, with 65.40% macro-F1 for rhetorical technique detection and 66.46% for persuasion technique detection, outperforming the best tuned classical baseline by 19.2 and 13.8 macro-F1 points, respectively. Class-level analysis indicates that errors are mainly associated with semantic overlap among labels, figurative language, and class imbalance. The results provide initial benchmark baselines for Bangla rhetorical and persuasion-aware political discourse analysis and highlight the need for context-aware and multi-label modeling.

</details>


### [29] [Goldsmith: Gold-Loss-Guided Definition Optimization with an Agentic Annotation Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09489)
*Yihan Li,Hanyi Zhang,Xiaoxi Jiang,Man Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Goldsmith 是一个代理式流水线，将少量专家标注的校准样本（gold set）转化为可复用的结构化标注定义，将该定义视为可训练的文本对象，通过 LLM 编辑器基于文本梯度进行迭代优化，仅在结构化损失下降时接受修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多标注项目在专家尚未形成稳定指南或积累足够训练标签前就已启动，亟需一种方法能利用稀缺的专家监督同时实现任务定义学习与可扩展标注。

Method: Goldsmith 以少量专家标注的校准样本为输入；候选定义在相同样本上运行并由可执行的结构化损失函数打分；LLM 编辑器将损失最高的失败案例转化为文本梯度修订；仅当实测损失下降时接受修订。输出模式、格式化、检索、修复、裁决与人工复核保留在外部套件中。

Result: 在提示优化对比中，Goldsmith 在相同评估协议下优于直接重写、OPRO、APE 与 PromptBreeder。结合检索、基于分数的路由与人工复核，生成的定义在类型化实体跨度、配对关系、固定触发词事件论元三类任务上均提升了下游标注质量。

Conclusion: 稀缺的专家监督足以支撑任务定义学习与大规模标注的双重需求。}

Abstract: Many annotation projects begin before experts have a stable guideline or enough labels to train a task-specific model. We present Goldsmith, an agentic pipeline that turns a small gold set---expert-annotated calibration examples representing the intended task boundaries---into a reusable structured annotation definition. Goldsmith treats this definition as a trainable textual object. Candidate definitions are run on the same gold examples and scored with an executable structured loss, while the output schema, formatting, retrieval, repair, judging, and human review remain in an external harness. A large language model (LLM) editor converts the highest-loss failures into textual-gradient revisions, which are accepted only when the measured loss decreases. In prompt-optimization comparisons, Goldsmith improves over direct rewriting, OPRO, APE, and PromptBreeder under matched evaluation protocols. The resulting definition also improves downstream annotation when combined with retrieval, score-based routing, and human review across typed span, pair-level relation, and fixed-trigger event-argument tasks. These results show that scarce expert supervision can support both task-definition learning and scalable annotation.

</details>

Abstract: There are more than 2,000 listed companies on the UK's London Stock Exchange, divided into 11 sectors who are required to communicate their financial results at least twice in a single financial year. UK annual reports are very lengthy documents with around 80 pages on average. In this study, we aim to benchmark a variety of summarisation methods on a set of different pre-trained transformers with different extraction techniques. In addition, we considered multiple evaluation metrics in order to investigate their differing behaviour and applicability on a dataset from the Financial Narrative Summarisation (FNS 2020) shared task, which is composed of annual reports published by firms listed on the London Stock Exchange and their corresponding summaries. We hypothesise that some evaluation metrics do not reflect true summarisation ability and propose a novel BRUGEscore metric, as the harmonic mean of ROUGE-2 and BERTscore. Finally, we perform a statistical significance test on our results to verify whether they are statistically robust, alongside an adversarial analysis task with three different corruption methods.
