### [31] [RELATE: An Evaluation Framework for measuring Relational Orientation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09569)
*Shivam Shukla,Jihye Kim,Shubham Gaur,Mahnaz Roshanaei,Magy Seif El-Nasr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出关系取向框架 RELATE，用于评估大语言模型在情感支持对话中是将用户引向 AI 依赖（内向型 IF）还是鼓励现实人际连接（外向支架型 OS），通过句级标注发现模型随轮次增加 IF 比例上升，且对犹豫型用户 OS 比例显著低于显性寻求安慰用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 情感支持评估聚焦安全性、共情度或有用性，忽视了模型将用户导向何处寻求持续支持这一关系性问题，担忧长期使用可能疏远用户现实人际关系。

Method: 基于心理学和社会学文献建立关系取向分类法，构建 RELATE 框架：将 76 个自然求助场景改编为三种模拟用户风格，生成 228 评估刺激；对七个 LLM 进行六轮对话，得 1596 个对话和 69194 句助手回复；采用基于评分细则的 LLM 主评委进行句级 IF/OS 标注，并用次级评委验证子集。

Result: 自动评估显示，第六轮助手回复中 IF 标签比例高于第一轮；对于犹豫、间接的模拟用户，OS 标签比例显著低于显性寻求安慰的用户。

Conclusion: RELATE 提供可复现的框架和句级信号，可用于审计和引导支持型 LLM 的关系取向，确保模型促进而非替代现实人际连接。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for emotional support, raising concern that sustained use may draw users away from their real-world relationships. Yet existing evaluations primarily focus on the safety, empathy, or helpfulness of responses, leaving under-examined a relational question: where does the model orient the user for continued support? To address this question, we introduce relational orientation, a property operationalized through two non-exclusive dimensions: inward-facing (IF) language, which positions the AI as the user's ongoing source of support, and outward-scaffolding (OS) language, which encourages real-world human connection. Grounded in psychological and sociological literature, we formalize a taxonomy of relational orientation and present RELATE, a persona-conditioned framework for measuring inward-facing and outward-scaffolding language at the sentence level in multi-turn dialogues. RELATE pairs 76 help-seeking situations adapted from naturally occurring questions with three simulated user styles, providing 228 evaluation stimuli. In our experiments, we evaluate seven LLMs using dialogues with six assistant turns each, yielding 1,596 dialogues and 69,194 assistant sentences. We assess these sentences using a primary rubric-based LLM judge and apply a secondary judge to a subset. Under automated evaluation, we find that the proportion of sentences labeled as IF is higher at the sixth assistant turn than at the first, while the proportion labeled as OS is substantially lower for hesitant, indirect simulated users than for explicit, reassurance-seeking users. RELATE provides a reproducible framework and a sentence-level signal for auditing and steering the relational orientation of supportive LLMs.

</details>


### [32] [How Do LLMs Change Predictions Under Negation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09571)
*Jongwook Yoon,Jongwon Lim,Sungjib Lim,Woojin Cho,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过机制性分析发现大语言模型处理否定语句时依靠抑制原答案并推广备选答案，而非像人类那样利用原答案信息确定排除内容；基于此提出新训练目标，有效减少否定失败且对通用能力影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理否定语句时表现不可靠（37-71% 情况下重复原答案），需深入理解其底层机制并针对性改进，而非仅靠常规微调。

Method: 构建否定基准评测开源/闭源模型；采用机制性可解释性方法（注意力头与 MLP 神经元分析）剖析否定处理电路；对比人类否定处理认知模型；设计基于原答案置信度的动态偏好偏移训练目标进行微调。

Result: 发现模型通过专用注意力头和 MLP 神经元联合实现否定：抑制原答案检索并推广类别内偏好候选，与人类利用原答案信息排除的机制截然不同；该机制差异导致抑制不足或偏向固定答案时出错；提出的训练目标显著降低否定失败率，且通用能力退化少于标准微调基线。

Conclusion: 机制性分析能揭示语言能力失效的根本原因，并指导针对底层局限的训练方法设计，为提升模型否定推理能力提供了可解释且有效的路径。

Abstract: Negation is an essential feature of human language, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable in processing it. We evaluate recent open-source and closed-source LLMs on our negation benchmark and find that, in 37-71% of cases, they repeat the same answer under negation (e.g., "Madrid" for "What is not the capital of Spain?"). To understand and address this brittleness, we mechanistically examine how models operate under negation. Our main finding is that specialized attention heads and MLP neurons jointly implement negation by (1) suppressing retrieval of the original answer (e.g., "Madrid") while (2) promoting a favored candidate within the answer category (e.g., "Paris"). This contrasts with accounts of human negation processing, in which information about the original answer helps to determine what should be excluded. Furthermore, we find that this difference from human processing is a key source of negation failures: the model's mechanism relies on suppressing the original answer rather than using it to determine what to exclude, so the model can repeat the original answer when suppression is too weak or when a bias toward particular answers prevents it from selecting an alternative. To address this weakness in the model's negation mechanism, we propose a training objective that requires larger shifts in answer preference for more confident original predictions, and show that it reduces negation failures with less degradation of general capabilities than standard fine-tuning baselines. Together, our results demonstrate how mechanistic analysis can reveal why a linguistic capability fails and guide training that targets the underlying limitation.

</details>


### [33] [Which Language Should a Skeleton Speak? Language Choices in Multilingual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09607)
*HyeonSeok Lim,SeungWoo Song,Inho Won,Hoyun Song,Jihyo Kim,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言感知骨架探索框架（LASEF），系统研究多语言数学推理中的骨架语言选择。实验表明英语骨架平均呈微弱正向倾向，主要在小模型和低资源语言中体现，但英语并非普遍最优；发现三类骨架语言效应模式（方向一致、评估与基准依赖、非对称负向），且不能完全由生成质量解释。结论认为骨架语言是依赖上下文的设计变量，需多层级探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨架的推理提示方法多假设英语为中心，缺乏对多语言数学推理中骨架语言选择的系统性实证研究，无法指导实际部署中的语言决策。

Method: 构建LASEF框架，结合贪心解码、多轮评估、翻译消融实验及跨基准验证，在多个数学基准、不同模型规模及多种语言上进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 英语骨架平均带来小幅正向增益，小模型和低资源语言更显著；经统计校正后语言级增益显著性大幅下降，英语非普遍最优。识别出三类效应模式：方向一致、依赖评估方式与基准、非对称负向效应；这些效应无法仅用生成质量解释。

Conclusion: 骨架语言选择是一个强依赖上下文（模型规模、语言资源、评估方式、基准任务）的设计变量，不能简单默认英语最优，需进行多层级、多维度的探索与验证。相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Skeleton-based reasoning prompting is a promising training-free approach for structuring LLM reasoning, but prior work largely assumes an English-centric setting. We propose the Language-Aware Skeleton Exploration Framework (LASEF) to study skeleton-language choice in multilingual mathematical reasoning. Across math benchmarks, model scales, and languages, we show that English skeletons yield a small positive tendency on average, most visible for smaller models and low-resource languages. However, few language-level gains remain significant after correction, and English is not universally optimal. Combining greedy decoding, multi-rollout evaluation, translation ablation, and cross-benchmark validation, we further find three patterns of skeleton-language effects: directionally consistent, evaluation- and benchmark-dependent, and asymmetric negative. These effects cannot be fully explained by generation quality alone. Overall, skeleton language is a context-dependent design variable that requires multi-level exploration. All resources are released at https://github.com/lhsstn/LASEF.

</details>


### [34] [On-Policy Distillation Teaches New Skills but Not New Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09639)
*Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究发现：在线策略蒸馏（OPD）主要传递组合推理技能而非事实知识。逆KL散度蒸馏能跨未见推理结构稳健传递组合技能，但极少传递事实知识。将逆KL改为正向KL可恢复事实传递，而学生自主生成轨迹特别提升多步推理执行能力。实验表明OPD不扩展模型参数化知识，而是教会模型组织和组合已有知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在线策略蒸馏（OPD）能增强语言模型推理能力，但学生模型究竟是获得了新的事实知识还是组合性的多步推理技能，此前尚不清楚。

Method: 采用受控合成框架分离两种能力：测量学生初始能力，独立控制教师的额外事实、组合技能或两者兼有。在三个模型家族的四个模型上实验，对比逆KL与正向KL蒸馏，并分析学生自主生成轨迹的作用。进一步在事实问答和竞赛数学任务上验证。

Result: 逆KL OPD可靠地跨未见推理结构传递组合技能，但事实知识传递极少。将逆KL替换为正向KL可恢复事实传递；学生自主生成轨迹特别改善多步推理执行。近期事实问答和竞赛数学实验显示相同不对称性：推理增益显著但事实记忆未扩展。

Conclusion: 在线策略蒸馏不会扩展模型的参数化知识库，而是教会模型组织和组合其已有的知识以进行推理。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) strengthens language-model reasoning, yet whether students acquire new factual knowledge or compositional skill for multi-step reasoning remains unknown. We separate these capabilities using a controlled synthetic framework that measures the student's initial capabilities and independently controls the teacher's additional facts, compositional skill, or both. Across four models from three families, reverse-KL OPD reliably transfers compositional skill across unseen reasoning structures, but transfers minimal factual knowledge. Decoupling the distillation recipe reveals the source of this asymmetry: replacing reverse KL with forward KL restores factual transfer, whereas student rollouts specifically improve the execution of multi-step reasoning. Experiments on recent factual QA and competition mathematics show a similar asymmetry under reverse-KL OPD, yielding notable reasoning gains without factual memory expansion. Together, these results demonstrate that on-policy distillation does not expand a model's parametric knowledge, but instead teaches it to organize and compose the knowledge it already possesses.

</details>


### [35] [Rubric Spans are Label Representations: Joint LLM Encoding for Short Answer Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09660)
*Zhifan Sun,Sebastian Gombert,Fabian Zehner,Leon Camus,Longwei Cong,Hendrik Drachsler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出RUSPAN，一种基于评分标准的自动简答评分(ASAS)框架，将评分标准描述作为语义标签表示，将题目上下文、学生回答和所有候选评分等级序列化为单一序列，通过单次语言模型前向传播从评分标准片段和全序列表示中列表式评分。进一步提出RUSPAN-RIM，引入评分标准无关掩码防止评分标准片段相互注意，使评分标准表示仅依赖答案和题目上下文，防止训练时过拟合评分标准模式，实现零样本迁移。在英、德、葡六个ASAS基准上，RUSPAN优于判别式和生成式基线，RIM结合位置重索引在保留基准PT-ASAG上实现显著且一致的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动简答评分需要模型能根据特定题目的评分标准评分，同时保持高效和跨评分标准集的可迁移性。现有方法可能过拟合评分标准模式，难以泛化到新语言或新评分标准结构。

Method: 1. RUSPAN：将题目上下文、学生回答、所有候选评分等级序列化为单一序列，利用语言模型单次前向传播获得评分标准片段表示和全序列表示，进行列表式评分。2. RUSPAN-RIM：引入评分标准无关掩码(RIM)，防止评分标准片段相互注意，强制评分标准表示仅依赖答案和题目上下文，配合位置重索引技术，防止训练时过拟合评分标准模式，实现零样本迁移。

Result: 在英、德、葡六个ASAS基准上，RUSPAN在单基准评分任务上优于判别式和生成式基线。RUSPAN-RIM结合位置重索引在保留基准PT-ASAG(具有最强语言和评分标准结构分布偏移)上实现一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUSPAN通过将评分标准作为语义标签并列表式建模，有效提升ASAS性能。RIM机制使模型学习到与评分标准无关的表示，显著增强跨语言和跨评分标准结构的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Automatic Short Answer Scoring (ASAS) requires models that can score student responses against question-specific criteria while remaining efficient and transferable across rubric sets. We propose RUSPAN, a rubric-conditioned ASAS framework that treats rubric descriptions as semantic label representations. RUSPAN serialises the question context, student answer, and all candidate rubric levels into a single sequence, then scores the levels listwise from the rubric-span and whole-sequence representations produced in a single LM pass. We further introduce RUSPAN-RIM, in which a Rubric-Independent Mask prevents rubric spans from attending to one another, making rubric representations depend only on the answer and question context and preventing overfitting to rubric patterns during training for zero-shot transfer. On six ASAS benchmarks spanning English, German, and Portuguese, RUSPAN improves mono-benchmark scoring over discriminative and generative baselines, while RIM with position reindexing delivers consistent and substantial gains on PT-ASAG, the held-out benchmark with the strongest combined language and rubric-structure shift.

</details>


### [36] [Alice: A Large-Scale German Benchmark for Rubric-Based Multi-Dimensional Automatic Short Answer Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09661)
*Zhifan Sun,Sebastian Gombert,Jannik Lossjew,Tobias Wyrwich,Berrit Katharina Czinczel,David Bednorz,Marcus Kubsch,Knut Neumann,Hendrik Drachsler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Alice，一个大规模、基于评分量表的德语自动简答评分（ASAS）数据集，包含三个子任务：学习表现、知识要素和技能。将基于评分量表的ASAS表述为评分量表检索任务，并用多种语言模型进行基准测试，发现大语言模型在零样本设置下难以评分知识要素和技能，评分量表文本对知识要素和技能子任务尤为有用，而在学习表现子任务上提升有限且随模型和输入格式变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASAS公开基准稀缺，且多关注学生回答问题的直接质量，而非其对底层概念（知识要素）或认知活动（技能）的掌握程度，导致缺乏教学对齐的评估资源。

Method: 构建了Alice数据集，包含三个子任务：Alice-LP（学习表现）、Alice-KE（知识要素）、Alice-SK（技能）。将基于评分量表的ASAS建模为评分量表检索任务，使用从仅编码器模型到轻量级大语言模型的多种模型进行基准测试，同时对比零样本提示和标准分类基线。

Result: 实验表明：大语言模型在零样本设置下难以准确评分知识要素和技能；评分量表文本对Alice-KE和Alice-SK子任务显著提升性能；在Alice-LP子任务上，评分量表带来的增益较小且随模型和输入格式波动。

Conclusion: Alice填补了教学对齐的ASAS基准空白，揭示了当前大语言模型在细粒度知识要素和技能评分上的局限，强调了评分量表文本对细粒度评分任务的关键作用，为未来研究提供了有价值的资源和基准。

Abstract: Automatic Short Answer Scoring (ASAS) is central to NLP for Education. However, openly available benchmarks remain scarce, and existing datasets largely address how well students answer a question directly rather than how well they master underlying concepts (knowledge elements) such as thermal energy or epistemic activities (skills) such as reasoning or claim.
  To address this gap, we introduce Alice, a large-scale, rubric-based German ASAS dataset that is pedagogically aligned and comprises three subtasks: (i) learning performance (Alice-LP), (ii) knowledge elements (Alice-KE), and (iii) skills (Alice-SK).
  We further formulate rubric-based ASAS as a rubric-retrieval task and benchmark the dataset with a range of language models, from encoder-only models to lightweight LLMs. We also benchmark the dataset with zero-shot prompting via LLMs and a standard classification baseline. The experiments show that LLMs, in particular, struggle to score knowledge elements and skills in the zero-shot setting. They also indicate that rubric text is often useful, especially for Alice-KE and Alice-SK, while on Alice-LP gains over sample-solution-focused inputs are more modest and vary by model and input format.

</details>


### [37] [SAPD: Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09665)
*Tianle Wang,Jiayu Liu,Ruizhi Zhao,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: On-policy post-training can improve large language models by learning from their own trajectories, but requires costly rollout generation. We ask whether fixed demonstrations can support competitive off-policy learning through better supervision. Our premise is that their usefulness depends not only on the training trajectories, but also on whether supervision provides informative preferences among continuations and connects this guidance to the reasoning decision being learned. We introduce Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation (SAPD), a rollout-free self-distillation method that turns demonstrations into step-aligned distributional supervision. Its key insight is to use the known progression of a reference solution to associate each reasoning transition with targeted privileged guidance, rather than treating the solution as undifferentiated context. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SAPD outperforms supervised fine-tuning and label smoothing on average while remaining competitive with on-policy reinforcement learning and self-distillation. Analyses support both the value of context-dependent distributional guidance and the benefit of aligning privileged information with the current step. SAPD also largely preserves out-of-domain coding performance and achieves approximately 2x training-loop speedups over the on-policy baselines. These findings suggest that carefully constructed supervision can make fully off-policy post-training a competitive and computationally efficient alternative. Our code is available at https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/SAPD.

</details>


### [38] [InsClaimBench: Benchmarking Insurance Claim Adjudication Across the Decision Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09671)
*Linqi Zhang,Chong Qi,Yan Cheng,Wanqing Cao,Yu Liu,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出InsClaimBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在保险理赔裁决任务中全链路决策能力的基准。实验显示模型在原子规则层面表现良好，但沿决策链向下传播时可靠性显著下降，局部正确不保证整体正确，事实变更下更易出现传播失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理型大语言模型发展，需评估其在专业决策任务中的能力。保险理赔裁决要求模型关联案件证据、保险规则、中间判断和赔付计算，涉及结构化决策链，现有基准缺乏对全链路一致性与传播正确性的系统评估。

Method: 构建InsClaimBench基准，基于真实理赔材料和结构化保险规则，包含车险、财险、健康险3,780个案例（375个案例族），86,656个原子规则判断。从原子规则、裁决模块到赔付决策与金额全链路评估，并设计受控事实变体测试变更在各层级的正确传播。评测六个主流LLM。

Result: 六模型赔付决策准确率74.23%-80.19%，联合决策-金额准确率仅47.54%-73.15%。原子规则准确率达95.48%，但规则向量精确匹配峰值仅36.90%。最频繁的模块错误不一定最导致最终决策失败。事实变更下，模块更新不如规则更新可靠，局部正确仍可能导致赔付错误，正确赔付可能掩盖中间错误。

Conclusion: 可靠的理赔裁决要求决策链上的一致组合与正确传播。单纯提升局部任务准确率不足以保证端到端正确性，需关注跨层级的传播一致性与组合推理能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning-oriented large language models (LLMs) have motivated increasing evaluation of their ability to perform professional decision tasks. Insurance claim adjudication is one such task, requiring models to connect case evidence, insurance rules, intermediate judgments, and payout calculations across a structured decision process. We introduce InsClaimBench, an end-to-end benchmark for evaluating insurance claim adjudication across the decision chain. Grounded in real claim materials and structured insurance rules, InsClaimBench contains 3,780 cases in 375 case families across auto, property, and health insurance, comprising 86,656 atomic rule judgments. It evaluates each claim from atomic rules through adjudication modules to payout decisions and amounts, with controlled factual variants testing whether required changes are correctly propagated across levels. Evaluation of six LLMs reveals a progressive loss of reliability along the decision chain. Payout-decision accuracy ranges from 74.23--80.19%, while joint decision--amount accuracy drops to 47.54--73.15%. Strong local performance also fails to ensure case-level correctness: atomic-rule accuracy reaches 95.48%, whereas rule-vector exact match peaks at only 36.90%, and the most frequent module errors are not necessarily those most associated with final-decision failure. Under factual changes, these inconsistencies further become propagation failures: module updates are less reliable than rule updates, correct local judgments can still yield incorrect payouts, and correct payouts can conceal intermediate errors. These results show that reliable claim adjudication requires consistent composition and propagation across the decision chain.

</details>


### [39] [From Pareto to Preference: Personalized Test-Time Scaling via Amortized Agentic Policy Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09684)
*Xinglin Wang,Zishen Liu,Tong Zheng,Shaoxiong Feng,Peiwen Yuan,Yiwei Li,Jiayi Shi,Yueqi Zhang,Chuyi Tan,Ji Zhang,Boyuan Pan,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersonTTS 框架，解决测试时缩放（TTS）中多维度用户需求（准确率、延迟、推理成本）的联合满足问题。通过摊销式智能体策略发现，复用跨用户搜索经验，在保持目标配置文件评估的同时，显著提升未见用户配置文件和保留问题上的联合需求满足率，并降低发现代理的时间和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 方法仅针对单一资源维度（准确率-成本或准确率-延迟）优化帕累托前沿，而实际用户需求是多维联合的（同时指定准确率、延迟、推理成本），且不同需求偏好不同的控制器。缺乏能够最大化用户特定多维需求联合满足率的可执行控制器发现机制。

Method: 提出 PersonTTS：一种摊销式智能体策略发现框架。核心组件包括：(1) 需求匹配的控制器初始化，基于相似用户配置文件复用历史最优控制器；(2) 源蒸馏程序指导，将跨用户搜索经验蒸馏为可复用的程序化启发式；(3) 保留对每个候选控制器的目标配置文件评估，确保策略适配性。框架在 AIME 和 HMMT 数学推理基准上实验验证。

Result: 在 AIME 和 HMMT 基准上，PersonTTS 在未见用户配置文件和保留问题上显著优于强 TTS 基线，实现更高的联合需求满足率。在相同候选评估预算下，跨用户经验复用进一步提升策略质量，同时大幅降低发现代理的时间和计算成本。

Conclusion: PersonTTS 通过摊销式智能体策略发现，有效解决了多维度个性化测试时缩放问题。跨用户经验复用机制（需求匹配初始化 + 源蒸馏指导）在保证目标评估严谨性的前提下，实现了策略发现的高效泛化，为实际部署中多样化用户需求的快速响应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by allocating additional inference computation. Existing approaches to improving TTS efficiency largely optimize accuracy against one resource dimension at a time, advancing either the accuracy--cost or accuracy--latency Pareto frontier. Yet user requirements are multidimensional: users may specify accuracy, latency, and inference-cost requirements jointly, and different requirements can favor different controllers. We formulate Personalized Test-Time Scaling as discovering executable controllers that maximize the joint satisfaction rate of user-specific requirements. To reduce the overhead of repeated policy discovery for new user profiles, we propose PersonTTS, an amortized agentic policy-discovery framework that reuses prior search experience through requirement-matched controller initialization and source-distilled procedural guidance, while retaining target-profile evaluation for every candidate. Experiments on AIME and HMMT show that PersonTTS substantially outperforms strong TTS baselines in joint requirement satisfaction on unseen user profiles and held-out problems. Under the same candidate-evaluation budget, cross-user experience reuse further improves policy quality while substantially reducing discovery-agent time and cost.

</details>


### [40] [SpikingVLA: Asynchronous Spiking Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09710)
*Jingya Wang,Dehao Zhang,Shuai Wang,Malu Zhang,Yang Yang,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpikingVLA，一种 ANN-to-SNN 转换框架，通过树突积分发放（DIF）神经元和异步执行机制，实现低延迟、高性能的脉冲视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ANN-to-SNN 转换方法在应用于大规模 VLA 模型时，往往需要大量时间步以维持性能，导致推理延迟过高，难以满足实时部署需求。

Method: 1. 提出树突积分发放（DIF）神经元，通过树突混合和自适应体细胞发放缓解通道级激活离群值，实现少时间步下的精准 ANN-to-SNN 转换。2. 基于 DIF 神经元设计异步执行机制，跨 VLA 组件重叠时序计算，降低同步开销与延迟。

Result: 大量实验表明，SpikingVLA 在导航任务上达到竞争性性能，同时显著提升推理效率。对比现有脉冲 VLA 方法，成功率（SR）提升 11.9%，路径长度加权成功率（SPL）提升 12.6%，首动作延迟降低 11.2 倍。

Conclusion: SpikingVLA 确立了一个将预训练 VLA 模型部署为高性能、低延迟脉冲推理模型的实用框架。

Abstract: ANN-to-SNN conversion offers a practical route toward energy-efficient spiking Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by bypassing the substantial cost of training large-scale SNNs from scratch. However, existing methods often require many timesteps to maintain competitive performance, resulting in substantial inference latency for real-time VLA deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SpikingVLA, an ANN-to-SNN conversion framework that enables accurate and low-latency spiking VLA inference. Specifically, we propose a Dendritic Integrate-and-Fire (DIF) neuron that alleviates channel-wise activation outliers through dendritic mixing and adaptive somatic firing, enabling accurate ANN-to-SNN conversion with fewer timesteps. Building on DIF neurons, we further introduce an asynchronous execution mechanism that overlaps temporal computation across VLA components, reducing synchronization overhead and latency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SpikingVLA achieves competitive navigation performance with substantially improved inference efficiency. Compared with existing spiking VLA methods, SpikingVLA improves SR and SPL by 11.9\% and 12.6\%, respectively, while reducing first-action latency by 11.2$\times$. These results establish SpikingVLA as a practical framework for deploying pretrained VLA models with high-performance and low-latency spiking inference.

</details>


### [41] [Bridge Routing Heads: Where Multilingual Multi-hop Reasoning Lives in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09733)
*Seunghan Kim,Minyeong Choe,Hyunil Kim,Haehyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在多语言大语言模型（Llama 3.1 70B 与 Qwen 2.5 72B）中发现了“桥接路由头”（Bridge Routing Heads, BRH），揭示了多跳推理中存在语言特异性的内部电路，且通过激活层面的干预（消融与放大）无需训练即可修复跨语言推理失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型能在不同语言中回答相同的多跳推理问题，但目前缺乏对其是否共享内部推理电路的机制性理解，亟需揭示跨语言推理的神经机制。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线在两个大型多语言模型中识别 BRH，分析五种语言下的头集合重叠度（Jaccard 相似度），通过消融实验验证因果作用，并尝试在目标语言失败时放大这些头以恢复性能。

Result: 不同语言的 BRH 集合几乎互不重叠（Llama 平均 Jaccard 0.017，Qwen 0.057），消融通用 BRH 导致两跳 NLL 比随机基线增加 39-89 倍，放大头可挽回高达 51.7% 的跨语言失败案例。两模型均呈现双电路模式，但头分配策略不同：Llama 集中在大型通用池，Qwen 依赖更大的语言特异性池。

Conclusion: 多语言大模型的多跳推理依赖于语言特异性的桥接路由头电路，仅通过激活层面的干预即可在无训练情况下恢复跨语言推理错误，为模型可解释性与跨语言迁移提供了新见解。

Abstract: Multilingual LLMs answer the same multi-hop reasoning question across languages, but we lack a mechanistic account of whether they share an internal circuit. We identify Bridge Routing Heads (BRH) in two large multilingual LLMs through a three-stage pipeline. The resulting language-specific head sets exhibit near-complete mutual exclusivity across the five languages, with a mean Jaccard similarity of only 0.017 for Llama 3.1 70B and 0.057 for Qwen 2.5 72B, revealing language-idiosyncratic circuits. Ablating general BRH increases two-hop Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) by 39-89x the random-head baseline, providing direct causal evidence of their role. Amplifying these heads in a failing target-language pass rescues up to 51.7% of cross-lingual failures, with no training. The two models share this dual-circuit pattern but allocate heads differently: Llama concentrates chaining in a large general pool, while Qwen leans on larger language-specific pools. Together these results show that activation-level intervention alone can recover correct answers from cross-lingual reasoning failures.

</details>

*Ahmad Abbas,Tamara Fakih,Nour Fakih,Ammar Mohanna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shaer 框架，实现可控的古典阿拉伯诗歌生成，联合以自然语言描述、韵律子形式、目标半行数为条件；构建含 116,032 首诗的丰富语料库，基于 Yehia-7B 采用 QLoRA 监督微调，在基础韵律准确率、子形式遵循率、长度控制上大幅超越基座模型，并通过多 LLM 评测与盲测人工评估验证语义与文学质量，且无训练集精确复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典阿拉伯诗歌生成需同时满足语义、语言及细粒度韵律约束，现有系统多仅控制宽泛诗歌属性，缺乏对语义意图、韵律子形式、诗歌长度的联合建模能力。

Method: 1. 从 Ashaar 衍生构建 116,032 首古典阿拉伯诗歌语料库，包含规范化韵律子形式标签与自动生成、验证的语义描述。2. 以 Yehia-7B 为基座，采用 QLoRA 进行完成式监督微调，仅优化生成部分的损失。3. 评估结合自动指标（基础韵律一致性、子形式遵循、长度控制）、三 LLM 裁判、盲测人工评估及记忆分析。

Result: Shaer 达到 95.17% 基础韵律准确率、91.75% 诗歌级韵律子形式准确率、83.40% 精确行数准确率；相对未微调基座模型分别提升 68.68、57.77、38.93 个百分点，并在所有评测系统中基础韵律准确率最高。多 LLM 评测与盲测人工评估显示其语义与文学质量具竞争力；3,481 条测试生成中未发现与训练语料或配对源诗的精确复制。

Conclusion: Shaer 成功实现了对古典阿拉伯诗歌的语义、韵律子形式、长度的联合可控生成，显著提升各项硬约束指标并保持高文学质量，相关代码、模型、数据已公开，为受约束诗歌生成提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Classical Arabic poetry generation requires simultaneously satisfying semantic, linguistic, and fine-grained prosodic constraints. Existing systems typically control broad poetic attributes but do not jointly model semantic intent, meter subform, and poem length. We present Shaer, a controllable Classical Arabic poetry generation framework jointly conditioned on natural-language descriptions, meter subforms, and target hemistich counts. To support this task, we construct an enriched corpus of 116,032 classical Arabic poems derived from Ashaar, containing normalized meter-subform labels and automatically generated, validated semantic descriptions. We then adapt Yehia-7B using QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning with a completion-only objective. Our evaluation combines automatic assessment of base-meter conformity, requested-subform adherence, and length control with three LLM judges, blinded human evaluation, and memorization analysis. Shaer achieves 95.17% base-meter accuracy, 91.75% poem-level meter-subform accuracy, and 83.40% exact count accuracy. Relative to its untuned foundation model, these results represent gains of 68.68, 57.77, and 38.93 percentage points, respectively; Shaer also attains the highest base-meter accuracy among all evaluated systems. Multi-LLM evaluation and a blinded human assessment of top-ranked outputs further indicate competitive semantic and literary quality. Finally, analysis of all 3,481 test generations finds no exact copies from the training corpus or paired source poems. Code, models, and datasets are publicly available.

</details>


### [43] [Decoupling Logic from Persona: Structural Immunity of Edge LLM Agents to Context Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09772)
*Masaaki Nakatsu,Reno Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种解耦架构（AO-DA），将逻辑推理与人格表达分离为两条推理路径，使用同一INT4基座模型并配备热插拔LoRA适配器，解决边缘设备上小语言模型智能体在长、误导性、人格密集的对话历史中出现的“人格-逻辑干扰”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边缘设备上小语言模型智能体在单一上下文窗口中同时维持人格和正确推理时，长、误导性、人格密集的对话历史对逻辑部分造成的干扰（人格-逻辑干扰）。

Method: 提出解耦架构（AO-DA），将逻辑推理（“是什么”）与人格表达（“怎么表达”）分离为两条推理路径，共用一个INT4量化的基座模型，通过热插拔LoRA适配器实现。逻辑路径仅接收核心轮次，输出可验证的结构化状态（Micro-State）；人格路径结合完整历史将结构化状态渲染为符合人格的回复。在Apple M2笔记本上对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Gemma-3-4B-it（4-bit）进行消融实验，共480次运行（4污染级别×3实验组×2任务×2人格×5随机种子）。

Result: (i) 解耦逻辑路径在结构上对污染不变：提示词长度固定为180（Llama）或167（Gemma）token，而混合单次通过提示词从242增长到1203 token，输出在各级别间字节级完全一致（40/40）；(ii) 混合单次通过单调退化（综合逻辑分数Llama从0.669降至0.150，Gemma从0.487降至0.150），主要因未能输出要求的结构化格式（Llama在高污染级别80-95%运行失败，Gemma 100%失败）；(iii) 相同污染输入解耦逻辑路径时，专用适配器/格式路径在8B模型上比单次通过更鲁棒（失败率0-20% vs 80-95%；配对Δ +0.30到+0.50，Cliff's δ 0.50-0.85，Holm校正p ≤ 0.03），但在4B模型上两者均崩溃。解耦带来首轮额外一次解码开销（Llama 28.2s vs 18.2s），但能在1.7ms内实现人格热插拔而无需重跑逻辑路径。

Conclusion: 解耦架构有效消除了人格-逻辑干扰，在8B模型上显著提升逻辑推理鲁棒性并实现极快人格切换，但4B模型容量不足导致双路径均失效；代码、评分标准、测试固件、适配器和日志已开源。

Abstract: Small language-model agents on edge devices must hold a persona and reason correctly at once, inside one context window that fills with conversational history and persona instructions. We study what happens to the logical part of such an agent when that history is long, misleading and persona-heavy (persona-logic interference), and present a Decoupling Architecture (AO-DA) that separates logical inference ("What") from persona expression ("How") into two inference paths on one INT4 base model with hot-swappable LoRA adapters. The logic path receives only the core turn and emits a verifiable structured state (Micro-State); the persona path renders it in character with the full history. In same-base-model ablations on an Apple M2 laptop (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Gemma-3-4B-it, 4-bit; 480 runs over 4 pollution levels x 3 arms x 2 tasks x 2 personas x 5 seeds) we find: (i) the decoupled logic path is structurally invariant to pollution: its prompt stays at 180 (Llama) or 167 (Gemma) tokens while the mixed single-pass prompt grows from 242 to 1,203, and its outputs are byte-identical across levels (40/40); (ii) the mixed single pass degrades monotonically (composite logic score 0.669 to 0.150 on Llama, 0.487 to 0.150 on Gemma), mostly by failing to emit the required structured output (80-95% of runs on Llama, 100% on Gemma at the two highest levels); (iii) with the same pollution fed into the decoupled logic path, the dedicated-adapter, dedicated-format path is still more robust than the single pass on the 8B model (failure 0-20% vs 80-95%; paired $Δ$ +0.30 to +0.50, Cliff's $δ$ 0.50-0.85, Holm-adjusted $p \le 0.03$) but not on the 4B model, where both collapse. Separation costs one extra decode on a topic's first turn (28.2 s vs 18.2 s on Llama) and buys persona hot-swapping in 1.7 ms without re-running the logic path. Code, rubric, fixtures, adapters and logs are released.

</details>


### [44] [Judging in Latent Space: Efficient Generative Reward Modeling via Semantics-Preserving Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09788)
*Mingqing Yuan,Xiaobo Liang,Junwei Yang,Ziwei Chen,Zeren Zhang,Hejin Wang,Yubin Wang,Juntao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentGRM 是一种基于语义分块、压缩和重建的潜在评估框架，利用评分标准引导的评估结构来学习紧凑的连续轨迹，实现无需生成文本评估的自主成对判断，在保持判断质量的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励建模通常需要联合表示和推理多个评估标准，但逐 token 语言化该过程会产生巨大的推理开销。现有的潜在推理工作表明连续状态可能更紧凑地支持这种计算，因此需要一种能够压缩评估轨迹同时保留判断质量的方法。

Method: 提出 LatentGRM 框架，包含语义分块、压缩和重建三个核心组件。利用评分标准引导的评估结构指导压缩过程，学习紧凑的连续轨迹以支持自主成对判断，并通过独立的解释器从轨迹重建评估文本，提供离线视角查看压缩保留的信息。

Result: 在匹配训练数据和骨干网络下，LatentGRM 在 4B 和 8B 规模上均达到与显式 SFT 评判器相当的聚合偏好准确率。在四个基准领域，LatentGRM-8B 将评估轨迹压缩 8.9-9.2 倍，在 vote@5 下将总评判推理时间减少 6.1-7.0 倍。受控评分标准干预实验表明，标准依赖的偏好信息通过潜在序列传递。

Conclusion: 连续潜在评估能够在保持竞争力判断质量的同时，大幅降低推理成本，证明了潜在空间推理在奖励建模中的有效性和效率优势。

Abstract: Reward modeling often requires jointly representing and reasoning over multiple evaluation criteria, yet verbalizing this process token by token can incur substantial inference cost. Recent work on latent reasoning suggests that continuous states may support this computation more compactly. We introduce LatentGRM, a latent evaluation framework built on semantic chunking, compression, and reconstruction. By using the structure of rubric-guided evaluations to guide compression, LatentGRM learns compact continuous trajectories that support autonomous pairwise judgments without generating textual assessments. A separate interpreter reconstructs evaluation text from these trajectories, providing an offline view of the information retained under compression. Under matched training data and backbones, LatentGRM achieves competitive aggregate preference accuracy relative to explicit Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) judges at both 4B and 8B scales. Across four benchmark domains, LatentGRM-8B compresses evaluation trajectories by 8.9--9.2x and reduces total judge inference time by 6.1--7.0x at vote@5. Controlled rubric interventions show that criterion-dependent preference information is carried through the latent sequence. Together, these results demonstrate that continuous latent evaluation can substantially reduce inference cost while preserving competitive judgment quality.

</details>


### [45] [MIRROR: From Imitation to Internalization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09795)
*Huayi Lai,Jicheng Yang,Min Yi,Chong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MIRROR，一种基于自蒸馏的大语言模型个性化框架，将个性化从风格模仿转向偏好内化，并引入 MIRROR-F 焦点插件强化关键标记监督，在多个基准上实现最优个性化性能、更高文本质量及更低灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化微调范式主要依赖参考文本的风格模仿，难以保证内容质量；需解决如何让模型内化用户偏好而非单纯复述参考措辞的问题。

Method: 1. MIRROR：采用参考揭示的在策略自蒸馏，将模型在自身生成轨迹上的下一标记分布与其参考条件下的自身分布对齐，实现偏好内化。2. MIRROR-F：在分布对齐基础上引入焦点插件，对信息量大的参考标记施加选择性监督，增强内容生成同时保留用户表达风格。

Result: 在三个个性化生成基准、两种模型规模及参考/大模型双重评估下，MIRROR 与 MIRROR-F 均达到最优整体个性化性能、更优文本质量，且在三个未见个性化任务上灾难性遗忘显著低于 SFT 基线。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏结合参考揭示的在策略对齐与焦点式选择性监督，能有效将大模型个性化从模仿推向偏好内化，提升内容质量与泛化能力，且跨规模、跨场景收益一致。

Abstract: The demand for personalized LLMs is shifting from style imitation toward content quality. We investigate whether self-distillation can bridge this gap in existing fine-tuning paradigm. To address this limitation, we introduce MIRROR(Meta- personalization by Internalizing Reference-Revealed On-policy Reflections), a novel self-distillation framework that shifts LLM personalization from imitation toward preference internalization. First, we replace reference-token imitation with reference-revealed on-policy self-distillation, aligning the model's next-token distributions along its own generation trajectories with those of its reference-conditioned self, thereby internalizing user preferences rather than reproducing reference wording.Second, we introduce MIRROR-F, a focal plug-in that augments on-policy distributional alignment with selective supervision over informative reference tokens, thereby strengthening content generation while preserving user-specific expression. Across three personalized generation benchmarks, two model scales, and complementary reference-based and LLM-based evaluations, MIRROR and MIRROR-F achieve leading overall personalization performance and superior text quality, while exhibiting less catastrophic forgetting than SFT-based baselines on three unseen personalized generation tasks. The gains are consistent across model scales and application scenarios, translating to improved performance in LLM personalization tasks.

</details>


### [46] [A Deafening Silence: Catastrophic Forgetting Lives in the Output Embeddings of Tokens the Data Never Speaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09835)
*Jonghyun Han,Younghoon Song,Jongyoul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型持续预训练/微调时的灾难性遗忘主要集中在新语料中罕见词的输出嵌入层，由词表缺陷导致的单向softmax梯度被Adam二阶矩放大引起；提出仅在输出投影层增大Adam的epsilon这一单行优化器修改，即可在不损害新任务学习且无需逐模型调参的情况下显著缓解遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练和微调不可避免地导致灾难性遗忘，常规缓解手段依赖原始数据重放，但原始数据往往不可获取；因此需要在无数据重放条件下分析遗忘发生的位置和原因，并寻找轻量级、通用的防御机制。

Method: 1. 在五个设置（至1.4B参数）中系统冻结参数，定位遗忘集中在新语料中低频token的输出嵌入行，而模型主体的sqrt(v-hat)带保持惰性。2. 机制分析：缺失token接收持续的单向softmax梯度，经Adam二阶矩归一化放大为全量更新。3. 提出干预：仅在训练期间对输出投影层提高Adam的epsilon。

Result: 跨越八个设置（160M–12B参数、四大模型家族），该干预在七个稳定配置上将遗忘降低39.4%–67.9%，不损害目标学习且无需逐模型调参；可与重放加性叠加（Qwen/Korean达79.8%），并挽救LoRA的23倍遗忘激增；事后编辑漂移行仅恢复<5%遗忘，证明干预必须在训练期生效。

Conclusion: 当语料词表覆盖不足导致词表饥饿时，仅通过一行优化器代码调整——提高输出投影层的Adam epsilon——即可作为主要防御手段大幅缓解灾难性遗忘，具有极强的实用性和泛化性。

Abstract: Continual pre-training and fine-tuning in Large Language Models (LLMs) inevitably induce catastrophic forgetting, typically mitigated by replay using often-inaccessible original data. In this data-free regime, we analyze where forgetting occurs and why. Systematic parameter freezing across five settings up to 1.4B reveals that forgetting concentrates selectively in the output embeddings of tokens rarely seen in the new corpus, whereas the same sqrt(v-hat) band of the body is inert and new learning resides elsewhere. This localization is governed by the vocabulary deficiency of the corpus rather than the training mode, allowing pre-retraining risk ranking from token counts alone within a fixed base model. Mechanistically, absent tokens receive persistent one-sided softmax gradients that Adam's second-moment (sqrt(v-hat)) normalization amplifies into full-sized updates. We therefore propose an intervention: raising Adam's epsilon exclusively for the output projection during training. Across eight settings spanning 160M to 12B parameters and four model families, this removes 39.4% to 67.9% of forgetting across all seven stable configurations without degrading target learning or requiring per-model tuning. The defense combines additively or better with replay (79.8% on Qwen/Korean) and rescues released-head LoRA from a 23-fold forgetting surge. Because post-hoc editing of the drifted rows recovers under 5% of forgetting, the intervention must operate during training. Our findings indicate that a single-line optimizer adjustment may serve as the primary defense against catastrophic forgetting where the corpus starves the vocabulary.

</details>


### [47] [Training Advisors for LLM Agents from Task Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09858)
*Sergei Polezhaev,Barys Liskavets,Ori Press,Alexander Golubev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Caddie，一种通过强化学习训练的批评模型，能为大语言模型智能体提供自然语言分析和建议。与依赖步骤级标签或参考批评的方法不同，Caddie仅从智能体最终是否成功这一结果信号学习，实现了跨模型架构和跨任务域的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明自然语言反馈能帮助智能体在任务执行中修正决策，但现有方法依赖步骤级标注或参考批评，获取成本高且难以扩展。需要一种仅从任务最终结果学习、能跨模型和跨域泛化的批评模型训练方法。

Method: Caddie采用强化学习训练批评模型，保持基座模型冻结。在多跳问答任务上仅使用单一基座模型（Qwen3-4B）训练，批评模型根据智能体最终是否成功获得奖励信号，无需步骤级监督。推理时智能体可自主决定何时寻求批评帮助。

Result: Qwen3-4B批评模型在四个不同规模和架构的基座模型上均提升成功率，其中三个模型未参与训练。在MuSiQue基准上，Qwen3-4B成功率提升超25个百分点，超越无批评的Kimi K3。同一批评模型在τ³和DeepDive等域外交互基准上也带来增益，无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 基于结果的批评模型训练能产生可跨基座模型架构和任务域迁移的指导信号。智能体可在推理时自主决定何时寻求批评帮助，这种方法为构建可扩展、通用的智能体辅助系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents tackle multi-step tasks by interleaving reasoning and tool calls with observations from the environment. Prior work has shown that natural-language feedback can help these agents revise their decisions during task execution. We introduce Caddie, a method for training critics to provide natural-language analysis and advice as agents work through a task. Unlike approaches that rely on step-level labels or reference critiques, Caddie learns from whether the agent ultimately succeeds after receiving the critic's feedback. We optimize the critic through reinforcement learning while keeping the base model frozen. Trained on multi-hop question answering with a single base model, our Qwen3-4B critic improves success rates across four base models of different scales and architectures, including three not used during critic training. On the MuSiQue benchmark, the trained critic improves Qwen3-4B's success rate by more than 25 percentage points, surpassing the performance of Kimi K3 without a critic. The same critic also yields gains on out-of-domain interactive benchmarks, including $τ^3$ and DeepDive, with no additional training. Our results show that agents can decide when to seek help from a critic at inference time and that outcome-based critic training can produce guidance that transfers across base models and task domains.

</details>


### [48] [Itgan at NADI 2026 shared task: Parameter-Efficient Whisper Adaptation for Robust, Mixed-Dialect and Code-Switched Arabic ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09934)
*Ibrahim Almajai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Itgan 团队在 NADI 2026 三个 ASR 子任务中的系统：稳健国家级 ASR (1.1)、混合方言 ASR (1.2) 和突尼斯代码切换 ASR (1.3)。三个任务共享同一配方：在消费级 GPU 上用 LoRA 适配 Whisper，每个任务通过不同的附加策略取得最佳效果。1.1 任务利用方言标签训练 per-dialect specialists，达到 57.1% 国家平均 WER；事后线性探针在无标签时恢复 44% 甲骨路由性能。1.2 任务发现基座模型选择比适配器容量更重要，引入去相关成员后系统融合才生效，最终 46.7% WER。1.3 任务以 14.49% WER 获第二，且在领先提交中 CER 最低 (5.38%)，最后 0.60 WER 提升来自独立训练运行的精确权重空间平均和 ROVER 投票，无需额外训练。所有对比均配对 bootstrap 检验，并报告了八个无效方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NADI 2026 设置三个阿拉伯语 ASR 子任务，涉及跨国家/方言的鲁棒性、混合方言识别和代码切换场景。作者旨在用统一、低成本的 LoRA 适配 Whisper 基线，通过最小化的任务特定增强（专家路由、基座模型选择、权重平均/系统融合）在消费级硬件上取得竞争力结果，并严格评估各设计选择的有效性。

Method: 统一配方：Whisper 大模型 + LoRA 低秩适配，在消费级 GPU 训练。任务 1.1：池化适配器继续训练 per-dialect specialists，测试时按给定方言标签路由；事后在冻结编码器特征上训练线性探针实现无标签路由。任务 1.2：对比不同基座模型与适配器容量，仅当加入去相关成员时系统融合 (ROVER) 才有增益。任务 1.3：多轮独立训练，精确权重空间平均 (model souping) 获得主要提升，ROVER 投票提供额外增益，无需额外训练数据或轮次。所有实验均配对 bootstrap 显著性检验。

Result: 任务 1.1：提交系统 57.1% 国家平均 WER；线性探针无标签路由恢复 44% 甲骨性能。任务 1.2：基座模型选择影响大于适配器容量，融合系统达 46.7% WER。任务 1.3：14.49% WER 列第二，领先提交中 CER 最低 5.38%；最后 0.60 WER 提升来自权重平均 (主要) 与 ROVER (次要)。八个无效方向被明确记录。

Conclusion: 统一的 Whisper+LoRA 配方配合轻量任务特定策略即可在 NADI 2026 三个阿拉伯语 ASR 子任务中取得强基线：专家路由利用方言标签，基座模型选择优于扩大适配器，权重空间平均与 ROVER 融合在无额外训练下进一步压低 WER/CER。严格的显著性检验与负结果报告增强了结论的可信度，为低资源多方言/代码切换 ASR 提供了可复现的实用范式。

Abstract: We describe the Itgan systems for the three ASR subtasks of NADI 2026, namely robust country-level ASR (1.1), mixed-dialect ASR (1.2), and Tunisian code-switched ASR (1.3). All three share one recipe, Whisper adapted with LoRA on consumer GPUs, and each was carried by a different addition to it. On 1.1, where the dialect label is given at test time, per-dialect specialists continued from a pooled adapter gave the largest gain, and the submitted system reached 57.1% country-average WER. A post-evaluation linear probe on frozen encoder features routes utterances without the label and recovers 44% of what oracle routing gives. On 1.2 the choice of base model mattered more than adapter capacity, and system combination helped only once we added a decorrelated member, reaching 46.7% WER. On 1.3 our system placed second at 14.49% WER with the lowest CER among the leading submissions, 5.38%. Its last 0.60 WER points came without further training, mostly from an exact weight-space average of independently trained runs, with ROVER voting adding the remainder. Every comparison carries a paired-bootstrap test, and we report eight directions that did not work.

</details>


### [49] [EASE: Entropy-Adaptive Distribution Shaping for Evading AI-generated Text Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09976)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kahim Wong,Jialong Wang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EASE框架，一种无需训练且与检测器无关的AI生成文本检测规避方法，通过基于预测熵自适应调整logit扰动和采样温度来规避检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现AI生成文本检测对源大语言模型的解码选择敏感，扰动next-token logits或调整采样温度可降低检测性能，揭示了检测器对解码时分布变化的脆弱性。

Method: EASE直接从源LLM的next-token分布计算预测熵，并利用该熵自适应地调整logit扰动和采样温度，无需检测器反馈或模型微调。

Result: 在三个源LLM和多个检测器上的实验显示，EASE持续降低检测性能，且文本质量损失可忽略、推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: EASE提供了一种有效的无训练、检测器无关的规避框架，通过基于熵的解码参数自适应调整实现对AIGT检测器的规避，同时保持文本质量。

Abstract: AI-generated text (AIGT) detection can be sensitive to the decoding choices of the source large language model (LLM). We observe that perturbing next-token logits or adjusting sampling temperature can reduce detection performance, providing a clear signal of detector vulnerability to decoding-time distribution changes. Building on this observation, we propose EASE (Entropy-Adaptive Distribution Shaping for Evasion), a training-free and detector-agnostic framework for evading AIGT detectors. EASE computes predictive entropy directly from the source LLM's next-token distribution and uses it to adapt both logit perturbation and sampling temperature, without detector feedback or model fine-tuning. Experiments across three source LLMs and multiple detectors demonstrate consistent reductions in detection performance, with negligible degradation in text quality and negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [50] [The Long Road to the Same Answer: Cognitive Bias Under Escalating Reasoning Budgets in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10049)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现推理模型（在推理时分配额外计算）并未比其非推理同类模型表现出更少的经典决策偏差；增加思考token并不能可靠地减弱偏差，甚至可能使模型偏离人类直觉方向。仅锚定效应符合人类方向，其余偏差多呈反向。简单的指令（如重述锚点）比单纯增加思考更有效。这反驳了将测试时推理视为理性保证的观点，主张逐个偏差审计部署模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双过程理论认为人类的慢速、深思熟虑思维能削弱快速直觉判断产生的偏差。推理模型在推理时分配额外计算并展示深思熟虑过程，研究旨在验证这种机制是否能类似地减弱经典决策偏差（锚定、框架、损失厌恶、承诺升级、可得性、确认偏差）。

Method: 采用剂量-反应设计，选用成熟基准中的30个情景（覆盖6类偏差，每类5项）。跨4个模型家族，将每个推理模型与匹配的非推理同类模型配对，设置思考上限为0、1024、4096、8192 tokens，共12,350次API调用。以实际消耗的推理token为剂量，分析偏差随思考量的变化。另测试单行指令（要求重述锚点后再回答）对锚定效应的影响。

Result: 1. 推理模型整体偏差不比同类模型小（Delta=+0.031, t(29)=1.45, p=.157），点估计反而倾向更大。2. 随实际推理token增加，偏差幅度无显著下降趋势；有变化时是符号分数进一步偏离人类方向。3. 仅锚定效应符合人类方向（d=1.89）；其余5类偏差中4类在所有7个模型中均呈反向，框架效应反向显著，承诺升级、确认、损失厌恶呈方向性反向，可得性偏差缺失。4. 单行指令要求重述锚点使所有5个锚定项的偏差降低，而额外思考未能实现（p=.057）。

Conclusion: 测试时推理不能作为模型理性的保证。部署前需逐个偏差进行审计，且简单的提示工程（如重述锚点）可能比单纯增加思考计算更有效地缓解特定偏差。

Abstract: Reasoning models allocate extra computation at inference time and present their answers as the product of deliberate thought. If this deliberation works the way dual-process accounts of human cognition suggest, longer thinking should weaken the classic decision biases that fast, intuitive judgment produces. Using 30 vignettes covering six biases (anchoring, framing, loss aversion, escalation of commitment, availability, confirmation) from an established benchmark, we run a dose-response study across four model families, pairing each reasoning model with a matched non-reasoning sibling and requesting thinking ceilings of 0, 1,024, 4,096, and 8,192 tokens, for 12,350 API calls. Because a requested ceiling is not the same as realized deliberation, we use the reasoning tokens each call consumed as the dose. First, reasoning models are not less biased than their siblings; the point estimate leans the other way in every family, but the item-level pooled contrast is not reliable (Delta = +0.031, t(29) = 1.45, p = .157). Second, bias magnitude does not reliably fall as realized deliberation grows: no slope is significantly negative, and where anything moves it is the signed score drifting further from the human direction. Third, anchoring is the only bias in the human direction (d = 1.89). Four of the other five lean the opposite way in all seven models; with five items per bias, that reversal is reliable for framing and directional for escalation of commitment, confirmation, and loss aversion, while availability is absent. A one-line instruction to restate the anchor before answering lowered anchoring on all five anchoring items, which no amount of additional thinking did, although the effect does not reach significance (p = .057). The results argue against treating test-time reasoning as a rationality guarantee and for auditing deployed models bias by bias.

</details>


### [51] [Cache the Encoder Within:Compact, Reusable Memory across LLM Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10058)
*Hanzuo Liu,Chunyu Liu,Chaofan Lin,Alex Lamb,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EncBank 利用预训练 LLM 的底层作为可复用的文档编码器，紧凑存储其输出供上层阅读器使用，通过自蒸馏后缀适配器实现跨存储精度共享，无需量化专用重训练，在多基准测试中 4-bit 存储仅损失 1 分以内，显存占用降至 28.1%，预填加速 1.40 倍，但任务保真度和端到端收益取决于工作负载、准备成本和复用频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对共享文档重复查询导致的冗余编码开销，以及缓存模型状态带来的持久化存储成本，论文旨在实现可复用计算与紧凑内存的结合。

Method: 基于 CoMem 的中间状态接口，将预训练 LLM 的底层作为文档编码器，紧凑存储其输出供适配后的上层阅读器使用；设计自蒸馏后缀适配器，在每个骨干网络内跨存储精度共享，无需针对量化进行专门重训练。

Result: 在三个 Qwen 骨干（不同规模、全注意力与混合架构）的五个基准套件上，4-bit 存储使各基准聚合分数均在原生精度 EncBank 1 分以内；Qwen3-8B 固定负载下持久 GPU 显存占用仅为原生精度的 28.1%；原生精度控制下，选定包预填相比同证据同适配器文本重放加速 1.40 倍，RULER 准确率下降 3.12 分；原生精度 Qwen3.8-27B 配置通过 89 项 Terminal-Bench 2.1 任务中的 70 项。

Conclusion: EncBank 成功结合了可复用计算与紧凑内存，但任务保真度和端到端收益仍取决于具体工作负载、准备成本和复用频率。

Abstract: Repeated queries over shared documents incur redundant encoding, while caching model states introduces persistent storage costs. Building on CoMem's intermediate-state interface, EncBank treats a pretrained LLM's lower layers as a reusable document encoder and compactly stores their outputs for an adapted upper-layer reader. A self-distilled suffix adapter is shared across storage precisions within each backbone, without quantization-specific retraining. Across five benchmark suites on three Qwen backbones spanning different sizes and full-attention and hybrid architectures, 4-bit storage keeps each reported benchmark aggregate within one score point of native-precision EncBank. In a fixed Qwen3-8B workload, it retains 28.1% of the native-precision persistent GPU store. Separate native-precision controls yield a 1.40x selected-pack prefill speedup over same-evidence, same-adapter text replay, at a 3.12-point RULER accuracy cost. A native-precision Qwen3.8-27B configuration also passes 70 of 89 Terminal-Bench 2.1 tasks. EncBank thus combines reusable computation with compact memory, while task fidelity and end-to-end benefits remain dependent on the workload, preparation costs, and reuse frequency.

</details>


### [52] [I would rather quit NLP than read another paper like this: The rise of antithesis in NLP papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10092)
*Olga Zamaraeva,Adrián Gude,Roi Santos-Ríos,Carlos Gómez-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了学术写作中 'rather than' 结构的使用频率和质量变化，发现从2019年到2026年及GPT生成的论文中该结构使用率大幅上升（7倍），且约半数2026年论文包含令人厌烦的用法。实验表明偏好训练和诚实指令会鼓励这种结构，推测是RLHF中成对偏好训练的副作用——单轮奖励无法捕捉跨文本的累积负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM常规用于科学写作，观察到近期模型过度使用 'rather than' 等反义结构，反复陈述工作不做什么，降低表达质量并惹恼审稿人。作者旨在量化这一现象，分析其语言学特征，并探究RLHF训练机制如何导致此类风格退化。

Method: 1) 语料库分析：统计ACL 2019论文、2026年arXiv ACL风格论文、GPT生成论文中 'rather than' 的出现率。2) 人工标注：两名盲审标注员评估各年代用法的厌烦程度。3) 语言学分析：对比厌烦用法与正当用法在呈现被拒替代方案时的倾向性。4) 偏好模型实验：测试奖励模型和偏好标注员对该结构的偏好，以及诚实指令的影响。

Result: 2026年 'rather than' 使用率是2019年的7倍，GPT论文更高；约10%的2026年用法被判厌烦（2019年近0）；标注员很少就具体厌烦实例达成一致，但各自约半数2026年论文含厌烦用法；厌烦用法更负面地呈现被拒替代方案；奖励模型和偏好标注员偏好该结构；诚实指令促进其使用。

Conclusion:  'rather than' 过度使用是LLM科学写作风格退化的标志，源于RLHF的成对偏好训练机制：单轮奖励鼓励在单个回复中展示自我反驳（显得诚实/平衡），却无法惩罚整篇文档中此类结构的累积冗余和厌烦感。需开发跨文本、长上下文的奖励建模以缓解此问题。

Abstract: For better or worse, LLMs are by now used routinely for scientific writing.\footnote{This paper is no exception; we did use AI to assist with writing some of the sections (see Acknowledgments).} Many have noticed that recent models fill papers with unnecessary antithesis, stating over and over what the work does not do, in ways that do not contribute to its precision or quality of expression and annoy reviewers \emph{rather than impressing them}. We study the construction \emph{rather than} in ACL papers from 2019, ACL-style arXiv papers from 2026, and papers written by GPT models from the same titles and abstracts. Its rate in 2026 is seven times the 2019 rate, and higher still in the GPT papers. Two annotators, blind to the source, find almost no 2019 use \emph{annoying} and about one in ten 2026 uses; they seldom agree on which, yet about half of 2026 papers contain a use that annoys each of them. \emph{Annoying} uses present the rejected alternative less favorably than legitimate uses. Raters of preference data and open reward models favor the construction, and an instruction to be honest promotes it. We conjecture that it is a side effect of post-training on pairwise preferences, which credit a disavowal in a single response and cannot register its cost across a text.

</details>


### [53] [Mechanics of Long-Context Hybrid Models Part 1.1: From Hybrid Attention to Hybrid Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10114)
*Xiaoran Liu,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出长上下文混合模型力学分析框架，研究全注意力与滑动窗口注意力(SWA)或门控线性注意力(LA)的混合架构。发现上下文扩展中的跷跷板效应：LA混合模型更受益于长上下文持续预训练，而SWA混合模型在长度外推上表现更好。归因于不同注意力机制诱导的位置归纳偏置差异。SWA混合模型存在短上下文学习陷阱、短窗口疲劳和长窗口懒惰问题，需扩展窗口提升持续预训练性能。对LA混合模型总结了马太效应并提出滑动窗口线性注意力，实现16倍无训练长度外推并在64k上下文NIAH-SK1任务达100%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型架构正从纯全注意力转向混合注意力架构，以提升长上下文效率和长度外推性能。现有研究缺乏系统性解释混合模型为何有效及如何更好设计，需要建立理论框架分析不同注意力机制混合时的位置归纳偏置差异及其对上下文扩展和长度外推的影响。

Method: 提出长上下文混合模型力学分析框架，重点研究全注意力与滑动窗口注意力(SWA)或门控线性注意力(GLA、GDN)的混合架构。通过实证分析对比不同混合模型在长上下文持续预训练和长度外推场景下的表现，归因于位置归纳偏置差异，识别SWA混合模型的三大问题并提出改进方案，总结LA混合模型的马太效应并设计滑动窗口线性注意力机制。

Result: 发现上下文扩展中的跷跷板效应：LA混合模型在长上下文持续预训练受益更大，SWA混合模型在长度外推更优。SWA混合模型存在短上下文学习陷阱、短窗口疲劳、长窗口懒惰三大问题，需扩展窗口改善。LA混合模型呈现马太效应，提出的滑动窗口线性注意力实现16倍无训练长度外推，在64k上下文NIAH-SK1任务达100%准确率。

Conclusion: 混合注意力架构的性能差异源于不同注意力机制诱导的位置归纳偏置。SWA混合模型适合长度外推但持续预训练需扩展窗口；LA混合模型适合持续预训练且具备强外推潜力。建立的力学分析框架为混合模型设计提供理论指导，滑动窗口线性注意力兼顾了训练效率和外推能力。

Abstract: The architectural design of Large Language Models (LLMs) is shifting from traditional full-attention-only models to hybrid models, which combine different attention modules to improve long-context efficiency and performance in length extrapolation and context extension. To explain why hybrid models work and how to design them better, we propose Mechanics of Long-Context Hybrid Models. As Part 1.1 of this series, we begin with hybrids of full attention and either sliding-window attention (SWA) or gated variants of linear attention (LA), represented by GLA and GDN. We first observe a Seesaw Effect in Context Extension: LA hybrids benefit more from long-context continual pretraining, whereas SWA hybrids perform better under length extrapolation. We attribute this behavior to differences in the positional inductive biases induced by these attention mechanisms. We find that SWA hybrids suffer from a Short-Context Learning Trap, Short-Window Weariness, and Long-Window Laziness, and require extended windows to enhance performance in continual long-context pretraining. For LA hybrids, we summarize the Matthew Effect of Hybrid Position Extrapolation and propose Sliding-Window Linear Attention, achieving 16$\times$ training-free length extrapolation while maintaining 100\% accuracy on NIAH-SK1 in 64k context length.

</details>


### [54] [LLM4Impact: Integrating Heterogeneous Information for Scientific Impact Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10138)
*Yong Cao,Markus Flicke,Haoyu He,Katrin Renz,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Predicting the future impact of a newly published paper is challenging because it must be inferred from heterogeneous evidence available at publication time. Existing approaches often rely on a single source of information or combine multiple sources without accounting for their different predictive roles. In this paper, we present LLM4Impact, an evidence-aware method for scientific impact prediction that learns to represent, integrate, and calibrate heterogeneous information. LLM4Impact combines semantic, graph, LLM, and temporal representations, and injects graph information into a frozen LLM through continuous prefix tokens. A context aware gating mechanism adaptively weights different evidence, while a separate calibration module accounts for domain and temporal variation in citation scales. We further construct a large-scale benchmark dataset with 2 million papers, leakage-safe point-in-time heterogeneous ego graphs, temporal splits, and both year-level and month-level citation targets. Experiments show that LLM4Impact consistently outperforms strong semantic, graph, and LLM based baselines, with a 10.13% reduction in year RMSE on the in distribution test set and a 6.87% reduction under out-of-domain distribution. Our results reveal that the value of such evidence is context dependent: different papers benefit from different sources, while domain and publication time affect how evidence translates into citations. This finding motivates adaptive evidence selection and context-conditioned calibration rather than simply richer representations. We will release our code, benchmark, and an interactive web demonstration upon publication.

</details>


### [55] [InterView-C: A Synchronized Multimodal Corpus of VR Avatar-Mediated Survey Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10145)
*Patrick Schrottenbacher,Leon Hammerla,Lydia Kleine,Doris Stingl,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InterView-C 是一个德语多模态语料库，包含 27 场在虚拟现实中进行的调查访谈，提供同步的行为数据（眼动、头部/身体运动、面部行为、手指追踪）和人工校正的逐字转录，支持口语互动的语言学分析并保持与丰富多模态行为的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动语音识别（ASR）系统在转录口语时会扭曲语言学相关信息（如短封闭式回答和数字词），且基于现有语料库训练的下游模型（如否定检测模型）在应用于转录口语数据时面临迁移挑战，性能下降且波动大。因此需要一个高质量、多模态对齐的语料库来支持口语互动的语言学研究。

Method: 构建了包含 54 个录音的德语多模态语料库 InterView-C，采集于虚拟现实环境中的 27 场调查访谈（双方均由化身代表）。提供词级计时的人工后编辑逐字转录、访谈条目时间戳、问卷回答、以及 1,422 句的否定线索和范围标注（其中 1,398 句双重标注，线索 α=0.87，范围 α=0.81）。实证评估了 9 个开放权重 ASR 系统及现有否定检测模型在该语料库上的表现。

Result: 九个开放权重 ASR 系统对短封闭式回答和数字词的误识别率显著偏高；基于现有语料库训练的否定检测模型在 InterView-C 转录访谈上的表现较差且波动大，而基于 InterView-C 标注训练的模型性能更优。语料库提供了可靠的多模态口语互动与文本 NLP 方法的接口。

Conclusion: InterView-C 通过高质量转录和丰富多模态行为对齐，解决了 ASR 转录失真和模型迁移难题，为口语互动的语言学分析提供了宝贵资源，并展示了针对口语领域定制标注和模型训练的必要性。

Abstract: We present InterView-C, a German multimodal corpus of 27 survey interviews conducted entirely in virtual reality, with both interlocutors represented by avatars. The corpus aligns spoken interaction with synchronized behavioral data, including gaze, head and body movement, facial behavior, hand and finger tracking. Its reference transcripts and linguistic annotations provide a reliable interface between this multimodal spoken interaction and predominantly text-based NLP methods. This interface is important because automatically transcribing speech can distort linguistically relevant information, while downstream models trained on existing resources may additionally face transfer challenges when applied to transcribed spoken data. InterView-C therefore provides word-timed and manually post-edited verbatim transcripts for all 54 recordings, interview-item timings, questionnaire responses and negation cue and scope annotations for 1,422 sentences, 1,398 of them doubly annotated (α=0.87 for cues; α=0.81 for scopes). We demonstrate both challenges empirically: nine open-weight ASR systems disproportionately misrecognize short closed answers and number words, while negation models trained on existing corpora show lower and highly variable performance on our transcribed interviews than a model trained on the InterView-C annotations. InterView-C thus enables linguistic analyses of spoken interaction while retaining their alignment with rich multimodal behavior.

</details>


### [56] [HySPE: Positional Encoding via Symplectic Dual Shears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10154)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出双曲辛位置编码，利用辛群Sp(2,R)的非紧双曲分支替代RoPE的紧椭圆旋转分支，通过对偶剪切的阻尼对称合成实现共形辛收缩，结合不变本征基对角化与分块坐标重定基消除指数表示漂移，实现了长度无关的数值界与RoPE相当的前向延迟，在零样本长度外推任务中显著优于RoPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE将位置编码限制在Sp(2,R)的紧椭圆分支（SO(2)旋转），导致长度外推能力受限；作者旨在开发基于非紧双曲分支的位置编码，以获得更强的长上下文外推鲁棒性，同时保持数值稳定性与计算效率。

Method: HySPE采用双曲辛变换：以阻尼对称的对偶剪切合成替代旋转，产生每通道对两个谱衰减率的共形辛收缩；为消除朴素绝对分解的指数表示漂移，在算子的不变本征基中对角化，并引入自适应中心化执行的分块坐标重定基，保证长度无关的数值有界性。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare上，HySPE-UltraLong在16倍零样本外推（L=4096）下困惑度保持4.810，RoPE退化至131.198；在51M参数的WikiText-103子词Transformer上（训练长度512），HySPE域内性能接近RoPE，外推至8192长度时尾部困惑度降低83.9%；前向延迟与缓存RoPE持平（RTX 4090上7.21ms）。

Conclusion: 受控实验确立了HySPE的外推鲁棒性与数值稳定性，但在大规模基础模型上的扩展行为仍需后续研究验证。

Abstract: We introduce Hyperbolic Symplectic Positional Encoding (HySPE), grounding positional attention in non-compact symplectic transformations. While canonical Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) parameterizes the compact, elliptic branch of $\Sp(2,\R)$ via rotations, HySPE operationalizes its hyperbolic branch via a damped symmetric composition of dual shears, yielding a conformally symplectic contraction with two spectral decay rates per channel pair. To eliminate the exponential representation drift inherent to naive absolute factorizations, we diagonalize the operator in its invariant eigenbasis and introduce blockwise coordinate rebasing with adaptive centered execution. This guarantees length-independent numerical bounds while matching cached RoPE forward latency (7.21\,ms on an RTX 4090). On TinyShakespeare, HySPE-UltraLong maintains an invariant perplexity of 4.810 up to $16\times$ zero-shot extrapolation ($L=4096$), whereas RoPE degrades to 131.198. Scaled to a 51M-parameter subword Transformer on WikiText-103 ($L_{\text{train}}=512$), HySPE closely matches RoPE in-domain while robustly extrapolating to length 8192, reducing tail perplexity by 83.9\% over RoPE. While these controlled experiments establish HySPE's extrapolation robustness and numerical stability, evaluating its scaling behavior on large-scale foundation models remains an important direction for future investigation.

</details>


### [57] [Beyond Outcome Rewards: Constructing and Assigning Retrieval Credit for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10179)
*Wenyu Huang,Xinyu Hou,Pavlos Vougiouklis,Ruofei Lai,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究如何通过中间监督改进搜索代理的强化学习，提出结合中间检索步骤信号与最终结果奖励的训练框架，实验表明奖励设计和信用分配对训练效果有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）依赖稀疏的结果监督，导致多步搜索轨迹中的信用分配困难，限制了学习效率，因此需要探索中间监督来改进训练。

Method: 系统研究多种奖励塑形和信用分配策略，利用中间检索步骤提供学习信号；开发结合中间信号与最终结果奖励的训练框架，在多个基准上进行匹配训练条件下的实验。

Result: 实验显示聚合搜索代理性能提升，且中间信号的选择及其信用分配位置显著影响训练行为。

Conclusion: 奖励设计和信用分配是训练高效搜索代理的关键设计维度，中间监督能有效缓解稀疏奖励下的信用分配问题。

Abstract: Search agents enable Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve and use information for complex multi-hop questions. Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising approach for post-training such agents, but its reliance on sparse, outcome-based supervision can make credit assignment difficult and limit learning efficiency. In this paper, we systematically investigate how intermediate supervision can improve reinforcement learning for search agents. We study a range of reward-shaping and credit-assignment strategies that provide learning signals from intermediate retrieval steps. Building on these insights, we develop a training framework that combines intermediate signals with final outcome rewards to improve learning from multi-step search trajectories. Experiments across multiple benchmarks under matched training conditions demonstrate improvements in aggregate search-agent performance and show that both the choice of intermediate signal and where its credit is assigned affect training behaviour. These findings show that reward design and credit assignment are important design dimensions for training effective search agents.

</details>


### [58] [LLM Persuasion Is in the Eye of the Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10232)
*Kamile Dementaviciute,Julija Vaitonyte,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了9种自动化说服力评估方法在15个大语言模型上的一致性，发现方法间一致性较弱（平均Spearman ρ=0.25）。拒绝执行任务（主要集中在操纵类任务）降低了一致性约四分之一，且通用能力与理性说服相关但与操纵说服无关。说服力得分同时反映模型的说服能力和执行意愿，单一得分仅对其特定设置有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的说服能力已达到或超越人类专家，虽在教育、健康传播等领域有益，但也可能被用于操纵和误导。现有评估碎片化，定义不一，且多基于狭窄场景。自动化评估方法可大规模、统一地测试高风险说服形式，因此需要系统比较不同评估方法的一致性及差异成因。

Method: 将9个已发表的自动化说服力评估方法改编为统一实验设置，在相同的15个大语言模型上运行，计算方法间排名的Spearman相关系数，并分析拒绝行为和通用能力对一致性的影响。

Result: 9种方法间平均Spearman ρ仅为0.25，一致性较弱。模型对部分任务拒绝执行（直接或间接）会降低一致性约25%，拒绝主要发生在操纵类任务。通用能力与大多数理性说服方法相关，但与大多数操纵说服方法无关。一致性更多取决于评估任务类型而非评分方式。

Conclusion: 说服力得分混合了模型的说服能力和执行意愿。单一评分仅能反映其特定评估设置下的表现，无法泛化到跨任务的说服力水平。未来评估需区分能力与意愿，并针对不同说服类型（理性vs操纵）设计专门指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have already been shown to match or exceed human experts in persuasion. While their persuasive capabilities hold promise for beneficial uses such as education and health communication, they can also be used to manipulate and misinform, making their evaluation a growing priority for developers and regulators. That evaluation, however, remains fragmented: studies differ in what they treat as persuasion, and broad claims often rest on narrow, situation-specific assessments. Automated methods, often modelled on human studies, offer a way to compare such assessments directly, as they can be run on the same models at scale and can include high-risk forms of persuasion that would be difficult or unethical to test on people. In this study, we adapt nine published automated methods to a shared setup, run them on the same fifteen LLMs, and ask whether their rankings agree and why. We find that the methods agree only weakly (mean Spearman $ρ= 0.25$). Our analyses point to two contributing factors. Models that refuse some tasks but not others, directly or indirectly, lower agreement by about a quarter, and these refusals fall mostly on manipulation tasks. General capability also plays a part: most rational persuasion (non-manipulative) methods track it, whereas most manipulation methods do not. Together, these findings suggest that agreement depends more on the task a method sets than on how it scores persuasion, although this pattern is only indicative given the eight methods available for analysis. More broadly, our results suggest that persuasion scores combine a model's ability to persuade with its willingness to do so. A single score is therefore informative about its own setting, but says little about a model's persuasiveness across tasks.

</details>


### [59] [Nobody Truly Agrees on Sentiment: Humans, Bespoke Tools, and LLMs Struggle with Social Media Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10318)
*Himarsha R. Jayanetti,Sivakanesan Dhanushkanda,Shuai Hao,Michael L. Nelson,Michele C. Weigle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种定制情感分析工具（TextBlob、VADER、Twitter-roBERTa-base）和三种大语言模型（Qwen3-32B、GPT-OSS-120B、Llama-4-Maverick-17B）与六名人工标注员在100条推文上的评分一致性。结果显示：人工标注员间仅达到一般一致性；二分类（负面/非负面、正面/非正面）优于三分类；Twitter-roBERTa-base与人工一致性最强，优于定制工具和LLMs；LLMs间一致性高，与人工一致性中等至较高，在正面/非正面分类表现更好。结论强调领域特定微调对社交媒体情感分析的可靠性至关重要，且以人为中心的评估是建立金标准标签的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体是实时公众情绪的丰富来源，但广泛使用的情感分析工具往往在不了解其局限性的情况下被应用。研究旨在系统评估主流情感分析工具和新兴大语言模型与人工标注员的一致性，揭示其在真实社交媒体数据上的可靠性差异，为工具选择和金标准构建提供实证依据。

Method: 选取100条推文，由六名人工标注员、三种定制情感分析工具（TextBlob、VADER、Twitter-roBERTa-base）和三种大语言模型（Qwen3-32B、GPT-OSS-120B、Llama-4-Maverick-17B）进行情感标注。使用Cohen's kappa（成对比较）和Fleiss' kappa（多评价者）两种统计指标量化一致性。对比二分类（负面/非负面、正面/非正面）与三分类下的一致性差异。

Result: 1. 人工标注员间仅达一般一致性，凸显情感分析的主观性。2. 二分类下人工和自动化工具的一致性均显著高于三分类。3. Twitter-roBERTa-base与人工评分一致性最强，尤其在负面/非负面区分上优于其他定制工具和LLMs。4. LLMs间表现出高度一致性，与人工一致性为中等至较高，在正面/非正面分类上表现更优。5. 定制工具整体表现不如领域微调模型和LLMs。

Conclusion: 领域特定微调（如Twitter-roBERTa-base）对于可靠的社交媒体情感分析仍至关重要，且优于通用大语言模型和传统工具。人工评估的主观性提示必须以人为中心建立金标准标签。二分类任务比三分类更可靠。未来研究应结合领域适应训练与人工标注质量控制，提升情感分析在真实场景中的实用性。

Abstract: Social media is a rich source of real-time public sentiment, but widely used sentiment analysis tools are often applied without understanding their limitations. In this study, we evaluate the inter-rater reliability of three bespoke sentiment analysis tools (TextBlob, VADER, and Twitter-roBERTa-base) and three large language models (LLMs: Qwen3-32B, GPT-OSS-120B, Llama-4-Maverick-17B) against six human raters across 100 tweets. We measured agreement using two statistical measures: Cohen's kappa for pairwise comparisons and Fleiss' kappa for multiple raters. Even among the human raters, our results showed only fair agreement, highlighting the subjectivity of sentiment analysis. Higher agreement was observed under the binary sentiment classification (negative vs. non-negative and positive vs. non-positive) than under the three-class classification across both humans and automated tools. The Twitter-roBERTa-base model showed the strongest alignment with human ratings, outperforming both bespoke sentiment tools and LLMs, particularly in distinguishing negative versus non-negative sentiment. LLMs showed substantial agreement among themselves and moderate to substantial alignment with humans, performing better in positive vs. non-positive classifications. Our findings underscore that domain-specific fine-tuning remains crucial for reliable social media sentiment analysis, and human-centered evaluation remains essential for establishing gold-standard labels.

</details>


### [60] [Learning to Act with Task Progress: Distilling Small Agents from Compact Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10332)
*Wenxi Gan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务进度蒸馏（TPD），一种离线方法，将演示动作与简短任务阶段标签配对，训练小模型完成任务。实验表明，TPD 与仅动作监督在 404 个演示下均达 72.4% 成功率，优于推理训练的 48.3%；在 200 个演示时，显式阶段标签显著提升性能（48.0%→67.7%），但在更多数据下两者差距缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型演示虽能教导小智能体，但核心设计选择是保留教师轨迹中的哪些信息（推理、动作、任务进度）。现有方法或保留完整推理（计算开销大），或仅监督动作（缺乏任务结构指导），需要一种紧凑且有效的监督信号。

Method: 任务进度蒸馏（TPD）：离线收集教师轨迹，将每个动作与描述当前任务阶段的短标签配对。学生模型学习预测这些紧凑目标，通过联合评分可执行的阶段-动作对来选择动作，再由确定性执行器在环境中执行。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 基准上，1.7B 学生模型：404 个演示下 TPD 和仅动作监督均达 72.4% 平均未见任务成功率，推理训练仅 48.3%；200 个演示时显式阶段标签将成功率从 48.0% 提升至 67.7%；808 个演示时两方法均达 76.9%。共享历史分析显示 TPD 局部优势源于子目标间转换（如物体获取到处理）的更好决策。

Conclusion: 紧凑监督（动作+任务阶段标签）能有效训练小任务智能体；显式任务进度在中等演示预算下提供额外指导，但在大量演示下仅动作监督可追平。TPD 为大模型知识蒸馏到小模型提供了高效范式。

Abstract: Learning from large-model demonstrations offers a way to train small agents that can complete recurring tasks without calling a large model at every step. A central design choice is what to retain from teacher trajectories that contain reasoning, actions, and information about task progress. We introduce Task-Progress Distillation (TPD), an offline approach that pairs each demonstrated action with a short label describing the current task stage. The student learns these compact targets and selects actions by jointly scoring admissible stage--action pairs, which a deterministic harness executes in the environment. On ALFWorld, a 1.7B student trained with 404 demonstrations achieves 72.4\% mean unseen task success with either TPD or action-only supervision, compared with 48.3\% for a reasoning-trained student using constrained action selection. Explicit stages provide an additional benefit at 200 demonstrations, improving success from 48.0\% to 67.7\% over action-only supervision. With more demonstrations, the action-only student closes the gap, and both approaches reach 76.9\% at 808 demonstrations. Shared-history analyses link part of TPD's local advantage to better decisions when moving between subgoals, particularly from object acquisition to processing. These results show that compact supervision can train effective small task agents, while explicit task progress provides additional guidance at an intermediate demonstration budget.

</details>


### [61] [Document-Level Text Simplification in Estonian Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10378)
*Meeri-Ly Muru,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种最先进的多语言大语言模型在爱沙尼亚语文档级文本简化任务上的表现，比较了三种提示策略，并提出了包含自动指标和人工标注的评估框架，发现Gemini-2.0和LLaMA-3.3表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级文本简化涉及超越句内编辑的转换，需解决语篇连贯、指代消歧和跨段落一致性等问题。尽管高资源语言的句子级简化已有进展，但形态丰富、低资源语言（如爱沙尼亚语）的文档级简化仍 largely unexplored。

Method: 选取五种最先进的多语言LLM，测试三种提示策略：单次生成、基于流水线的模块化智能体、指南增强流水线。构建评估框架，整合自动指标（可读性、语义保持、语篇连贯）和结构化人工标注协议。

Result: Gemini-2.0和LLaMA-3.3生成的输出具有接近母语者的流畅度和强语义保持，其他模型显示出明显的语法和语义局限。

Conclusion: 本工作贡献了新颖的文档级连贯性指标、基于证据的提示策略，并发布了公开资源以支持复现。

Abstract: Document-level text simplification involves transformations that go beyond sentence-internal edits, addressing discourse coherence, anaphora resolution, and cross-paragraph consistency. Despite advances in sentence-level simplification for high-resource languages, document-level simplification in morphologically rich, low-resource languages such as Estonian remains largely unexplored. This study presents a comprehensive evaluation of five state-of-the-art multilingual large language models (LLMs) for document-level simplification in Estonian. Three prompting strategies are examined: single-pass generation, pipeline-based modular agents, and guideline-augmented pipelines. The evaluation framework integrates automatic metrics assessing readability, semantic preservation, and discourse coherence, alongside a structured manual annotation protocol. The findings indicate that Gemini-2.0 and LLaMA-3.3 produce outputs with near-native fluency and strong meaning preservation, whereas other models display notable grammatical and semantic limitations. This work contributes novel document-level coherence metrics, evidence-based prompting strategies, and publicly available resources for reproducibility.

</details>


### [62] [CoTrace: Data Recipes for Training Terminal Agents with Harness-Model Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10426)
*Jixuan Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Qinyuan Ye,Yada Pruksachatkun,Haoxiang Zhang,Jingming Zhuo,Yifan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Juntao Tan,Xiangyu Peng,Silvio Savarese,Zeyuan Chen,Lianhui Qin,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoTrace，一种感知运行时 harness 的数据配方，通过解耦 harness 搜索与策略训练、基于 provenance 匹配的轨迹路由与课程刷新，在 Tmax 上将 Qwen3.5-9B 从 78 任务提升至 88（SFT）与 90（在线 RL），并揭示 harness 兼容性是分布外迁移的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness-model 协同进化方法将搜索产生的轨迹视为无差别回放缓冲区，忽略了轨迹价值取决于生成它的 harness，导致训练数据噪声大、计算效率低，且评估时运行时不匹配引发程序执行崩溃。

Method: 建立交替协同进化框架，解耦 harness 搜索与策略训练，引入 CoTrace：1) 基于反复执行失败引导 harness 合成；2) 策略训练严格条件化于与采用运行时匹配的验证过 rollout（SFT）或新鲜在线交互（RL）；3) 显式管理轨迹路由、溯源匹配与课程刷新。

Result: 在 Tmax promotion split 上，Qwen3.5-9B 解决任务数从 78 提升至 88（SFT）与 90（在线 RL）；紧凑的 harness-matched 语料以显著更低算力实现稳定增益，优于跨 sibling harness 汇聚的大规模语料；Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 SWE-bench Lite 实验证实 OOD 迁移根本依赖 harness 兼容性，训练-评估运行时一致性可避免程序执行崩溃。

Conclusion: Harness-aware 数据配方与运行时一致性是终端智能体高效协同进化的核心；显式的 provenance 匹配与课程控制能大幅提升数据效率与分布外泛化，为工具增强 LLM 的工程化部署提供了系统性范式。

Abstract: Terminal-agent capability depends jointly on model weights and the runtime harness that formats prompts, binds tools, and handles error recovery. Existing harness-model co-evolution approaches improve both components, yet often treat trajectories produced during harness search as an undifferentiated replay buffer. This practice overlooks that a trajectory's value for model training depends on the harness under which it was generated. To systematically analyze this interface, we establish an alternating co-evolution framework that decouples harness search and policy training through component-wise promotion decisions. Within this framework, we introduce CoTrace, a harness-aware data recipe that explicitly governs trajectory routing, provenance matching, and curriculum refresh. Under CoTrace, recurring execution failures guide harness synthesis, while policy training is strictly conditioned on verified rollouts matched to the adopted runtime for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or fresh online interactions for reinforcement learning (RL). On the Tmax promotion split, CoTrace advances Qwen3.5-9B from 78 to 88 solved tasks under supervised fine-tuning while an online reinforcement variant reaches 90. Specifically, a compact harness-matched corpus produces steady model gains at substantially lower compute than much larger corpora pooled across sibling harnesses. Furthermore, evaluations on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-bench Lite show that out-of-distribution transfer depends fundamentally on harness compatibility, where maintaining consistency between training and evaluation runtimes prevents procedural execution breakdowns observed under foreign scaffolds.

</details>


### [63] [PHRBench: A Behavioral Evaluation of Post-Hallucination Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10455)
*Linghao Meng,Feng He,Xuan Yang,Junyuan Mao,Pinze Ren,Deqing Mu,Hesen Yang,Qiankun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出PHRBench基准，系统评估大语言模型在多阶段推理中面对幻觉前提时的行为表现，发现成功纠错较少见且与信念更新频率相关，幻觉提示词属性可预测恢复成功率（AUROC 0.847）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注幻觉对最终答案的影响，缺乏对模型在推理过程中如何逐步解决幻觉前提的细粒度行为分析，需要一种结构化的基准来刻画推理轨迹层面的幻觉合规、规避与启发式纠正。

Method: 构建PHRBench基准，涵盖四个领域和18个大语言模型，设计4820个受控实例；定义三个行为指标——幻觉合规、幻觉规避、启发式纠正，并引入“洞察轨迹”概念表示最终纠正并得出正确答案的推理路径；分析推理轨迹中的信念更新频率，并训练轻量级预测器利用幻觉提示词特性预测恢复成功率。

Result: 成功恢复现象相对罕见；成功恢复的轨迹伴随更频繁的信念更新；幻觉提示词的内在属性包含显著预测信号，轻量级预测器达到AUROC 0.847。

Conclusion: 该工作为后幻觉推理提供了行为视角，揭示了大模型如何解决错误上下文以及成功恢复发生的条件，为提升模型鲁棒性提供了可量化的评估框架和预测依据。

Abstract: Hallucinated information can propagate through multi-stage LLM systems and become part of the context for subsequent reasoning. Existing studies of post-hallucination reasoning (PHR) mainly characterize changes in final outcomes and aggregate reasoning dynamics, leaving how models resolve hallucinated premises at the response level insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce PHRBench, a controlled benchmark for behaviorally structured PHR across four domains and 18 large language models. PHRBench characterizes each reasoning trajectory independently of final-answer correctness through Hallucination Compliance, Hallucination Avoidance, and Heuristic Correction, and defines an insightful trajectory as successful correction that ultimately reaches the correct answer. Across 4820 controlled instances, we find that successful recovery remains relatively rare and is associated with more frequent belief updates along the reasoning trajectory. We further find that properties of the hallucinated prompt contain substantial predictive signal for successful recovery, with a lightweight predictor achieving an AUROC of 0.847. These findings provide a behavioral view of post-hallucination reasoning, characterizing how LLMs resolve erroneous context and when successful recovery is likely to occur.

</details>


### [64] [Your Prompt Should Do More: Effects of Retrieval Instructions in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10508)
*Amanda Myntti,Jenna Kanerva,Veronika Laippala,Filip Ginter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提示嵌入模型在检索任务中难以可靠遵循指令，尤其当查询侧存在干扰项时；通过在微调中加入查询侧干扰项可显著改善此问题，且对其他任务影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前嵌入模型在检索任务中难以可靠遵循详细指令，尤其在查询侧包含干扰项的不对称检索评估中表现不佳，需探究指令如何影响查询表示的机制并改进训练方法。

Method: 研究指令如何影响不对称检索任务中查询的表示机制；通过在微调阶段引入查询侧干扰项进行训练，评估其对指令遵循能力的影响。

Result: 模型在查询侧存在干扰项时甚至无法遵循简单任务指令；加入查询侧干扰项进行微调后，指令遵循能力大幅提升，且对其他任务性能影响极小。

Conclusion: 当前嵌入模型的训练设置和评估方式导致其对指令遵循不稳健；在微调中引入查询侧干扰项可有效缓解该问题，提升模型在复杂检索场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Prompted embedding models have recently received increasing attention, particularly for retrieval, where detailed retrieval instructions are provided as part of the retrieval prompt. Several new datasets and studies have examined this setting, showing that the current embedding models often struggle to follow such instructions reliably. In this paper, we study the mechanism of how instructions actually affect the representations of retrieval queries in asymmetric retrieval tasks. We show that models can fail to follow even simple task instructions when query-side distractors are included in the evaluation. We hypothesize that this behavior is driven by the training setup of current embedding models and their evaluation, and show that fine-tuning with added query-side distractors leads to substantial improvements, with minimal effect on other tasks.

</details>


### [65] [EngramEdit: Decoupled Knowledge Updates in LLMs through Conditional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10533)
*Hongru Cai,Ran Wei,Wenjie Wang,Chengfa Wu,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EngramEdit方法，利用条件记忆架构（如DeepSeek Engram）实现大语言模型中事实知识的解耦更新，通过计算目标记忆表示并联合优化共享n-gram嵌入，在保持无关知识和通用能力的前提下，实现近乎完美的知识编辑效果，并在未见表达和多跳推理中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆架构通过输入n-gram查找学习到的嵌入来扩展LLM容量，展示了将事实知识存储与通用计算解耦的潜力，为在保持Transformer骨干固定的情况下更新事实知识提供了新路径。然而，同一事实的不同表达可能激活不同的n-gram嵌入，而更新共享嵌入可能无意中改变模型对其他事实的预测，实现精准解耦更新面临挑战。

Method: EngramEdit首先计算目标记忆表示，使模型能在多种表达下预测更新后的事实；然后联合更新共享的n-gram嵌入以匹配这些跨表达和编辑的目标，并对频繁复用的嵌入施加更强的惩罚，以保留无关知识。

Result: 实验表明EngramEdit实现了通过条件记忆的独立事实知识更新，达到近乎完美的编辑成功率。修订后的知识可在未见表达和多跳推理中使用，在CoT提示下准确率是最强基线的近三倍。即使事实更新累积，无关知识和通用能力也基本得以保留。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明EngramEdit将条件记忆转化为可编辑的知识接口，将其作用从单纯的模型扩展扩展到支持解耦知识更新，为LLM的知识管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conditional memory architectures such as DeepSeek Engram use input n-grams to look up learned embeddings, expanding the capacity of large language models (LLMs) with limited additional computation. Beyond model scaling, this architecture has demonstrated the potential to decouple factual knowledge storage from general-purpose computation, offering a promising route to updating factual knowledge while keeping the Transformer backbone fixed. Realizing this potential is challenging because different expressions of a fact may activate different n-gram embeddings, while updating shared embeddings can unintentionally change the model's predictions about other facts. We propose EngramEdit for decoupled knowledge updates through conditional memory.
