We introduce two realistic scenarios, one with an infinite state space and one with signal mixture. In these scenarios, we prove that even a perfect agent cannot guarantee safe behaviour. It will be argued that for any proof of AI safety or alignment, the environment and associated safe actions need to be specifically considered together with the agent.

</details>


### [165] [The Handover Problem: Governing Autonomy Transitions in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10352)
*Vicente Pelechano,Antoni Mestre,Manoli Albert,Miriam Gil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对人机协作中AI自主权动态切换的“移交问题”，提出了一种可审计的多信号综合指标——移交就绪分数（HRS），结合滞后回差的过渡策略，实现了可逆、可恢复、可审计的自主权治理框架，并在软件工程与制造领域验证了其与硬安全防护的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应自动化、监督控制、自动化信任和去技能化研究虽解释了部分问题，但缺乏一个可审计、多信号的判据来决定何时在多周期工作流中调整自主权级别。人机系统很少在固定自主权级别运行，随着协作演进，控制权需在升级、维持、回退间动态转移，急需一个形式化、透明的治理机制。

Method: 形式化定义“移交问题”；构建移交就绪分数（HRS），融合操作员就绪度、人机信任、学习稳定性、操作性能四个信号维度；设计基于滞后回差的过渡策略——升级自主权需持续正向证据，恶化时快速回退；引入硬安全防护作为单指标越界的即时纠正机制；在软件工程和制造两个领域进行实证评估。

Result: HRS与硬安全防护覆盖互补失效模式：防护处理单指标临界越界的即时故障，HRS捕捉单一防护无法观测的多信号缓慢侵蚀；框架将自主权移交确立为需要显式、复合、可审计判据的治理问题，为自适应自动化研究提供了前所未有的概念与形式化基础。

Conclusion: 本文建立了首个可审计的自主权移交治理框架，通过HRS和滞后回差策略解决了动态自主权调整的可逆性、可恢复性与可审计性难题，证明了复合指标与硬防护的互补价值，为未来人机协作系统的安全可信自主权管理奠定了理论与实践基石。

Abstract: Human-machine systems rarely operate at a fixed level of AI autonomy. As operators and AI systems collaborate over time, control must shift: the AI can take on more responsibility when collaboration is stable, maintain its current role when evidence is ambiguous, or return control to the human when conditions deteriorate. Existing work on adaptive automation, supervisory control, trust in automation, and deskilling explains parts of this problem, but provides no auditable, multi-signal criterion for governing when autonomy should change across multi-cycle workflows.
  We formalise this challenge as the Handover Problem: deciding, at each operational cycle, whether to escalate, maintain, or revert AI autonomy while keeping the process reversible, recoverable, and auditable. We introduce the Handover Readiness Score (HRS), a transparent composite measure that integrates four signal dimensions: operator readiness, human-AI trust, learning stability, and operational performance. It is combined with a hysteresis-based transition policy that requires sustained positive evidence before increasing autonomy but reverts promptly when conditions worsen.
  Across software engineering and manufacturing domains, the HRS and hard safety guards address complementary failure regimes: guards enforce immediate corrective action when a single indicator breaches a critical threshold, while the HRS detects the slow, multi-signal erosion of operator readiness that no individual guard can observe. The framework establishes autonomy handover as a governance problem requiring explicit, composite, and auditable criteria. This provides a conceptual and formal foundation that adaptive automation research has not previously provided.

</details>


### [166] [Open-MMUnlearning: Unifying Methods and Evaluation for MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10358)
*Junkai Chen,Yuhao He,Qianshan Wei,Junxiang You,Jingwen Shao,Junkai Lin,Zhongkai Yue,Xiaotian Ye,Zhengbo Jiao,Jiali Cheng,Zhijie Deng,Kening Zheng,Ruiqi Liu,Hadi Amiri,Yi Yu,Zhenan Sun,Qi Li,Ka-Ho Chow,Sijia Liu,Liang Wang,Jiaqi Li,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Open-MMUnlearning开源框架，系统整合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）遗忘的模型准备、数据处理、遗忘算法与评估，支持5个基准、8个模型、12种遗忘方法，并引入指标元评估协议。实验显示GD与MIP-Editor并列总分最高，前者遗忘质量最优，后者效用保留更好；BLEU指标综合可靠性最高，KS-Test忠实度AUC最高但鲁棒性较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MLLM能力增强与广泛部署，隐私与安全顾虑日益突出。机器遗忘可移除指定知识并保留无关能力，但现有实现碎片化、评估协议不一、鲁棒性测试不足、指标可靠性理解有限，导致MLLM遗忘研究难以系统推进。

Method: 构建Open-MMUnlearning框架：通过共享接口与结构化配置统一目标模型准备、多模态数据处理、遗忘算法与评估流程；涵盖隐私、安全、版权5大基准，4个模型家族共8个MLLM，12种遗忘方法；评估套件联合考量遗忘效果、保留效用、模型干预/对抗输入/成员推理攻击鲁棒性；另引入指标元评估协议，利用受控知识暴露模型、量化与重学习场景测试指标忠实度与鲁棒性。

Result: 在统一协议下对比10种代表性遗忘方法：GD与MIP-Editor总分并列第一，GD获最高Forget Quality，MIP-Editor保留更多Model Utility。13个评估指标中，BLEU综合可靠性得分最高；KS-Test达最高faithfulness AUC，但在鲁棒性维度表现较弱。

Conclusion: Open-MMUnlearning为MLLM遗忘提供可复现的统一比较平台，并通过指标元评估揭示评价指标的可靠性差异，推动该领域系统化、严谨化发展。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) become more capable and widely deployed, concerns about privacy and safety have become increasingly pressing. Machine unlearning offers one approach to addressing these concerns by removing designated information from trained models while preserving unrelated capabilities. However, fragmented implementations and evaluation protocols, incomplete robustness testing, and limited understanding of metric reliability make progress in MLLM unlearning difficult to assess systematically. We introduce Open-MMUnlearning, an open-source, extensible framework that integrates target-model preparation, multimodal data processing, unlearning, and evaluation through shared interfaces and structured configurations. The framework supports five benchmarks spanning privacy, safety, and copyright, eight MLLMs from four model families, and twelve unlearning methods. Its evaluation suite jointly assesses forgetting effectiveness, retained utility, and robustness to model interventions, adversarial inputs, and membership inference attacks. Using a common evaluation protocol, we compare ten representative unlearning methods. In this comparison, GD and MIP-Editor tie for the highest overall score: GD achieves the highest Forget Quality, while MIP-Editor preserves more Model Utility. We further introduce a metric meta-evaluation protocol that tests faithfulness using models with controlled exposure to target knowledge and robustness under quantization and relearning. Among the thirteen evaluated metrics, BLEU achieves the highest aggregate reliability score. KS-Test attains the highest faithfulness AUC but performs less well on robustness. Together, the framework and these findings support reproducible comparison of MLLM unlearning methods and systematic assessment of evaluation reliability.

</details>


### [167] [Reasoning-Token Spikes Under Prompted Untruthful Responding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10405)
*Maverick Morales,Tomáš Dominik,Vermut Gao,Katrina Shirey,Paulius Rimkevičius,Aaron Schurger,Uri Maoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认知负荷理论的低带宽信号——推理token数量，用于在无法访问或不信任思维链内容时检测模型欺骗行为。实验表明，三个推理型大模型在被提示说实话时生成的推理token显著少于被提示撒谎或无视真假时的情况，证明了推理token计数可作为区分非真实与真实模型行为的简单、内容无关候选信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链监控依赖推理轨迹的可读性、忠实性及可访问性，但证据表明思维链输出可能变得不可读、不忠实甚至不可访问。因此需要一种不依赖推理内容、低带宽的替代信号来检测欺骗等不当行为。

Method: 基于认知负荷理论，选取三个具备推理能力的大语言模型，让其回答210道涵盖分析性、描述性、规范性推理类型及道德/非道德领域的多项选择题。通过系统提示分别指令模型：真实作答、虚假作答、不考虑真假作答。统计并对比三种条件下生成的推理token数量。

Result: 跨所有三个模型，真实导向作答引发的推理token数量显著少于虚假导向和真假不计导向作答。显式提示的非真实响应策略在测试时推理token使用上产生了稳健的组间差异。

Conclusion: 推理token计数可作为简单、内容无关的候选信号，在原始推理轨迹不可用或不可靠时区分非真实与真实模型行为。尚未确立其作为自发欺骗或一般错位的实例级检测器，未来需测试实例级检测率、分布外泛化、习得欺骗策略、隐性目标及对抗鲁棒性。

Abstract: Monitoring the chain-of-thought of reasoning artificial intelligence (AI) models remains a key approach to detecting deception and other forms of misbehavior in such models. However, semantic chain-of-thought monitoring depends on reasoning traces being legible and sufficiently faithful to the underlying computations that produced the model's behavior, not to mention accessible. Moreover, there is increasing evidence that chain-of-thought outputs may soon become illegible or unfaithful, if they even remain accessible. Based on cognitive load theory, we investigate a lower-bandwidth signal -- the number of reasoning tokens generated -- which does not require access to the content of the reasoning trace. Three reasoning-capable large language models answered 210 multiple-choice questions -- across analytic, descriptive, and normative reasoning types as well as moral and non-moral domains -- under system prompts instructing them to respond truthfully, falsely, or without regard for truth. Across all three models, truth-directed responding elicited fewer reasoning tokens than both lie-directed and truth-indifferent responding. These findings show that explicitly prompted untruthful response policies can produce robust group-level differences in test-time reasoning-token use. While not yet establishing reasoning-token count as a detector of spontaneous deception or general misalignment, our results are a proof of concept that it can serve as a simple, content-independent candidate signal for differentiating untruthful from truthful model behavior when raw reasoning traces are unavailable or unreliable. Future work should test instance-level detection rates, out-of-distribution generalization, learned deceptive policies, hidden objectives, and robustness under adversarial pressure.

</details>


### [168] [SOTA: Stock Options Trading Agents Guided by Option-Implied Return Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10407)
*Yizhen Xie,Mengyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SOTA框架，利用大语言模型（Qwen3.8-27B）经监督微调和强化学习训练，实现期权策略选择，在九只大盘股和SPY期权上实现18.3%收益、夏普1.60、最大回撤8.96%，并发现新闻在训练中作用不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着期权市场增长和AI进步，基于语言模型的智能体交易受关注，但期权交易决策复杂：单一股票有数千合约，需决定交易哪些合约及如何组合。现有方法常限制固定策略结构（如跨式），无法随市场条件切换策略。

Method: 提出SOTA框架，将庞大期权合约空间抽象为策略级决策，由确定性解析器处理组合实现。采用Qwen3.8-27B模型，先监督微调后强化学习训练。在九只大盘美股及SPY期权上评估，对比规则基础和机器学习策略选择器。

Result: 六个月样本外测试中，SOTA获得18.3%总收益，夏普比率1.60，最大回撤8.96%。消融实验显示新闻在训练中作用不对称：新闻改善前沿教师轨迹，但强化学习阶段保留新闻会导致样本外收益从18.3%降至-2.7%。

Conclusion: SOTA展示了大语言模型在期权策略选择中的有效性，策略级抽象与确定性执行相结合可处理大规模合约空间。新闻对训练有益但对强化学习阶段有害，提示需谨慎设计信息输入。

Abstract: As option markets grow and AI advances, agentic systems for option trading are gaining increasing attention. Language-model-based agents can reason over contextual information such as news, but option trading presents a particularly challenging decision problem: a single stock can have thousands of contracts, and the agent must decide both which contracts to trade and how to combine them. Existing approaches often sidestep this complexity by restricting the policy to a fixed strategy structure, such as a straddle, limiting their ability to switch strategies as market conditions change. We present SOTA (Stock Options Trading Agents), an agentic trading framework for structured option-strategy selection. SOTA abstracts the large option universe into strategy-level decisions while deterministic resolvers handle portfolio implementation. We develop SOTA by post-training Qwen3.8-27B with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. SOTA is evaluated on options on nine large-cap U.S. equities and SPY against rule-based and machine-learning strategy selectors in the same trading environment. Over a six-month out-of-sample period, SOTA earns an 18.3% total return with a Sharpe ratio of 1.60 and a maximum drawdown of 8.96%. We also document an asymmetric role of news: news improves frontier-teacher trajectories, but retaining news during reinforcement learning reduces out-of-sample return from 18.3% to -2.7%.

</details>


### [169] [RunningTab: Direct Workspace Interaction with Environment-Side Tabs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10444)
*Jinheon Baek,Soyeong Jeong,Yumin Choi,Dongsu Han,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RunningTab 框架，为直接工作空间交互（DWI）增加环境侧标签页，记录任务需求、已读文件摘录与未打开候选文件，解决 LLM 智能体在生成交付物时遗漏关键信息的问题。实验表明 RunningTab 在三个基准上持续优于纯 DWI 及模型内记录基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识工作常需从工作空间现有文件生成新交付物，LLM 智能体通过直接工作空间交互（DWI）可直接搜索读取文件。但现有 DWI 缺乏任务级记忆：任务需求、已读内容、列出但未打开的文件均会随上下文窗口滑动而丢失，导致智能体可能提取了数据却未纳入最终报告。

Method: RunningTab 在环境侧维护每任务的“待办记录”：智能体添加需求条目；环境自动记录每次读取的文件摘录及其来源，并将列出但未打开的文件标记为候选。智能体可查看每个需求旁的最佳匹配摘录与顶部未打开候选，据此解决需求或搁置并说明理由；若尝试在仍有未决需求时结束任务，环境会触发完成检查并提醒未决需求。

Result: 在三个基准（具体基准未在摘要中列出）上使用三种 LLM 进行验证，RunningTab 稳定超越纯 DWI 及将记录保存在模型内部的基线方法；其标签页通常能在文件被读取后保留交付物所需的关键值。

Conclusion: RunningTab 通过环境侧标签页显式跟踪任务需求与文件交互状态，有效缓解了 DWI 中上下文窗口遗忘导致的信息遗漏，显著提升了 LLM 智能体生成完整交付物的能力，且记录机制轻量、可解释。

Abstract: Much knowledge work produces new deliverables from files a workspace already holds, and LLM agents are beginning to take such work over. Through direct corpus interaction, an agent can search and read any of those files from a terminal with no indexing, and producing a deliverable from many of them in this way is what we call direct workspace interaction (DWI). Reaching the files, however, is only half the task: nothing keeps track of what the task asks for, what has been read, and what was listed but never opened, all of which slip through the context window without leaving a trace, so an agent may extract a figure and still deliver a report without it. To address this, we present RunningTab, a framework that equips direct workspace interaction with an environment-side tab: a per-task record of what the task still owes, kept by the environment alongside the agent. Specifically, the agent adds its requirements, while the environment records every file read as an excerpt with its provenance and every listed but unopened file as a candidate; the agent can then see each requirement beside its best-matching excerpts and top unopened candidates, resolve it against matching content or set it aside with a reason, and, should it try to finish with requirements still open, receive them in a finish check. We validate RunningTab on three benchmarks with three LLMs, where it consistently outperforms plain DWI and baselines that keep the record in the model, while its tab usually holds the values a deliverable needs once seen.

</details>


### [170] [Before They Can Solve: Predicting Post-Training Coding-Agent Performance from Base Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10478)
*Tan Yu,Alexander Bukharin,Khushi Bhardwaj,Jennifer Williams,Zirui Liu,Jonathan Lingjie Li,Soumye Singhal,Joseph Jennings,Sanjeev Satheesh,Yash Jain,Ashish Vaswani,Venkat Krishna Srinivasan,Matthew Papakipos,Hyunwoo Kim,Jian Zhang,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Markus Kliegl,Mostofa Patwary,Mohammad Shoeybi,Bryan Catanzaro,Jonathan Cohen,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种基于成功智能体轨迹的筛选方法，用于预测哪些基础模型检查点值得进行昂贵的智能体后训练。通过回放成功轨迹并识别“决定性步骤”，设计了决定性动作BPB、补丁MCQ和前缀条件pass@K三个无需冷启动即可评估基础模型潜力的筛选器。实验表明三种方法在十对公开基础/后训练模型上与SWE-bench Verified pass@1排名高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端pass@K评估不适合智能体编码，因为基础模型难以可靠产生格式良好的工具调用；单步任务虽然避免了工具调用失败，但绕过了核心能力——在仓库演进过程中跨多工具步骤维持连贯状态。需要一种能评估基础模型在多步工具使用中潜力的方法。

Method: 将成功的后训练智能体轨迹作为前瞻信号。回放每条轨迹，在每个改代码步骤后重新运行测试，识别决定性步骤：累积补丁首次将仓库从失败翻转为通过的步骤。在此步骤构建三个筛选器：(i) 决定性动作BPB测量认证动作的概率质量；(ii) 补丁MCQ测试检查点在认证动作与同一验证器拒绝的备选动作间的选择；(iii) 前缀条件pass@K评估功能正确生成的支持度，认可测试接受的任何续写。

Result: 在十对公开基础模型与后训练模型上，三种筛选器对模型队列的排序与后训练SWE-bench Verified pass@1结果高度一致。方法仅需基准的成功轨迹及其验证器，可推广至未来智能体编码基准。

Conclusion: 通过利用成功后训练轨迹中的决定性步骤，无需基础模型从冷启动驱动工具链，即可构建有效的基础模型潜力评估筛选器，为智能体后训练的检查点选择提供低成本、可泛化的预测工具。

Abstract: How can we predict which base checkpoint is worth an expensive round of agentic post-training? End-to-end pass@$K$ tests whether successful behavior already appears in a base model's distribution, but it is a poor fit for agentic coding: many base checkpoints cannot reliably produce the well-formed tool invocation required to complete a task end-to-end. Single-shot or short-horizon tasks avoid these tool-calling failures by collapsing a multi-step interaction into a fixed prompt and a single patch, but they sidestep the core capability we care about: maintaining coherent state over many tool-using steps as the repository evolves. To bridge this gap, we treat successful post-trained agent trajectories as a lookahead signal of base-model potential. Replaying each trajectory and rerunning tests after every code-changing step identifies the decisive step: the first step whose cumulative patch flips the repository from failing to passing, certifying that the recorded action solves the task given the prior context. Motivated by a coverage principle for agentic traces, we build three screens at this step that do not require a base checkpoint to drive the harness from a cold start: (i) Decisive-Action BPB (bits per byte) measures the probability mass on the certified action, (ii) Patch MCQ tests the checkpoint's choice between that action and alternatives rejected by the same verifier, and (iii) prefix-conditioned pass@$K$ evaluates support for functionally-correct generations and credits any continuation that the tests accept. Across ten pairs of public base and post-trained models, all three screens rank the cohort in close agreement with post-trained SWE-bench Verified pass@$1$. As our methods need only a benchmark's successful trajectories and its verifier, they can be applied to turn future agentic coding benchmarks into base-model evaluations.

</details>


### [171] [Validity Without Ground Truth: What Stated-Preference Economics Offers the Evaluation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10506)
*Daniel Robert Kling Alexander,Catherine Louise Kling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将所述偏好经济学中的效度框架（内容效度、构念效度、标准效度、信度、激励相容性、后果性）作为大语言模型评估的通用方法，并以水质价值评估调查为例，对六个模型进行测试，发现新模型通过理论效度测试但趋同效度存在分歧，强调通过效度测试仅表明回答连贯而非正确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型面临许多无标准答案的评估问题（如政策价值、用户偏好、价值权衡），传统评估依赖真值不可行。所述偏好经济学数十年来在无真值情况下评估调查回答，其效度框架可迁移为LLM评估的通用方法论。

Method: 采用已发表的水质所述偏好经济估值调查（Vossler et al. 2023），向六个大语言模型施测。效度测试基于经济理论预测：需求曲线向下倾斜、支付意愿随商品范围和收入变化。检验内容效度、构念效度、标准效度、信度、激励相容性、后果性等维度。

Result: 两个旧模型在家庭收入75,000美元时无法通过最基本测试；两个最新模型通过所有可评分的理论效度测试，但在趋同效度上表现分歧。结果显示模型间差异显著，效度框架能有效区分模型表现。

Conclusion: 所述偏好经济学的效度框架为LLM评估提供了系统性方法。通过效度测试表明模型回答具有内部连贯性和理论一致性，但不代表回答在实质上正确。该方法适用于无标准答案的主观、价值导向型评估场景。

Abstract: Many of the questions now put to large language models have no correct answer to score against: what a policy is worth, which option a user should choose, how to weigh competing values. Stated-preference economics has faced this problem for decades. It judges survey responses without knowing the true value, through a framework of validity and related concepts: content, construct, and criterion validity, reliability, incentive compatibility, and consequentiality. We argue that this framework is a general method for evaluating language models, and we set out what each concept means for LLM evaluation. We demonstrate the approach using a published water-quality stated preference economic valuation survey (Vossler et al. 2023) administered to six models. In this economic application, the validity tests take the form of predictions from economic theory: demand should slope down, and willingness to pay should respond to the scope of the good and to income. The tests separate the models sharply. Two older models fail the most basic test at a household income level of \$75,000, and the two newest pass every test of theoretical validity we can score, but diverge on convergent validity. Passing validity tests shows that a model's answers are coherent, not that they are correct.

</details>


### [172] [EmbodiedRSI: Active Continual Robot Learning Through Hypothesis-Guided Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10498)
*Python Song,Zhixuan Liang,Kelsey Fu,Mengdi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmbodiedRSI 是一种自我进化的智能体框架，采用快慢双系统架构，通过假设图维护竞争性代码和技能假设，利用信息价值实验选择指导物理探索，实现代码-技能协同进化，并在分层记忆和奖励接地记忆学习支持下，显著提升机器人基础模型的泛化与适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型在物体位置或任务指令变化时性能下降，后训练需要大量昂贵的遥操作数据；现有自我进化框架在决定代码和技能改进方向时，机器人试验利用效率低。

Method: 提出快慢双系统架构：快速系统维护假设图存储竞争性代码/技能假设，通过信息价值实验选择选择最能区分假设的物理实验，实验结果指导代码-技能协同进化；慢速系统构建分层记忆，奖励接地记忆学习根据对快速系统改进的价值选择有效记忆。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 达到 77.0% 总体成功率和 71.3% Composite-Unseen 成功率（基线最好 40.1%）；在 LIBERO-Pro 达到 86.8% 总体成功率；零样本迁移到真实机器人，在多个挑战性任务上达到 71.3% 总体成功率。

Conclusion: EmbodiedRSI 通过自主探索和代码-技能协同进化，有效解决了机器人基础模型的适应性问题，在基准测试和真实世界部署中均展现出卓越性能，为机器人自主持续学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robot foundation models provide strong visuomotor control, yet their performance can degrade when object positions or task instructions change. Further improvements often require post-training on substantial robot data, which can be costly to collect through methods such as teleoperation. Agentic harnesses can adapt around the model, but current self-evolving harnesses use robot trials inefficiently when deciding which code and skill changes to pursue. We introduce EmbodiedRSI, a self-evolving agentic harness that autonomously decides where to explore next and turns the resulting physical interaction into improved code and skills. EmbodiedRSI realizes this through a Fast-Slow Dual-System Architecture, in which competing code and skill hypotheses are maintained in a Hypothesis Graph. Value-of-Information Experiment Selection chooses physical experiments that can distinguish these hypotheses. Their outcomes guide Code-Skill Co-Evolution. The Slow System builds Hierarchical Memory, and Reward-Grounded Memory Learning selects effective memory according to their value for later Fast-System improvement. On RoboCasa365, EmbodiedRSI reaches 77.0% overall success and 71.3% on Composite-Unseen, compared with 40.1% for the best baseline. EmbodiedRSI also reaches 86.8% overall success on LIBERO-Pro. Beyond benchmark performance, EmbodiedRSI transfers zero-shot to real-world robot, achieving 71.3% overall success across multiple challenging tasks.

</details>


### [173] [RECAST: Learning to Compute the Right Context through Adaptive Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507)
*Yilun Hao,Krishna Sayana,Isabella Ye,James S Ren,Sukhdeep Sodhi,Craig Boutilier,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied to tasks grounded in long, heterogeneous information sources. Conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on fixed similarity-based retrieval, while agentic variants adapt queries and tool use but remain largely retrieval-centric. However, in many tasks, the evidence required for a solution is not explicitly present in any single source item. Instead, it must be derived through filtering, aggregation, or computation across multiple source items. In this work, we introduce RECAST (Routing Evidence through Computation, Access, and Synthesized Tools), a learned framework that formulates evidence construction as a sequential decision process over heterogeneous retrieval and computation operations, allowing evidence to be actively derived rather than merely retrieved. A lightweight RouterLM iteratively selects and formulates primitive operations or specifies customized operations for a frozen CompilerLM to translate into executable code. Once it judges the evidence sufficient, RouterLM passes the accepted evidence to a frozen AnswerLM to produce the final solution. We train RouterLM with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across six heterogeneous benchmark families, RECAST achieves a mean success rate of 75.6%, outperforming the strongest large-model baseline by 15.9%. Moreover, training enables the Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM to outperform a training-free Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM by 5.0%. On three held-out benchmarks, RECAST improves over the strongest baseline by 15.0% on average, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization across tasks and heterogeneous source representations.

</details>


### [174] [SciExam for ENSO: Can AI Agents Build Climate Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10513)
*Yinling Zhang,Langchen Liu,Dongbin Xiu,Xueyan Zou,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个名为SciExam for ENSO的基准测试，用于评估语言模型智能体进行开放式科学研究的能力。智能体在六小时内从真实观测数据构建ENSO的低阶随机模型，通过隐藏评分器测试模型的统计复现、未观测变量恢复和预测能力。12个智能体系统中有6个超越已发表模型，其模型结构与ENSO暖冷不对称性的两种竞争解释相兼容，表明智能体已能构建有竞争力的科学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型智能体在开放式科学研究中的评估通常依赖已知答案、评分标准或语言模型评审，这些方法无法判断新科学模型的有效性。需要一种无标准答案的评估框架，测试智能体从真实观测中发现科学规律并构建可验证模型的能力。ENSO作为年际气候变率的主导模式，具有未解的暖冷不对称性机制争论，适合作为测试平台。

Method: 构建SciExam for ENSO基准：智能体在6小时预算内处理真实观测数据，编写诊断代码（冻结后不可改），仅使用诊断结果作为反馈开发低阶随机模型。隐藏评分器从三个维度测试模型：1)复现ENSO统计特性，2)恢复未观测变量，3)预测保留年份。同等标准评分已发表模型作为基线。测试12个智能体系统，并对顶级系统进行受控实验（变换输入信息）验证非记忆性。

Result: 12个智能体系统中有6个生成的模型得分高于已发表基线模型，主要优势在于重构和预测能力。表现最强的模型的简化形式分别与ENSO暖冷不对称性的两种竞争理论解释相兼容，而任务从未提及该争论。受控实验显示顶级系统得分非源于记忆过时观测记录，接收信息的差异会塑造其模型构建方式。

Conclusion: SciExam for ENSO成功建立了无标准答案的智能体科学研究评估范式。结果表明当前智能体已能构建与已发表模型竞争甚至超越的科学模型，且模型结构能触及科学家仍在争论的核心科学问题。这证明语言模型智能体具备从观测数据中发现科学规律并生成有理论意义模型的潜力。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out open-ended scientific research, yet their results are usually graded against a known answer, a rubric, or a language-model reviewer, none of which can tell whether a new scientific model is valid. The AI Science Exam for El Nino-Southern Oscillation (SciExam for ENSO) is a benchmark in which agents build low-order stochastic models of ENSO, the dominant mode of interannual climate variability, from real observations. Within a six-hour budget, agents process the observations, write their own diagnostics, which are then frozen, and develop a model using only these diagnostics as feedback. Hidden graders then test whether the model reproduces ENSO's statistics, recovers unobserved variables, and forecasts held-out years, and score a published model in the same way. Across twelve agent systems, six produce models that score higher than the published model, mainly through better reconstruction and forecasting. The simplified forms of the stronger models are each compatible with one of the two competing explanations of ENSO's warm-cold asymmetry, an open debate that the task never mentions. Controlled runs of the top system under varied information suggest that its scores do not come from recalling the dated observational record and that the information it receives shapes how it builds its model. SciExam for ENSO can thus evaluate agent research where no answer is known, and the results suggest that agents can already build competitive models whose structures bear on questions that scientists still debate.

</details>


### [175] [RoboJEPA: Scaling Robotic Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10515)
*Artem Zholus,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Sarath Chandar,Tushar Nagarajan,Daniel Severo,Koustuv Sinha,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero Soriano,Jeannette Bohg,Nicolas Ballas,Mahmoud Assran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboJEPA，一个基于联合嵌入预测架构 (JEPA) 的潜在世界模型，在包含 12 种机器人实体的大规模真实机器人数据集上训练。研究建立了多实体机器人世界模型的首个缩放定律：想象误差随算力呈二阶幂律变化，能准确预测模型质量；下游规划性能随算力可预测提升，且与想象误差强相关，可作为真实机器人评估的可靠代理指标。RoboJEPA (8B 参数) 可零样本部署为机器人智能体，仅凭单目标图像即可在真实硬件上完成长时规划任务。所有模型检查点、训练及部署代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型虽能预测未来状态并规划，但缺乏 principled 的方法来估算其能力如何随模型规模、数据量和算力缩放，这一开放问题阻碍了领域进展。

Method: 基于联合嵌入预测架构 (JEPA) 构建 RoboJEPA，在覆盖 12 种机器人实体的大规模真实机器人数据集上训练；系统测量想象误差随算力的缩放行为，拟合二阶幂律；评估下游机器人规划性能随算力的变化，并分析想象误差与真实规划性能的相关性；在真实硬件上进行零样本部署验证。

Result: 1. 想象误差遵循二阶幂律缩放，可外推预测更大规模模型质量。2. 下游机器人规划性能随算力可预测提升，且与想象误差强相关 (r > 0.9)，想象误差是可靠的真实机器人评估代理。3. RoboJEPA (8B 参数) 可零样本部署，仅凭单目标图像在真实硬件上解决长时规划任务。4. 发布了目前最大的 JEPA 预测模型检查点及完整训练/部署代码。

Conclusion: 本工作为多实体机器人世界模型建立了首个真实数据上的缩放定律，证明了想象误差作为评估代理的有效性，并展示了大规模 JEPA 模型在真实机器人上的零样本规划能力，为未来机器人基础模型的规模化发展提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Latent world models have shown a remarkable ability to predict future states and to plan in the real world. In practice, however, we lack a principled way to estimate how their capabilities scale with model size, data, and compute, an open problem that slows progress in the field. In this work we present RoboJEPA, a world model based on the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and trained on a large-scale dataset spanning 12 robotic embodiments. We show that RoboJEPA's imagination error, the error of its latent rollouts, follows a second-order power law in compute, allowing us to predict model quality well beyond the scale at which the law is fit. We further show that downstream robotic planning performance improves predictably with compute, and that imagination error is strongly correlated with it, making it a reliable proxy for real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate that latent world models can be deployed zero-shot as robotic agents, planning toward a single goal image to solve tasks requiring long-horizon planning on real hardware. We release all model checkpoints together with our training and robot deployment code. To our knowledge, this is the first work to establish scaling laws for multi-embodiment robotic world models trained on real robot data, and RoboJEPA, at 8B parameters, is the largest JEPA predictor model trained to date.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [A Bayesian Mirror Architecture for Emergent Consciousness: Circular Hierarchies, Self-Manifolds, and Hybrid Event-Self Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08792)
*Eduardo Righi Capanema de Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了贝叶斯镜像架构（BMA），一种自我参照的生成框架，通过感官抽象、元抽象和自我潜变量的循环递归实现意识作为架构属性。核心约束为闭合更新 S_t <- H_{t-1}，其中混合事件-自我潜变量 H_t 将自我表征与世界模型耦合并再注入自我状态。推理在概率测度空间上进行，配备最优传输几何，稳定性和连贯性用 2-Wasserstein 度量形式化。定义了因果学习机制（CLR）通过 Wasserstein 信念漂移界和整合指数诊断环境可学习因果结构。系统可表现出多个连贯盆地，自我流形定义为局部 Wasserstein 压缩下不变测度的支撑，Wasserstein epsilon-necks 作为传输瓶颈导致盆地解耦，在消失电导极限下产生唯一实现延续，解释为选择。学习通过变分自由能最小化进行，稳定性和能动性源于环境提供的可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在建立一个数学严谨的基础框架，将意识形式化为特定循环架构的内在属性，而非优化目标或语义标签。通过引入最优传输几何和 Wasserstein 度量，为自我稳定性、混合连贯性、因果学习机制和选择提供坐标自由的定量表征，解决自我参照系统中稳定性、能动性和因果学习的统一建模问题。

Method: 1. 定义贝叶斯镜像架构（BMA）：感官抽象、元抽象、自我潜变量通过循环递归交互，核心约束为闭合更新 S_t <- H_{t-1}。2. 状态空间建模为概率测度空间 P_2 配备 2-Wasserstein 度量。3. 稳定性和连贯性用 Wasserstein 几何形式化。4. 定义因果学习机制（CLR）：Wasserstein 信念漂移界 + 整合指数。5. 引入盆地局部 Wasserstein 压缩定义自我流形。6. 识别 Wasserstein epsilon-necks 作为盆地解耦的传输瓶颈。7. 学习采用变分自由能最小化。

Result: 1. 意识作为具有闭合循环结构系统的架构属性被形式化。2. CLR 可诊断环境是否包含可学习因果结构，但非意识标记。3. 系统不需要全局严格压缩性，可存在多个连贯盆地。4. 自我流形作为局部不变测度支撑被良定义。5. Wasserstein epsilon-necks 在消失电导极限下产生唯一实现延续，被解释为选择：内部决定但有限分辨率下外部不可预测。6. 稳定性和能动性从环境可学习性中涌现。

Conclusion: BMA 提供了一个统一的数学框架，将自我参照生成建模、意识架构属性、因果学习诊断、多盆地动力学、选择机制和能动性涌现整合在最优传输几何和变分自由能原理中。该框架不预设全局收敛，而是通过局部 Wasserstein 压缩和传输瓶颈解释选择的内在决定性与外在不可预测性的二重性。

Abstract: We present a foundational formulation of the Bayesian Mirror Architecture (BMA), a self-referential generative framework in which sensory abstractions, meta-abstractions, and a self-latent interact through circular recursion. The defining constraint is a closed update S_t <- H_{t-1}, where a hybrid event-self latent H_t binds self-representations to abstract world models and reinjects this coupling into the self-state. Consciousness, in a restricted sense, is not an optimization objective nor a semantic label, but an architectural property of systems possessing this circular structure.
  Because inference operates over posterior beliefs, BMA's intrinsic state space is a space of probability measures equipped with optimal-transport geometry. Stability and coherence are formulated in the 2-Wasserstein metric on P_2, yielding coordinate-free notions of self-stability and hybrid coherence along belief trajectories. We define a Causal Learning Regime (CLR) via bounds on Wasserstein belief drift together with an integration index capturing sustained coupling between self and world latents. CLR diagnoses whether the environment contains learnable causal structure; it is not a marker of consciousness.
  Global strict contractivity is not required: BMA may exhibit multiple coherent basins. We define self-manifolds basin-wise as supports of invariant measures under local Wasserstein contractivity. We identify Wasserstein epsilon-necks, transport bottlenecks where basins decouple, yielding a unique realized continuation in a vanishing-conductance limit. We interpret this selection as choice: internally determined yet externally unpredictable at finite resolution. Learning proceeds via variational free-energy minimization, with stability and agency emerging from what the environment affords to learn.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Baseline Severity: Temporal and Disease-Specific Predictors of Depression Outcomes Following Mindfulness Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08809)
*Muhammad Jawad Chowdhury,Sultanus Salehin,Akib Jayed Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用可解释机器学习模型，基于多中心纵向临床队列数据，预测正念干预后12周和24周的BDI-II抑郁评分，发现基线严重程度是最强预测因子，短期预后更依赖临床和医院背景，长期预后更依赖行为依从性和人口学因素，支持个性化心理健康支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究慢性或急性疾病患者抑郁严重程度受基线心理状态、人口学特征、临床背景和行为干预参与度复杂交互影响，旨在通过可解释机器学习模型预测正念干预后的短期和长期抑郁结局，为个性化心理健康支持提供依据。

Method: 方法包括使用人口学变量、临床状况信息、医院中心标识、基线BDI-II评分和治疗参与度指标构建特征，采用基于模型的随机插补处理缺失随访结果，评估五种回归模型（含正则化线性回归和树集成方法），并进行疾病特异性和层级亚组分析。

Result: 结果显示Ridge回归在12周预测中表现最佳（RMSE=5.186, R²=0.474），LightGBM在24周预测中表现最佳（RMSE=5.038, R²=0.525）。基线严重程度为最强整体预测因子；短期预后更强相关于临床和医院背景；长期预后更依赖行为依从性和人口学因素；预测因子在不同临床类别内外存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 结论支持使用可解释、情境感知的建模方法，以指导正念干预后的个性化心理健康支持，强调需考虑临床背景、行为依从性和人口学因素的差异化影响。

Abstract: Depression severity among patients with chronic or acute medical conditions is influenced by a complex interaction of baseline psychological state, demographic characteristics, clinical context, and engagement with behavioral interventions. This paper presents an interpretable machine-learning analysis of a multi-center longitudinal clinical cohort to predict Beck Depression Inventory-II (BDI-II) scores at 12 and 24 weeks following mindfulness-based intervention participation. The study uses demographic variables, clinical condition information, hospital-center identifiers, baseline BDI-II scores, and therapy engagement measures to model short-term and long-term depression outcomes. Missing follow-up outcomes were addressed using a model-based stochastic imputation procedure to preserve the modest sample size while maintaining outcome variability. Five regression models were evaluated, spanning regularized linear regression and tree-based ensemble methods. Ridge Regression achieved the best 12-week performance with an RMSE of 5.186 and R^2 of 0.474, while LightGBM achieved the best 24-week performance with an RMSE of 5.038 and R^2 of 0.525. Beyond prediction accuracy, the analysis reveals three clinically relevant patterns: baseline severity remains the strongest overall predictor, short-term outcomes are more strongly associated with clinical and hospital context, and long-term outcomes show greater dependence on behavioral adherence and demographic factors. Disease-specific and hierarchical subgroup analyses further indicate that predictors differ substantially across and within clinical categories. These findings support the use of interpretable, context-aware modeling to inform personalized mental-health support following mindfulness-based interventions.

</details>


### [178] [Transferability and operational reliability of a Prithvi crop classification foundation model under phenological and geographic shift across three continents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08810)
*Venkatesh Kolluru,Rajat Shinde,Abdelhak Marouane,Caden Helbling,Deepak Shah,Othneil Drew,Srinivas Kolluru,Iksha Gurung,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了广泛采用的地理空间基础模型 Prithvi-EO-2.0 在作物分类任务中的分布外性能，发现跨大陆迁移时准确率显著下降（美国0.65→欧洲0.40），主要归因于观测窗口与当地作物物候期错配而非空间域迁移问题。通过合并混淆类别和调整观测窗口长度可在不重训练的情况下恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在大型卫星档案上预训练并微调的地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）能提升作物分类精度和地理迁移性，但其在训练分布之外的操作性能尚未被充分表征，特别是跨大陆部署时的可靠性未知。

Method: 在12个国家37个事件上评估 Prithvi-EO-2.0 的分布外性能，对比区域参考产品；从整体精度、置信度校准、观测窗口敏感性、类别合并效果、波段缺失与云影鲁棒性五个维度分析；引入蒙特卡洛 Dropout 熵量化分布偏移；提出无需重训练的两种调整策略：基于主导混淆合并13类为10类，以及压缩观测窗口至近实时长度。

Result: 平均整体精度从美国的0.65跌至欧洲的0.40；观测窗口与物候期错配导致精度崩塌但确定性置信度仍高，7/8对比事件的期望校准误差增加，12-51%受影响场景被高置信度错误标记；蒙特卡洛 Dropout 熵在所有8个事件中均检测到分布偏移，证实跨大陆下降主要源于物候错配。合并类别使平均OA提升8.4个百分点；窗口压缩在45-90天平台期保持精度（峰值约75天），30天以下下降约0.11。

Conclusion: 微调作物 GeoFMs 仅在观测窗口匹配当地生长季节时才能有效迁移。论文据此提出了发布模型可靠部署的操作指南：需确保观测窗口与物候对齐，适当合并易混淆类别，并利用不确定性量化（如MC Dropout熵）监测分布偏移。

Abstract: Fine-tuned geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) pretrained on large satellite archives have been shown to improve crop classification accuracy and geographic transferability. However, their operational performance beyond the training distribution remains poorly characterized. We evaluated the out-of-distribution performance of a widely adopted GeoFM [Prithvi-EO-2.0] across 37 events in 12 countries on three continents and validated against regional reference products. Results indicated that the mean overall accuracy (OA) declined from 0.65 in the United States to 0.40 in Europe. Beyond accuracy metrics, we assessed five key aspects of model performance: whether model confidence indicates signal failure, sensitivity to observation windows, the effect of coarsening class schemes, and robustness to both band loss and cloud- and shadow-contamination. Accuracy collapsed when the observation window misaligned with local crop phenology, while deterministic confidence remained high. Expected calibration error increased for seven of eight paired events, and 12-51% of each affected scene was confidently mislabeled at near-zero precision. Monte Carlo dropout entropy registered the shift in all eight, indicating that much of the apparent cross-continent decline reflected phenological misalignment rather than spatial transfer. Two adjustments recovered accuracy without retraining. Consolidating 13 classes into 10, based on the model's dominant confusions, raised the mean OA by 8.4 percentage points. Compressing the window toward near-real-time use preserved accuracy across a 45- to 90-day plateau, peaking near 75 days, though arms tighter than 30 days fell about 0.11 below that plateau. Fine-tuned crop GeoFMs therefore transfer usefully only where observation windows match local growing seasons. We translate these findings into operational guidance for the reliable deployment of the released model.

</details>


### [179] [KVFetch: Temporal Prefetching for the Missing Half of KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08811)
*Linfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 KVFetch，一种无需训练的即插即用框架，为基于分数的 KV 缓存压缩器增加了时间召回通道，解决了“顺序遗忘”问题，显著提升了需要逐字复现上下文任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存压缩方法仅基于内容相关性决定保留什么，忽略了缓存的顺序遍历访问模式，导致在需要逐字复现上下文（如 RAG、代码补全、结构化数据抽取）的任务中出现“顺序遗忘”现象，即保留了序列头部但丢弃了后续内容，造成不可逆的复制中断，这是压缩下剩余质量损失的主要来源。

Method: KVFetch 将被驱逐的候选项降级到量化的冷存储层，通过单调读取指针检测活跃复制行为，并将位置后继预取到固定大小的热存储槽位中，且不增加注意力计算开销，可作为任何基于分数的压缩器的即插即用增强模块。

Result: 在 RULER-16K 基准上（等预算控制），KVFetch 将逐字复制准确率从 0.8 提升至 78.4，13 项任务平均分提升 +8.4，增益集中在需要顺序访问的任务；在 LongBench 上（无需顺序访问的任务），该通道保持休眠且无额外开销。

Conclusion: KVFetch 通过引入时间维度的召回机制，弥补了现有压缩器仅支持内容检索而忽略顺序遍历的结构性缺陷，有效解决了顺序遗忘问题，在需要逐字复现的任务上带来显著提升，且在不需要时零开销，是一种通用、高效的 KV 缓存压缩增强方案。

Abstract: As context windows scale to tens or hundreds of thousands of tokens, KV cache compression has become essential for efficient LLM inference. Existing methods fall into three families: score-based eviction, summary compensation, and offload-and-recall. Yet all three decide what to keep or recall by content relevance to the current query. We show this shared design is structurally incomplete. A cache supports two access modes: associative lookup by content and sequential traversal by position; current compressors implement only the first. The gap matters in practice: retrieval-augmented generation, code completion, and structured-data extraction all require the model to reproduce identifiers, field values, or code tokens verbatim from the context. Under compression, content-based eviction retains the head of such a sequence but discards its continuation, causing verbatim copying to break irreversibly midway, a failure we call sequential forgetting. This failure resists better scoring, larger budgets, summary compensation, and dynamic re-scoring; it is the dominant source of remaining quality loss under compression. We propose KVFetch, a training-free, drop-in framework that opens a temporal recall channel for any score-based compressor. It demotes evicted candidates to a quantized cold tier, detects active copying through a monotone read pointer, and prefetches positional successors into fixed-size hot-tier slots without increasing attention cost. On RULER-16K under an iso-budget control, KVFetch recovers verbatim copying from 0.8 to 78.4 and raises the 13-task average by +8.4, with gains concentrating on tasks that require sequential access. On LongBench, where no task requires sequential access, the channel remains dormant and imposes no cost.

</details>


### [180] [Bounded Autonomy and Verifiable Safety for Agentic AI Enabled Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08815)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Deveeshree Nayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BRaVeS（有界推理与安全治理框架），通过 SME 定义的不变锚点、MoDA-Style 深度感知注意力、SMARtAutonomy 状态层级以及 Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF) 实现有界恢复，在工业控制系统仿真中验证了有限步收敛且无安全护栏违规，为高风险环境下的 Agentic AI 提供了可仿真验证的有界治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI 在高风险环境中仅凭概率推理无法安全部署，核心风险是认知漂移：随着推理深入，系统行为可能偏离领域专家约束。需要一种形式化框架，将专家约束编码为不变锚点，并在推理过程中保持其可见性，同时根据认知风险动态降低自主权，实现有界恢复与安全收敛。

Method: 1. 将 SME 约束编码为不变锚点；2. 提出 MoDA-Style（混合深度注意力）深度感知访问机制，保持锚点在推理时可见；3. 引入 SMARtAutonomy 状态层级，随认知风险升高降低自主权；4. 构建 Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF)，将连续认知风险信号映射为有限 K-bag 抽象，采用屏蔽状态转移强制 Lyapunov 能量下降或路由至人工终端状态；5. 通过离散事件蒙特卡洛仿真，使用 HAI 22.04 工控系统时序数据并注入合成噪声与传感器退化模式进行评估。

Result: 在测试的参数分组策略与阈值下，LBCF 过程实现有限步收敛，且未出现安全护栏违规。仿真结果表明，在既定抽象假设（固定阈值、可行屏蔽）下，有界治理行为可被强制执行；但形式化收敛结论仅适用于有限 LBCF 抽象，不覆盖完整连续神经激活空间。

Conclusion: BRaVeS 为高风险环境下的 Agentic AI 提供了一个可仿真验证的有界推理与安全治理框架，通过不变锚点、深度感知注意力、自主权分级与 Lyapunov 有界共识实现了有界恢复。仿真证据支持其在抽象层面的有效性，后续工作需落地至部署式 Transformer 实现、实时人在回路验证及更广泛的对抗场景测试。

Abstract: Agentic AI-enabled automation cannot be safely deployed in high-stakes environments on probabilistic reasoning alone. A recurring risk is epistemic drift: as reasoning deepens, system behavior may move away from subject-matter-expert constraints for safe operation. This paper presents BRaVeS, a bounded reasoning and safety-governance framework termed the Defensible Next-Gen Reasoning System (DNRS). BRaVeS encodes SME-defined constraints as invariant anchors, proposes MoDA-Style (Mixture of Depths Attention) depth-aware access as a candidate mechanism for keeping these anchors visible during inference, and uses a state hierarchy (SMARtAutonomy) to reduce autonomy as epistemic risk increases. To formalize bounded recovery, we introduce the Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF), which maps continuous epistemic-risk signals into a finite K-bag abstraction and applies shielded state transitions that enforce Lyapunov-style energy descent or route the system to a human-mediated terminal state. The formal convergence result applies to the finite LBCF abstraction under fixed thresholds and feasible-shield assumptions; it does not prove safety of the full continuous neural activation space. We evaluate the framework through a discrete event Monte Carlo simulation using HAI 22.04 industrial-control-system time-series data with synthetic noise and sensor-degradation regimes. Across the tested parameter-grouping strategies and thresholds, the LBCF process achieved finite-step convergence and no safety-guard violations. These results provide simulation-based evidence that bounded governance behavior can be enforced under the stated abstraction, while motivating future work on deployed transformer implementations, live human-in-the-loop validation, and broader adversarial settings.

</details>


### [181] [The Cost of Long Memory: State, Context, and Stability Complexity in Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08816)
*Yuheng Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究长程时间依赖下的序列模型预测资源需求，证明了代数衰减预测记忆的指数/有限状态模式的匹配上下界，揭示了预测误差与状态数的平方对数关系，并分析了分数阶长记忆、非线性收缩循环及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在给定预测记忆律下，序列模型需要多少状态、上下文或动力学临界性才能准确预测，解决长程依赖预测中的资源问题。

Method: 理论分析：为代数衰减预测记忆建立指数模式和有限状态模式的匹配上下近似界；研究分数阶长记忆下的预测几何变化；推导非线性收缩状态动力学下的指数一阶混沌包络及必要条件；有限样本KL散度计算连接预测几何与统计信息；实验验证使用收缩状态空间、门控循环和注意力模型。

Result: 最佳r模式预测误差衰减为e^{-Θ(√r)}，达到误差τ需r=Θ(log²(1/τ))状态；分数阶长记忆改变几何：有限上下文长度L预测误差首项为1/L，固定分数强度d保持平方对数状态复杂度律；短记忆边界识别d²和d⁴尺度；收缩循环需定量趋向临界性才能使代数目标预测误差消失，这是必要非充分条件；实验复现理论预测。

Conclusion: 论文确立了长程依赖预测的最优资源指数，揭示了分数阶长记忆的几何特性，证明了收缩循环预测准确性与收缩边际的关系，并通过实验验证了理论预测，为序列模型设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Long-range temporal dependence poses a resource question for sequence models: for a specified predictive-memory law, how much state, context, or dynamical criticality is required in order to forecast accurately? We study this question directly in forecasting risk. For algebraically decaying predictive memory, we prove matching upper and lower approximation bounds for exponential and finite-state modes. The best $r$-mode forecast error decays as $e^{-Θ(\sqrt r)}$, so reaching forecast error $τ$ needs $r=Θ(\log^2(1/τ))$ states or modes. Earlier curse-of-memory results establish broad limitations of stable recurrent models under different approximation notions; here both sides match for one canonical predictive target in forecast risk, which fixes the optimal resource exponent for that target. We then show that genuine fractional long memory changes the geometry itself. In particular, forecast error is measured after fractional integration, prediction from a finite context of length $L$ has an exact $1/L$ leading order, and a fixed fractional strength $d$ keeps the square-log state-complexity law. Near the short-memory boundary, we identify the relevant $d^2$ and $d^4$ scales and give a uniform constructive law in the intermediate regime. For nonlinear contextual recurrences with uniformly contractive state dynamics, we derive an exponential first-chaos envelope and an explicit necessary condition that relates forecast accuracy to the contraction margin. Vanishing forecasting error on an algebraic target forces the recurrence quantitatively toward criticality, a condition that is necessary and not by itself sufficient. Finite-sample Kullback--Leibler calculations further connect the predictive geometry to statistical information. Theorem-matched experiments with contractive state-space, gated recurrent, and attention models reproduce the state and stability predictions.

</details>


### [182] [DenoFlow: Flow Matching for SSVEP Denoising under Real Physiological Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08817)
*Zhentao He,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于修正流的SSVEP去噪方法DenoFlow，将去噪建模为传输问题，通过场网络回归速度场并积分，结合分类器监督，无需对抗训练，在两个公开数据集上优于七种基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSVEP系统高度易受噪声和伪影影响，严重降低解码准确率；现有去噪方法缺乏配对真值、易陷入复现输入、仅优化波形距离而忽略输出的可解码性。

Method: DenoFlow将去噪建模为修正流传输：场网络（编码器-解码器）回归污染试验与纯净试验间直线路径的速度场，从观测值积分前向去噪；网络每层接收污染试验，瓶颈层接收路径位置；联合训练分类器监督积分输出；训练简化为回归，避免对抗极大极小博弈；通过注入生理EMG/EOG伪影构建配对训练数据。

Result: 在两个公开SSVEP数据集上，使用五种流行解码器评估，DenoFlow在信号保真度和下游解码准确率上均显著优于七种基线去噪模型。

Conclusion: DenoFlow通过修正流框架有效解决SSVEP去噪中缺乏配对数据、优化目标与解码任务不一致等核心问题，实现了高保真的去噪并显著提升BCI解码性能。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain-computer interfaces (BCIs), particularly steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) systems, are highly vulnerable to noise and artifacts, which severely degrade decoding accuracy. Although recent denoising approaches have shown promise, they are fitted without paired ground truth, can settle on reproducing their input, and are optimized on waveform distance alone, which says nothing about whether the output stays decodable. To address these issues, we propose DenoFlow, which casts SSVEP denoising as transport: instead of learning a direct map from a contaminated trial to a clean one, a field network regresses the velocity of the straight path between them, following the rectified-flow formulation, and denoising integrates that field forward from the observation. The field network is an encoder-decoder that sees the contaminated trial at every layer and the path position at its bottleneck, and a classifier trained alongside it supervises the integrated output. Because the observation itself is both the conditioning input and the starting point of the integration, the model never generates a trial from noise, and training reduces to regression, removing the adversarial min-max game. To obtain paired data on datasets with no ground truth, we injected physiological artifacts of the recorded electromyography (EMG) and electrooculography (EOG) signals under a controlled signal-to-noise target. Experiments on two public SSVEP datasets with five popular SSVEP decoders showed that DenoFlow outperformed seven baseline denoising models on both signal fidelity and downstream decoding accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/wzwvv/DenoFlow.

</details>


### [183] [Just for FUNS: LLM-Guided Spatio-Temporal Graph Node Generation for Forecasting Unobserved Node States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08818)
*Shuhao Li,Weidong Yang,Changan Liu,Wei Zhuo,Yingbo Zhou,Fan Zhang,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenST 框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）作为语义桥梁，通过两阶段生成架构（时空 VAE + 生成式 Transformer）解决时空预测中未观测节点状态预测（FUNS）问题，在零样本预测任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器网络受限于部署成本和维护资源，空间覆盖往往不全面，导致缺乏历史观测的未观测节点状态预测（FUNS）成为关键挑战。传统模型依赖历史观测，在无先验记录的节点上失效。

Method: 将问题重新定义为时空图上的条件生成任务。设计两阶段生成架构：第一阶段，时空 VAE 将时空动态压缩到潜在空间；第二阶段，生成式 Transformer (GenT) 从噪声重建未观测节点的未来状态，由语义特征（节点描述如功能区、路网结构）、地理坐标和邻域上下文等多模态条件引导。利用预训练 LLM 微调提取丰富语义特征，补偿缺失的时空信号。

Result: 在六个交通和两个非交通数据集上实验，GenST 在零样本预测任务中显著优于现有基线，展示了语义引导生成缓解时空数据稀疏的实际潜力。

Conclusion: GenST 通过引入 LLM 作为语义桥梁，结合生成式建模，有效解决了未观测节点的时空预测问题，为时空数据稀疏场景下的预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting is a cornerstone of logistics, urban planning, and intelligent transportation systems. However, constrained by deployment costs and maintenance resources, sensor networks often lack comprehensive spatial coverage, rendering Forecast Unobserved Node States (FUNS) a critical yet formidable challenge. Conventional models rely on historical observations and typically falter when encountering nodes without prior records. To address this, we redefine the problem as a conditional generation task on spatio-temporal graphs and propose GenST, a framework that introduces Large Language Models (LLMs) as a semantic bridge, leveraging a pre-trained LLM fine-tuned to extract rich semantic features from node descriptions, such as functional zones and road network structures, to compensate for missing spatio-temporal signals. Specifically, we design a two-stage generative architecture: a Spatio-Temporal VAE first compresses spatio-temporal dynamics into a latent space, followed by a Generative Transformer (GenT) that reconstructs the future states of unobserved nodes from noise, guided by multi-modal conditions including semantics, geographic coordinates, and neighborhood contexts. Experiments on six traffic and two non-traffic datasets show GenST significantly outperforms existing baselines in zero-shot prediction tasks, demonstrating the practical potential of semantic-guided generation for mitigating spatio-temporal data sparsity.

</details>


### [184] [HydroSphere: A Framework for Governed, Self-Healing Wastewater Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08819)
*Prabu,Fancy C,Suresh A,Srini Ramaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HydroSphere，一个受控的数据驱动框架，用于实时水质监测、预测、处理优化和故障恢复。该框架整合了混合 TCN-LSTM 预测模型、基于 PPO 的自适应投加优化模块和基于深度自编码器的 SHADE 异常检测模块，并在 282 万条历史水质数据上验证，实现了多步预测（RMSE 0.1417）、投加奖励提升（1.059 vs 1.017）和异常检测 F1 0.651，同时引入分级治理和安全边界保障系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速工业化和城市化加剧了水质与污水处理系统的压力，传统处理厂依赖静态监控和控制策略，难以适应动态变化的污染物条件，亟需一种能实时监测、预测、优化投加并自动故障恢复的智能化框架。

Method: HydroSphere 包含三大核心组件：1) 混合 TCN-LSTM 模型对七项水质参数进行多步预测；2) 自适应投加优化模块采用 PPO 强化学习动态调整化学药剂投加量；3) SHADE 异常检测模块利用深度自编码器识别传感器与工艺异常。框架还融合分级治理、确定性安全边界和人工监督机制。

Result: 在 1940-2023 年 282 万条实测数据上：预测模型 RMSE 0.1417、MAE 0.1047、R² 0.3596；PPO 投加策略平均步奖励 1.059，优于固定投加基线 1.017，但无约束优化会导致过量投加，证明需显式安全约束；SHADE 在可控故障注入下 F1 得分 0.651。

Conclusion: HydroSphere 通过预测、优化、检测的集成与治理机制，为污水处理提供了可扩展的智能化管理基础，支撑联合国可持续发展目标 6（清洁饮水）和 13（气候行动）的实现。

Abstract: Rapid industrialization and urban growth are increasing pressure on water quality and wastewater treatment systems, while conventional treatment plants often rely on static monitoring and control strategies that cannot easily adapt to changing pollutant conditions. This paper presents HydroSphere, a governed, data-driven framework for real-time water quality monitoring, forecasting, treatment optimization, and fault recovery. HydroSphere is evaluated using 2.82 million water-quality measurements collected between 1940 and 2023. The framework integrates three main components. First, a hybrid TCN-LSTM model performs multi-step forecasting across seven water-quality parameters, achieving an RMSE of 0.1417, MAE of 0.1047, and R2 of 0.3596. Second, the Adaptive Dosage Optimization Module uses PPO reinforcement learning to adjust chemical dosing, achieving a mean step reward of 1.059 compared with 1.017 for a fixed-dose baseline. The results also show that unconstrained reward optimization can lead to excessive dosing, demonstrating the need for explicit operational safeguards. Third, the SHADE anomaly detection module uses a deep autoencoder to identify sensor and process anomalies, achieving an F1 score of 0.651 under controlled fault injection. HydroSphere combines these capabilities with tiered governance, deterministic safety bounds, and human oversight to support safer and more adaptive water infrastructure. The framework provides a scalable foundation for intelligent wastewater management and supports the objectives of UN Sustainable Development Goals 6 and 13.

</details>


### [185] [Task-Oriented Key-Layer KV Communication for Efficient Latent Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08820)
*Dongsen Zhang,Peipei Li,Zekun Li,Wenjun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KITE框架，针对基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中潜在通信的问题，从任务导向角度重新定义通信目标，仅传输关键层的KV缓存，显著降低通信开销并提升推理效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于KV的潜在通信方法优先保证发送端状态保真度，导致通信和计算开销巨大，且可能引入冗余信息，限制了多智能体协作的效率。

Method: 提出KITE框架：1) 使用接收端轨迹失真准则识别任务有效的关键层；2) 仅传输与关键层相关的潜在工作记忆；3) 将同一层作为自回归潜在推理的入口点。这是一个无需训练的框架。

Result: 在两个模型家族、三种模型规模的七个基准测试上，相比全层KV通信：通信量减少28-36倍，端到端推理加速高达3倍，准确率最高提升23.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 从发送端状态保真转向接收端任务充分性的任务导向潜在通信范式，能够在大幅降低通信和计算成本的同时显著提升多智能体系统的协作性能。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through collaboration, while latent communication directly transmits model internal states to avoid the high inference costs of natural language. However, existing KV-based latent communication methods prioritize sender-side state fidelity, leading to substantial communication and computation overhead and potentially introducing redundant information. To address these limitations, we revisit latent communication from a task-oriented perspective, shifting its objective from sender-side state fidelity to receiver-side task sufficiency. Under this formulation, we propose KITE, a training-free framework for task-oriented key-layer KV communication. KITE identifies a task-effective key layer using a receiver trajectory distortion criterion, transmits only the latent working memory associated with the key layer, and further uses the same layer as the entry point for autoregressive latent reasoning. Experiments on seven benchmarks across two model families and three model scales show that, compared with full-layer KV communication, KITE reduces communication volume by 28-36$\times$, achieves up to 3$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and improves accuracy by up to 23.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [186] [A Vehicle-Integrated Approach to Digital Twin Deployment for Bridges Through Drive-By Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08822)
*Zihao Liu,Daigo Kawabe,Jiaji Wang,Chul-Woo Kim,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种车辆集成数字孪生框架，结合基于物理的建模和机器学习，利用搭载传感器的检测车辆通过车桥互作用和车路互作用实现桥梁和路面的连续监测，解决了传统监测成本高、难以规模化及目视检查主观性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老化桥梁基础设施监测需求，传统结构健康监测（SHM）系统成本高、难以大规模部署，常规目视检查主观性强。驶过式桥梁检测虽具可扩展性，但仍面临桥梁响应与路面粗糙度分离、正常交通下损伤检测、跨桥型泛化等关键挑战。

Method: 构建三大支柱：1) 基于傅里叶神经算子的代理模型，学习从工况到车辆响应的函数映射，融合仿真与现场数据，实现毫秒级推理；2) 通过贝叶斯优化设计专用电动检测车辆、传感器布局及信号处理链，最大化桥梁信息提取并抑制路面与车辆噪声，开发基于对抗自编码器、矩阵剖面和Transformer的无监督损伤评估流程；3) 在澳大利亚和日本开展协同多现场试验，覆盖多种桥型、交通与环境条件验证全流程。

Result: 代理模型替代了计算密集的全阶分析，实现毫秒级推理；优化的车辆与信号处理链有效提取桥梁信息；无监督损伤评估流程经验证可处理车辆数据；多现场试验证明框架在多样桥型、交通与环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该车辆集成数字孪生框架统一了基于物理的建模与机器学习，为桥梁和路面状况的连续、可扩展监测提供了可行方案，有望推动驶过式桥梁检测的实际应用。}

Abstract: Ageing bridge infrastructure is a growing global concern, yet conventional Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) systems are costly and difficult to scale, and routine visual inspections remain subjective. Drive-by, or indirect, bridge inspection, in which a sensorised vehicle recovers structural information from vehicle-bridge interaction (VBI) and vehicle-road interaction (VRI) responses, offers a scalable alternative. However, key challenges remain unresolved, including separating bridge responses from road roughness, detecting damage under normal traffic, and generalising across diverse bridge types. This paper presents a vehicle-integrated digital twin framework that unifies physics-based modelling and machine learning for continuous monitoring of bridge and road conditions. The framework comprises three pillars. First, surrogate models of VBI and VRI are constructed using a Fourier Neural Operator that learns function-to-function mappings from operating conditions to vehicle responses. Trained on both simulated and field data, these surrogates deliver millisecond-scale inference, replacing computationally intensive full-order analyses. Second, the design of a custom electric inspection vehicle, its sensor layout, and signal processing chain are optimised through Bayesian optimisation to maximise bridge information yield while suppressing road and vehicle noise. Unsupervised damage-assessment pipelines based on adversarial autoencoders, matrix profiles, and transformer architectures have been developed and validated to process the resulting vehicle data. Third, the complete workflow is validated through coordinated multi-site field trials in Australia and Japan, covering a range of bridge types, traffic conditions, and environmental settings.

</details>


### [187] [HCPN-GCN: Scaling Hierarchical Prototype Networks with Cone Geometry for Continual Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08823)
*Sammuel R. Silva,Vander L. S. Freitas,Gladston Moreira,Eduardo J. S. Luz,Rodrigo Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HCPN-GCN，一种图感知的层次原型网络扩展，通过 GCN 替代线性特征提取器并引入锥形原型与多样性正则化，在六个持续图学习基准上显著提升分类准确率且近零遗忘，同时以约 30 倍更少的原子原型实现更紧凑的记忆表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图学习面临灾难性遗忘挑战。现有的层次原型网络（HPN）虽通过原型记忆避免存储历史数据，但其线性特征提取器难以利用图拓扑，且基于点的原型在结构多样图上导致原型低效增长，限制了表示能力和记忆紧凑性。

Method: 提出 HCPN-GCN：(1) 将 HPN 的线性特征提取器替换为图卷积网络（GCN）以捕获图拓扑信息；(2) 引入锥形原型替代点原型，配合多样性正则化目标，紧凑建模嵌入空间，抑制原型扩增同时保持判别力。

Result: 在六个持续图学习基准上，HCPN-GCN 持续优于原始 HPN 和代表性持续学习基线，平均分类准确率提升且保持近零遗忘；分析显示其学习到更丰富的类级原型层级，仅用约 30 倍更少的原子原型即实现更紧凑有效的记忆表示。

Conclusion: HCPN-GCN 通过图感知特征提取和锥形原型机制，有效解决了持续图学习中的灾难性遗忘和原型冗余问题，提供了一种更紧凑、判别力强的层次原型记忆框架，为持续图学习提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual Graph Learning (CGL) aims to incrementally learn from graph-structured data while preserving knowledge acquired from previous tasks. A major challenge in this setting is catastrophic forgetting, where learning new tasks degrades performance on previously learned ones. Hierarchical Prototype Networks (HPNs) address this problem through a prototype-based memory mechanism that avoids storing historical data, but their reliance on linear feature extractors limits their ability to exploit graph topology, while point-based prototypes often lead to inefficient prototype growth on structurally diverse graphs. In this work, we propose HCPN-GCN, a graph-aware extension of HPN that replaces the original linear feature extractors with Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) and introduces cone-based prototypes with a diversity regularization objective. The proposed design produces richer graph-aware representations while compactly modeling the embedding space, reducing prototype proliferation without sacrificing discriminability. Experimental results on six continual graph learning benchmarks demonstrate that HCPN-GCN consistently improves average classification accuracy over the original HPN and representative continual learning baselines while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Furthermore, our analysis shows that the proposed model learns substantially richer class-level prototype hierarchies using approximately $30\times$ fewer atomic prototypes than the original HPN, providing a more compact and effective memory representation for continual graph learning.

</details>


### [188] [FinVector-Market-4B: A Controlled Study of LoRA Adaptation for Structured Financial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08882)
*Alina Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FinVector-Market-4B 使用 LoRA 微调 Qwen3.5-4B，在 22k 金融语料上适配结构化任务，显著提升 JSON 有效性与多项下游指标，但增益受限于任务分布与提示契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究紧凑型金融领域适配（LoRA）能否在结构化输出任务中超越单纯的格式学习，并量化显式 JSON Schema 约束对基础模型与适配模型的提升幅度。

Method: 基于 Qwen3.5-4B 应用 rank-16 LoRA，在 22,000 条金融示例上训练；在 600 条基准上对比基础模型与适配模型，分别使用隐式与显式 JSON Schema 提示，评估 FinQA 精确匹配、计算器表达式正确性、场景分支标签一致性、蕴含方向一致性等指标，并进行事后策略评分审计。

Result: 显式 Schema 使基础模型 JSON 有效性从 0% 跃升至 91.3%；在匹配显式提示下，适配模型相对基础模型提升：FinQA 精确匹配 14.7%→40.0%，计算器表达式正确性 48.0%→82.7%，场景分支标签一致性 20.1%→89.5%，蕴含方向一致性 52.4%→87.2%。宏观 F1 下降归因于标签集变更，固定三类标签下基础模型 77.4%、适配模型 83.1%。基准存在文件重叠与计算器目标不一致，泛化性受限。

Conclusion: 紧凑金融领域适配能在匹配提示契约下带来显著任务特定增益，超越单纯输出格式学习，但增益受评估任务分布与提示契约约束，基准构建需注意数据泄露与标签一致性。

Abstract: FinVector-Market-4B adapts Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B with rank-16 LoRA on a 22,000-example corpus for structured financial tasks. We evaluate the base and adapted models on the same 600-example benchmark under implicit and explicit JSON-schema contracts. Supplying the schema alone raises base-model JSON validity from 0% to 91.3%. Under matched explicit prompting, the frozen scores improve from 14.7% to 40.0% for FinQA answer exact match, from 48.0% to 82.7% for calculator-expression correctness, from 20.1% to 89.5% for scenario branch-label agreement, and from 52.4% to 87.2% for implication-direction agreement. A post-hoc policy-scoring audit shows that the reported macro-F1 decline reflects a changing label set; using the same three target classes gives 77.4% for the base and 83.1% for the adapter. Filing overlap and calculator-target inconsistencies qualify the benchmark's generalization claims. The results show that compact financial domain adaptation can produce substantial task-specific gains beyond output-format learning under matched prompting, with gains bounded by the evaluated task distribution and prompt contract.

</details>


### [189] [GraphOPD: Graph-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08959)
*Bohan Lin,Liyi Chen,Zhuoning Guo,Muyang Li,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Yao Hu,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GraphOPD，首个将基于图的结构增强引入在线策略蒸馏的方法，解决多轮智能体中教师-学生发散信号失效的问题，通过环境状态变化构建依赖图并结合随机游走稳态分布与发散信号，在ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA三个基准上显著超越基线（最高+5.8 pp），并验证了结构信用对真实因果影响的准确追踪及跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏方法依赖教师-学生逐步发散大小分配监督，假设大发散标记错误。但在多轮决策链中，早期偏移会污染后续上下文，导致教师与偏移轨迹一致而非标记根因，且可互换步骤产生大但与结果无关的发散。实验表明，基于最高发散步骤蒸馏不优于随机选择，亟需一种免疫偏移的结构化信用分配机制。

Method: GraphOPD利用环境自身的状态变化记录构建步骤依赖图（哪些步骤启用了哪些后续步骤），该图免疫教师-学生发散的偏移腐败。在图上计算随机游走稳态分布作为结构信用分数，再将其与发散信号融合为轨迹相对掩码，集中监督于每次rollout的最高能力步骤。

Result: 在三种模型规模、十一基线及ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA三大基准上，GraphOPD全程具竞争力，最高比最强基线提升+5.8个百分点。执行回放审计显示：结构信用分数远超随机水平追踪真实因果影响，两个融合信号各自必要，且该信号能迁移至域外工具集成推理任务。

Conclusion: GraphOPD通过图结构增强修正了在线策略蒸馏中发散信号的失效，实现了更精准的步骤级信用分配，显著提升了大模型智能体在复杂多轮任务上的性能，并展示了结构信用的因果有效性与跨域泛化潜力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation post-trains large language model agents by supplying dense, step-level guidance from a teacher policy when the reinforcement-learning reward is sparse and arrives only once per trajectory. Existing instantiations allocate this guidance by the size of the teacher-student divergence at each step, on the single-turn intuition that a large disagreement marks a mistake worth correcting. Once decisions chain over many turns, that rule misfires, since an early drift enters every later context both policies condition on, leaving the teacher consistent with the drifted trajectory instead of flagging its cause, while interchangeable steps register large but outcome-irrelevant divergences. We demonstrate this on an agentic benchmark, where distilling the highest-divergence steps brings no consistent benefit over random selection. To this end, we introduce GraphOPD, the first method to bring graph-based structural augmentation into on-policy distillation for agent capabilities. It reads which steps enabled which later ones from the environment's own record of state changes, immune to the drift that corrupts the teacher-student gap, organizes them into a dependency graph, scores each step by a random-walk stationary distribution over it, and fuses that structural credit with the divergence signal into a trajectory-relative mask concentrating supervision on each rollout's highest-aptitude steps. Across three model scales and eleven baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, GraphOPD shows competitive performance throughout, improving over the strongest baseline by up to +5.8 pp. An executed-replay audit further shows that this structural credit score tracks true causal impact far above chance, that both fused signals are independently necessary, and that the same signal transfers to out-of-domain tool-integrated reasoning.

</details>


### [190] [Two-Level Softmax Sampling Done Right: Correcting Bias from Size Imbalance and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10483)
*Walid Bendada,Guillaume Salha-Galvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出两级 Softmax (2LS) 采样因忽略簇大小不平衡和簇内相似度离散度而引入系统性偏差，提出 S-2LS 和 SD-2LS 两种修正方法，在几乎无额外计算开销下提供可证明更好的 Softmax 近似，实验验证其优越性，建议替代标准 2LS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax 采样在大规模场景下因线性复杂度而不切实际，2LS 虽然实现了亚线性采样，但忽略簇大小不平衡和簇内相似度离散度导致系统性采样偏差，需要修正这些偏差以获得更准确的 Softmax 近似。

Method: 提出两种修正采样方法：Size-Corrected 2LS (S-2LS) 修正簇大小不平衡带来的偏差；Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS (SD-2LS) 进一步修正簇内相似度离散度带来的偏差。两者均保持亚线性复杂度，计算开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在五个大规模数据集上的深入实验验证了 S-2LS 和 SD-2LS 具有更优的采样性质，能提供可证明更好的 Softmax 近似，且计算开销微乎其微。

Conclusion: 2LS 采样存在因忽略簇大小和簇内离散度导致的系统性偏差；S-2LS 和 SD-2LS 能有效修正这些偏差，在无显著额外开销下显著提升采样质量，建议在未来工作中持续使用这两种方法替代标准 2LS。

Abstract: Sampling from a softmax distribution is a fundamental operation in machine learning, but its linear complexity in the number of items makes exact sampling impractical at scale. Two-level softmax (2LS) sampling is a popular alternative enabling sublinear-time sampling. Assuming items are partitioned into clusters, 2LS first samples a cluster and then an item within it. In this paper, we show that, despite its advantages, 2LS introduces systematic and undesirable sampling biases, which arise from misweighting clusters by ignoring both cluster size imbalance and intra-cluster similarity dispersion. We propose two sampling methods, Size-Corrected 2LS (S-2LS) and Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS (SD-2LS), which correct these biases and provide provably better softmax approximations with negligible to non-existent computational overhead. In-depth experiments on five large-scale datasets validate the improved sampling properties of our methods. We recommend their consistent use in place of standard 2LS in future work.

</details>


### [191] [Directed Temporal Representations for Offline Visual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08960)
*Chenyang Yuan,Haoyu Wang,Zhuo Sun,Xiaoyuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DTRC 从离线视觉轨迹学习有向时间拟度量，作为时序评论家指导直接目标条件策略学习，在十个视觉控制任务上超越规划与直接策略基线，测试时无需迭代轨迹搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测世界模型虽提供紧凑视觉表示，但控制需要与时序可达性而非仅预测相似性对齐的潜在几何结构。

Method: 在冻结的 LeWM 特征上构建有向时间拟度量：短程时间偏移校准距离尺度，引导目标扩展长程时序可达性，动作条件一致性对齐局部转移动态。距离估计时序到达成本，其变化定义目标相对时序进展，用作时序评论家训练目标条件策略；模型辅助目标在行为支持与动力学一致约束下进一步细化训练。

Result: 十个视觉控制任务中 DTRC 实现强目标条件控制性能。四个 LeWM 任务的保留诊断显示：一致的短程时序校准、任务依赖的长程与方向结构、正的转移级进展。时序监督改进所有四任务的流策略参数化，测试时策略直接行动无需规划。

Conclusion: DTRC 通过学习与时序可达性对齐的有向时间表示，实现了高效的直接目标条件视觉控制，消除了测试时的迭代轨迹搜索需求。

Abstract: Predictive world models provide compact visual representations for control. Control requires a latent geometry aligned with temporal reachability rather than predictive similarity alone. We introduce Directed Temporal Representations for Control (DTRC), which learns such a geometry from offline visual trajectories on top of frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM) features. DTRC constructs a directed temporal quasimetric over the learned control representation. Short-range temporal offsets calibrate the distance scale. Bootstrapped targets extend temporal reachability across longer horizons. Action-conditioned consistency aligns the representation with local transition dynamics. The resulting distance estimates temporal reaching cost, and its change across a transition defines goal-relative temporal progress. We use this progress signal as a temporal critic for direct goal-conditioned policy learning. Model-assisted targets provide an additional training-time refinement under behavior-support and dynamics-agreement constraints. Across ten visual control tasks, DTRC achieves strong goal-conditioned control performance relative to planning and direct-policy baselines. Held-out diagnostics on the four LeWM tasks show consistent short-range temporal calibration, task-dependent long-range and directional structure, and positive transition-level progress. Temporal supervision improves the same flow-policy parameterization across all four LeWM tasks, while the resulting policy acts directly without iterative trajectory search at test time.

</details>


### [192] [On KL-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08963)
*Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 KLPO (KL-Regularized Policy Optimization)，一种用于异步 LLM 智能体强化学习的无 Critic、单次 Rollout、无需重要性加权的策略优化框架。它将 KL 正则化锚定在采样端，通过最小二乘拟合对数比最优性条件，用采样器自身轨迹直接计算梯度，避免了 GRPO 那样昂贵的多响应采样和重要性采样的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步 RL 训练 LLM 智能体时，推理引擎使用的陈旧检查点导致其概率分布与训练器不同。现有方案要么裁剪重要性比率（引入偏差），要么如 GRPO 每个提示采样一组响应（长 Episode 时成本高）。需要一种无偏、低成本、无需 Critic 和重要性加权的更新规则。

Method: KLPO 将 KL 正则化项锚定在采样器分布上，正则化改进步骤具有闭形式的 Gibbs 解。通过在采样器自身轨迹上用最小二乘拟合对数比最优性条件，采样器概率以对数比形式进入，无需重要性权重。消去回归截距后，难以处理的对数配分函数被替换为信号的采样器均值加采样器到训练器的 KL 散度。Token 级镜像下降目标的梯度可仅用终端回报通过采样器中心化得分或单轨迹残差计算，甚至适用于随机工具输出。

Result: 证明独立 Monte Carlo 估计 KL 项保持梯度无偏；推导更廉价的 top-K 和二值近似的精确 KL 缺口；指出 SPPO、GPO、REBEL、BPO 均为 KLPO 特例。实现了单次 Rollout、无 Critic、无学习归一化器、无需分组响应的更新。

Conclusion: KLPO 为异步 LLM 智能体 RL 提供了理论完备且工程高效的统一框架，消除了重要性采样偏差与多响应采样开销，兼容随机环境，并统一了多种现有算法。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) for large language model (LLM) agents trains one policy on trajectories generated by another: rollouts come from stale checkpoints, and the inference engine's probabilities differ from the trainer's even at identical parameters. Standard remedies either clip importance ratios, which biases the update, or, as in GRPO, sample a group of responses per prompt, which is costly when episodes are long. We propose KL-Regularized Policy Optimization (KLPO), a framework that anchors the KL regularizer at the sampler. The regularized improvement step then has a closed-form Gibbs solution, and KLPO fits its log-ratio optimality condition by least squares on the sampler's own trajectories, so the sampler probability enters through a log-ratio and no importance weights are needed. Profiling out the regression intercept replaces the intractable log-partition function with the signal's sampler mean plus a sampler-to-trainer KL divergence. For token-level policy mirror descent targets, we show that the resulting gradient can be computed from terminal returns without a critic, via sampler-centered scores or a single trajectory residual, even under stochastic tool outputs. We further prove that independent Monte Carlo estimates of the KL term keep these gradients unbiased, derive the exact KL gap of cheaper top-$K$ and binary approximations, and show that SPPO, GPO, REBEL, and BPO arise as special cases of KLPO. The result is a critic-free update that uses one rollout per prompt and requires neither a learned normalizer nor a group of responses.

</details>


### [193] [Work While They Sleep: Exploiting Evaluation Latency for Fully Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08969)
*Gustavo Sutter,Alejandro Comas-Leon,David Holzmüller,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ELF-BO 算法，利用目标函数评估的延迟时间预先采样超参数后验，仅在观测到目标值后进行重加权，实现了全贝叶斯优化且决策延迟不增加，使全贝叶斯优化在实际应用中变得实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）常用于昂贵黑盒优化，标准 BO 使用超参数点估计，而全贝叶斯方法通过模型平均考虑超参数不确定性，在低数据量下提供更好的不确定性估计，但计算代价高昂，鲜有实际应用。

Method: ELF-BO 在目标函数评估期间并行采样超参数后验分布，待目标值返回后仅需对已有样本进行重要性重加权，避免了决策时刻的完整后验推断开销。

Result: 在合成函数和真实任务上，ELF-BO 的优化性能与全贝叶斯方法相当，决策延迟与标准 BO 相当甚至更低，显著降低了全贝叶斯优化的计算门槛。

Conclusion: ELF-BO 通过利用目标评估延迟掩盖超参数采样开销，使全贝叶斯优化在实际昂贵黑盒优化场景中成为可行选择，兼顾了不确定性建模质量与决策效率。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems are ubiquitous across science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions. This objective latency has two consequences during optimization: (i) the objective evaluation dominates execution time, and (ii) sample-efficient algorithms are crucial to accelerate development and avoid wasting resources. Bayesian optimization (BO) methods are the \textit{de facto} choice of planners for suggesting the next point to try. Standard BO fits the surrogate model's hyperparameters with a point estimate. Alternatively, a fully Bayesian approach uses model averaging to account for uncertainty over the hyperparameters, leading to better uncertainty estimates---useful in the low-data regime that is pervasive in BO. However, it is often prohibitively expensive and thus rarely used. In this work, we propose ELF-BO, an algorithm that uses the objective evaluation latency to headstart the computation of the next suggestion, allowing for fully Bayesian optimization without incurring substantial decision-time costs. This is done by sampling from the hyperparameter posterior \emph{while} the objective is being evaluated, only requiring reweighting of the samples once the objective value is observed. Across synthetic functions and real-world applications, we show that ELF-BO matches the performance of fully Bayesian methods while only incurring decision latency on par with or better than standard BO. Thus, ELF-BO makes fully Bayesian optimization practical in real-world use cases.

</details>


### [194] [The Best Optimizer Depends on Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08975)
*Xingyu Dang,Kaiyue Wen,Sadhika Malladi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出自适应优化器通常仅在单一批量大小下基准测试，超参数缩放规则无法保证跨批量大小的性能一致性，且最优优化器随批量大小变化而改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器（如Muon）设计利用小批量梯度统计量，但评估往往局限于固定批量大小，超参数缩放规则声称能保持性能随批量大小和梯度噪声变化而不变，作者质疑这一假设。

Method: 通过实验验证：（1）测试Muon在不同训练设置下的原则性缩放规则；（2）在语言模型预训练中，经过大量超参数调优后，比较不同批量大小下的最优优化器表现。

Result: 发现没有针对Muon的原则性缩放规则能在各训练设置中一致工作；即使经过广泛超参数调优，语言模型预训练的最佳优化器仍随批量大小变化而改变。

Conclusion: 挑战了依赖超参数缩放规则进行优化器开发和评估的通用做法，表明优化器性能与批量大小强相关，需在多批量大小下综合评估。

Abstract: A plethora of new adaptive optimizers are designed to efficiently estimate and use minibatch gradient statistics to shape parameter updates, but they are typically benchmarked at a single batch size. Hyperparameter scaling rules promise to preserve performance as batch size and gradient noise change, suggesting that the best optimizer at one batch size should remain the best at another. We challenge this approach to developing and evaluating optimizers by showing: (1) no principled scaling rule for Muon works consistently across training settings, and (2) the best optimizer for language model pretraining changes with batch size even after extensive hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [195] [SNR-Gated LSTM-Conditioned Diffusion Model for MIMO Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08977)
*Jixing Zhou,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间序列条件扩散模型的MIMO信道估计框架，在角度域进行去噪，利用LSTM编码短观测序列捕捉时域动态，引入可学习的SNR门控后期融合捷径平衡观测保真度与生成先验，并采用SNR自适应截断和步数分配的DDIM确定性反向更新以降低推理延迟。在标准时变信道模型上实验表明，该方法在保持低延迟的同时持续超越现有基于扩散的信道估计基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MIMO系统，特别是在移动性场景下，需要准确且低延迟的信道估计。信道表现出结构化稀疏性和强时域相关性，现有逐快照估计方法无法充分利用时域动态，且现有基于扩散的方法在宽SNR范围内平衡观测保真度与生成先验、以及推理延迟方面仍有改进空间。

Method: 1. 角度域扩散去噪：从最小二乘（LS）观测出发，训练扩散去噪器。2. 时间序列条件编码：利用长短期记忆网络（LSTM）对短观测序列编码，作为扩散模型的条件信息，捕捉时域动态。3. 可学习SNR门控后期融合捷径：在解码最后阶段通过带有可训练中心和尺度的Sigmoid门将网络输入注入，自适应平衡观测保真度与生成先验。4. SNR自适应DDIM反向更新：采用确定性DDIM风格反向步骤，根据SNR自适应截断和分配步数，高SNR时大幅减少步数，低SNR时保持性能。

Result: 在时演化标准信道模型上仿真，该方法在宽SNR范围内持续超越现有基于扩散的信道估计基线，同时通过SNR自适应推理保持低延迟。

Conclusion: 提出的时间序列条件扩散框架有效利用信道时域相关性，结合可学习SNR门控融合与自适应DDIM推理，实现了高精度、低延迟的MIMO信道估计，为移动性场景下的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate and low latency channel estimation is critical for modern MIMO systems, particularly under mobility, where channels exhibit structured sparsity and strong temporal correlation. This paper proposes a time-series conditioned diffusion framework for channel estimation that performs denoising in the angular domain. Starting from least squares (LS) observations, we train a diffusion denoiser whose conditioning information is encoded by a long short-term memory (LSTM) network over a short observation sequence, enabling the model to exploit temporal dynamics beyond per-snapshot estimation. To robustly balance observation fidelity and learned generative priors across a wide signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range, we introduce a learnable SNR-gated late-fusion shortcut that injects the network input into the final decoding stage through a sigmoid gate with trainable center and scale. To reduce inference latency, we adopt deterministic denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) style reverse updates with SNR-adaptive truncation and step allocation, which significantly reduces the number of reverse diffusion steps at high SNR while maintaining strong performance in low SNR regimes. Simulations on time-evolving standardized channel models demonstrate that the proposed method achieves consistent performance gains over existing diffusion-based channel estimation baselines, while retaining low latency through SNR-adaptive inference.

</details>


### [196] [LASER: Latent Space Adjoint Matching for Support-Constrained Entropy-Regularized Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08989)
*Songyuan Zhang,Oswin So,Eric Yang Yu,Matthew Cleaveland,Peter Crowley-Dolen,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 LASER，一种基于潜在空间伴随匹配的离线强化学习算法，通过熵正则化解决潜在空间策略崩溃和利用评论家尖锐伪影的问题，在 40 个 OGBench 任务上以固定超参数达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习受限于执行分布外动作的风险。现有方法虽通过流匹配学习行为克隆策略并在其约束的潜在空间中进行 RL，但直接优化潜在策略易导致策略崩塌为脆弱模式或利用学习到的评论家的尖锐伪影。因此需要熵正则化来缓解这些问题。

Method: LASER 采用潜在空间伴随匹配实现熵正则化的潜在空间 RL，使用表达力强的流策略，同时避免了反向传播穿越时间（BPTT），从而高效地在约束的潜在空间中优化策略。

Result: 在 40 个具有挑战性的 OGBench 任务上（涵盖不同数据集质量），LASER 以固定的方法专用超参数达到最先进性能，优于包括针对任务和数据集专门调优的基线方法。

Conclusion: LASER 通过熵正则化的潜在空间伴随匹配有效解决了离线 RL 中的 OOD 动作问题和策略崩塌问题，展现出跨任务、跨数据集质量的鲁棒适用性，且无需针对性调参即可超越现有最优基线。

Abstract: While offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables policy optimization from static datasets without costly online interaction, it remains bottlenecked by the risk of executing out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. Recent approaches mitigate this by learning a behavior-cloning policy through flow matching and then performing RL within its constrained latent space. However, naively optimizing the latent policy can easily cause the policy to collapse into a brittle mode or exploit sharp artifacts of the learned critic. In this work, we find that entropy regularization is essential in latent-space RL for addressing these challenges. We introduce LASER, a novel offline RL algorithm that applies latent-space adjoint matching to achieve entropy-regularized latent-space RL with expressive flow policies while avoiding backpropagation through time. Through comprehensive experiments on 40 challenging OGBench tasks with varying dataset qualities, we show that LASER achieves state-of-the-art performance.
