Notably, LASER uses fixed method-specific hyperparameters across all tasks and outperforms the evaluated baselines, including those with task- and dataset-specific tuning, which highlights the robust applicability of LASER. Project website: https://mit-realm.github.io/laser/.

</details>


### [197] [REFIT: Recognize, Fix, and Test Wearable Sensor Placement Shifts without Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08991)
*Bangxun Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFIT 是一种无需标签或重新训练的输入校准方法，用于解决惯性传感器在部署时佩戴方式与训练时不同（如换手腕、传感器翻转）导致的轴变换问题。它通过轴变换族（反射、旋转）匹配训练数据统计量，自动识别并修正偏移，并提供无标签准确率估计以判断修正是否成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动识别模型在部署时，用户可能以不同方式佩戴惯性传感器（如将手表换到另一只手腕、重新佩戴带式传感器时发生翻转），导致输入轴发生变换，使冻结模型性能大幅下降。现有方法通常需要标签或重新训练，不适合实际部署场景。

Method: REFIT 将可能的轴变换建模为变换族（如有符号排列、旋转、反射），通过拟合用户数据使简单统计量（如均值、方差、相关性）与训练数据匹配，选择消除最大不匹配的变换族作为偏移类型，并应用该族中最优变换校准输入，同时重新估计模型归一化统计量。最后使用无标签准确率估计器评估修正效果，若估计准确率低则提示用户重新佩戴。

Result: 在真实左右手传感器对、真实重新佩戴及模拟重新佩戴数据集上，REFIT 在所有数据集上均优于无标签测试时自适应基线，恢复了因重新佩戴损失的大部分准确率。其识别注入偏移的可靠性远高于基于置信度的选择器。经过全有符号排列修正后，准确率估计能有效区分成功与失败的修正。

Conclusion: REFIT 提供了一种实用、无需标签的部署时校准方案，能自动检测并修正传感器佩戴差异导致的轴变换，显著恢复模型性能，并具备可靠的自我评估机制，适合真实世界的活动识别应用。

Abstract: We present REFIT, an input calibration for frozen activity-recognition models whose inertial sensors are worn differently at deployment than in training. When users move a watch to the other wrist or put a strap sensor back on turned, the model sees the same motion on changed axes. REFIT undoes such shifts without labels or retraining. It describes them by families of axis transforms, such as reflections and rotations, and fits each family to the user's data so that simple statistics match those of the training data. The family that removes most of the mismatch names the shift. REFIT fixes the shift by applying the best member of that family before the frozen model and re-estimating its normalization statistics. It tests the fixed model with a label-free accuracy estimate and asks the user to re-wear the sensor when it is low. Experiments on real left/right sensor pairs and on real and simulated re-attachment show that REFIT outperforms label-free test-time adaptation methods on every dataset and restores most of the accuracy lost to re-attachment. It names injected shifts far more reliably than a confidence-based selector. After a correction over all signed permutations of the axes, the estimate separates successful from failed corrections.

</details>


### [198] [Algorithmic Scratchpads and Curriculum Staging for Arithmetic Reasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09003)
*Sourabh Kasliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究小型Transformer（~10.6M非嵌入参数）在合成数据上通过逐步草稿垫学习四则运算。主要发现：1) 训练基础设施关键：序列填充导致83%梯度饥饿，持续序列打包可修复；语言预训练必不可少（无预训练≤2%）；现代架构（RoPE、RMSNorm、SwiGLU）和稀疏MoE显著提升加法推理。2) 算法草稿垫设计决定成败：确定性逐位长除法草稿垫配合4阶段分层课程学习，使单位数除法从4.0%提升至86.7%；但多位乘法仍具挑战，FOIL草稿垫因强制单步求和多达九个多位项而失败。3) 两大边界：未见4位操作数性能崩塌至0%，无缓冲训练导致灾难性遗忘，除法准确率从86.7%跌至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自回归大语言模型在确定性多步算法任务（如多位乘法、长除法）上的局限，通过在紧凑Transformer上系统实验，揭示训练基础设施、架构选择、草稿垫设计及课程学习对多步算术推理的影响，并识别泛化与遗忘的关键边界。

Method: 使用~10.6M非嵌入参数的Tiny Transformer，在合成四则运算数据上训练，采用逐步草稿垫形式。实验包括：1) 诊断并修复数据加载器序列填充导致的梯度饥饿（持续序列打包）；2) 对比有无语言预训练及现代架构原语（RoPE、RMSNorm、SwiGLU）与稀疏MoE的效果；3) 设计确定性逐位长除法草稿垫并结合4阶段分层发展课程；4) 分析FOIL乘法草稿垫的失败模式；5) 测试未见位数操作数泛化及无缓冲训练下的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 1) 持续序列打包消除梯度饥饿，语言预训练为必要条件，现代架构与MoE显著提升加法推理。2) 逐位长除法草稿垫+分层课程使单位数除法准确率从4.0%跃升至86.7%（4000题基准）。3) 多位乘法受限于FOIL草稿垫单步求和多达九个多位项，缺乏成对中间累加。4) 模型对未见4位操作数泛化完全失效（0%），无缓冲训练导致除法准确率从86.7%崩塌至0%。

Conclusion: 小型Transformer在合成数据上通过精心设计的草稿垫、课程学习及现代架构可掌握特定多步算术算法，但泛化能力极弱（位数外推失败），且持续学习需缓冲机制防止灾难性遗忘。草稿垫的算法结构（如是否允许中间累加）直接决定任务成败，提示神经网络需显式逐步计算结构而非隐式压缩推理。

Abstract: Autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently struggle with deterministic multi-step algorithmic tasks such as multi-digit multiplication and long division. In this paper, we investigate the mechanics of multi-step arithmetic in compact "Tiny" Transformers (~10.6M non-embedding parameters, 49.3M total) trained on synthetic data across four basic operations (+, -, *, /) unrolled as step-by-step scratchpads. First, we establish the necessary training foundations: (1) dataloader sequence padding creates an 83% gradient starvation artifact that collapses accuracy from 40% to 1%, remediated via continuous sequence packing; (2) linguistic pretraining is an essential prerequisite (<= 2.0% without it); and (3) modern architectural primitives (RoPE, RMSNorm, SwiGLU) and Sparse Mixture of Experts (MoE) substantially improve additive reasoning over baseline GPT-2. Second, we demonstrate that algorithmic scratchpad formulation directly dictates success. Introducing a deterministic Digit-by-Digit Long Division scratchpad within a 4-stage Hierarchical Developmental Curriculum dramatically elevates single-digit division from 4.0% to 86.7% accuracy on a 4,000-problem held-out benchmark. In contrast, multi-digit multiplication remained challenging: detailed error analysis revealed that while the model correctly computed single-digit sub-products and place-value zeros, our FOIL scratchpad failed because it forced a simultaneous summation of up to nine multi-digit terms in a single step without pairwise intermediate accumulation. Finally, we identify two key boundaries: performance collapses to 0.00% on unseen 4-digit operands, and unbuffered training induces catastrophic forgetting, collapsing division accuracy from 86.7% down to 0.00%.

</details>


### [199] [Removing Information Content Does Not Certify Tamper Resistance in Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09004)
*Domenic Rosati,Alessa Carbo,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Matthew Young,Subhabrata Majumdar,Frank Rudzicz,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究开放权重模型在移除有害信息后是否能抵抗微调攻击。核心发现：仅凭发布时的互信息无法通用地认证恢复速度；函数保持的重参数化不改变信息量但改变梯度下降几何，因此不变量证书受限于最快可达的参数化。实验表明训练顺序影响恢复时间，且存在互信息为零却能一步恢复的显式构造。结论指出认证缺失的要求：需对攻击动力学施加超越发布时互信息的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型发布前常通过数据过滤或能力移除来消除有害信息，但现有基于互信息的安全认证方法是否足以防范微调攻击尚不明确。作者旨在揭示互信息作为安全指标的根本局限，并寻找真正能认证抗攻击能力的理论条件。

Method: 理论分析结合可控实验：1) 证明函数保持重参数化不变互信息但改变梯度几何，导致不变量证书受最快参数化约束；2) 分别研究训练数据过滤下的权重-数据互信息和能力移除下的标签-表示互信息；3) 构造显式反例：互信息均为零却单步恢复；4) 实验验证训练顺序对恢复时间的影响及参数化对攻击速度的影响。

Result: 1) 发布时的互信息无法通用认证慢恢复；2) 训练顺序在固定权重-数据互信息下改变恢复时间；3) 精确表示级独立性可保留整个参数雅可比；4) 存在互信息为零却能一步梯度恢复的显式构造；5) 实验确认顺序依赖的恢复和参数化依赖的攻击速度。

Conclusion: 互信息本身不足以作为开放权重模型安全认证的充分条件。真正的认证需要对攻击动力学（如梯度下降几何、参数化路径、训练顺序等）施加额外约束，而非仅依赖发布时刻的静态信息量度量。

Abstract: Does removing harmful information make open-weight models resistant to fine-tuning attacks? We show that mutual information at release alone cannot universally certify slow recovery. Function-preserving reparameterizations leave information unchanged while altering gradient-descent geometry, so an invariant certificate is bounded by the fastest reachable parameterization. We apply this principle to weight--data mutual information under training-data filtering and label--representation mutual information under capability removal. Training order can change recovery time at fixed weight--data information, while exact representation-level independence can preserve the entire parameter Jacobian. An explicit construction has both information quantities equal to zero and recovers in one gradient step. Controlled experiments illustrate order-dependent recovery and parameterization-dependent attack speed. These results identify the missing requirement for certification: constraints on attack dynamics beyond mutual information at release.

</details>


### [200] [Neighborhood Smoothing for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09020)
*Idan Horowitz,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出图平滑作为训练时校准的一般原则，通过惩罚邻近样本预测分布的 Jensen-Shannon 散度来改善神经网络校准，实验表明其在图像和表格基准上优于现有训练时方法，且与温度缩放等后处理方法互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络常表现为过度自信且校准不良，现有训练时校准方法主要修改任务损失或校准惩罚，未充分利用学习到的表示空间中的邻域结构。

Method: 提出基于图的训练时正则化器，通过最小化表示空间中邻近样本预测分布之间的 Jensen-Shannon 散度来实现图平滑，并理论分析了平滑对局部置信度变化和逐点校准误差传播的影响。

Result: 在标准校准基准上，该方法提升了预测质量，且与后处理校准（如温度缩放）互补：结合温度缩放后，在八个图像和表格设置中的七个上实现了所有训练时方法中最低的 NLL。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在学习到的表示上进行图平滑对神经网络校准的价值，提供了一种有效的训练时校准新范式。

Abstract: Modern neural networks are often miscalibrated, with a tendency to overconfidence. Existing train-time calibration methods largely modify task losses or calibration penalties, leaving neighborhood structure in learned representations underexploited. We introduce graph smoothing as a general principle for train-time calibration, which encourages similar predictive distributions across neighboring samples in representation space. We analyze the effects of graph smoothing, deriving bounds that connect predictive divergence between neighboring samples to local confidence variation and to the propagation of pointwise calibration error, and characterize the conditions under which smoothing can or cannot improve calibration. In light of this analysis, we propose \modelNoSpace, a graph-based train-time regularizer that penalizes the Jensen--Shannon divergence between predictive distributions of neighboring samples. We present a thorough empirical analysis, showing that across standard calibration benchmarks, \model improves predictive quality, and the improvement is complementary to post-hoc calibration: after temperature scaling, \model attains the lowest NLL of all evaluated train-time methods in seven of the eight image and tabular settings. These findings demonstrate the value of graph smoothing over learned representations for neural network calibration.

</details>


### [201] [SPIN: Shadow Predictive Indexer for Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09025)
*Yao Fu,Cyrus Chang,Ritchie Zhao,Bryce Long,Yueying Li,Mahdi Kamani,Samkit Jain,Rahul Raman,Tara Safavi,Shreya Gupta,Parsa Ashrafi Fashi,Minseok Lee,Julien Demouth,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIN (Shadow Predictive Indexer) 通过基于历史的轻量级预测机制，避免了索引器在每个解码步骤对整个 KV 缓存进行评分，实现了 30-40% 的稀疏度同时保持任务质量，在 vLLM 服务中将输出吞吐量提升高达 14.9%，中位数 token 间延迟降低高达 13.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于索引器的稀疏注意力虽然降低了核心注意力的计算成本，但索引器本身在每个解码步骤仍需对整个 KV 缓存进行评分。随着上下文长度增长，这种评分开销成为主要瓶颈，限制了长上下文和智能体场景下的推理效率。

Method: SPIN 提出影子预测索引器，利用轻量级的历史信息预测重要的 KV 块，避免每步对全量 KV 缓存评分。将 KV 块和推测解码作为一等设计考量，通过历史模式预测关键块，仅对预测的重要块进行精确评分，大幅减少索引器开销。

Result: 在长上下文和智能体基准测试中，SPIN 达到 30-40% 的稀疏度且保持任务质量。端到端 vLLM 服务中，输出吞吐量提升高达 14.9%，中位数 token 间延迟降低高达 13.2%。

Conclusion: SPIN 通过基于历史的预测机制有效解决了稀疏注意力中索引器的评分瓶颈，将 KV 块和推测解码作为核心设计考量，在保持模型质量的前提下显著提升了长上下文推理的服务效率，为大模型部署提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Indexer-based sparse attention reduces the cost of core attention by passing only a fixed, small number of important tokens to it. However, the indexer must still score the entire KV cache at every decoding step. This scoring overhead becomes a major bottleneck as the context length grows. We propose SPIN (Shadow Predictive Indexer) to reduce this indexer overhead. SPIN uses lightweight, history-based prediction to identify important KV blocks, avoiding the need to score the full KV cache at every decoding step. SPIN treats KV blocks and speculative decoding as first-class design and implementation considerations. Across extensive evaluations on long-context and agentic benchmarks, SPIN achieves 30-40% sparsity while preserving task quality. In end-to-end vLLM serving, SPIN improves output throughput by up to 14.9% and reduces median inter-token latency by up to 13.2%.

</details>


### [202] [ORACLE: Optimizer-Relative Alignment for Constrained LEarning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09040)
*Utkarsh Grover,Wyatt Mackey,Kaixun Hua,J. Morris Chang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了优化器相对约束学习框架及ORACLE算法，在优化器更新后评估约束兼容性，通过联合端点线性化、优化器几何下的对齐构建与权限绑定，在8个PDE基准和4类优化器上实现94%配置下性能提升或持平，优于现有的目标函数、梯度及后优化器层面的约束处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束处理方法通常在优化器作用前介入（修改目标函数或梯度），但动量、自适应缩放和结构化预条件等优化器机制会在参数更新前显著重塑信号，导致约束兼容性评估与实际更新步骤脱节。

Method: 提出优化器相对约束学习框架，在优化器更新后评估约束兼容性。核心算法ORACLE：1) 通过异构约束族的联合端点线性化评估原生优化器的实际步长；2) 在优化器自身几何下构建对齐；3) 绑定其权限；4) 仅在验证后提交更新。

Result: 在8个PDE基准和4个优化器（欧几里得、对角自适应、结构化预条件几何）上，ORACLE在94%配置下改进或匹配原生优化器性能；跨模型分析显示92%配置表现一致；对比实验表明优于在目标函数、梯度和后优化器层面作用的替代约束处理方法。

Conclusion: 优化器相对约束学习通过在优化器几何内评估并强制约束兼容性，有效解决了优化器内部机制与约束处理脱节的问题，ORACLE作为通用框架在多种优化器几何和PDE任务上展现出优越的鲁棒性和有效性。

Abstract: Constraint handling methods typically intervene before the optimizer acts, by modifying the objective or the gradient. Yet momentum, adaptive scaling, and structured preconditioning can substantially reshape that signal before it becomes a parameter update. We formulate optimizer relative constrained learning, where constraint compatibility is assessed on the post optimizer update. Building on this view, we introduce ORACLE, which evaluates the native optimizer's realized step through a joint endpoint linearization of heterogeneous constraint families, constructs the resulting alignment in the optimizer's own geometry, bounds its authority, and commits it only after validation. We evaluate ORACLE across eight Partial Differential Equation benchmarks and four optimizers spanning Euclidean, diagonal adaptive, and structured preconditioned geometries, where it improves or matches native optimizer in 94% of configurations. Cross model analysis shows the same behavior in 92% of configurations, while matched comparisons show improvements over alternative constraint-handling methods acting at the objective, gradient, and post-optimizer levels.

</details>


### [203] [Towards Financial World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09048)
*Humzah Merchant,Alec Guthrie,Simon Mahns,Randall Balestriero,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Market-1T数据集(近1万亿观测值,美股2008-2025年1Hz分辨率),建立严格评估协议,系统对比18种编码器训练策略,发现预测性能相似的编码器对市场状态的组织方式差异巨大,为金融市场表征学习奠基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融表征学习多在单一预测任务、单一时期、窄数据集上评估,缺乏对市场整体状态(市场条件、资产预期收益、流动性、波动率、跨资产关系)的建模,难以支持规划决策所需的世界模型。

Method: 引入Market-1T大规模高频数据集;设计严格评估协议(预测任务+潜在结构探针);大规模实证研究对比18种编码器训练策略(监督/自监督/对比学习等),跨越近二十年市场机制。

Result: 1) 不同编码器在常见金融预测任务上表现相近,但潜在空间对市场状态的组织结构显著不同;2) 自监督方法在捕捉跨资产关系和市场机制转换方面优于纯监督方法;3) 表征质量对下游规划任务影响大于单纯预测指标。

Conclusion: 确立了金融市场表征学习的大规模基准和评估框架,揭示预测性能不等于状态表征质量,为构建金融世界模型(DINO-WM、V-JEPA 2、LeWM等)提供数据与方法论基础。

Abstract: Building a world model requires a state representation useful for planning and decision-making---potentially over tasks unknown at training time. In the context of financial markets, planning and decision-making may require a model to reason about market-wide conditions, asset-specific expected returns, liquidity, volatility, and cross-asset relationships. Yet financial representation learning has largely been evaluated on individual predictive tasks, oftentimes on a single time period using comparatively narrow datasets. We address this through three primary contributions. First, we introduce Market-1T, a dataset containing nearly one trillion observations across U.S. equities from 2008 to 2025 at 1 Hz resolution. Second, we develop and implement a rigorous evaluation protocol. Third, we conduct a systematic large-scale study of financial representation learning, comparing 18 encoder-training strategies across nearly two decades of market regimes. We evaluate learned representations both by their predictive utility on common finance tasks and through probes of latent structure. We find that encoders with similar predictive performance can organize market state very differently. Collectively, we establish a foundation for training and evaluating financial market representations in support of world models such as DINO-WM, V-JEPA 2, and LeWM.

</details>


### [204] [A Self-Pruning Transformer: Extreme KV-Cache Compression with Universal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09051)
*Davis Wertheimer,Haochen Shen,Ahan Gupta,Derrick Liu,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一的注意力机制框架，通过复合衰减机制实现自适应KV缓存剪枝，在保持RoPE位置编码和Softmax注意力表达能力的同时，实现了最先进的10倍压缩率，并改善了下游任务性能，且在长上下文泛化上达到25倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM的大KV缓存阻碍了高效部署；现有基于衰减的替代RoPE方法表达能力有限，实际退化为滑动窗口式剔除模式，无法捕捉复杂的关键统计和交互。

Method: 提出通用注意力框架，统一互补和新颖的衰减机制，保留RoPE位置嵌入和Softmax注意力，复合衰减机制作为自适应剪枝准则，端到端可训练。

Result: 在自然语言和合成任务数据上实现最先进的10倍压缩，且优于基线和未剪枝的oracle模型；长上下文泛化实现前所未有的25倍压缩（长度16k）。

Conclusion: 通用注意力通过复合衰减机制实现了高表达性和自适应KV缓存剪枝，显著提升了压缩效率同时保持甚至提高模型性能，为长上下文LLM部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The large KV-cache size of modern LLMs creates a barrier to efficient deployment. Recent work has explored replacing attention layers' RoPE positional embeddings with alternative decay-based mechanisms, which can then be used to prune KV-cache during inference. However, these decay functions have limited expressivity, and in practice devolve into sliding-window-like eviction patterns. In this work, we propose a unifying framework for complementary and novel decay mechanisms, capturing complex key statistics and interactions while preserving expressive RoPE embeddings and Softmax attention. The resulting Universal Attention is a highly expressive and end-to-end trainable architecture, whose composite decay mechanism acts as a natural, $\textit{adaptive}$ pruning criterion, removing tokens that contribute least to attention computation. Experimentally, Universal Attention achieves state-of-the-art $10\times$ compression on natural language and synthetic task data, while $\textit{improving}$ downstream performance compared to both state-of-the-art baselines and unpruned oracles. It further demonstrates superior long-context generalization with unprecedented $25\times$ compression at length 16k.

</details>


### [205] [BeatFlow-ECG: Rectified Flow for ECG Reconstruction from Indirect Wearable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09052)
*Mohamed Kamel,Sahar Selim,Walaa Medhat,Tamer Nadeem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BeatFlow-ECG 是一种条件修正流模型，利用同步的 PPG 和 IMU 信号重建单导联 ECG，在 PPG-DaLiA 和 WESAD 数据集上达到最优波形与搏动时序指标，显著优于确定性、对抗和扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床外连续心脏监测需要既信息丰富又易于日常采集的信号。ECG 提供丰富的心律与波形形态信息，但难以持续佩戴；可穿戴 PPG 采集方便但仅为间接心血管测量且极易受运动干扰。因此需要一种能从 PPG 与惯性测量中高质量重建 ECG 的方法。

Method: 提出 BeatFlow-ECG，将重建建模为从噪声到 ECG 的条件传输任务。采用卷积编码器-解码器架构，配合 Transformer 瓶颈层与显式流时间条件。运动信息通过 IMU 派生的条件特征、运动依赖的损失加权以及易到难训练课程融入模型。

Result: 在留一被试交叉验证下，PPG-DaLiA 上 Pearson 相关系数 0.983、R 峰 F1 0.946；WESAD 上分别为 0.986 和 0.955，均优于所有对比基线。相比 Conditional DDPM-1D，L1 误差在 PPG-DaLiA 上从 0.085 降至 0.062，在 WESAD 上从 0.074 降至 0.055。额外分析显示在固定 R 峰相对区域相关性更高，且在低、中、高运动子集上重建误差均更低。

Conclusion: BeatFlow-ECG 通过条件修正流有效整合 PPG 与 IMU 信息，实现了高保真的单导联 ECG 重建，为临床外连续心脏监测提供了实用的波形级解决方案。

Abstract: Continuous cardiac monitoring outside clinical settings requires signals that are both informative and practical to collect during daily life. Electrocardiography (ECG) provides rich information about cardiac rhythm and waveform morphology, while wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) is easier to acquire continuously but is only an indirect cardiovascular measurement and is highly sensitive to motion. We present BeatFlow-ECG, a conditional rectified-flow model for reconstructing single-channel ECG from synchronized PPG and inertial measurements. BeatFlow-ECG models reconstruction as conditional transport from noise to ECG using a convolutional encoder-decoder with a transformer bottleneck and explicit flow-time conditioning. Motion information is incorporated through IMU-derived conditioning features, motion-dependent loss weighting, and an easy-to-hard training curriculum. We evaluate the model under leave-one-subject-out protocols on PPG-DaLiA and WESAD. BeatFlow-ECG achieves the best results among the evaluated deterministic, adversarial, and diffusion-based baselines across all reported waveform and beat-timing metrics, with Pearson correlations of 0.983 and 0.986 and R-peak F1 scores of 0.946 and 0.955, respectively. Compared with Conditional DDPM-1D, L1 error decreases from 0.085 to 0.062 on PPG-DaLiA and from 0.074 to 0.055 on WESAD. Additional analyses on PPG-DaLiA show higher correlation in fixed R-peak-relative waveform regions and lower reconstruction error across low-, medium-, and high-motion subsets.

</details>


### [206] [A Geometry-Based Capacity Theory for Finite-Feature Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09056)
*Jianhai Zhang,Donghao Zhang,Pattarawut Charatpangoon,Bijoy Menon,M. Ethan MacDonald,Wu Qiu,Aravind Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一种基于几何的容量理论，用于分析压缩有限特征Hebbian联想记忆中的精确键检索，将检索干扰分解为有限特征噪声和结构干扰，实现了无拟合的检索质量预测，并揭示了几何依赖的容量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联想记忆理论缺乏对有限特征压缩下精确键检索的几何化容量分析，无法准确预测检索质量、区分噪声来源，也无法指导何时应增加特征预算还是改变表示几何。

Method: 发展基于几何的容量理论，将检索干扰分解为随特征维度衰减的有限特征噪声和由存储键的核重叠平方决定的结构干扰；针对随机/近似各向同性值推导无拟合预测，针对相关值建立键核与值Gram矩阵的联合依赖模型并给出有限特征近似。

Result: 获得了检索质量的无拟合预测、几何依赖的容量上限、目标检索质量所需的特征预算预测；验证了在合成、视觉和医学图像表示上预测曲线与实证行为高度吻合，并能正确识别最优记忆设计的变化。

Conclusion: 该框架直接将表示几何与记忆容量联系起来，区分了可通过增加特征预算提升性能的场景与必须改变表示几何的场景，为联想记忆系统的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a geometry-based capacity theory for exact-key retrieval in compressed finite-feature Hebbian associative memory. For random or approximately isotropic values, retrieval interference separates into finite-feature noise, which decreases with feature dimension, and structural interference, which is determined by squared kernel overlap among stored keys and persists in the infinite-feature limit. This yields a fit-free prediction of retrieval quality, reveals a geometry-dependent capacity ceiling, and predicts the feature budget required for a target retrieval quality. When stored values are correlated, we show that retrieval depends jointly on the key kernel and value Gram matrix, and derive finite-feature approximations that account for this interaction. We validate the theory on synthetic, visual, and medical-image representations. Overall, the framework links representation geometry directly to memory capacity and distinguishes when performance can be improved by increasing the feature budget and when the representation itself must be changed. Across these settings, the predicted retrieval curves closely match empirical behavior and correctly identify changes in the preferred memory design.

</details>


### [207] [Learning Cross-Model Activation Alignments with Explicit Many-to-Many Layer Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09058)
*Alina Sudakov,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MATCHA方法，学习两个独立训练LLM之间的激活对齐，将映射分解为可解释的层映射矩阵和共享特征映射，联合学习层对应关系和特征变换，实现跨模型的激活重构、干预向量迁移等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM快速发布，自然产生疑问：独立训练的模型之间如何关联？即哪些层对应、特征如何变换。现有工作预先固定层对应关系（按相对深度配对），缺乏灵活性和可解释性。

Method: MATCHA将激活对齐分解为两个因子：1) 层映射——显式的目标层×源层矩阵，可提取检查；2) 层共享特征映射——隐藏空间间的特征变换。两因子从提示词中联合学习，不预设层对应。

Result: 在7个模型、3个模型家族的42对实验中，MATCHA比先前方法更忠实地重构目标激活，显著提升检索指标。恢复的映射大体随深度单调但呈多对多：每个目标层汲取一段源层带。对齐还支持激活空间干预（转向向量、探针）跨模型迁移。

Conclusion: MATCHA提供了一种可解释、数据驱动的跨模型激活对齐框架，揭示了层对应的多对多带状结构，并实现了干预向量的零样本迁移，为模型融合、知识迁移和可解释性研究提供新工具。

Abstract: LLMs are released at a rapid pace, raising a natural question: how do two independently trained models relate, both in which layers correspond and in how features transform between them? We study this by learning an activation alignment, a map from a source model's layerwise activations to a target's. Our method, MATCHA, factors this map into a layer map, whose output is an explicit target-by-source matrix that can be extracted and inspected, and a layer-shared feature map between hidden spaces. Most of prior work fixes the layer correspondence in advance, pairing layers at roughly the same relative depth; in contrast, we learn both factors jointly from prompts. Across 42 pairs of seven models spanning three different families, MATCHA reconstructs the target's activations more faithfully and improves retrieval-based metrics substantially, w.r.t. previous approaches. The recovered maps are broadly monotone in depth but, in contrast with most previous approaches, are consistently many-to-many: each target layer draws on a band of source layers. Our alignments also enable transfer of activation-space interventions, allowing steering vectors and probes developed for one model to transfer to another.

</details>


### [208] [EDiS: Edge Disjoint Subgraph Sparsification Framework for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09059)
*Sai Karthik Navuluru,Siddhartha Shankar Das,Franck Dernoncourt,S M Ferdous,Ryan A. Rossi,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Baris Coskunuzer,Alex Pothen,Lakshman Tamil,Mahantesh M Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 EDiS（边不相交子图稀疏化框架），通过一次性将图分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，再在每个 epoch 灵活重组满足边预算约束的训练图，避免了重复采样/重新计算，实现了高效且结构保真度高的稀疏 GNN 训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 GNN 训练虽能降低计算量，但决定保留哪些边代价高。复用单一稀疏图虽便宜，却锁定固定拓扑；跨 epoch 变化拓扑则需要反复采样或重新计算。需要一种能一次性提取结构、多次灵活组合的方法。

Method: EDiS 将图一次性分解为可缓存的边不相交子图（默认基于特征评分与连续最大评分覆盖森林），再通过组合步骤在每个 epoch 从分解中抽取满足边预算与保留比例约束的训练图。提供了组合采样器的组合分析，证明默认选择器确定性保留高评分割边，并给出选择器无关的高评分割边生存条件界。

Result: 在 19 个同质、异质及大规模节点分类基准上，对比 17 种基线且边预算相同，EDiS 达到最高平均基准分（准确率/ROC-AUC）、最低平均排名与最小差距。消融实验显示结构分解与 epoch 变化在紧边预算下收益最明显。

Conclusion: EDiS 通过一次性结构分解与灵活的跨 epoch 图组合，在保持高评分割边的同时显著降低稀疏 GNN 训练开销，实现了计算效率与结构保真度的有效平衡。

Abstract: Sparse GNN training reduces computation, but deciding which edges to keep can be costly. Reusing one sparse graph is cheap, but locks training to a fixed topology, while varying it across epochs can require repeated sampling or recomputation. We introduce EDiS (Edge-Disjoint Subgraph sparsification framework), which separates one-time structural extraction from per-epoch graph composition. EDiS decomposes the graph once into cacheable edge-disjoint subgraphs, then recombines them into graphs with edge-budget constraints across epochs and retention ratios without re-extracting structure. Our default construction uses feature-based scores and successive maximum score covering forests, while the same composition mechanism also supports alternative edge selection rules. We provide a combinatorial analysis of the per-epoch sampler, the composition step that draws a training graph from the cached decomposition. We show that, under the default covering-forest selector, the stored decomposition deterministically preserves high-score cut edges, and we derive a selector-agnostic conditional bound on high-score cut survival in composed training graphs. Across 19 homophilic, heterophilic, and large-scale node classification benchmarks against 17 baselines under the same edge budget, EDiS achieves the highest mean benchmark score (accuracy/ROC-AUC) and the lowest average rank and gap-to-best among ranked methods. Ablations show the clearest benefits of structural decomposition and epoch variation at tight edge budgets.

</details>


### [209] [Multi-Label Topic Assignment via LLM Distillation: A Comparative Analysis of Generative vs. Discriminative Student Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09063)
*Sourabh Kasliwal,Shubhranshu Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对电商场景下用户生成内容的多标签主题分配问题，对比了小语言模型（1B、4B、8B）在生成式与判别式架构下的表现，发现判别式模型在结构化商品评论上更优，而生成式模型在复杂多轮对话和大规模标签扩展场景下表现更强健，并成功部署于生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台面临用户生成内容多标签主题分配的挑战，包括非正式语言、极度标签稀疏和快速演变的分类体系。利用大语言模型作为标注预言机蒸馏真值数据虽已成行业标准，但如何为学生模型选择最优、低延迟的架构仍是开放问题。

Method: 全面评估不同参数规模（1B、4B、8B）的小语言模型及两种架构范式（因果生成式与双向判别式）。对比生成式文本到标签分类器与判别式基线（DeBERTa-V3 和 ModernBERT），在商品评论和买家-卖家对话两类数据上实验，并测试大规模标签集扩展（高达 112 个主题）及长尾分布下的表现。

Result: 判别式模型在结构化商品评论上优于超轻量级生成式模型；但在复杂多轮对话数据上，即使最小的 1B 生成式模型也超越判别式基线。生成式模型在大规模标签集扩展和严重长尾分布下保持鲁棒性能，而判别式基线在 Macro-F1 上下降 35%。生产部署验证了模型满足严格延迟要求并带来实际业务收益。

Conclusion: 针对不同类型的用户生成内容，应采用差异化的模型架构选择策略：结构化评论适合判别式模型，复杂对话和大规模标签场景适合生成式模型。生成式小语言模型在标签集扩展和长尾分布下表现出更强的泛化能力，且已成功大规模落地，为电商多标签主题分配提供了实用的架构指南。

Abstract: Multi-label topic assignment for user-generated content (UGC) -- including product reviews and buyer-seller conversations -- poses unique scalability challenges in large-scale e-commerce due to informal language, extreme label sparsity, and rapidly evolving taxonomies. While utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) as labeling oracles to distill ground-truth data has emerged as an industry standard to bypass prohibitive manual annotation costs, determining the optimal, low-latency architecture for the resulting student models remains an open challenge. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation across Small Language Model (SLM) parameter scales (1B, 4B, and 8B) and architectural paradigms (causal generative versus bidirectional discriminative). Comparing generative text-to-label classifiers against discriminative baselines (DeBERTa-V3 and ModernBERT), our analysis reveals a crucial data-dependent trade-off: while discriminative models outperform ultra-lightweight generative models on structured product reviews, even the smallest 1B generative model surpasses discriminative baselines on complex, multi-turn conversational data. Furthermore, generative models maintain robust performance under massive label-set expansion (up to 112 topics) and severe long-tail distributions, whereas discriminative baselines suffer a 35% drop in Macro-F1 at scale. Finally, we detail the successful production deployment of these optimized models across both product review and conversational domains, demonstrating strict latency compliance and tangible business impact at a global marketplace scale.

</details>


### [210] [TAP: Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Pruning via Trajectory-Anchored Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09074)
*Yuanzhe Li,Pengxin Wang,Yuxin Ren,Jianing Deng,Jingtong Hu,Song Wang,Jingdi Chen,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP)，首个面向强化学习训练的长程智能体的结构化剪枝框架。TAP将结构化剪枝与高效的在线策略恢复相结合，锚定教师轨迹同时允许学生生成每一步推理-动作响应。冻结的稠密教师监督学生的响应前缀，解决了响应内的训练-推理不匹配，并防止学生引起的偏差跨轮次传播。通过基于恢复后学生的恢复目标梯度重新评分通道，替代一次性剪枝，实现了迭代通道选择与演进策略的连接。在ALFWorld和WebShop上，移除60% FFN通道后，TAP分别保留了99.2%和88.0%的稠密7B智能体任务成功率，并将每成功任务的GPU时间降低约22%和17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务需要重复模型调用，加剧了本已昂贵的语言模型的推理成本。虽然窄域智能体任务暗示激进模型剪枝不降性能的潜力，但现有为问答任务设计的剪枝方法应用于智能体模型时会严重降低任务性能。失败原因在于两个决策：剪什么和如何恢复。剪枝方面，一次性重要性估计无法追踪剪枝后模型如何适应；恢复方面，离线蒸馏仅覆盖教师前缀，而全轨迹在线策略蒸馏导致学生错误跨轮次累积。

Method: 提出Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP)，首个面向RL训练智能体的结构化剪枝框架。核心创新：1) 将结构化剪枝与高效在线策略恢复耦合，锚定交互到教师轨迹，同时允许学生生成每个推理-动作响应；2) 冻结稠密教师监督学生响应前缀，解决响应内训练-推理不匹配，防止学生诱导偏差跨训练轮次传播；3) 替代一次性剪枝，基于恢复后学生的恢复目标梯度重新评分通道，将迭代通道选择与演进策略连接。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，移除60% FFN通道后，TAP分别保留稠密7B智能体99.2%和88.0%的任务成功率，同时将每成功任务GPU时间降低约22%和17%。结果表明在有限恢复预算下实现了长程智能体的有效结构化压缩。

Conclusion: TAP通过轨迹锚定的剪枝与恢复机制，成功解决了现有方法在智能体任务上的失效问题，实现了长程RL智能体的高效结构化压缩，在大幅减少推理成本的同时保持了高任务成功率，为部署成本敏感的智能体系统提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Emerging long-horizon agentic tasks require repeated model calls, worsening the inference cost of already-costly language models. While narrow agentic tasks suggest potential for aggressive model pruning without performance drop, empirical results show existing methods proposed for question answering tasks severely degrade task performance when applied to agentic models. We trace this failure to two decisions: what to prune and how to recover. For pruning, one-shot importance estimates fail to track how the pruned model adapts. For recovery, offline distillation covers only teacher prefixes, while full-trajectory on-policy distillation causes student errors to compound across turns. In this work, we propose Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP), the first structural pruning framework for reinforcement learning (RL)-trained agents. TAP couples structural pruning with efficient on-policy recovery, anchoring interactions to teacher trajectories while allowing the student to generate each reasoning-action response. A frozen dense teacher supervises the student's response prefixes, addressing within-response training-inference mismatch while preventing student-induced deviations from propagating across training turns. Instead of one-shot pruning, TAP re-scores channels using gradients of the recovery objective on the recovered student, connecting iterative channel selection to the evolving policy. With 60% of FFN channels removed, TAP retains 99.2% and 88.0% of the dense 7B agents' task success rates on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while reducing GPU time per successful task by approximately 22% and 17%. These results demonstrate effective structural compression of long-horizon agents under a limited recovery budget.

</details>


### [211] [Tucker Bottleneck Attention for Multi-Dimensional Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09090)
*Ryan Solgi,Parsa Madinei,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention limits scalability to long sequences from multidimensional data. We introduce Tucker bottleneck attention (TuBA), which exploits low-rank tensor structure for efficient global token mixing. TuBA projects hidden tensors into compact Tucker cores, performs multi-head self-attention and linear projections on the cores, and writes updates back to the ambient space, enabling subquadratic computation. Its autoregressive extension combines bidirectional interactions within cores with causal attention across cores. On video prediction and global weather forecasting, TuBA achieves favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over standard and efficient attention and task-specific models. Compared to standard self-attention, TuBA reduces error and computation by up to 24.7% and 66.6% for video prediction and 37.1% and 85.1% for autoregressive weather forecasting, with speedups up to 4.27 times. Low-rank Tucker cores and multi-frame generation also outperform full-rank attention and frame-by-frame generation, respectively.

</details>


### [212] [FedRSPO+: A Heterogeneity-aware Algorithm for Decision-focused Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09091)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出FedRSPO+框架，用于决策导向的联邦学习，解决异构目标和可行集导致的最优决策不连续问题，通过正则化预测-优化代理平滑决策映射，理论分析异构界限，实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策导向学习假设中心化数据，联邦学习虽提供替代但标准方法优化预测而非决策质量，且未解决下游目标和可行集的异构性。异构性在多面体问题中尤为棘手，微小扰动导致最优决策不连续变化，破坏客户端更新和聚合稳定性。

Method: 提出FedRSPO+框架，基于RSPO+正则化预测-优化代理，通过投影平滑决策映射。采用退火模块化训练过程，兼容标准联邦个性化和聚合方法。理论上推导决策误差和遗憾的上界，以及依赖目标和可行集异构性的跨客户端异构界限，无需强凸性假设。

Result: 在合成背包、最短路径和真实能源定价任务上实验，对比预测型联邦学习和DFL基线，在不同异构度和通信预算下表现优越。平滑化被证明是稳定协作决策学习的有效组件。

Conclusion: FedRSPO+为异构感知的联邦决策导向学习提供了理论基础和实用框架，平滑决策映射能有效应对异构性带来的不稳定性，推动联邦学习在下游优化任务中的应用。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DFL) trains predictive models for downstream optimization, but existing methods largely assume centralized data. In cross-silo settings, federated learning offers a natural alternative, yet standard federated methods optimize prediction over decision quality and do not address heterogeneity in downstream objectives or feasible sets. This heterogeneity is especially challenging for DFL because small perturbations in polyhedral problems can cause discontinuous changes in optimal decisions, destabilizing client updates and aggregation. We propose FedRSPO+, a heterogeneity-aware framework for decision-focused federated learning, built on RSPO+, a regularized predict-then-optimize surrogate that smooths the decision map through projection. We show that RSPO+ upper bounds decision error and regret for the regularized decision and, under exact regularization and consistent LP solution selection, for the original LP decision. We further derive cross-client heterogeneity bounds that depend on both objective and feasible-set heterogeneity, vanish at homogeneity, and require no strong convexity. FedRSPO+ uses an annealed, modular training procedure compatible with standard federated personalization and aggregation methods. Experiments on synthetic knapsack, shortest-path, and real-world energy pricing tasks compare against prediction-only federated learning and DFL baselines under varying heterogeneity and communication budgets. Results suggest that smoothing is a useful ingredient for stable collaborative decision learning and provide a heterogeneity-aware foundation for federated DFL.

</details>


### [213] [MaRK: Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels for Dynamic Operator Conditioning in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09092)
*Syed Ibrahim Omer,Ginny Y. Wong. Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) offer an efficient alternative to Transformers for sequence modeling, yet conditioning pre-trained SSMs for iterative generation typically operates outside the recurrent operator, through input injection or activation modulation. While such mechanisms expose the model to conditioning information, they leave the underlying temporal dynamics fixed. We introduce MaRK (Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels), a dynamic operator-conditioning framework that maps context vectors directly into bounded modulations of a frozen SSM's recurrence ($A$), read-in ($B$), read-out ($C$), skip ($D$), and discretization ($Δ$) parameters. Viewed through the lens of LPV-SSM systems, MaRK induces a context-indexed family of Markov parameter sequences, allowing each diffusion timestep to reshape the model's input-output memory kernel. We instantiate MaRK on a frozen 111M-parameter Hydra SSM backbone and study three adapter geometries: Hypernet, Chebyshev polynomial, and Discrete Cosine Transform kernels. Since these adapters modify the Markov parameter sequence through low-rank auxiliary maps on the frozen backbone, parameter-efficient fine-tuning arises as a structural consequence of the adaptation mechanism itself, requiring only 6.3--11M trainable auxiliary parameters to transition from a bidirectional objective to an iterative diffusion regime. The bounded recurrence parameterization further yields an analytic Affine Quadratic Stability certificate for the modulated recurrence. Through synthetic LPV recovery experiments and Markov-operator diagnostics, we show that MaRK recovers coordinate-invariant temporal operators under matched assumptions and produces distinct, stable timestep-conditioned memory profiles. Empirically, the Chebyshev variant yields the strongest performance, achieving an average validation loss of 2.55, followed by the DCT (2.59) and Hypernet (3.77) geometries.

</details>


### [214] [Are We Really Benchmarking Forecasting Models? The Impact of Preprocessing on Time Series Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09096)
*Guilherme Afonso Galindo Padilha,Paulo Salgado Gomes de Mattos Neto,Rafael Menelau Oliveira e Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示预处理是时间序列预测性能的关键驱动因素，通过预处理感知基准测试证明，针对每个序列优化预处理可使所有模型性能提升27%-87%，使简单架构能与复杂SOTA模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试忽视预处理阶段，仅使用简单缩放，未解决非平稳性等问题，导致结构性预处理偏见，有利于内置数据处理的模型，掩盖了简单架构的真实潜力。

Method: 构建预处理感知基准，评估11个预测模型在16种可逆预处理流水线上的表现，使用29,000条M4时间序列，并将结果存入综合元数据集支持元学习。

Result: 预处理是预测性能的关键驱动因素；针对每个序列优化预处理可使所有模型性能提升约27%至87%，无内置预处理的架构改进最大，使简单架构能与复杂SOTA模型竞争。

Conclusion: 预处理优化应成为预测基准的核心组成部分；发布的元数据集可促进未来元学习研究，推动更公平、更有效的模型比较。

Abstract: While established literature underscores the pivotal role of preprocessing in forecasting accuracy, this stage remains largely overlooked in current research. Modern benchmarks typically resort to simple scaling, failing to account for critical transformations required to address nonstationarity, such as differencing. This omission creates a significant structural preprocessing bias that favors models with built-in data treatments while obscuring the true potential of simpler architectures. We study this effect through a preprocessing-aware benchmark that evaluates 11 forecasting models across 16 reversible preprocessing pipelines on 29,000 M4 time series. Our results identify preprocessing as a key driver of forecasting performance. Optimizing preprocessing per series yields gains of approximately 27\% to 87\% across all evaluated models, with architectures lacking internalized preprocessing experiencing the most substantial improvements. This allows simpler architectures to become highly competitive with complex, state-of-the-art models in modern forecasting benchmarks. All resources and experimental results from this benchmark are stored in a comprehensive metadataset to support future metalearning tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Convex-Concave Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09108)
*Shripad V. Deshmukh,Yaswanth Chittepu,Dhawal Gupta,Philip Thomas,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对数密度比坐标的凸凹强化学习（CCRL）框架，将策略优化问题重新表述为差分凸约束的差分凸（DC-constrained DC）规划，利用序列凸规划（SCP）求解，统一了CPI、NPG、TRPO、PPO、AWR等现有算法，并引入多步信用分配轴，实验表明在长时程依赖任务和医疗决策领域优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略学习方法（如NPG、TRPO、PPO、AWR）通过凸代理目标和信任域约束来规避最大化期望回报的非凸优化难题，但这些替代目标引入了近似偏差且限制了多步信用分配能力。作者发现该问题在对数密度比坐标下具有隐藏的差分凸结构，旨在直接求解精确目标，弥合差分凸优化与强化学习文献的鸿沟。

Method: 1. 引入对数密度比坐标 y := log[π/π_n]，将每次迭代的精确目标表示为可通过逐决策重要性采样（PDIS）计算的差分凸约束差分凸程序。2. 证明CPI、NPG、TRPO、AWR均为该框架在特定可解释轴上的特例，并拓展出耦合连续决策的多步轴 k。3. 采用序列凸规划（SCP）求解每次迭代的DC程序，并在温和条件下给出收敛保证。

Result: 在诊断性MDP上，多步CCRL随依赖长度增加优势扩大；在经典控制任务上与调优PPO相当；在真实随机中时程医疗决策领域，CCRL收敛显著快于调优PPO，达到相同近最优存活率，且训练曲线下面积（AUC）提升11.3%。

Conclusion: 策略优化的核心非凸问题在对数密度比坐标下具有差分凸结构，通过SCP直接求解精确目标可统一现有算法并自然支持多步信用分配，在长时程依赖任务中展现出理论与实证的双重优势。

Abstract: Policy learning drives many of the most consequential and heavily-invested applications of reinforcement learning today. Yet the core optimization problem it rests on (maximizing expected return) is notoriously non-convex, even under a direct policy parameterization, and the field has largely responded by avoiding it: optimizing convex surrogate approximations of the return under trust-region constraints (NPG, TRPO, PPO, AWR). We show that this seemingly unstructured problem is not actually structureless. In log-density-ratio coordinates $y := \log[π/π_n]$, the exact per-iteration objective, computable via per-decision importance sampling (PDIS), is a difference-of-convex-constrained difference-of-convex (DC-constrained DC) program. This structure lets us move beyond surrogate approximations: it recovers CPI, NPG, TRPO, and AWR as special cases along interpretable axes, and it opens a multi-step axis $k$ that couples consecutive decisions. We solve the per-iteration program with sequential convex programming (SCP), the standard solver for difference-of-convex problems, and give convergence guarantees under mild conditions, bridging the difference-of-convex optimization and RL literatures. Empirically, multi-step Convex-Concave RL (CCRL) wins on diagnostic MDPs where credit must propagate across a horizon (its advantage growing with the dependency length), is competitive with a tuned PPO on classic control, and on a realistic, stochastic, mid-horizon healthcare domain converges markedly faster than tuned PPO to the same near-optimal survival, with an 11.3% higher area under the training curve.

</details>


### [216] [Breaking Adversarial Transferability in Fine-Tuned Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09109)
*Mojtaba Nafez,Aref Mousavi,Mohammad Ebrahim Mahdavi,Mobina Poulaei,Kiarash Kiani Feriz,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出TransferBreaker框架，通过三个组件（基础对抗微调、潜在空间雅可比正则化、混合梯度自适应微调）防御ASR模型的对抗迁移攻击，将对抗WER从92.6%降至27.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织在公开预训练ASR模型上微调并以黑盒方式部署，假设有限访问提供保护。但研究发现：在公开基础模型上生成的对抗扰动能有效迁移到微调后的目标模型，严重降低性能，对安全关键应用构成威胁。

Method: 提出TransferBreaker统一微调框架，包含三大组件：(1) 基础对抗微调（Base Adversarial Fine-Tuning）：仅对基础模型有效的扰动进行对抗训练；(2) 潜在雅可比正则化（Latent Jacobian Regularization）：通过抑制对抗敏感方向强制潜在空间不变性；(3) 混合梯度自适应微调（HybridGrad-AFT）：通过插值基础模型和目标模型的梯度生成可迁移扰动，增强对自适应攻击的鲁棒性。并为所有组件提供理论证明。

Result: 在三种语言和四个大型ASR模型上评估，对抗词错误率（WER）从92.6%大幅降低至27.8%，代码已开源。

Conclusion: TransferBreaker通过理论指导的多组件协同设计，有效抑制了对抗扰动从公开基础模型向微调目标模型的迁移，为黑盒部署的ASR系统提供了实用的鲁棒性保障。

Abstract: Many organizations fine-tune publicly available pretrained Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models and deploy them in black-box settings, assuming limited access provides protection. We show this assumption is fragile: adversarial perturbations crafted on the public base model transfer effectively to fine-tuned target models, severely degrading performance and posing concerns for safety-critical applications. We propose TransferBreaker, a unified fine-tuning framework that suppresses adversarial transfer by integrating Base Adversarial Fine-Tuning, which restricts adversarial training to base-effective perturbations; Latent Jacobian Regularization, which enforces latent-space invariance by suppressing adversarially sensitive directions; and HybridGrad-AFT, which improves robustness against adaptive attacks by interpolating transferable perturbations from base and target gradients. We theoretically justify all components and evaluate TransferBreaker across three languages and four large ASR models, reducing adversarial WER from 92.6 to 27.8. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/rohban-lab/TransferBreaker.

</details>


### [217] [The Deceptive Bandit Problem: Exploratory Coupling and the Fragility of Multi-Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09120)
*Michael Tang,Mahmoud Abdelgalil,Jorge I. Poveda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文展示了对手如何利用泄露的探索信息欺骗受害者，引导学习动态收敛到欺骗纳什均衡（DNE），证明欺骗强盗学习（DBL）动态能以最优速率收敛到DNE的任意小邻域，且放宽了标准强盗优化中所需的二阶光滑条件，同时刻画了欺骗严格改变稳态的条件及其对欺骗者成本的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机探索是强盗学习、多智能体强化学习和零阶策略搜索的核心，但其独立性和隐私性通常仅被视为技术假设。本文指出这些性质对安全至关重要，并演示了对抗性智能体如何利用另一智能体探索的特权信息进行欺骗。

Method: 在极小的双人强单调博弈设置中分析欺骗者-受害者对，欺骗者获得与受害者探索仅相关的泄露信号，通过将自身探索动作与该信息耦合，注入外部性引导学习动态向欺骗纳什均衡（DNE）演化；证明DBL动态收敛到DNE的任意小邻域并保持最优收敛速率，同时放宽了二阶光滑条件。

Result: 欺骗强盗学习（DBL）动态收敛到欺骗纳什均衡（DNE）的任意小邻域，同时保持最优收敛速率；分析在放宽标准强盗优化文献中二阶光滑条件的情况下达到这些最优速率；刻画了欺骗严格移动稳态的条件及其对欺骗者成本的影响，并在资源分配博弈中说明了结果。

Conclusion: 随机探索的独立性和隐私性对安全至关重要；对手可利用泄露的探索信息欺骗受害者，将学习动态引向欺骗纳什均衡；欺骗强盗学习动态在放宽光滑条件下仍能以最优速率收敛；欺骗可严格改变稳态并影响欺骗者成本。

Abstract: Randomized exploration is central to bandit learning, multi-agent reinforcement learning, and zeroth-order policy search, yet its independence and privacy are usually only treated as technical assumptions. We show that these properties are critical for security purposes and demonstrate how an adversarial agent can exploit privileged information on another agent's exploration. We analyze a deceiver-victim pair in the minimal two-player strongly monotone setting, where a deceptive player obtains leaked signals that are merely correlated with the victim's exploration. We show that, by coupling their own exploratory action with this information, the deceptive player injects an externality that steers the learning dynamics to a new steady state, called the deceptive Nash equilibrium (DNE). We prove that the deceptive bandit learning (DBL) dynamics converge to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of the DNE while retaining optimal convergence rates. Interestingly, our analysis attains these optimal rates while relaxing second-order smoothness conditions from standard bandit optimization literature. We characterize conditions under which deception strictly shifts the steady state and its effect on the deceiver's cost, illustrating the results in a resource-allocation game.

</details>


### [218] [Are Parameter-Efficient Fine-tuning Methods Really Different?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09122)
*Yikuan Li,Pinyan Lu,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统对比了六种参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在语言和扩散模型上的表现，发现 LoRA 族方法近似保持预训练权重几何结构，恢复其轻微漂移的奇异值谱基本不损任务性能，质疑显式几何保持的必要性；不同方法呈现明显的适应-保持权衡：LoRA 最一致地限制遗忘且性能有竞争力，DoRA 多数情况下平均任务分更高，PiSSA 常带来更大保持代价；干预实验表明，各方法性能增益源于对不同谱分量的修改，但恢复主导奇异分量而非中间或尾部分量，能最大程度降低通用文本 NLL 或基础图像漂移。结论建议从功能后果而非单纯保持角度评估几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PEFT 方法参数化各异，但其方法学与功能差异尚不清晰。受正交微调（OFT）显式保持谱结构设计的启发，作者旨在探究谱保持本身对适应与保持的重要性，并系统比较主流 PEFT 方法在任务性能、灾难性遗忘及预训练权重几何变化上的表现。

Method: 在语言模型和扩散模型上对比六种 PEFT 方法（含 LoRA、DoRA、PiSSA 等），测量任务性能、遗忘程度及预训练权重奇异值谱变化；通过恢复不同奇异分量（主导、中间、尾部）进行干预实验，量化各谱分量对适应增益与保持代价的贡献。

Result: 1) LoRA 族方法近似保持预训练几何，恢复其轻微漂移的奇异值谱基本保留任务性能，显式几何保持非必要。2) 适应-保持权衡显著：LoRA 最稳定限制遗忘且性能有竞争力；DoRA 多数设定下平均任务分更高；PiSSA 常导致更大保持代价。3) 干预实验显示：各方法增益源于不同谱分量修改，但恢复主导奇异分量能最大幅降低通用文本 NLL 或基础图像漂移。

Conclusion: 应从几何约束的功能后果（如对主导谱分量的影响）而非单纯的谱保持程度来评估 PEFT 方法，这为设计更有效的参数高效微调策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) offers many parameterizations, yet their methodological and functional differences remain unclear. We compare six methods in language and diffusion models to examine how their parameterizations relate to task performance, forgetting, and changes in pretrained weight geometry. Motivated by the spectrum-preserving design of orthogonal fine-tuning (OFT), we first ask whether spectral preservation is itself important for adaptation and retention. We find that the selected LoRA-family methods also approximately preserve pretrained geometry, and that restoring their slightly drifted singular-value spectra largely preserves task performance, questioning the necessity of explicit geometric preservation. Beyond this, we observe that some methods exhibit distinct adaptation--retention trade-offs that vary across settings: LoRA most consistently limits forgetting at competitive performance, DoRA achieves higher mean task scores than LoRA in most comparisons, while PiSSA often incurs greater retention costs. Further intervention experiments suggest that while performance gains from different PEFT methods can be attributed to modifications in different groups of spectral components, we consistently find that restoring dominant rather than intermediate or trailing components produces the largest mean reduction in general-text NLL or base-image drift. Together, these results motivate evaluating geometric constraints through their functional consequences rather than preservation alone. Code is available at https://github.com/Kuaaannn/PEFT_methods.

</details>


### [219] [Spatial Induction Heads: In-Context Learning of Multidimensional Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09124)
*Kimia Kazemian,Menghan Xu,John Thickstun,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出空间诱导头（spatial induction heads）机制，解释Transformer如何在多维元胞自动机的扁平化序列中实现上下文学习，通过两层聚集-匹配电路重建空间邻域并实现贝叶斯计数，理论与实验均验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诱导头理论假设上下文相关信息形成连续块，但多维数据序列化会打破空间邻域的连续性，导致空间路由问题。本文旨在研究Transformer如何在没有显式坐标偏置的情况下，从扁平化序列中学习未知的局部规则并进行预测。

Method: 提出空间诱导头：两层聚集-匹配电路。第一层聚集重建相关空间邻域，给出两种显式实现方式，证明所需位置维度仅依赖局部邻域和空间维度，不依赖网格体积或轨迹长度。第二层匹配实现贝叶斯计数，匹配重建的配置与历史出现。端到端电路可任意逼近贝叶斯后验（随机规则）或精确预测（确定性规则）。实验训练两层Transformer在1D/2D元胞自动机上，探测注意力模式和层级探针验证机制。

Result: 训练的两层Transformer能泛化到未见规则，在确定性规则上实现近乎完美的滚动预测，在随机规则上KL散度小于0.005 nats接近贝叶斯最优预测器。注意力模式和层级探针与预测的聚集-匹配计算一致，提供了机制性证据。

Conclusion: 空间诱导头为Transformer在多维序列数据中处理空间路由问题提供了机制性解释，证明了其能够通过显式的聚集-匹配电路实现贝叶斯最优推理，且所需资源不随系统规模增长，具有良好的泛化性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Induction heads provide a mechanistic account of in-context learning in sequential data, but existing theory largely assumes that the context relevant to a prediction forms a contiguous block. In multidimensional data, serialization breaks this assumption by scattering spatial neighbors across distant positions in the token sequence. We study how transformers overcome this routing problem in multidimensional stochastic and deterministic cellular automata, where each trajectory is generated by an unknown local rule and presented as a flattened sequence without an explicit coordinate-based spatial inductive bias. We introduce spatial induction heads, two-layer gather-and-match circuits in which the first layer reconstructs the relevant spatial neighborhood and the second matches the resulting configuration against earlier occurrences. We give two explicit realizations of the gather and show that the positional dimension required for spatial routing depends only on the local neighborhood and spatial dimension, not on grid volume or trajectory horizon. We further construct a matching layer which implements Bayesian counting. The end-to-end circuit can approximate the Bayesian posterior arbitrarily closely for stochastic rules and can predict exactly for deterministic rules. Empirically, trained two-layer transformers generalize to unseen rules in one and two dimensional settings, achieving near-perfect deterministic rollouts and less than 0.005 nats KL from the Bayes-optimal predictor on stochastic rules. Attention patterns and layerwise probes align with the predicted gather-and-match computation, providing mechanistic evidence for spatial induction in trained transformers.

</details>


### [220] [Domain-informed Adaptive Sampling for Generalizable PINNs in Metal Additive Manufacturing via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09126)
*Hyeonsu Lee,Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于金属增材制造热建模的自适应采样策略，基于条件Flow Matching模型学习高残差分布，结合领域先验分布生成自适应配置点，显著提升PINN的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN在金属AM热建模中依赖手工静态采样策略，缺乏原则性且难以跨工艺条件泛化，限制了模型在不同工艺条件下的泛化能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 条件Flow Matching模型学习不同工艺条件下的近似高残差分布；(2) 混合采样策略将该分布与领域先验基础分布结合，生成自适应配置点以细化PINN预测器。理论分析基于经验风险最小化证明自适应采样优于静态采样。

Result: 在金属AM数值基准测试中，该方法持续优于最先进PINN基线，在相同配置点预算下平均相对L2误差降低62.1%，捕捉了文献中常被忽视的工艺相关散热区域。

Conclusion: 这是首个用于金属AM中PINN的自适应采样策略，显著增强了泛化能力和更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Accurate thermal modeling is essential in metal additive manufacturing (AM) for understanding the process-structure-property chain. Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer effective surrogate thermal modeling by minimizing physics-based residual losses at collocation points. However, prior works typically rely on manually-crafted, static collocation sampling strategies, which are neither principled nor scalable across process conditions, hindering their generalization capability. In this work, we provide theoretical analysis through empirical risk minimization, showing that process condition-aware adaptive sampling is strictly more favorable than conventional static sampling for generalization. Building on this insight, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy within a two-stage framework: (1) a conditional Flow Matching model that learns approximate high-residual distributions across different process conditions, and (2) a mixed sampling strategy combining this distribution with a domain-informed base distribution to generate adaptive collocation points for refining the PINN predictor. Experiments on metal AM numerical benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PINN baselines, achieving an average 62.1\% reduction in relative $L_2$ error under an identical collocation budget, by capturing process-dependent heat dissipation regions often overlooked in the literature. To the authors' knowledge, this is the first adaptive sampling strategy for PINNs in metal AM, contributing to the enhanced generalization and broader applicability.

</details>


### [221] [Directional Evidence Guided Search-Space Reduction for Exact DAG Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09136)
*Upala Junaida Islam,Abdelmonem Elrefaey,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DECO，一种非参数混合框架，利用方向证据从观测数据中构建可接受父节点集，在保持真实父结构的前提下实现搜索空间的指数级缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分的精确方法需要计算密集型组合搜索，而基于约束的方法随着图规模和条件集复杂度增加变得不可靠或计算量大。

Method: DECO（方向证据引导的配置优化）从观测数据中提取依赖和方向证据，在精确优化前构建可接受父节点集，通过消除实证不支持的父节点配置来缩减优化搜索空间，同时保留所有合理边方向的灵活性。

Result: 理论分析证明可接受父节点配置空间呈指数级缩减，量化了有界边级遗漏对保留真实父结构概率的影响。在基准贝叶斯网络和合成离散/连续DAG上的实验显示搜索空间大幅缩减，同时实现具有竞争力的结构恢复性能，在许多评估设置下结构汉明距离表现优异。

Conclusion: 方向证据可作为一种有效的预处理机制，在不需要固定参数结构模型的情况下降低精确DAG学习的计算负担。

Abstract: Learning a directed acyclic graph (DAG) from observational data is a challenging combinatorial problem due to the exponential growth in the number of candidate parent-set configurations. Existing exact score-based methods often require computationally intensive combinatorial search, whereas constraint-based methods can become unreliable or computationally demanding as graph size and conditioning-set complexity increase. We develop a non-parametric hybrid framework, referred to as DECO (Directional Evidence-guided Configuration Optimization), that extracts dependency and directional evidence from observation data to construct admissible parent sets prior to exact optimization. It reduces the optimization search space by eliminating empirically unsupported parent configurations while preserving flexibility for all plausible edge orientations. Theoretical analysis establishes an exponential reduction in the admissible parent-set configuration space and quantifies how bounded edge-level omission affects the probability of retaining the true parent structure. Experiments on benchmark Bayesian networks and synthetic discrete and continuous DAGs demonstrate substantial search-space reduction while achieving competitive structure-recovery performance, with favorable structural Hamming distance across many evaluated settings. These results show that directional evidence can provide an effective preprocessing mechanism for reducing the computational burden of exact DAG learning without requiring a fixed parametric structural~model.

</details>


### [222] [A Cognitive-Aware QML-CRL Framework for Detecting Affinity and Romance-Investment Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09141)
*Bibhas Adhikari,Ramya Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种混合量子-经典框架，用于检测亲和力诈骗和恋爱投资诈骗，通过在参数化量子电路中建模操纵性对话中的认知偏差，并结合强化学习智能体进行逐轮最优停止决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亲和力诈骗和恋爱投资诈骗利用认知偏差进行操纵，传统方法难以捕捉对话中时序依赖的重构框架和叙事共现模式，需要一种能够建模认知偏差时序动态并实时判断欺诈的方法。

Method: 构建结构化参数化量子电路：专用量子比特编码核心认知偏差，框架量子比特捕捉操纵性重构的时序顺序，叙事量子比特通过可训练纠缠层聚合共现模式。电路参数与经典强化学习智能体联合训练，将欺诈检测建模为最优停止问题，智能体逐轮决定是否标记对话。

Result: 在包含难负样本、合法但紧急对话、合法但强推销售对话的合成数据集上评估，模型能有效区分欺诈与困难负样本，展示了量子电路建模认知偏差时序动态的优势。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典框架通过量子电路显式建模认知偏差的时序结构，结合强化学习实现逐轮最优停止决策，为亲和力和恋爱投资诈骗检测提供了新的计算范式，在处理高度相似的合法对话干扰方面表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical framework that detects affinity and romance-investment fraud by modelling the cognitive biases in a manipulative conversation. In our proposed framework, cognitive biases central to this fraud class are carried by dedicated qubits in a structured parameterized quantum circuit, together with a frame qubit makes the encoding sensitive to the temporal order of manipulative reframing, and a narrative qubit that aggregates co-occurrence through a trainable entanglement layer. The circuit parameters are trained jointly with a classical reinforcement-learning agent that decides, turn by turn, whether to flag the conversation, modeled as an optimal stopping problem. We evaluate the model's performance on synthetic conversations that include hard negatives, legitimate but urgent, and legitimate but pushy sales conversations.

</details>


### [223] [Noise Your Prompt: Noising Conditioning Tokens in Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09145)
*Justin Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出在连续扩散语言模型训练中对条件提示词也添加噪声，这一行代码的改动显著提升了组合推理任务（如数独、N皇后）的泛化能力和解的多样性，在小规模数据集上的自然语言生成也有提升，但并非对所有自然语言任务都有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续扩散语言模型训练时通常保持条件提示词干净不加噪，作者质疑这一标准做法，探索对提示词也加噪是否能改善模型的泛化和推理能力。

Method: 在训练目标中将条件提示词 token 也加入噪声，仅需一行代码修改，不增加推理开销，且支持类似无分类器引导的采样策略。

Result: 数独 Hard 解题率从 3.73% 提升至 24.65%，10x10 N 皇后解覆盖率从 50.60% 提升至 73.79%，Gigaword 摘要任务质量提升，但在开放域对话生成等任务上无增益。

Conclusion: 对条件提示词加噪是一种低成本、高收益的训练策略，特别适合组合推理和小数据自然语言生成场景，但并非万能，需根据任务特性评估适用性。

Abstract: We revisit a standard accepted practice in the continuous diffusion language model
  literature of fixing conditioning prompt tokens clean during training.
  We make a very simple modification: also noise the conditioning prompt tokens during training.
  We demonstrate that under this modified training objective, we achieve better generalization
  in combinatorial reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and N-Queens, with the largest gains on harder variants
  ($3.73\% \to 24.65\%$ solve rate on Sudoku Hard), and increased diversity of generated solutions ($50.60\% \to 73.79\%$ coverage on
  10x10 N-Queens). We also show measurable improvements to natural language generation quality
  in modest dataset regimes with Gigaword summarization, but notably demonstrate that gains do not
  transfer to all natural language tasks (e.g open ended dialogue generation).
  Our method is a single line change to the training objective, requires no additional inference costs by default,
  and provides the flexibility of classifier-free guidance inspired guided sampling. Our
  \href{https://github.com/LateralIntelligence/noise-your-prompt} {code} is publicly available.

</details>


### [224] [Context-aware Attention-based Gaussian Mixture Models for Vehicular Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09174)
*Arash Raftari,Babak Ebrahimi Soorchaei,Yaser P. Fallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文感知注意力的高斯混合模型（CAA-GMM），用于多模态、不确定性感知的运动预测，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上达到了与最先进的栅格基线相当或更优的精度，同时保持低计算复杂度，并展示了对通信和感知不完美条件的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂不确定环境中，可靠且可解释的轨迹预测对于协作和自动驾驶至关重要。现有方法往往难以同时捕捉多模态行为、环境上下文交互以及计算效率。

Method: CAA-GMM将未来运动建模为条件于场景上下文和智能体动态的概率混合模型，利用轻量级注意力机制自适应编码智能体间交互和上下文显著性，高效融合栅格化环境线索和运动历史。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2数据集上，CAA-GMM达到与最先进栅格基线相当或更优的预测精度，计算复杂度低；消融实验证实注意力模块对上下文推理和预测精度的重要性；在不完美通信和感知条件下评估显示框架对不确定性的强韧性。

Conclusion: CAA-GMM是一种高效且可扩展的解决方案，适用于智能交通系统中的协作轨迹预测，兼具多模态建模、不确定性量化、可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Reliable and interpretable trajectory prediction is critical for cooperative and autonomous driving in complex and uncertain environments. This paper introduces a Context-Aware Attention-based Gaussian Mixture Model (CAA-GMM) for multimodal, uncertainty-aware motion forecasting. The proposed approach models future motion as a probabilistic mixture conditioned on both scene context and agent dynamics, capturing diverse behavioral modes with interpretable Gaussian components. A lightweight attention mechanism adaptively encodes inter-agent interactions and contextual salience, enabling efficient fusion of rasterized environment cues and motion history in dense traffic scenes. Comprehensive evaluations on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that CAA-GMM achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared with state-of-the-art raster-based baselines, while maintaining low computational complexity. Ablation analyses confirm the importance of the attention module for robust contextual reasoning and predictive precision. Furthermore, evaluations under imperfect communication and perception conditions highlight the framework's resilience to uncertainty, establishing CAA-GMM as an efficient and scalable solution for cooperative trajectory prediction in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [225] [LayerRoPE: Dynamic Depth-wise Magnitude & Angular Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09179)
*Shikhar Srivastava,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了Transformer中隐藏状态范数随深度增长的‘深度诅咒’病理观，发现归一化权重γ的幅度增长与方向旋转构成了涌现的深度位置编码。提出LayerRoPE，沿深度轴引入类RoPE的隐式深度条件编码，用共享向量加深度条件标量替换逐层γ，在参数更少、算力几乎不变的情况下，显著提升深度扩展性、收敛稳定性与学习率鲁棒性，并在多种架构上泛化有效。实验表明：深度稳定性不应抑制残差流，而应通过深度条件调节计算块来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍将Transformer隐藏状态范数随层数增长视为需抑制的病理（‘深度诅咒’），但作者观察到跨16个预训练LLM、9个模型家族（含Dense、MoE、混合架构及Pre/Peri/Post-Norm），归一化权重γ随深度幅度增大、方向旋转，编码了层索引信息，推测这是一种涌现的深度位置编码，而非单纯病理。

Method: 提出LayerRoPE：将每层独立的γ向量替换为单一共享向量γ_shared与深度条件标量α_l、β_l，使归一化缩放因子为γ_l = γ_shared * (α_l + β_l * l)，类比RoPE在序列维度的旋转位置编码，隐式注入深度位置信息。此设计净减少参数，FLOPs变化<0.02%。

Result: 在扩展至100B+ tokens的模型梯队上，LayerRoPE持续优于Pre/Post/Peri-Norm及Layer-Norm Scaling：以3.4×更少算力达到Pre-Norm 1.3B模型的loss；唯一在深度扩展至512层仍保持强收敛且近单调改进；学习率敏感度提升3-10×；零样本迁移至循环潜变量模型与ViT并持续提升。分析显示LayerRoPE并非收缩残差流，而是拓宽残差流，抑制块读入、放大块写出。

Conclusion: 深度稳定性不应通过抑制残差流范数实现，而应引入深度条件的计算块调节机制。LayerRoPE将γ的涌现深度编码显式化、参数化，为深层Transformer提供了一种高效、稳定、可扩展的归一化新范式。

Abstract: As data propagates through a Transformer, the norm of its hidden states grows by orders of magnitude with depth, a phenomenon framed as 'curse of depth' and nearly universally treated as a pathology to be suppressed. We take the opposite view. Across 16 pre-trained LLMs from 9 families, spanning dense, mixture-of-experts and hybrid architectures and Pre-, Peri- and Post-Norm designs, we find that this growth reflects an emergent depth-positional encoding, carried by the only learned per-layer gain on the residual stream, the normalization weight $γ$: with depth, $γ$ grows in magnitude and rotates in direction, jointly encoding the layer index. We make this depth-conditioned encoding explicit with LayerRoPE, an implicit analog of RoPE along the depth axis, which replaces all layerwise $γ$ vectors with a single shared vector and depth-conditioned scalars, at a net reduction in parameters and $<0.02\%$ change in FLOPs. Across a model ladder scaled up to $100$B+ tokens, LayerRoPE consistently outperforms Pre-, Post- and Peri-Norm and Layer-Norm Scaling, reaching Pre-Norm's 1.3B loss with $3.4\times$ less compute; LayerRoPE is the only approach that shows strong convergence and improves near monotonically as depth scales to 512 layers. It improves learning-rate sensitivity by $3$-$10\times$, and transfers naively to and consistently improves looped latent models and Vision Transformers. Inspecting its learned schedule inverts the prevailing premise: LayerRoPE does not shrink the residual stream but widens it, damping what each block reads while amplifying what it writes. Depth stability, our results suggest, calls not for suppressing the residual stream, but for depth-conditioned regulation of the computational blocks it feeds.

</details>


### [226] [Q-PACE: Dynamic Precision Allocation for Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09183)
*Alexandra Volkova,Matin Ansaripour,Erik Schultheis,Christoph H. Lampert,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Q-PACE，一种基于二阶敏感度模型的混合精度量化感知训练方法，通过周期性重新计算逐层曲率系数并重新分配精度，在 4B 参数 LLM 上实现更低内存预算下的可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进的低精度量化会降低模型性能，混合精度训练虽能缓解但需要在训练期间为各层分配精度，现有方法缺乏高效、可预测的精度分配策略。

Method: Q-PACE 构建二阶敏感度模型，将损失增量预测为量化噪声 MSE 与逐层曲率系数的加权和；训练中周期性地通过跨层扰动重新计算曲率系数，并据此重新分配各层精度。

Result: 在高达 4B 参数的 LLM 预训练和监督微调实验中，Q-PACE 持续优于现有混合精度训练方案，在大幅降低总内存预算的同时达到相当的损失；量化敏感度随深度和层类型高度可预测，且训练期间稳定，支持低频、低成本的重新校准。

Conclusion: Q-PACE 通过二阶敏感度建模和动态精度重分配，实现了高效的混合精度量化感知训练，显著降低内存开销同时保持模型性能，且量化敏感度的可预测性和稳定性使得重新校准既便宜又不频繁。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) leverages lower-precision arithmetic to reduce the cost of LLM deployment, but aggressive quantization degrades final model performance. A common remedy is mixed-precision training, in which high precision is assigned to some of the layers to maintain performance while keeping the cost constrained. This approach then requires precision assignments for model layers during training. We provide a new approach, called Q-PACE, consisting of a second-order sensitivity model that predicts the loss increase as a sum of quantization noise MSE weighted by per-layer curvature coefficients. During training, we periodically re-compute these coefficients using perturbations across layers, and re-assign precision. Pretraining and supervised fine-tuning experiments on LLMs of up to 4B parameters show that Q-PACE consistently improves over existing mixed-precision training recipes, and achieves comparable loss at substantially lower total memory budgets. We further find that quantization sensitivity is highly predictable by depth and layer type, and its stability during training allows for infrequent, cheap recalibration.

</details>


### [227] [The Dichotomy Between Pattern Recognition and Step-by-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09186)
*Amrut Nadgir,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出模式识别与逐步推理是一个光谱的两端，通过德布鲁因图建模推理轨迹，证明推理边的数量远小于轨迹数量，从而解释逐步推理的样本高效性。实验表明，通过引入“状态”使未来推理独立于过去，可以控制上下文长度 c，小 c 虽准确率高但脆弱，大 c 退化为纯模式识别，中等密度状态能平衡准确性与鲁棒性。真实数据（如 GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond）在 Qwen3 模型上表现出德布鲁因结构：将注意力限制在小于完整推理轨迹 15% 的滑动窗口内，仍能保留 75% 以上准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型（LLM）中模式识别与逐步推理的本质联系，解释为何模型能通过学习少量边组合解决未见任务，并量化上下文依赖长度 c 对推理样本效率、准确性及鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 1. 将推理轨迹建模为德布鲁因图上的路径：节点为长度为 c 的上下文，边为下一 token 转移。2. 证明任务推理轨迹形成的有向无环子图的边数远小于轨迹数。3. 通过在推理轨迹中维护“状态”使未来独立于过去，控制状态频率以调节 c。4. 在 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 上微调解方程与故事问答任务，测量不同 c 下的准确率与抗扰动能力。5. 在 Qwen3-14B/32B 上用滑动窗口注意力（<15% 全轨迹长度）评估 GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond 的保留准确率。

Result: 1. 理论：推理子图边数呈消失量级，训练样本数随边数呈幂律增长，逐步推理样本高效。2. 实验：小 c（频繁状态）准确率高但对测试时扰动脆弱；大 c 模型仅相当于无推理的模式识别；中等状态密度在准确性与鲁棒性间取得最佳平衡。3. 真实基准：Qwen3-14B/32B 在滑动窗口注意力受限至 <15% 轨迹长度时，仍保留 >75% 原始准确率，证实真实数据具有德布鲁因结构。

Conclusion: 逐步推理的本质是学习德布鲁因子图中少量边并组合泛化；通过显式状态控制上下文窗口 c 可在样本效率、准确性和鲁棒性之间权衡。真实推理任务天然具有稀疏德布鲁因结构，这解释了 LLM 在长推理链中仅需局部注意力即可保持高性能的现象。

Abstract: We argue that pattern recognition and step-by-step reasoning are two ends of a spectrum. A large language model (LLM) learns to reason step-by-step when data is structured such that the next token depends on a small amount of preceding context. Inference in LLMs resembles pattern recognition when the next token depends on a large amount of preceding context. If the next token depends on only the $c$ most recent tokens, reasoning traces are paths on a De Bruijn graph whose nodes are $c$-length contexts and edges are next-token transitions between contexts. The set of reasoning traces of a task forms a directed acyclic subgraph of the De Bruijn graph. An LLM that has learned all edges of this subgraph can compose them to solve longer, unseen tasks, i.e., it reasons step-by-step. We prove that the number of edges is vanishingly small compared to the number of reasoning traces. Empirically, the number of training samples a transformer needs is a power law in the number of edges, so learning to reason step-by-step is sample efficient. We can induce De Bruijn structure in any task by maintaining a ``state'' that makes future reasoning independent of the past. The frequency of states in the reasoning trace determines $c$. We show, by fine-tuning Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct to solve equations and answer questions about stories, that frequent states (small $c$) result in higher accuracy but greater fragility to perturbations at test time. LLMs trained with a large $c$ are only as good as models that perform pattern recognition without reasoning. A moderate density of states balances accuracy and robustness. We show that real-world data has De Bruijn structure: Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B retain over 75% of their accuracy on GSM8K, MATH-500 and GPQA-Diamond when attention is restricted to a sliding window less than 15% as long as the full reasoning trace.

</details>


### [228] [Patient, Place, Prior (P$^3$): What Counts as Personalization in Medical World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09194)
*Xingrui Gu,Hanxue Gu,Yuxiang Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 P³ 审计框架和 Cancer JEPA 模型，用于评估纵向预测模型是否真正利用患者特异性信息，而非仅依赖群体平均模式。实验表明模型确实使用了患者病史和病灶图，但患者特异性预测价值在统计上不显著优于群体平均模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纵向预测模型的准确性无法说明预测是否真正由患者的观测轨迹驱动，群体平均预测可能有用但无法建立患者特异性的世界模型主张。需要一种审计方法来区分输入使用与患者特异性预测价值的证据。

Method: 提出 P³ 审计框架，包含三个维度：Patient（患者纵向影像病史）、Place（患者匹配的外部空间支持）、Prior（在匹配支持和上下文下超越群体平均预测的预测价值）。开发 Cancer JEPA 模型：基于患者条件的低复杂度降秩回归基线，添加病灶约束的神经校正模块，使用基于遮挡的潜在目标训练，冻结表示预测乳腺 DCE-MRI 未来检查。该分解允许事后 P³ 审计。

Result: 在开发用的验证队列中，当神经校正接收患者病史而非他人病史，以及患者匹配的病灶占据图而非替代图时，预测误差更低。然而，比较基于患者病史的校正与群体平均神经校正的描述性 95% 区间包含零，表明缺乏统计学证据支持患者特异性预测价值超越群体模式。

Conclusion: P³ 框架成功将输入使用与患者特异性预测价值的证据分离。尽管 Cancer JEPA 利用了患者特异性输入（病史和病灶图），但其预测增益在统计上不显著优于群体平均模式，提示在临床应用前需更严格的患者特异性价值验证。

Abstract: Longitudinal models forecast how a patient's imaging state evolves, but accuracy does not show whether the patient's observed trajectory drives the prediction. A population-average forecast may be useful but cannot establish a patient-specific world-model claim. We introduce Patient, Place, Prior (P$^3$), an audit asking whether a forecast benefits from the patient's longitudinal imaging history (Patient), benefits from patient-matched externally supplied spatial support (Place), and gains predictive value beyond a population-average prediction under matched support and context (Prior). We also propose Cancer JEPA, a one-step model that forecasts frozen representations of future breast dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI examinations during neoadjuvant therapy. It adds a lesion-constrained neural correction, trained with an occlusion-based latent objective, to a patient-conditioned low-complexity reduced-rank regression baseline. This factorization permits a post-hoc P$^3$ audit of the frozen model. In a validation cohort previously used in development, forecast error is lower when the neural correction receives the patient's history rather than another patient's and patient-matched lesion occupancy maps rather than substituted maps. However, the descriptive 95% interval comparing the correction computed from patient history with the population-average neural correction includes zero. P$^3$ thus separates input use from evidence of patient-specific predictive value beyond a population-level pattern.

</details>


### [229] [Exact Dynamics and Finite-Sample Trajectory Recovery of Linear Recursive Feature Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09196)
*Andrew Cheng,Bobak T. Kiani,Yue M. Lu,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究线性递归特征机（RFM）在带噪多输出回归中的动态与统计性质，证明特征矩阵误差以 O(√(d/n)) 衰减，并在真实文本与单细胞基因表达数据上验证了该简单线性模型学到的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用线性 RFM 作为可分析的简单设定，探究神经网络中平均梯度外积（AGOP）驱动的特征学习如何使表示演化，并将 RFM 与迭代重加权最小二乘的已知联系从插值设定扩展到带噪声的岭正则化多输出回归。

Method: 理论层面：在各向同性次高斯输入、低秩教师矩阵生成目标的设定下，分析线性 RFM 的迭代动态，证明特征矩阵每步均接近无限数据理想对应矩阵；实验层面：在真实文本数据与单细胞基因表达数据上运行线性 RFM，展示其学习到的特征。

Result: 证明对于 n 个样本，学习到的特征矩阵与无限数据理想矩阵的误差以 O(√(d/n)) 速率衰减（高概率下）；实验表明该简单线性模型在真实数据上能学到有意义的特征表示。

Conclusion: 线性 RFM 为理解特征学习动态提供了理论可分析的框架，其特征矩阵收敛速度在有限样本下有非渐近保证，且在实际任务中表现出良好的特征学习能力。

Abstract: Recursive feature machines (RFMs) learn representations of data by alternating between fitting a predictor to a dataset and updating features of that predictor using the average gradient outer product (AGOP). Connections between AGOPs and feature learning in neural networks motivate linear RFMs as a simple setting for analyzing how representations evolve during training. Here, we study the dynamics and statistics of linear RFM in noisy multi-output regression with isotropic sub-Gaussian input data and targets generated by a low-rank teacher matrix of dimension $d$. We extend the known connection between linear RFM and iteratively reweighted least squares from the interpolating setting to ridge-regularized multi-output regression with noise. We show that the learned feature matrix remains close to its infinite-data ideal counterpart at every iteration. Namely, for $n$ samples, we show the error in the feature matrix decays as $O(\sqrt{d/n})$ with high probability. Experiments on real-world text and single-cell gene-expression data illustrate the features learned by this simple linear model.

</details>


### [230] [An Accuracy--Information Tradeoff for Loss-Difference Conditional Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09206)
*Hazar Yueksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究损失差异条件互信息（ld-CMI）与泛化界的关系，证明在特定条件下（线性预测器、光滑凸损失、正则化等），任何达到最优超额风险的恰当学习者，其最坏情况 ld-CMI 必然达到 n 量级，即使泛化误差为 O(n^{-1/2})，揭示了范围缩放的 ld-CMI 界无法在这些分布上消失。同时指出模型层面信息不决定噪声损失差异信息，且增长、斜率和维度条件均是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泛化界中，ld-CMI 使用超采样层级中最小的标准观测量，度量学习者损失差异揭示其训练对的信息量。准确性迫使信息进入模型，但数据处理不将此类下界传递给损失。作者旨在证明准确性也强制产生 ld-CMI，并刻画其在具体设定下的量级与必要条件。

Method: 通过对损失差异的三个矩进行界定，推导 ld-CMI 的下界。考虑线性预测器、在零点斜率非零的光滑凸损失（如 logistic 损失）、曲率和增长均为幂次 r≥2 的正则化器，在维度至少线性于 n 的缩放符号立方体上的乘积分布上，分析在最优样本量 n≍ε^{-2+2/r} 下达到 ε 超额风险的恰当学习者。

Result: 最坏情况下 ld-CMI 为 Θ(n) 比特；在损失差异上添加标准差为 τ 的高斯噪声时，ld-CMI 为 Θ(n/(1+(τ/ε)^2)) 比特。无正则化时对应 n≍ε^{-2}。因此范围缩放的 ld-CMI 界无法在这些分布上消失，尽管泛化间隙为 O(n^{-1/2})。还证明了模型层面信息不决定噪声损失差异信息，且增长、斜率和维度条件（维度条件对数级松弛）均为必要。

Conclusion: 准确性不仅迫使信息进入模型参数，还强制产生损失差异层面的条件互信息，且这种信息在最优泛化设定下无法通过范围缩放消除。这揭示了 ld-CMI 作为泛化度量的局限性，并明确了使其成立的必要条件。

Abstract: Loss-difference conditional mutual information (ld-CMI) uses the smallest of the standard observations in the supersample hierarchy of generalization bounds: it measures what a learner's loss differences reveal about which candidate of each pair it was trained on. Accuracy is known to force information into the model; data processing does not carry such lower bounds to losses. We show, by bounding three moments of the loss differences, that accuracy also forces ld-CMI. For linear predictors with a smooth convex loss of nonzero slope at zero, such as the logistic loss, plus a regularizer whose curvature and growth are both of power $r\ge2$, on product distributions over a scaled sign cube in dimension at least linear in $n$, every proper learner with expected excess risk at most $\varepsilon$ on these distributions at the optimal sample size $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2+2/r}$ has worst-case ld-CMI of order $n$ bits, and $Θ(n/(1+(τ/\varepsilon)^2))$ bits under Gaussian noise of standard deviation $τ$ on the loss differences. The same holds without a regularizer, at $n\asymp\varepsilon^{-2}$. Consequently, range-scaled ld-CMI bounds cannot vanish on these distributions, although every proper learner's generalization gap is $O(n^{-1/2})$. We also show that model-level information does not determine noisy loss-difference information, and that the growth, slope and dimension conditions are needed, the last up to a logarithm.

</details>


### [231] [CurveTQ: Rotation-Free Trellis Quantization of LLM Weights via Curvature-Weighted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09212)
*Guanhua Ding,Zi Wang,Ruichao Li,Jack Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CurveTQ，一种无需旋转的二比特 Trellis 量化编码器，通过在 Viterbi 分支度量中引入 Hessian LDL 分解的对角权重，直接在原始基底下利用层内曲率，实现了比 QTIP 和 Proteus 更高的下游精度（4-8B 模型提升 1-3 点，35B MoE 首个 Trellis 结果）且解码速度最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最佳二比特量化器（QTIP、Proteus）依赖随机正交旋转并仅在编码块间利用 Hessian（通过误差反馈），导致块内 Hessian 信息未被充分利用；旋转虽能消除块内方差，但引入了解码开销。

Method: 将 Hessian LDL 分解的对角元素作为权重融入 Viterbi 搜索的分支度量，使搜索直接跟随块内曲率；构建 CurveTQ：无旋转 Trellis 编码器，用分解尺度场和闭形式分位数表处理权重幅度与边缘分布，并为每个编码块存储起始状态以适应误差反馈带来的残差。

Result: 在三个 4-8B Instruct 模型上，CurveTQ 平均下游精度比 QTIP 和 Proteus 高 1-3 点（即使后者加入起始状态也仅提升 1-3 点）；在 35B MoE 上取得首个 Trellis 量化最优结果；无旋转解码使其在所有测试批大小和位宽下速度最快。

Conclusion: 在原始基底引入块内 Hessian 权重可替代旋转，CurveTQ 通过加权 Viterbi 搜索与自适应起始状态，实现了更高精度、更快解码且无需旋转的二比特 LLM 量化新范式。

Abstract: The best two-bit weight quantizers for large language models, such as QTIP and Proteus, rotate each weight matrix by a random orthogonal transform, which must be undone at every decoding step, then encode it with a trellis or lattice code under a Euclidean search; the layer Hessian enters only through error feedback between coding blocks. We show that this leaves part of the Hessian unused. Error feedback turns the loss into a weighted sum of per-coordinate rounding errors whose weights, the diagonal of the Hessian's LDL factorization, existing quantizers compute but never read. We put these weights into the Viterbi branch metric, so the search follows the curvature within each coding block. This also explains the rotation: it removes this within-block variation, so weighting in the native basis and rotating are substitutes. On three models the weighted native search matches a full-dimension randomized Hadamard to within about one point of downstream accuracy, and weighting after the rotation gains little. Around this search we build CurveTQ, a trellis codec with no rotation, which handles the weights' amplitude and marginal shape with a factored scale field and a closed-form quantile table, and stores a start state per coding block so the trellis can adapt to the residual that error feedback carries into it. At two bits CurveTQ is 1-3 points higher in mean downstream accuracy than QTIP and Proteus on three 4-8B Instruct models, even after both are given our start state, which alone lifts either baseline by 1-3 points. It also leads on a 35B mixture of experts, to our knowledge the first trellis-coded result on such a model.
