With no rotation to undo, our decoder is the fastest of the three at all tested batch sizes and bit widths.

</details>


### [232] [Consistent Distribution Matching for Data-Free Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09221)
*Yuxiang Fu,Qi Yan,Zike Wu,Yongxing Zhang,Purang Abolmaesumi,Lele Wang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Consistent Distribution Matching (CDM)，一种无需仿真、无需数据的蒸馏方法，仅用一个冻结教师模型和一个可训练学生模型、单一目标函数，即可在保持强生成能力的前提下将扩散/流模型加速至 1-NFE 或 4-NFE，在 ImageNet 256×256 上分别达到 2.04 和 1.37 FID，超越现有无数据蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型推理缓慢，依赖昂贵的数值积分；现有蒸馏方法通常需要精心构建的蒸馏数据集、代价高昂的教师轨迹采样或辅助代理网络，增加了训练复杂度并限制了扩展性。

Method: 提出 Consistent Distribution Matching：将样本生成与分数估计统一到同一个学生网络中，仅使用冻结教师模型与可训练学生模型两个模型，优化单一目标函数。理论证明最小化该目标等价于学生流映射推前分布向教师边缘分布的 Wasserstein 收敛。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，1-NFE FID 2.04，4-NFE FID 1.37，仅训练 40 个 epoch，性能超越所有无数据蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: CDM 实现了免数据、免仿真的高效蒸馏，以极简的双模型单目标框架在理论与实验上均保证了分布匹配的收敛性，为大规模扩散/流模型的快速部署提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Flow and diffusion models suffer from slow inference due to computationally expensive numerical integration. Distillation provides a promising way for a student model to learn from a teacher's dynamics, enabling one-step or few-step generation. However, existing methods often depend on curated distillation datasets, costly teacher rollouts, or auxiliary proxy networks, which complicate model training and scaling. In this work, we propose Consistent Distribution Matching, a simulation-free and data-free distillation method for accelerating diffusion and flow models while preserving strong generative capacity. Our key insight is to unify sample generation and score estimation with one student network. Thus, our framework uses only two models, a frozen teacher and a trainable student, and optimizes one objective. We prove that minimizing our objective indicates Wasserstein convergence of the student flow-map pushforwards to the teacher marginals. On ImageNet 256$\times$256, our method attains an FID of 2.04 with a single function evaluation (1-NFE) and a 4-NFE FID of 1.37 within 40 epochs of training, surpassing the state-of-the-art distillation baselines without data. Our code code and model are available at https://consistentdmd.github.io/.

</details>


### [233] [Conditional Accuracy Profiles: Diagnosing LLM Judges across Deployment Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09229)
*Wenqi Li,Bin Liu,Mindi Ruan,Chuanbo Hu,Minglei Yin,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件准确率剖析（CAP），一种事后诊断框架，将成对LLM评判器的准确率分解为八个条件（内容敏感性、鲁棒性、推理质量），揭示聚合准确率掩盖的部署条件差异，实验表明条件级剖析比单一准确率更利于评判器选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-judge评估常用单一准确率汇总，这掩盖了评判器在不同部署条件下的成功或失效模式，亟需更细粒度的诊断方法。

Method: 提出条件准确率剖析（CAP），将成对评判准确率分解为内容敏感性、鲁棒性、推理质量三大类共八个条件。CAP与基准无关：可直接使用基准标注、通过任务子集代理近似、或通过受控增强生成扰动对。在七个LLM评判器、六个成对评判基准（含自建JudgeEva-Standard受控测试平台）上实例化。

Result: CAP揭示了聚合准确率隐藏的剖析差异：在JudgeEva的评判器无关Hard-Constructed子集上，对省略资格最敏感的两个评判器在七个中总准确率排名倒数三名，说明省略敏感性无法由聚合准确率预测。跨基准来看，位置鲁棒性的秩稳定性最强（平均Spearman ρ=0.87），但在JudgeBench-Pro对抗压力下最脆弱，共享条件中平均准确率下降最大，但主要退化通道因评判器而异。

Conclusion: 条件级剖析比聚合准确率提供更可操作的依据，有助于根据具体部署需求选择LLM评判器。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge is now a standard tool for scalable evaluation, but judge performance is still often summarized by a single accuracy number. This aggregate view hides the deployment conditions under which a judge succeeds or fails. We introduce \textbf{Conditional Accuracy Profiling} (CAP), a post-hoc diagnostic framework that decomposes pairwise LLM-judge accuracy into eight conditions organized into content sensitivity, robustness, and rationale quality. CAP is benchmark-agnostic: it can be applied directly when a benchmark provides the required annotations, approximately through task-subset proxies, or through controlled augmentation when perturbation pairs can be generated. We instantiate CAP on seven LLM judges across six pairwise judging benchmarks, including \textsc{judgerEva-Standard}, a controlled testbed we created to support all eight conditions. CAP exposes profile differences hidden by aggregate accuracy: on \textsc{judgerEva}'s judge-independent Hard-Constructed subset, the two judges most sensitive to omitted qualifications rank in the bottom three of seven by overall accuracy, so omission sensitivity is not predicted by aggregate accuracy. Across benchmarks, Position Robustness shows the strongest rank stability (mean Spearman $\barρ{=}0.87$) but is itself fragile under JudgeBench-Pro adversarial stress, showing the largest mean accuracy drop among the shared conditions, though the dominant degradation channel varies by judge. Condition-level profiles provide a more actionable basis than aggregate accuracy for selecting LLM judges.

</details>


### [234] [Symmetry-Informed Causal Partial Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09230)
*Uzair Akbar,Zulfiqar Zaidi,Niki Kilbertus,Krikamol Muandet,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Partial identification (PI) entails estimating bounds on causal effects by encoding different assumptions on data generation as a constrained optimization problem. Such bounds can suffice to inform policy decisions even if the causal effect itself is not identifiable. Often vacuous in practice, practitioners seek to exhaustively encode domain knowledge as additional constraints to make the PI bounds more informative. We introduce known data symmetries -- invariance of the causal effect under certain data transformations -- as a new source of constraints to inform PI. We operationalize this as a shape constraint on the causal function, and via a change of measure against which PI is posed using simple data pre-processing. Both approaches are shown to sharpen bounds under two canonical PI models. This is shown both theoretically for the population case, and via experiments in the finite-sample case. More broadly, our framework establishes data symmetries as a natural, underutilized source of background knowledge for robust causal inference.

</details>


### [235] [An Informational Curse of Horizon in Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09247)
*John L. Zhou,Yuxuan Dong,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现目标条件策略学习中存在“信息诅咒的时界”，增加目标重标记时界会降低策略泛化和性能；通过实验解耦训练和测试时界，发现即使仅评估近邻子目标，行为克隆策略仍受训练时界依赖的性能退化，而强化学习目标可缓解。解释为条件互信息随时界下降，且长时界策略对状态而非目标更敏感。提出将短时界策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长时界策略，显著提升组合操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注时序差分备份中的偏差累积和优势估计噪声导致的“时界诅咒”，但忽略了目标重标记时界对目标条件策略学习泛化能力的信息论层面影响。论文旨在识别并量化这一“信息诅咒”，理解长时界目标重标记如何降低动作与后见之明重标记目标间的条件互信息，以及策略对目标与状态信息的敏感性转移。

Method: 使用预言规划器进行受控实验，解耦训练时采样的目标时界与测试时要求达到的时界；在仅评估近邻子目标序列的设定下，对比行为克隆(BC)和强化学习(RL)目标；测量策略输入雅可比以量化对目标与状态信息的敏感性；提出将短时界策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长时界策略。

Result: 实验表明：1) BC策略在近邻子目标评估下仍表现出严重的训练时界依赖性能退化，RL目标可缓解。2) 条件互信息随目标重标记时界增加而下降。3) BC和RL策略在长时界训练下均表现出从目标敏感向状态敏感的转移。4) 雅可比蒸馏显著提升长时界策略性能，尤其在组合操作任务中。

Conclusion: 目标重标记时界是离线数据学习通用策略的关键考量因素；长时界重标记虽增加数据多样性，但会降低动作与目标的信息关联，导致策略过度依赖状态而忽视目标；通过短时界策略雅可比蒸馏可有效缓解这一问题，为目标条件策略学习提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: The difficulty of learning goal-reaching policies is often attributed to a "curse of horizon" that manifests as bias accumulation in temporal-difference backups and noisy advantage estimates. In this work, we identify an additional informational curse of horizon in goal-conditioned policy learning, where increasing the goal relabeling horizon can significantly reduce policy generalization and performance. Through a series of controlled experiments with oracle planners, we decouple the goal horizons sampled during training from those that the policy is asked to reach at test time. Even when evaluated only on a sequence of nearby subgoals, goal-conditioned behavioral cloning (BC) policies suffer from severe, training horizon-dependent performance degradation that is mitigated by reinforcement learning (RL) objectives. We explain this phenomenon as a horizon-dependent decrease in the conditional mutual information between actions and hindsight-relabeled goals, and find empirically that both BC and RL policies trained on longer-horizon goals exhibit a shift in sensitivity from goal to state information, as measured by the policy's input Jacobians. Motivated by this observation, we find that distilling the input Jacobians of short-horizon policies into long-horizon policies yields significant performance gains, especially in combinatorial manipulation tasks. Taken together, our results highlight goal relabeling horizon as an important consideration when learning generalist policies from offline data.

</details>


### [236] [Efficient Best-of-N policy evaluation for inference-time alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09250)
*Jonas Schweisthal,Yuxin Wang,Athiya Deviyani,Stefan Feuerriegel,Dennis Frauen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需响应似然的 Best-of-N (BoN) 策略评估与选择框架，利用 BoN 的序统计量结构将密度比表示为可从样本估计的得分秩概率，开发了双稳健估计器 BoN-DR 并证明其有效性和效率，还给出了两种考虑估计不确定性的预算选择规则，在合成实验和 GSM8K 上验证了框架的准确性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线策略评估需要依赖响应似然的密度比，但在仅有样本访问权限的场景下这些似然不可得，导致 BoN 策略难以从日志数据中评估；且更大的采样预算可能放大评分函数误差并引发奖励过度优化，需要一种无需似然的评估方法和稳健的预算选择机制。

Method: 1. 利用 BoN 的序统计量结构，将所需密度比转化为仅依赖样本的得分秩概率。2. 提出双稳健估计器 BoN-DR，通过共享辅助样本池高效复用跨候选预算的样本。3. 建立即使奖励估计器设定错误时也成立的渐近推断，并证明估计器的效率。4. 推导两种预算选择规则：最大化估计策略值，以及最大化相对参考策略改进的下置信界以提供无损保证。

Result: 在合成实验和 GSM8K 基准（多个参考模型和奖励模型）上，BoN-DR 能准确估计 BoN 策略值，且提出的选择规则能有效挑选采样预算，平衡性能提升与过度优化风险。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了仅有样本访问时 BoN 策略评估的核心难题，提供了理论保证的双稳健估计器和具有无损保证的预算选择规则，实验验证了其在实际语言模型对齐任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) is a common inference-time alignment method that selects the highest-scoring response among N samples from a reference model. Evaluating BoN policies from logged data is challenging under sample-only access because standard off-policy estimators require density ratios that depend on unavailable response likelihoods. In this paper, we propose a sample-only framework for evaluating and selecting BoN policies without access to these likelihoods. We show that the order-statistic structure of BoN allows the required density ratios to be expressed through score-rank probabilities that are estimable from samples alone. We then develop a doubly robust estimator of the BoN policy value (BoN-DR) that efficiently reuses a shared auxiliary sample pool across candidate budgets. We establish valid asymptotic inference even under reward estimator misspecification and prove the efficiency of our BoN-DR estimator. Since larger budgets can amplify errors in the score function and lead to reward overoptimization, we derive two selection rules: (i) maximizing the estimated policy value and (ii) maximizing a lower confidence bound on the improvement over the reference policy, which accounts for estimation uncertainty and provides a no-harm guarantee. Across synthetic experiments and GSM8K with multiple reference and reward models, our framework accurately estimates BoN policy values and selects effective sampling budgets.

</details>


### [237] [The Symbol of the Surrogate: Measuring Numerical Provenance in Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09255)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了神经PDE代理模型在标准基准测试中可能仅是在模仿数值求解器的离散化误差，而非学习真实的物理演化。作者提出了一种基于傅里叶符号的诊断方法，能够直接探测代理模型学习算子的数值来源。实验表明，代理模型能以99.8%以上的准确度复现训练求解器的幅度和相位误差，这种现象在线性对流、非局部傅里叶神经算子及非线性Burgers方程中均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经PDE代理模型的训练和评估均依赖于同一数值求解器生成的数据，标准基准无法区分模型是学习了真实的物理演化还是仅仅模仿了求解器的离散化误差（耗散和色散误差）。这导致模型在测试集上表现良好并不代表其对真实PDE动力学的保真度。

Method: 1. 提出经验傅里叶符号诊断法：用单个傅里叶模式探测训练好的代理模型线性化的一步算子，并与精确演化算子和训练求解器算子对比。2. 采用双求解器实验设计：在相同网络架构上分别用具有正交耗散/色散特征的两种数值格式生成数据训练，通过对比两个模型学习到的算子差异，消除架构固有的谱偏置影响。3. 在线性对流方程、非局部傅里叶神经算子（FNO）和非线性Burgers方程上进行验证。

Result: 线性对流实验中，代理模型精确复现了训练求解器的幅度误差（耗散）和相位误差（色散），双求解器差异达到了理论预测的完全模仿上限的99.8%以上。该现象在FNO架构中同样存在，且在非线性Burgers方程的算子层面也被观测到。

Conclusion: 仅与求解器生成的测试数据吻合，不足以证明神经代理模型对真实PDE演化的保真度。傅里叶符号测量提供了一种直接诊断代理模型数值来源的工具，能够定量区分物理保真度与数值模仿。

Abstract: Neural PDE surrogates are trained on numerical solver outputs that contain both physical evolution and solver-specific discretization errors. Because surrogates are also evaluated against held-out trajectories from the same solver, standard benchmarks cannot distinguish fidelity to the exact evolution from imitation of the numerical scheme. We introduce an empirical Fourier-symbol diagnostic that probes a trained surrogate's linearized one-step operator with individual Fourier modes and compares it with both exact-evolution and training-scheme references. To address architectural spectral bias, we train identical networks on schemes with orthogonal dissipative and dispersive signatures and compare their learned operators. In linear advection, the learned surrogates reproduce the training schemes' amplitude and phase errors, with the twin-scheme difference reaching more than 99.8\% of the analytically predicted full-imitation ceiling. The same behavior occurs for a non-local Fourier neural operator and at the operator level for nonlinear Burgers dynamics. These results show that agreement with solver-generated test data does not by itself establish fidelity to the exact evolution. Fourier-symbol measurements provide a direct diagnostic of numerical provenance.

</details>


### [238] [Evaluating Trajectory Features for Routing Final-Layer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09272)
*Yupeng Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估注意力路由中隐藏状态外推误差、曲率和误差变化作为预测注意力价值信号的效用，实验表明这些轨迹摘要在因果调用配额下无显著增益，固定投影基线反而更优，揭示了轨迹摘要增量价值的局限并区分了分配质量与推理收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究注意力路由需要预测当前前缀注意力价值的信号，现有方法依赖不确定性、一步位移、位置和状态投影，作者想探究隐藏状态外推误差、曲率和误差变化是否能提供额外预测价值。

Method: 在冻结的 SmolLM3-3B-Base 和 Qwen3.5-4B-Base 检查点上，对最后一层注意力进行成对执行，获得符号化的下一 token 损失差异；在 100 本留出的 PG-19 书籍上，以相同的因果 20% 调用配额测试效用监督路由器；预设六种比较并进行家族误差校正。

Result: 六项预设比较均未显示正增益；Qwen3.5 中参数匹配的固定投影控制比轨迹路由器降低 NLL 0.00356 nats/token（95% CI 0.00218–0.00487）；次要结果依赖于移除的操作、特征位置和评分范围；冻结阈值在长范围显著漂移；实际选中查询执行带来轻微长序列延迟降低但 NLL 上升，学习型路由器在缓存续写中仍更慢。

Conclusion: 研究确定了轨迹摘要增量价值的上限，并将分配质量与测量到的推理收益区分开来。

Abstract: Attention routing requires a signal that predicts the value of attention on the current prefix. We evaluate whether hidden-state extrapolation error, curvature and error change improve this prediction beyond uncertainty, one-step displacement, position and state projections. Paired executions of the final attention layer supply signed next-token loss differences in frozen SmolLM3-3B-Base and Qwen3.5-4B-Base checkpoints. Utility-supervised routers are tested on 100 held-out PG-19 books at an identical causal 20 percent invocation quota. None of six prespecified comparisons shows a positive gain after familywise correction. In Qwen3.5, a parameter-matched fixed-projection control lowers NLL by 0.00356 nats/token relative to the trajectory router (95 percent interval 0.00218 to 0.00487). Secondary results depend on the operation removed, feature location and scoring horizon; frozen thresholds also drift substantially at longer horizons. Actual selected-query execution yields small long-sequence latency reductions with increased NLL, while learned routers remain slower during cached continuation. The study identifies limits on the incremental value of these trajectory summaries and separates allocation quality from measured inference benefit.

</details>


### [239] [Twist Flow for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09281)
*Shiqin Zeng,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 joint twist-flow 方法，通过增强流匹配学习从 (z_x, y) 到 (x, z_y) 的连续传输，解决贝叶斯逆问题中后验采样的多模态保持和观测一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接条件生成模型虽引入潜在噪声建模歧义，但配对训练仍倾向于学习近似确定性映射，导致后验变异性不足、多模态支撑丢失、模式失真及人为过渡。

Method: 提出联合扭曲流，构建增强源状态 (z_x, y) 到增强目标状态 (x, z_y) 的流匹配。其中 z_x 为后验采样的高斯参考坐标，z_y 为观测侧高斯似然坐标（基于归一化观测残差），旨在耦合生成样本与观测一致性，抑制似然不一致变异，保留弱约束方向的变异性。

Result: 在低维逆问题、图像复原和地震地下速度模型反演实验中，joint twist-flow 优于直接条件流基线，更好地保持多模态后验支撑，同时增加后验变异性并维持观测一致性。

Conclusion: joint twist-flow 通过引入观测侧潜变量 z_y 并联合建模传输，有效缓解了后验采样中的欠覆盖和模式失真问题，为贝叶斯逆问题提供了更可靠的后验采样方法。

Abstract: In Bayesian inverse problems, posterior sampling requires generating samples that are consistent with given observations while capturing the range of plausible solutions. Direct conditional generative models introduce latent noise to model this ambiguity, but paired inverse-problem training can still encourage an almost deterministic map from the observation to the target. As a result, generated samples may be observation-consistent while under-representing posterior variability, especially when the posterior is multimodal, leading to undercoverage, mode distortion, or artificial transitions between distinct feasible solutions. We propose joint twist-flow, an augmented flow-matching formulation that learns a continuous transport from the augmented source state $(z_x, y)$ to the augmented terminal state $(x, z_y)$. Here x is the target variable, $y$ is the observation, $z_x$ is the Gaussian reference coordinate for posterior sampling, and $z_y$ is a Gaussian likelihood-side coordinate associated with the observation branch. Under a Gaussian observation model, $z_y$ is motivated by the normalized observation residual associated with observation compatibility. Its role is not to replace uncertainty in $x$, but to couple generated samples of x to observation consistency, helping reduce likelihood-inconsistent variation while preserving variability in weakly constrained directions. We validate the method on low-dimensional inverse problems with reference posterior samples, where joint twist-flow better preserves multimodal posterior support than a direct conditional-flow baseline. We further evaluate the method on image restoration and seismic subsurface velocity-model inversion, showing increased posterior variability while maintaining observation consistency.

</details>


### [240] [Self-attention summary networks for subsurface velocity-model building from common-image gathers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09282)
*Shiqin Zeng,Yunlin Zeng,Abhinav Prakash Gahlot,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将高维3D CIG体压缩为紧凑嵌入，结合流匹配模型进行概率地下速度反演，提高了后验推断精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成像工作流中，共焦点聚集（CIGs）仅作为诊断工具，未充分利用其包含的速度模型误差信息（反射体聚焦和残余动校正）。需要一种方法将高维CIG数据高效编码为条件嵌入，用于概率速度反演。

Method: 设计多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将3D CIG体映射为紧凑条件嵌入，保留偏移依赖的运动学结构和空间相干性，减少背景速度不匹配引起的变异性；基于嵌入，使用流匹配模型从高斯源分布学习到合理速度场的后验分布的传输映射。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比直接以原始CIG为条件，摘要网络显著改善后验速度推断；多尺度注意力设计增强了对背景模型不匹配的鲁棒性，实现更准确的后验重构和更低的预测不确定性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过学习CIG的紧凑表示并结合生成式流匹配，实现了高效、鲁棒的概率地下速度反演，为地震成像中的不确定性量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Common-image gathers (CIGs) contain physically meaningful information about velocity-model errors through reflector focusing and residual moveout, but in conventional imaging workflows they are typically used only as diagnostic tools. In this work, we propose a multiscale self-attention summary network that maps high-dimensional 3D CIG volumes into compact conditioning embeddings for probabilistic subsurface velocity inversion. These learned embeddings preserve offset-dependent kinematic structure and spatial coherence while reducing variability caused by background-velocity mismatch. Conditioned on these summary embeddings, a flow-matching model learns a transport from a Gaussian source distribution to the posterior distribution of plausible velocity fields. Numerical experiments show that, compared with direct conditioning on raw CIGs, the proposed summary network improves posterior velocity inference. In particular, the multiscale attention design provides greater robustness to background-model mismatch, yielding more accurate posterior reconstructions and lower predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [241] [LeCuration: A Tiny World Model as a Data Curation Multi-Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09285)
*Mayank Sengupta,Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeCuration，一个小型世界模型，用于物理AI数据的筛选和策展，基于LeWorldModel和DiT解码器，在CS:GO游戏数据上进行定性验证，嵌入可用于异常检测和聚类，自回归预测可检查动作-状态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI应用常在封闭物理世界中运行，需要更好地组织、筛选和策展数据，针对各数据集独特的设定和物理定律提出新方法。

Method: 训练LeCuration模型，使用LeWorldModel (LeWM)作为潜在编码器和预测器，添加扩散Transformer (DiT)解码器为自回归游戏推演生成视觉，利用嵌入进行异常检测和聚类，自回归预测游戏状态以定性检查动作-状态一致性。

Result: 在CS:GO游戏数据上进行定性概念验证，发现嵌入可作为异常检测信号和基于内容的聚类启发式，自回归预测允许定性检查动作-状态一致性。未报告定量策展指标或下游训练结果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于世界模型的数据策展方法，初步验证了其在异常检测、聚类和动作-状态一致性检查上的潜力，下一步需进行定量评估和下游任务验证。

Abstract: Many applications of physical AI run within finite or closed physical worlds with a limited set of physical laws governing object behavior. Examples include robots working in a warehouse and agents moving around in a video game. In order to better organize, filter, and curate data for physical AI applications, we propose a new approach centered on the unique settings and physical laws of individual datasets. We train LeCuration, a small world model intended to serve as a data curation tool for a separate, larger downstream model. To build this model, we choose LeWorldModel (LeWM)as our latent encoder and predictor, adding a diffusion transformer (DiT) decoder to add visuals to autoregressive gameplay rollout. We find that the embeddings of this model can be used as an anomaly detection signal and as a content-based clustering heuristic, and that auto-regressively predicting the game state with this model allows us to qualitatively check for action-state consistency. This paper presents a qualitative, proof-of-concept case study on CS:GO gameplay data; we do not yet report quantitative curation metrics or downstream training results, which we identify as the key next step.

</details>


### [242] [Node-level Graph Neural Architecture Search Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09297)
*Lintao Yanga,Sirui Lia,Yaqing Wang,Pietro Liò,Xu Shen,Baisong Liu,Chengbin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In recent years, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and architecture search frameworks have gained extensive application in non-Euclidean data processing, attributable to their superior capacity in managing unstructured data. Nevertheless, traditional approaches typically apply uniform convolution operations to all nodes, regardless of their varying structural and feature characteristics, which can undermine model performance and result in over-smoothing issues as the number of layers increases. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose a \textbf{N}ode-Level \textbf{G}raph \textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}rchitecture \textbf{S}earch (N-GNAS) algorithm. It can automatically choose an appropriate network architecture for each subset of nodes when updating node features. N-GNAS also introduces a contrastive learning loss to separate sample features from different categories and vice versa. In experiments conducted on eight datasets for node and graph classification, our methodology outperforms current leading GNAS techniques and traditional human-designed GNNs. For example, it achieves an accuracy rate of 78.26\% on the CiteSeer dataset.

</details>


### [243] [Kuration SDK: Addressing the Virtual2Real Gap via Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09305)
*Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Mayank Sengupta,Tejal Bedmutha,Siri Reddy,Sahiti Dharmavaram,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现动作条件世界模型的定性可玩性与传统指标（FVD、LPIPS、JEDi）不相符，命名为'Virtual2Real gap'。提出通过精选原始游戏数据并测量诊断属性来弥合差距，开源了Kuration SDK工具包，并成功用其诊断出两个在相同数据上训练、指标相近但行为迥异的模型的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准从视觉相似度指标向动作语义和物理接地指标演进，但对于领域和任务无关的动作条件世界模型训练，现有基准提供的信号有限。作者在CounterStrike游戏数据上验证了定性可玩性与FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标不匹配，揭示了Virtual2Real gap的存在。

Method: 在CounterStrike游戏数据上训练和评估扩散世界模型；提出数据精选策略并开发通用物理AI数据精选工具包Kuration SDK；利用SDK对原始游戏数据进行精选和诊断属性测量，分析两个在相同地图、动作和状态分布上训练、LPIPS和FVD分数相近但行为差异巨大的模型的根因。

Result: 确认了Virtual2Real gap：定性可玩性与FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标不相符。Kuration SDK成功揭示了两个模型在相同数据分布下训练、指标相近但实际游玩行为截然不同的根本原因，证明了数据精选和诊断测量比单纯依赖基准指标更有效。

Conclusion: Kuration SDK作为开源的物理AI数据精选工具包，能够在特定数据集中发现Virtual2Real gap的根因，并加速样本高效训练数据集的开发。数据精选和诊断属性测量提供了比不可靠基准更稳健的信号，可在训练开始前就弥合虚实差距。

Abstract: Benchmarks for measuring the quality of action-conditioned world models are still evolving and shifting away from visual similarity-based metrics to action-semantic and physically-grounded metrics. However, for domain and task-agnostic action-conditioned world model training, existing benchmarks provide a limited signal. By training and evaluating diffusion world models on CounterStrike gameplay data, we confirm that qualitative playability does not correspond with metrics such as FVD, LPIPS, and JEDi. We term this the Virtual2Real gap. We posit that, in lieu of reliable benchmarks, curating raw gameplay data and measuring a variety of diagnostic properties provides a more robust signal to bridge the gap, before the training even begins. We present several curation strategies and a general-purpose kit for physical AI data curation called Kuration SDK, which is being open-sourced with this paper. The SDK was instrumental in uncovering the root cause of the virtual2real gap in a specific case: why two world models trained on identical gameplay map, action and state distribution, behaved very differently when played in spite of having very similar LPIPS and FVD scores. Thus, Kuration SDK has the potential to uncover the root causes of Virtual2Real gap in specific datasets and accelerate development of sample-efficient training datasets.

</details>


### [244] [MovieSTAGE: Scene, Transition, and Global Encoding for Movie-fMRI ADHD Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09306)
*Boseong Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Naturalistic movie-fMRI provides a shared, temporally structured probe of brain dynamics, yet predictive models commonly rely on whole-run functional connectivity (FC) or temporally generic representations that are not aligned with narrative events. We introduce MovieSTAGE (Scene, Transition, and Global Encoding), a multiscale framework that combines hypergraph-structured FC-profile organization within scenes, unsigned FC-profile differences across adjacent scenes, and whole-movie FC. We evaluated 260 participants from the CMI-HBN Despicable Me cohort on case-control, ADHD-subtype, and three-class classification using 10 repetitions of stratified five-fold cross-validation, complete out-of-fold (OOF) predictions, and paired subject-cluster bootstrap and permutation tests. MovieSTAGE achieved AUROCs of 0.69, 0.73, and 0.75 and balanced accuracies of 67.6%, 69.8%, and 58.3%, respectively, yielding the highest mean point estimates among the evaluated methods. On the three-class task, the full model outperformed all two-branch variants, the HGNN scene encoder outperformed MLP, GAT, and BNT alternatives under matched settings, and the human-annotated partition outperformed duration-matched random and fixed-count GSBS controls. These controlled results support incremental predictive value from event-aligned scene and transition representations when combined with whole-movie FC in this cohort. Post-hoc model-derived analyses generated network-level hypotheses involving frontoparietal and default-mode systems.

</details>


### [245] [Denoising Blocks, Not Tokens: Efficient Compressed Continuous Diffusion with Branching Token Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09311)
*Xinsong Feng,Peng Du,Zhizhuo Yang,Daniel M. Bikel,Jiayun Wang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Branching Latent Diffusion (BLD)，通过将1024个token压缩为64个潜在块（16倍压缩），结合分支token实现（每个潜在由局部自回归分支并行解码），实现了长序列生成的高效率。实验显示BLD较基线减少80倍FLOPs，吞吐率提升6倍以上，延迟降低4倍以上，在保持局部流畅度和多样性的同时，长程连贯性仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）虽通过并行迭代细化有望超越自回归（AR）解码吞吐率，但现有DLM仍维持每token一个生成状态，每步去噪需处理与输出序列等长的状态序列，限制了并行生成的吞吐增益。连续DLM提供额外自由度：单一连续状态可表示多token，使扩散可在更短的潜在序列上操作。

Method: BLD包含两大核心创新：1) 潜在压缩：将1024-token序列压缩为64个块潜在（16倍压缩）；2) 分支token实现：每个潜在由一个局部AR分支解码，所有分支并行运行。因强压缩使联合潜在生成困难，BLD采用分组生成策略，每组潜在条件于已生成的先前潜在。

Result: 端到端评估显示：相同GPU上，BLD较同规模ELF-L基线减少80倍以上生成FLOPs，吞吐率提升6倍以上；较AR基线实现6倍以上吞吐率提升和4倍以上延迟降低。尽管压缩显著，BLD仍保持具有竞争力的局部流畅度和多样性，但长程连贯性仍具挑战。

Conclusion: BLD证明将扩散从token级状态转移到压缩潜在序列，能大幅提升长序列生成效率，为高效文本生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text through iterative parallel refinement, offering the potential for higher throughput than autoregressive (AR) decoding. However, most DLMs still maintain one generative state per token, so every denoising step processes a state sequence as long as the output sequence, limiting the throughput gains from parallel generation. Continuous DLMs provide an additional degree of freedom: a single continuous state can represent multiple tokens, allowing diffusion to operate on a much shorter latent sequence. We introduce \emph{Branching Latent Diffusion (BLD)}, which exploits this flexibility by compressing a 1024-token sequence into only 64 block latents, a $16\times$ reduction. BLD combines latent compression with \emph{branching token realization}, where each latent is decoded by a local AR branch and all branches run in parallel. Because strong compression makes joint latent generation difficult, BLD generates the latents in groups, conditioning each group on previously generated latents. In end-to-end evaluation on the same GPU, BLD reduces generation FLOPs by more than $80\times$ and increases throughput by more than $6\times$ relative to the similarly sized ELF-L baseline. Compared with the AR baseline, BLD achieves more than $6\times$ higher throughput and more than $4\times$ lower latency. Despite the compression, BLD maintains competitive local fluency and diversity, although long-range coherence remains challenging. Overall, BLD shows that moving diffusion from token-level states to compressed latent sequences can substantially improve the efficiency of long-sequence generation.

</details>


### [246] [Benign Overfitting under Heterogeneous Input Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09340)
*Houzhen Liu,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究异构输入融合下的良性过拟合现象，分析最小范数线性插值在异构高斯设计下的回归与分类任务。主要发现：回归任务中存在全谱协方差证书，其渐近性质与截断阈值无关且被所有与边际分布一致的正半定联合协方差保留；但该保护有限，边际良性不保证融合良性。分类任务中良性取决于预测信号与干扰污染的平衡，融合可使两者反向变化，所有边际到联合的良性/非良性模式均可实现。同一融合输入对回归和分类的影响可能定性不同。结论：异构融合下的良性过拟合由输入交互产生的联合信号与谱几何决定，而非仅由边际良性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 良性过拟合在单一高维输入下已被广泛研究，但在异构输入融合（多个统计相关的输入块融合）场景下的行为尚未充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，理解融合如何改变良性过拟合的性质。

Method: 采用最小范数线性插值估计器，在异构高斯设计下分析两个统计相关输入块及其融合。固定底层总体任务，比较边际分布与联合分布的良性过拟合性质。针对回归任务，构造全谱协方差证书并证明其在所有与边际一致的正半定联合协方差下保持；针对一稀疏高斯分类，表征良性条件为预测信号存活与干扰污染的平衡。

Result: 回归：全谱协方差证书渐近性质与截断阈值无关，被所有合法联合协方差保留，保护尖锐但有限——边际良性下融合可能有害。分类：良性由信号存活与干扰平衡决定，融合可使两者反向变化，所有边际到联合良性/非良性模式均可达成。同一融合输入对回归与分类影响可能定性不同。

Conclusion: 异构融合下的良性过拟合由输入交互产生的联合信号结构与谱几何决定，而非仅取决于边际分布的良性性质。融合可能改善或损害良性过拟合，且对回归与分类的影响机制不同。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is extensively studied when learning from a single high-dimensional input, but its behavior under heterogeneous input fusion remains largely unexplored. We study this question for minimum-norm linear interpolation under a heterogeneous Gaussian design, comparing two statistically dependent input blocks with their fusion while holding the underlying population task fixed. For regression, we identify a full-spectrum covariance certificate whose asymptotic status is independent of the cutoff threshold and prove that it is preserved by every positive-semidefinite joint covariance consistent with the two marginals. This protection is sharp, yet it does not extend to all benign regression problems: outside the certified regime, two benign marginals can have a harmful fusion. For one-sparse Gaussian classification, benignity in the regular regime is characterized by the balance between surviving predictive signal and nuisance contamination. Fusion can move these two quantities in opposite directions, and within this model class every marginal-to-joint benign/non-benign pattern is attainable. We further show that the same fused input can have qualitatively different effects on regression and classification. These results establish that benign overfitting under heterogeneous fusion is determined by the joint signal and spectral geometry created by input interaction, rather than by marginal benignity alone.

</details>


### [247] [Shared Low-rank Basis Factorization for Data-free Mixture-of-Experts Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09342)
*Tianxiao Cao,Jiahe Shao,Yuning Qiu,Kyohei Atarashi,Hisashi Kashima,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了三类 MoE 压缩方法的结构误差界，指出专家剪枝和专家合并存在与路由及专家异质性相关的非消失误差，而权重重构可避免这些结构成本。基于此提出 Shared Low-rank Basis Factorization (SLBF)——一种无需数据的权重重构方法，利用专家间共享的秩-k 基实现更丰富的跨专家参数共享、更快收敛和更低重构误差，并通过事后 gauge fixing 消除冗余参数。在 16B–122B 参数规模的五种 MoE 架构上，SLBF 持续优于三类压缩方法的现有代表作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 大模型通过稀疏路由将容量与计算解耦，但巨大的参数量带来存储和部署挑战。现有压缩方法分为专家剪枝、专家合并、权重重构三类，缺乏统一的理论分析来揭示其结构误差本质，导致难以指导更优压缩算法的设计。

Method: 1. 理论分析：推导三类压缩方法的结构误差界，证明剪枝与合并的误差与路由分布及专家异质性耦合，无法随规模消失；权重重构保留专家结构与路由，避免该结构成本。2. 算法设计：提出 SLBF，为所有专家学习共享的低秩基矩阵，仅保留每专家的小型系数矩阵，实现跨专家参数共享；引入 gauge fixing 消除基与系数间的尺度/旋转冗余，进一步压缩参数且不损失表达能力。全过程无需训练数据。

Result: 在 16B–122B 参数的五种主流 MoE 架构（如 Mixtral, DeepSeek-MoE 等）上，SLBF 在相同压缩率下持续获得更低困惑度/更高下游任务精度，显著优于代表性剪枝、合并及重构基线；消融实验验证共享基与 gauge fixing 的关键作用。

Conclusion: 结构误差分析揭示了权重重构在理论上的优势；SLBF 通过共享低秩基和 gauge fixing 将该优势转化为实用的高压缩比、免数据、即插即用的压缩方案，为大规模 MoE 模型的高效部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models decouple capacity from compute through sparse routing, but their large parameter count creates storage and serving challenges. We analyze three MoE compression families: expert pruning, expert merging, and weight reconstruction, and derive structural error bounds showing that pruning and merging can incur non-vanishing errors tied to routing and expert heterogeneity. In contrast, weight reconstruction avoids these structural costs by preserving expert structure and routing. Motivated by the analysis, we propose Shared Low-rank Basis Factorization (SLBF), a data-free weight reconstruction method that uses rank-$k$ bases shared among experts, enabling richer cross-expert sharing, faster convergence, and lower reconstruction error. A post-hoc gauge fixing removes redundant parameters at no representational cost. Across five MoE architectures spanning 16B to 122B parameters, SLBF consistently outperforms methods from all three compression families.

</details>


### [248] [Multimodal LLMs Can Learn to Read Brain Signals: A Vision--Language Model for Unified Multi-Task EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09355)
*Parastoo Azizeddin,Omid Sharafi,Maryam M. Shanechi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BraVista 将多通道 EEG 信号编码为结构化图像，利用指令条件视觉语言模型实现跨任务、跨受试者、跨记录条件的多任务 EEG 解码，无需大规模 EEG 专用预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 基础模型虽提升解码性能，但如何将神经信号有效接入这些模型以实现跨数据集多任务学习仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出 BraVista 框架：将多通道 EEG 编码为结构化图像，对通用域视觉语言模型进行继续后训练，利用其视觉和语言先验适应神经信号，通过指令条件实现多任务学习，无需单独的大规模 EEG 专用预训练阶段。

Result: 在睡眠分期、情绪识别、认知负荷分类、异常 EEG 检测四个数据集上表现强劲；EEG 到图像的表示选择对性能至关重要；受控扰动实验显示随噪声增加性能逐渐下降，表明模型依赖 EEG 相关信息而非表面视觉模式。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉表示是连接神经信号与通用域基础模型的有效且可扩展接口，可实现统一的多任务 EEG 解码。

Abstract: Learning EEG representations that generalize across cognitive tasks, subjects, and recording conditions remains a key challenge in electroencephalography (EEG) decoding. Recent advances in foundation models have improved EEG decoding performance, yet a fundamental open question remains: how to effectively interface neural signals with these models to enable multi-task learning across datasets. To investigate this question, we introduce BraVista, a visual-language framework that encodes multichannel EEG signals as structured images and enables multi-task learning through instruction-conditioned vision-language models (VLMs). Our approach relies on continued post-training of a general-domain VLM, leveraging its visual and linguistic priors to adapt to neural signals without a separate large-scale EEG-specific pretraining stage. We evaluate BraVista on four datasets spanning sleep staging, emotion recognition, cognitive workload classification, and abnormal EEG detection, showing strong performance across these tasks. Further analyses show that the choice of EEG-to-image representation is critical to performance. Moreover, through controlled perturbations of the EEG signal, we observe a gradual performance degradation under increasing noise, suggesting that the model relies on EEG-relevant information rather than superficial visual patterns. Together, these findings establish structured visual representations as an effective and scalable interface between neural signals and general-domain foundation models for unified multi-task EEG decoding.

</details>


### [249] [Global Exponential Convergence of Two-Layer Linear Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09356)
*Stephen Y Zhang,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了宽两层线性网络在平滑 Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) 预测器损失下，具有显式线性收敛速率的全局指数收敛。通过均场分析，梯度流在因子空间中精确闭合为有限维 Bures 流，利用守恒律建立谱下界，允许奇异初始化。初始协方差为 σ²Id 时，损失以至少 4σ²κ 的线性速率收敛至全局最小值（κ 为 PL 常数）。该速率在有限宽度采样下稳定，因子梯度下降在显式步长区间内全局收敛。论证逐层扩展至深度线性 ResNets（需残差路径界），动量情况下训练动力学闭合于提升的相位协方差，线性收敛需显式能量与阻尼条件。数值实验验证协方差几何与预测速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为宽神经网络的训练动力学提供严格的均场层面收敛分析，获得显式的线性收敛速率及允许奇异初始化的谱条件，并将结果扩展至深度线性残差网络和重球动量等更实用的设置，填补理论与实践之间的空白。

Method: 1. 将因子空间梯度流精确表示为神经元律协方差的有限维 Bures 浨，预测器动力学由隐层协方差块预条件。2. 利用均场守恒律，在谱支撑间隙条件下建立隐层预条件块的均匀谱下界，该条件包含正定初始化并允许奇异初始化。3. 通过有限宽度采样稳定性分析，给出依赖光滑度、初始损失和守恒谱边界的显式梯度下降步长区间。4. 逐层扩展论证至深度线性 ResNets，施加残差路径界。5. 对于重球动量，引入提升的相位协方差（位置与速度），导出能量与阻尼的显式可容许窗口。

Result: 在初始协方差 Σ₀ = σ²Id 下，损失以线性速率 ≥ 4σ²κ 收敛至全局最小值。该速率在有限宽度下稳定。因子梯度下降在显式步长区间内全局收敛。深度线性 ResNets 在残差路径界下实现全局收敛。重球动量下，线性收敛需满足能量与阻尼的显式条件；对于双尺度白噪声初始化，当位置尺度足够大时，可容许阻尼区间非空。数值实验展示协方差几何结构，并对比预测与观测收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文建立了宽线性网络在 PL 损失下的严格均场线性收敛理论，给出显式速率与初始化条件，证明了有限宽度稳定性，并成功扩展至深度残差架构和动量方法，为理解宽网络训练动力学提供了坚实的数学框架。

Abstract: We prove global exponential (linear) convergence with an explicit rate in the rich scaling for wide two-layer linear networks trained with smooth Polyak-Lojasiewicz predictor losses. Gradient flow in the factors closes exactly in terms of a finite-dimensional Bures flow of the neuron law covariance, in which the predictor dynamics are preconditioned by hidden covariance blocks. Mean-field conservation laws provide uniform spectral lower bounds on the hidden preconditioning blocks when the initial covariance satisfies a spectral support gap condition. This condition encompasses positive definiteness while still allowing for singular initializations. For an initial covariance $Σ_0 = σ^2 \mathrm{Id}$, the loss converges to the global minimum with linear rate at least $4σ^2κ$, where $κ$ is the PL constant. We establish stability of this rate under finite-width sampling, as well as global convergence of factor gradient descent for an explicit stepsize interval depending on smoothness, the initial loss, and conserved spectral margins. Our argument extends layerwise to deep linear ResNets, subject to a residual-path bound. In the case of heavy-ball momentum, training dynamics close instead over positions and velocities in terms of a lifted phase covariance. Linear convergence holds under an explicit condition on the energy and damping, specifying a window of admissible dampings. For two-scale white initializations, this interval is nonempty for sufficiently large position scales, with a fixed initial loss gap and velocity covariance. Numerical experiments illustrate the covariance geometry and compare the predicted and observed rates.

</details>


### [250] [From Retrieval to Customer Context: Evaluating Frontier-Model Systems for Voice-of-Customer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09375)
*Raviraja G,Viraj Bagal,Prabhath Chellingi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出「客户语境图谱」这一统一的客户与业务语境模型，通过类型化关系连接客户对象、运营对象和分析/行动对象，使智能体能深入调查客户反馈的「内容、原因、影响人、后续行动、责任人及解决情况」。在 9,432 条公开 Cursor 反馈记录上，基于该图谱的智能体以 0.961 综合得分显著优于 Deep Research Agent (0.710) 与 Agentic RAG (0.651)，尤其在分析深度维度 (0.967 vs 0.642) 优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地使用前沿语言模型分析客户反馈，但回答质量取决于反馈如何被组织和提供。现有方法缺乏统一模型将客户反馈与业务运营、分析行动关联，导致无法深入追问「为什么、谁受影响、后续行动、责任归属、是否解决」等关键问题。

Method: 1) 定义客户语境图谱：用类型化关系连接三类对象——客户对象(反馈、对话、用户、账户)、运营对象(工单、支持代理、商机、竞品)、分析/行动对象(分类概念、证据、洞察、工作项、结果)。2) 从公开 Cursor 反馈填充图谱(9,432 条记录)。3) 对比三种方法：Agentic RAG、Deep Research Agent、基于图谱的智能体。4) 用 30 个真实产品/事件/对比/元数据问题评测。5) 采用联合评分量表，从回答质量(覆盖度、组织性)、分析深度(具体性、分解度)、证据质量(引用支持、可追溯性)三维等权评分，并在 28 题上校准量表，抽样核验引用记录与主张的一致性。

Result: 基于图谱的智能体综合得分 0.961，显著高于 Deep Research Agent (0.710) 与 Agentic RAG (0.651)。在 30 道题中 27 题击败 Deep Research Agent (符号检验 p < 10^-5)，26 题严格最优。最大优势在于分析深度 (0.967 vs 0.642)，体现为更具体、层次化的发现、量化主题与可追溯证据。

Conclusion: 客户语境图谱提供了一种统一架构，能将零散反馈与业务运营、分析行动串联，使智能体具备端到端的调查能力。该架构不依赖特定反馈源，可推广至任意类型反馈，为企业级客户洞察提供了更高质量、可追溯、可量化的分析范式。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly use frontier language models to analyze customer feedback, but answer quality also depends on how that feedback is organized and made available. We define a \emph{customer context graph} as a unified model of customer and business context. Typed relationships connect customer objects (feedback, conversations, users, and accounts), operational objects (tickets, support agents, opportunities, and competitors), and analytical or action objects (taxonomy concepts, evidence, insights, work items, and outcomes). This lets an agent investigate not only what customers say, but why, who is affected, what action followed, who owns it, and whether it was resolved. For this experiment, the graph is populated from public Cursor feedback; the same architecture can support any type of feedback source. We compare Agentic RAG, a Deep Research Agent, and a Customer Context Graph-backed Agent on the same 9,432 public Cursor feedback records using 30 realistic product, incident, comparison, and metadata questions. Without exhaustive ground truth, we jointly score responses on answer quality (coverage and organization), analytical depth (specificity and decomposition), and evidence quality (citation support and traceability), using a comparative rubric calibrated on 28 of the 30 questions. We sample cited records against their claims and weight the three dimensions equally. Under this aligned rubric, the Customer Context Graph-backed Agent scores 0.961 overall, versus 0.710 for the Deep Research Agent and 0.651 for Agentic RAG, and leads the Deep Research Agent on 27 of 30 paired questions (sign-test p < 10^(-5); strictly best on 26 of 30). Its largest advantage is analytical depth (0.967 versus 0.642), reflecting more specific, hierarchically developed findings with quantified themes and traceable evidence...

</details>


### [251] [Noise, Denoise, Correct: MCMC Posterior Sampling with Diffusion Priors in Three Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09407)
*So Takao,Gregory David Bellchambers,Luke Ye,Sanmitra Ghosh,Michalis Michaelides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models are powerful priors for inverse problems, but posterior sampling under nonlinear, non-differentiable forward models remain hard. We introduce diffusion waltz, an MCMC method using SDEdit-style noising-denoising as a proposal, corrected via Metropolis-Hastings for exact posterior sampling without prior evaluation. We further propose injecting observations into the proposal while preserving exactness, using a gradient-free ensemble Kalman update. On a non-differentiable Navier-Stokes initial condition recovery task, diffusion waltz outperforms existing baselines across different noise and nonlinearity regimes.

</details>


### [252] [Stability and Diversity of Networked Self-Consuming Generative Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09409)
*Xiukun Wei,Yang Zhang,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出网络化自耗尽生成模型的理论框架，将多模型交互建模为有向加权图，分析了再训练动态下的收敛条件、不动点特征，并揭示真实数据访问、跨模型数据消费及交互图结构对长期稳定性与多样性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式 AI 广泛部署导致合成数据难以与真实数据区分，必然进入未来模型训练管线，形成自耗尽训练回路。现有研究多局限于单模型自耗尽或双模型简化交互，缺乏对多模型通过复杂路径相互消费合成数据的网络化场景的系统性理解。

Method: 构建理论框架：将模型表示为有向加权图中的节点，边权重控制合成数据在模型间的流动；基于此框架，利用动态系统理论分析网络化模型在再训练动态下的长期行为，推导收敛条件并刻画不动点；进一步研究真实数据访问比例、跨模型消费强度及图拓扑结构对系统稳定性与多样性的协同作用。

Result: 建立了网络化自耗尽模型收敛的充要条件，刻画了不动点处的分布特性；证明了真实数据访问可作为稳定性的锚定机制，跨模型数据消费能缓解模式崩塌但过度同质化会降低多样性，交互图的连通性与谱半径决定收敛速度与平衡态的多样性上界。

Conclusion: 网络化自耗尽训练的长期行为取决于真实数据注入、跨模型交互强度与图结构的三元博弈；为设计鲁棒的生成模型生态系统提供了理论指导：需保证关键节点持续接入真实数据，优化交互拓扑以平衡稳定性与多样性，避免高度同质化的合成数据回路导致系统性退化。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of generative AI has made it increasingly difficult to distinguish synthetic content from real data. Consequently, synthetic data is inevitably incorporated into the training pipelines of future model generations, forming a self-consuming training loop. Prior work has studied the effects of such recursive self-consuming training, but analyses have largely been limited to isolated models, where a model consumes only its own synthetic data, or to simplified interactions between two models. This paper takes a first step toward understanding networked self-consuming generative models, in which multiple models consume synthetic data generated by one another through complex interaction pathways. We introduce a theoretical framework representing models as nodes in a directed, weighted graph, with edge weights governing the flow of synthetic data among models. Using this framework, we analyze the long-term behavior of networked models under retraining dynamics, establishing conditions for convergence and characterizing the resulting fixed points. We further investigate how the system's long-term stability and diversity are shaped by each model's access to real data, cross-model data consumption, and the structure of the interaction graph.

</details>


### [253] [Shared Geometry As A Rosetta Stone: Cross-Modal Alignment Without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09411)
*Dominik Schnaus,Thomas Dagès,Daniel Cremers,Xi Wang,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Wasserstein Procrustes方法，利用粗略几何初始化估计正交映射，无需配对数据即可实现跨模态对齐，且在极少配对情况下显著优于现有方法，甚至支持无配对文生图，证明独立训练模型间常共享足够几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表示虽支持零样本分类检索，但跨模态对齐通常依赖大量配对数据。柏拉图表示假说表明不同模态模型可能自发收敛至共享几何，因此探究是否完全不需要配对示例即可建立跨模态对应。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Procrustes方法，结合粗略几何初始化，通过估计单一正交映射对齐两个不相交的嵌入集合，无需任何配对数据；在极少配对情况下自然融合配对信息以提升性能。

Result: 跨数据集、模态及单模态模型均能一致对齐独立训练表示，标准几何对齐指标准确预测可行性；极少配对下大幅超越现有方法，更多配对时仍具竞争力；最终演示无配对文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 独立训练模型间往往共享足够几何结构，仅需极少或无配对数据即可建立跨模态对应，揭示了模态间自发几何收敛的普遍性，为低资源跨模态学习提供新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal representations enable zero-shot classification and retrieval, but aligning independently trained models usually requires large amounts of paired data. Yet, the Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that models trained on different modalities may converge spontaneously toward a shared representation geometry. But then, do we even need paired examples for cross-modal alignment? Remarkably, we show that paired examples are unnecessary for coarse cross-modal alignment. Our simple Wasserstein Procrustes method with a coarse geometric initialization aligns two disjoint embedding sets by estimating a single orthogonal map without seeing any pairs. Across datasets, modalities, and unimodal models, we show that we can consistently align independently trained representations without pairs, and standard geometric alignment metrics accurately predict when this is possible. Nevertheless, we can naturally benefit from paired examples. In the very few-pair regime, our method substantially outperforms existing ones, while staying competitive with pair-based methods with more added examples. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting alignments can enable text-to-image generation without paired examples. These results show that independently trained models often share enough geometry to establish cross-modal correspondence with little or no paired data.

</details>


### [254] [Self-Consuming Generative Models with Co-Evolving Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09415)
*Xiukun Wei,Tian Xie,Ding Zhu,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly trained in self-consuming iterative loops, where users curate preferred samples from model-generated candidates and the curated samples are used to train future generations of the model. Prior work has largely assumed fixed user preferences, but in practice exposure to model outputs gradually reshapes what users perceive as desirable, creating a feedback loop in which model distributions and user preferences co-evolve. We take a first step toward understanding the long-term behavior of such coupled dynamics. We show that when training relies entirely on user-curated synthetic data, iterative curation amplifies initial biases and drives the system toward one of multiple singleton equilibria in which the instance holding an initial advantage eventually dominates. In contrast, injecting reference data into training at a sufficiently large rate fundamentally changes the dynamics and yields a unique globally attracting equilibrium. Building on this insight, we study how reference-data injection can be used to control long-term outcomes, and propose an efficient algorithm that jointly selects a reference distribution and its mixing weight to steer the coupled system toward equilibria that preserve desired attributes while minimizing data collection costs.

</details>


### [255] [What a Reporting Convention Hides: A Matched-Budget Audit of Quantum Natural Gradient with an Exactly Computed Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09425)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究变分量子优化器（Adam、SPSA、QNG）的评估惯例对性能判定的影响，发现仅统计达标运行或单一目标会掩盖真实差距，建议对所有未达标运行计满预算并在多目标下报告结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比常只计算达到目标损失的运行时间，或在单一目标下判定优劣，这两种惯例可能错误判断开销较大的步骤是否划算。需要系统量化评估惯例对优化器排名的影响。

Method: 在保留的初始化上对比 Adam、SPSA、QNG。为 QNG 计算精确度量，以电路评估次数为步长代价，给每种方法相同预算。跨电路宽度、代价族及设置扫描取中位数汇总；在全局代价族上固定为严格目标选定的设置，再考察宽松与严格目标。

Result: 汇总中位数下，SPSA 达宽松目标所需评估次数超 Adam 两倍以上，丢弃未达标运行会隐藏该差距。全局代价族上，QNG 通常在宽松目标上落后 Adam 但在严格目标上领先；若将度量的模拟器代价（参数数线性）改为假设硬件代价（参数数平方），QNG 的严格目标优势消失。

Conclusion: 评估惯例显著改变优化器排名。建议对每个未达标运行收取完整预算，并在多个目标阈值下报告判定结果，以获得鲁棒的性能比较。

Abstract: Several published comparisons of variational quantum optimizers time only runs that reach a target loss, or read the verdict at a single target. Either convention could decide whether an optimizer's costlier steps pay off. We measure how much each convention changes verdicts among Adam, simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA) and quantum natural gradient (QNG), on initializations held out from the selection of settings. We compute the exact metric that preconditions QNG, price every step in circuit evaluations and give every method the same budget. In a median pooled over circuit widths, cost families and a sweep of settings with common misses, SPSA needs more than twice Adam's evaluations to reach a loose target. Dropping the censored runs that miss the target hides this gap. On the global-cost family we hold fixed the settings selected for a strict target. QNG then usually reaches the loose target after Adam but the strict target first. QNG's strict-target lead disappears when the metric's simulator price, linear in the number of parameters, is replaced by an assumed hardware count quadratic in that number. We recommend charging every miss the budget and reporting verdicts across targets.

</details>


### [256] [An extended deep energy method for thermo-mechanical crack propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09433)
*Han Zhang,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari,Babak Shahbodagh,Mohammad Vahab,Cosmin Anitescu,Timon Rabczuk,Elena Atroshchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的深度能量法，用于求解热-力耦合裂纹扩展问题。该方法利用两个神经网络分别表示温度场和位移场，通过标量嵌入函数引入尖锐裂纹，无需正则化长度即可捕捉场量在裂纹处的跳跃，并结合Williams展开丰富裂纹尖端位移场。采用交错序列最小化增量传导泛函和热弹性势能，利用蒙特卡洛积分和自适应采样训练网络。通过交互积分提取应力强度因子，基于最大环向应力准则和能量释放率判据驱动裂纹扩展。数值验证显示方法在热边缘裂纹、功能梯度材料剪切试验和缺口十字试件中均达到高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络能量求解器主要针对相场裂纹或仅处理力学裂纹，尚未将热传导与裂纹扩展在热应力作用下耦合求解。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立一种能够同时处理瞬态热传导、热应力产生及裂纹尖锐扩展的统一深度学习框架。

Method: 1. 使用两个独立神经网络近似温度场和位移场；2. 引入标量嵌入函数隐式表示尖锐多线段裂纹，使场量可在裂纹处不连续；3. 位移场在裂纹尖端通过Williams展开及可训练振幅进行富集；4. 采用交错最小化策略：先最小化增量传导泛函求温度场，再最小化热弹性势能求位移场；5. 采用分层背景网格上的蒙特卡洛积分，尖端区域加密采样并在训练中重绘采样点；6. 利用Wilson-Yu面积项的交互积分提取应力强度因子，通过轮廓半径扫描验证；7. 基于最大环向应力角度和裂纹尖端温度下的临界能量释放率判据驱动裂纹扩展。

Result: 1. 静止热边缘裂纹测试中，提取的应力强度因子与文献值误差仅0.11%；2. 功能梯度材料剪切试验中，裂纹起始载荷步与独立尖锐裂纹有限元解相差一个载荷步以内；3. 缺口十字试件在力学、热学及耦合载荷下的裂纹扩展路径与文献发表解吻合良好。

Conclusion: 提出的扩展深度能量法成功实现了热-力耦合裂纹扩展的尖锐界面求解，无需相场正则化即可准确捕捉裂纹尖端奇异性和场量跳跃，交错求解策略与自适应蒙特卡洛积分保证了计算效率与精度，为复杂热-力耦合断裂问题提供了有前景的无网格深度学习求解器。

Abstract: Thermo-mechanical fracture couples transient heat conduction on a cracked domain with a crack that grows as the temperature and the displacement evolve. Neural energy solvers have been proposed for phase-field fracture and later extended to represent a sharp crack through the network input, but heat conduction on the cracked domain and crack propagation under the resulting thermal stresses have not yet been treated together in these solvers. We present an extended deep energy method for thermo-mechanical crack propagation in which the crack remains a sharp polyline. Two networks represent the temperature and the displacement and receive the crack through a scalar embedding function, discontinuous across the crack and smooth elsewhere, so that both fields can jump across it without a regularization length, and the displacement is enriched near the tip by the Williams expansion with trainable amplitudes. The two fields are obtained by minimizing an incremental conduction functional and the thermoelastic potential energy in a staggered sequence, with Monte Carlo integration on points stratified over background elements, densified near the tip and redrawn during training. The stress intensity factors are extracted by the interaction integral with the area term of Wilson and Yu and checked by a sweep of the contour radius, and the crack advances at the maximum hoop stress angle when the energy release rate of the kink reaches the critical value at the crack-tip temperature. On a stationary thermal edge crack the extracted stress intensity factor agrees with the published value to 0.11%, in a functionally graded shear test initiation agrees with an independent sharp-crack finite element solution to within one load step, and on a notched cruciform specimen the crack paths follow the published solutions under mechanical, thermal and combined loading.

</details>


### [257] [GeoPrior-Mamba: Structured Process Priors with Mamba for Fine-Resolution XCO2 Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09456)
*Zhao Meng,Yinan Cai,Siru Zhong,Juepeng Zheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoPrior-Mamba，一种融合离线大语言模型诱导的结构化过程先验的多方向 Mamba 框架，用于从稀疏卫星观测重建高分辨率柱平均干燥空气 CO2 浓度（XCO2）。实验表明，该方法在 OCO-2 数据上实现 RMSE 0.81 ppm、R² 0.93，较基线显著提升精度并加速收敛，且经独立 TCCON 验证具有一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的 XCO2 重建方法将环境协变量视为普通数值输入，需从稀疏监督中学习异质的源汇关系，导致空间结构仅受弱约束。需要一种机制在不进入数值预测循环的前提下，利用语言模型构建全球一致的结构化过程先验。

Method: GeoPrior-Mamba 包含：(1) 多方向 Mamba 重建骨干网络；(2) 离线阶段利用大语言模型将生物圈吸收、生态系统呼吸、人为排放等过程知识组织为确定性先验表；(3) 利用地理、生态、排放、季节信息将先验空间化实例化；(4) 通过轻量级知识适配器自适应注入骨干网络。

Result: 在 OCO-2 2018-2020 数据上，保留观测测试集 RMSE 0.81 ppm、R² 0.93；较 CAMS 背景插值降低 RMSE 48.2%，较 Trans-XCO2 降低 3.1%。消融实验显示知识先验分支有可测贡献且收敛显著加快。独立 TCCON 评估进一步验证重建场与地基柱状 CO2 测量的一致性。

Conclusion: 当难以直接获得全球一致的过程-响应表征时，大语言模型可提供构建结构化过程先验的实用机制，且始终位于数值预测循环之外，为稀疏观测下的高分辨率温室气体场重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing fine-resolution column-averaged dry-air CO2 (XCO2) fields from sparse satellite observations requires models to infer spatial structure that is only weakly constrained by direct measurements. Existing learning-based methods typically treat environmental covariates as ordinary numerical inputs and must therefore learn heterogeneous source-sink relationships largely from sparse supervision. We introduce GeoPrior-Mamba, a multi-directional Mamba framework augmented with offline language-model-induced structured process priors. Rather than using a language model to predict XCO2, we use it before training to organize relative process knowledge for biospheric uptake, ecosystem respiration, and anthropogenic emissions into deterministic prior tables. These priors are spatially instantiated using geographic, ecological, emission-related, and seasonal information and are adaptively injected into the reconstruction backbone through a lightweight knowledge adapter. Using OCO-2 observations from 2018-2020, GeoPrior-Mamba achieves an RMSE of 0.81 ppm and an R2 of 0.93 on held-out observations, reducing RMSE by 48.2% relative to CAMS background interpolation and by 3.1% relative to Trans-XCO2 under the same evaluation protocol. Ablation experiments show a measurable contribution from the knowledge-prior branch and substantially faster convergence than the knowledge-free Mamba backbone. Independent TCCON evaluation further supports the consistency of the reconstructed fields with ground-based column CO2 measurements. These results suggest that language models can provide a practical mechanism for constructing structured process priors when globally consistent process-response representations are difficult to obtain directly, while remaining outside the numerical prediction loop.

</details>


### [258] [DSReg: Provably Recovering Individual World Latents without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09457)
*Yujia Zheng,David Klindt,Randall Balestriero,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DSReg（依赖稀疏正则化），在无需重构、解码器或标签的情况下，通过“结构多样性”条件，证明了可以将单个世界潜变量恢复至符号置换等价，实现了首个完全可识别的 JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如非线性 ICA、字典学习、因果表示学习）依赖重构、辅助监督或分布不对称性（如非高斯性）来锚定潜变量；而无这些锚点的 JEPA 仅能将潜在状态识别至线性变换等价类，导致个体潜变量混合。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现无监督、无重构的个体潜变量可识别恢复。

Method: 引入“结构多样性”条件：不同潜变量在观测上留下独特的依赖足迹。基于 LeJEPA 提供的线性可识别性，证明在该条件下，DSReg（依赖稀疏正则化）可将任意线性可识别表示后处理恢复为个体世界潜变量（符号置换等价），无需重构或解码器，且可复用预训练检查点且无性能损失。

Result: 理论上：结构多样性严格弱于所有先前可识别潜变量模型的结构条件；DSReg 建立了首个完全可识别的 JEPA。实验上：在合成数据、世界模型探针、学习视觉编码器及外部渲染器等多种设置中，DSReg 在保持稠密预测性能的同时，显著提升个体潜变量恢复质量及下游任务表现。

Conclusion: DSReg 通过仅施加依赖稀疏正则化，在无重构、无解码器、无标签的前提下实现了个体潜变量的可证明恢复，为自监督学习中的潜变量可识别性提供了更弱条件且更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Methods that recover individual latent variables of the world, from nonlinear ICA to dictionary learning and causal representation learning, anchor the latents to observations through reconstruction, auxiliary supervision, or distributional asymmetries such as non-Gaussianity. Methods without these anchors, including joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs), identify the latent state only up to a linear transformation, so individual latents remain mixed. We close this gap: individual world latents can be provably recovered with no reconstruction, no decoder, and no labels. The key condition is Structural Diversity: different latents leave distinct dependency footprints on observations, just as no two snowflakes are alike. Building on the linear identifiability that LeJEPA provides, we prove that under Structural Diversity, DSReg (Dependency-Sparsity Regularization) recovers individual world latents up to signed permutation, without reconstruction or a decoder. It applies post hoc to any linearly identified representation, reusing trained checkpoints at no loss over joint training, and establishes the first fully identifiable JEPA that recovers every world latent. Moreover, as a condition on dependency footprints, Structural Diversity is strictly weaker than all structural conditions of prior identifiable latent variable models. Across synthetic regimes, world model probes, learned visual encoders, and external renderers, DSReg preserves dense prediction while improving individual-latent recovery and downstream use with scales.

</details>


### [259] [CHASE: Channel-Aligned Structure Exploitation for Geometry-Aware Model Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09476)
*Wei Wang,Wei Jiang,Ziran Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CHASE 框架，利用几何与频谱对齐（GSA） 所揭示的网络内在结构，设计了六种模型修改、重组与压缩方法：CORA、COEC、CORAM 分别针对参数高效微调、结构化剪枝补偿、模型融合；新增 CAGA、SAKV、CAPS 分别用于多头注意力共享 KV 表示、相邻层低秩 KV-cache 共享、输出神经元分组与输入通道选择。实验表明 GSA 结构可直接指导各类模型操作，显著提升 MHA 转 GQA、KV-cache 压缩与结构化剪枝性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩与适配方法多依赖启发式或任务特定设计，缺乏统一的理论指导。GSA 通过频谱集中、物理通道对齐、支撑结构及奇异基变化等几何/频谱特性刻画训练后网络，为模型结构操作提供了统一、可解释的结构先验。

Method: 1. GSA 分析：提取网络的频谱集中度、通道对齐度、支撑结构及奇异基演化。2. CHASE 框架：将 GSA 结构映射为六个具体算子——CORA（参数高效微调）、COEC（剪枝补偿）、CORAM（模型融合）、CAGA（几何对齐构建共享 KV 头）、SAKV（层间低秩 KV-cache 共享与秩分配）、CAPS（输出神经元分组与分组通道剪枝）。每个算子直接利用 GSA 指标决定共享策略、秩保留或分组方式。

Result: CORA、COEC、CORAM 验证了 GSA 在适配、剪枝补偿、模型融合上的有效性。CAGA 将 MHA 转 GQA 的性能损失大幅降低；SAKV 在 KV-cache 压缩上超越代表性基线；CAPS 在结构化剪枝中保持更高精度。整体实验证明 GSA 识别的结构可直接指导多样模型操作。

Conclusion: 几何与频谱对齐揭示的网络内在结构具有普适性，可作为统一设计原则直接用于参数高效微调、剪枝补偿、模型融合、注意力头共享、KV-cache 压缩及结构化剪枝等任务，显著提升模型压缩与重组的效率与效果。

Abstract: Geometric and Spectral Alignment (GSA) characterizes trained networks through spectral concentration, physical-channel alignment, support structure, and changes in singular bases. In this paper, we propose CHASE (Channel-Aligned Structure Exploitation) to use these structures in practical model design. CHASE covers six applications across model modification, reconfiguration, and compression. CORA, COEC, and CORAM apply GSA to parameter-efficient finetuning, structured-pruning compensation, and model merging. We further develop three new methods. CAGA uses GSA to identify multi-head attention heads that can share a KV representation and constructs the shared key and value heads through geometric alignment and low-rank subspace extraction. SAKV uses GSA to determine which adjacent layers can share a low-rank KV-cache representation and the retained rank for each layer group. CAPS uses GSA spectral structure to group output neurons and selects retained input channels separately for each group. Results from CORA, COEC, and CORAM establish the effectiveness of GSA for adaptation, pruning compensation, and model merging. Experiments on CAGA show that geometric shared-head construction substantially improves MHA-to-GQA conversion, and SAKV and CAPS improve over representative baselines for KV-cache compression and structured pruning. These results show that the structures identified by GSA can be used directly to design methods for a range of model operations.

</details>


### [260] [When Should an In-Context Learner Expand Its Hypothesis Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09471)
*Weihan Li,Xinlei Chen,Junhao Wu,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究学习系统何时应扩展模型族的决策问题，将其建模为代价高昂的序贯决策。提出了结构修订环境，允许精确贝叶斯计算与规范解。结果显示最优修订是价值边界而非证据阈值，Transformer能从效用中学会该边界，但大语言模型虽能感知失败信号，却无法将其与扩展代价和时间范围权衡，事后训练也无法修正这一准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习系统在熟悉模型族内适应迅速，但更难的是：根据可能来自噪声、例外、族内变化或族外结构的观测，决定开启更丰富模型族是否值得其代价。这是一个代价高昂的序贯决策：需将预测失败转化为结构证据、证据转化为扩展价值、价值转化为行动。

Method: 构建结构修订环境，生成来自各源头的匹配失败，独立于证据地改变扩展价格与剩余时域，并允许精确贝叶斯计算与单步决策的精确规范解。

Result: 规范解表明修订是价值边界而非证据阈值：同一历史在不同价格、时域和查询下有不同最优动作；局部修补与扩展的边界由规则预测但修补无法覆盖的输入决定；对更丰富模型族的信念早于决策跨越阈值。在该环境中训练的Transformer仅从效用中复现了该边界。三个事后训练谱系的语言模型在预测中携带失败敏感信号，但未在修订决策中体现；给定扩展收益时，它们读取信号而不权衡价格和时域。允许推理的三个模型按参考比例权衡陈述收益，仍无法将历史转化为扩展价值估计。受控事后训练移动先验和预测锐度，均未移动决策准则。

Conclusion: 最优结构修订由价值边界决定，而非简单证据阈值。Transformer可通过元学习从效用中习得该边界，但现有大语言模型虽能感知预测失败，却不能将失败证据与扩展代价和时间范围进行规范性权衡，事后训练也无法纠正这一根本缺陷。

Abstract: Learning systems adapt quickly inside a familiar family of models. The harder step comes earlier: deciding, from observations that could be noise, an exception, a change within the family or structure outside it, whether opening a richer family is worth its cost. We treat this as a costly sequential decision: prediction failure must be turned into structural evidence, evidence into a value of expansion, and value into action. The Structural Revision Environment produces matched failures from each source, varies the price of expansion and the remaining horizon independently of the evidence, and admits exact Bayesian calculations and an exact normative solution of the one-shot decision. Its solution shows that revision is a value boundary and not an evidence threshold: one history has different optimal actions under different prices, horizons and announced queries, the boundary between local repair and expansion is set by the inputs a rule predicts and a repair cannot cover, and belief in the richer family crosses long before the decision does. Transformers trained in the environment reproduce this boundary from utility alone. Language models of three post-training lineages carry a failure-sensitive signal in their predictions that is not reflected in their revision decisions, and given the gain of expanding they read it without weighing it against price and horizon. Three models allowed to reason weigh the stated gain in the reference's proportions and still do not turn the history into an estimate of what expansion would buy. Controlled post-training of the meta-trained learners moves the prior and the sharpness of predictions, and neither moves the criterion.

</details>


### [261] [Collaborative Reasoning Distillation via Cross-Feedback and Coherent Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09587)
*Taehoon Kim,Seunggeun Cho,Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出协作推理蒸馏（CRD）框架，通过教师模型间的交互式交叉反馈、细粒度逐步质量评估和感知连贯性的步骤拼接，结合推理质量优化（RQO），在仅使用50K训练样本的情况下，使4B参数模型在MATH-500和AIME'25上分别达到97.3%和70.3%，超越基线且数据量比同类模型少12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型推理能力提升依赖海量计算资源，且现有蒸馏方法多基于结果奖励，难以区分合理推理与侥幸猜测，导致小模型推理能力提升受限。

Method: CRD包含三大创新：(1) 交互式交叉反馈，教师模型迭代互评推理过程；(2) 细粒度逐步质量评估，独立于最终答案捕捉逻辑有效性；(3) 连贯性感知步骤拼接，综合多教师优势。学生模型通过预算约束下的推理质量优化（RQO）训练。

Result: CRD-4B在MATH-500上达97.3%，AIME'25上达70.3%，显著优于基线方法，且仅使用50K训练样本，数据量比可比模型减少多达12倍。

Conclusion: 协作推理蒸馏结合多教师交互、过程级评估与连贯性拼接，能在极低数据预算下高效将强推理能力蒸馏至小模型，解决了结果导向蒸馏无法区分推理质量的核心难题。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities are critical for advancing Large Language Models, yet current approaches either require massive computational budgets or struggle to effectively distill reasoning to smaller models. Standard distillation methods rely on outcome-based rewards, failing to distinguish between sound reasoning and lucky guesses. We propose Collaborative Reasoning Distillation (CRD), a framework that enhances reasoning in compact models through three innovations: (1) interactive cross-feedback where teachers iteratively critique each other's reasoning, (2) fine-grained step-wise quality assessment capturing logical validity independent of final answers, and (3) coherence-aware step stitching that synthesizes complementary strengths. Students are trained via Reasoning Quality Optimization (RQO) with budget constraints. Our model, CRD-4B, achieves 97.3% on MATH-500 and 70.3% on AIME'25, surpassing baselines while using only 50K training examples, up to 12 times smaller than the datasets of comparable models.

</details>


### [262] [MORA: Modeling Observed Changes for Drift-Robust Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09473)
*Xudong Mou,Tiejun Wang,Rui Wang,Hui Wang,Pin Liu,Tianyu Wo,Xudong Liu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MORA 框架，通过配对短长期视角重构局部目标，利用重构差距量化上下文支持，并采用数据依赖的修正机制保守调整局部异常分数，解决了非平稳时间序列中分布漂移与真实异常难以区分的“时序变化消歧”问题，无需漂移标注或在线适应即可在四个基准上实现对非平稳性的强鲁棒性并保持对真实异常的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳时间序列异常检测中，分布漂移与真实异常常表现为相似的局部偏差，现有方法要么适应检测到的漂移，要么学习漂移不敏感表示，但都未能从根本上消除这种歧义。因此需要明确定义“时序变化消歧”问题，并设计一种能在无监督漂移标注、无需在线适应的情况下，同时兼顾漂移鲁棒性与异常敏感度的方法。

Method: MORA 框架包含两大核心组件：(1) 配对短长期视角重构同一局部目标，计算重构差距作为上下文支持度的度量；(2) 数据依赖的保守修正机制，仅当长期上下文能改善对同一目标的重构时，才降低主要局部异常分数。该方法无需漂移标注，也无需在线适应，直接在离线训练阶段学习漂移鲁棒的表示。

Result: 在四个主流 TSAD 基准（如 Yahoo S5、NAB、UCR、Exathlon 等）上实验表明，MORA 在各类非平稳场景（突然漂移、渐变漂移、季节性变化等）下均显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持对真实点异常、集体异常的高检测率，验证了其强鲁棒性与敏感度的平衡。

Conclusion: MORA 通过显式建模短长期上下文的一致性，实现了时序变化消歧，证明了无需显式漂移监督即可实现鲁棒的非平稳时间序列异常检测。其核心思想——利用重构差距作为上下文证据并保守修正异常分数——为后续研究提供了可推广的范式。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) identifies deviations from patterns learned from historical data. In non-stationary settings, distribution drift and true anomalies can cause similar local changes, making it difficult to tell whether a deviation reflects abnormality or evolving context. Existing methods typically adapt to detected shifts or learn drift-insensitive representations, but do not resolve this ambiguity. We define this problem as \emph{temporal change disambiguation}: determining whether a local deviation is explained by broader temporal evolution. We introduce MORA, a drift-robust TSAD framework that reconstructs the same local target from paired short- and long-term views. The reconstruction gap measures contextual support for a local deviation, and a data-dependent correction mechanism conservatively adjusts the primary local anomaly score. Context can only reduce the score when it improves reconstruction of the same target. MORA needs neither drift annotations nor online adaptation. Experiments on four TSAD benchmarks show strong robustness to non-stationarity while preserving sensitivity to genuine anomalies.

</details>


### [263] [Coding-Agent Benchmarks Should Match Their Users' Task Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09633)
*Igor Slinko,Yaroslav Golubev,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文收集了4,782个真实工程师在JetBrains IDE中的代理交互会话，分析发现长会话与基于issue的基准任务在任务类型分布和类型切换上存在显著差异；提出SWE-TaskFlow方法，通过提示拆分和可验证仓库QA将基准任务转向目标任务流，并引入任务流对齐分数(TFAS)进行轨迹选择。实验表明，顺序多步求解使代理成本约翻倍但解决率无稳定提升，证明交互协议本身是评估的重要维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理评估基准多基于issue衍生任务，缺乏真实交互会话中任务类型的多样性（如代码问答、规划、审查、重构、执行等）及用户在会话中动态切换任务类型的特性，导致评估不够真实，亟需一种能够校准至真实交互分布的评估框架。

Method: 1. 收集4,782个真实工程师IDE会话（Production Sessions），筛选至少三轮用户消息的长会话（占33%）；2. 定义任务流为会话长度、任务类型及类型转移的分布，对比三个公开交互语料库发现差异显著；3. 提出SWE-TaskFlow：保留验证过的任务和测试，通过提示拆分和可验证仓库QA将交互引导至目标任务流，引入任务流对齐分数(TFAS)用于生成轨迹选择；4. 在700个SWE-Bench Pro任务上进行试点实验。

Result: 长会话包含更广泛的任务类型且用户会在会话中切换类型；不同公开语料库的任务流差异显著，不存在单一通用的真实交互分布；SWE-TaskFlow实验显示，将任务顺序拆解为多步求解会使代理成本约翻倍，但解决率无稳定变化，表明交互协议本身是评估的关键维度。

Conclusion: 基准评估应明确目标用例并校准至该场景的实测数据；交互协议（如单轮vs多轮、任务类型切换）是评估的重要维度；SWE-TaskFlow提供了一种将任意issue衍生基准转向目标任务流的通用方法，为更真实的代理评估奠定基础。

Abstract: The evaluation of coding agents generally strives to be as realistic as possible. In our study, we collect 4,782 agent sessions of real software engineers in JetBrains IDEs, which we call Production Sessions. Since our subject is interactive agents, we study the sessions with at least three user messages (33% of the sample). These long sessions differ from issue-derived benchmark tasks in two ways: (i) user requests span a far wider mix of task types - questions about the project's code, planning, review, refactoring, execution - and (ii) users switch between types throughout a session. Long-session samples from three public interaction corpora exhibit markedly different Task Flows (the distributions of session lengths, task types, and type-to-type transitions), so no single interaction distribution is universally realistic: benchmarks should name a target use case and calibrate to measurements from it. We present SWE-TaskFlow, an approach for transforming any issue-derived benchmark: it preserves the verified tasks and tests while steering the interaction toward a target Task Flow through prompt splitting and verifiable repository QA, with a TaskFlow Alignment Score (TFAS) for selecting among generated trajectories. In a pilot on 700 SWE-Bench Pro tasks, solving the task sequentially in several steps approximately doubles agent cost without a stable change in resolve rate: the interaction protocol itself is an important dimension of evaluation.

</details>


### [264] [When Rank Rises as LLMs Degrade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09647)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM后训练中基于谱统计（如RankMe）的表征健康监测，发现秩下降假设不成立，不同退化模式下秩统计量表现方向不一，提出双向多通道监测器能诊断故障模式但无法比held-out loss更早预警，且需要held-out健康数据校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者常用RankMe等谱统计监测表征健康，假设秩下降表示退化，但该假设在LLM后训练中是否安全尚未经过系统验证。

Method: 在Qwen3-0.6B上进行受控研究，设置四种退化模式和三个随机种子，对比RankMe、centered RankMe、协方差有效秩等统计量；区分RankMe（归一化奇异值）与协方差有效秩（归一化特征值）的定义差异；测试双向多通道顺序监测器，采用预注册的共享前缀留一种子交叉验证。

Result: 数据复制导致held-out loss恶化75%，但RankMe和centered RankMe均上升（后者变化13.5 pooled std），协方差有效秩接近健康值两倍，属谱分散而非坍缩；学习率配置错误降低centered RankMe和k95，而uncentered RankMe跨种子不一致；双向监测器在分叉后10-60步检测出所有三种损伤模式，能区分分散和向下秩损伤，但从未早于held-out probe loss预警，且两种子校准会在held-out健康种子上产生误报。

Conclusion: 谱监测可诊断故障模式，但无法比held-out loss更早预警，有效性声明需要held-out健康数据支持。

Abstract: Post-training adapts language models in non-stationary environments. Practitioners monitor representation health with RankMe and related spectral statistics, often assuming that rank falls when representations degrade. We show that this assumption is unsafe for LLM post-training. In a controlled study of Qwen3-0.6B with four degradation modes and three seeds, data duplication worsens held-out loss by 75% relative to healthy while increasing both original and centred RankMe; the latter changes by 13.5 pooled standard deviations. Covariance effective rank rises to nearly twice its healthy value. This failure is spectral dispersion rather than collapse, so a one-sided monitor rates the worst checkpoint as the healthiest. By contrast, a learning-rate misconfiguration lowers centred RankMe and k95, while uncentred RankMe is inconsistent across seeds. Direction is therefore a property of the regime-statistic pair and cannot be fixed by recalibration alone. We also distinguish two often-conflated statistics: RankMe normalises singular values, whereas covariance effective rank normalises eigenvalues. On raw intermediate-layer states in the pretrained model, massive activations pin the latter near 1 out of dimension d while RankMe retains usable range. We then test a two-sided, multichannel sequential monitor with separate calibration and test data. In a pre-registered shared-prefix, leave-one-seed-out evaluation, it detects all three damage regimes in every fold 10 to 60 steps after the fork and separates dispersion from downward-rank damage by firing direction. However, it never precedes held-out probe loss, and calibration with two seeds produces false alarms on the held-out healthy seed. Spectral monitoring can diagnose failure regimes, but it does not warn earlier than held-out loss, and validity claims require held-out healthy data.

</details>


### [265] [Safe on Average, Unsafe in the Tail: When Is the Episodic-Cost Tail Controllable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09508)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用 CVaR_0.1（最差 10% 回合的平均成本）作为安全强化学习中的尾部安全指标，揭示了仅满足平均成本约束的策略可能在尾部不安全，并通过实验评估多种算法和约束族在导航和运动任务上的尾部控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法主要约束期望回合成本，忽略了成本分布的尾部风险。平均成本达标的策略在最差回合中可能严重违规，而标准评估无法识别这种尾部违规，也不清楚是否能在保持回报的同时控制尾部风险。

Method: 采用 CVaR_0.1 作为尾部安全度量，将策略分类为尾部安全（CVaR_0.1 在安全预算内）或尾部不安全。在 Safety-Gymnasium 的三个导航任务上评估五个标准算法，识别尾部不安全策略。随后在密集危险导航任务上测试四种约束族，并在四个导航和四个运动任务上评估尾部控制效果。

Result: 实验发现标准算法在平均成本满足约束时，尾部成本（CVaR_0.1）往往超出预算，即存在尾部不安全现象。不同约束族对尾部控制效果差异显著，部分约束族能在保持回报的同时有效降低尾部成本。

Conclusion: 仅关注平均成本约束不足以保证安全，需引入尾部风险度量如 CVaR。通过适当的约束设计，可以在不牺牲回报的前提下实现尾部安全，为安全强化学习提供了更严格的评估和控制框架。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning seeks policies that maximize return while satisfying constraints on cumulative cost. Most methods impose these constraints on expected episodic cost. Consequently, standard evaluations report mean episodic cost without characterizing how cost is distributed across episodes. A policy that satisfies the mean-cost criterion may therefore remain unsafe in its worst episodes. Mean-cost reporting neither identifies this tail violation nor shows whether it can be brought within budget while preserving return. In this work, we measure the episodic-cost tail using $\mathrm{CVaR}_{0.1}$, the average cost of the worst $10\%$ of episodes. We classify a policy as tail-safe when $\mathrm{CVaR}_{0.1}$ is within the safety budget. This allows us first to identify policies that are safe on average but unsafe in the tail and then to study whether their tail violations can be controlled while preserving return. To identify tail-unsafe policies, we evaluate five standard algorithms on three Safety-Gymnasium navigation tasks. We then examine four constraint families on dense-hazard navigation and assess tail control across four navigation and four locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [266] [Physics-Informed Neural Plasticity: PDE Solvers That Reshape Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09510)
*Chun-Wun Cheng,Bingcheng Hu,Angelica I. Aviles-Rivero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCAP 是一种基于高斯局部化的物理信息神经 PDE 求解器，通过动态调整表示能力（局部丰富、残差导向分裂、门控剪枝、函数感知合并）实现物理信息神经可塑性，在五个 3D/4D 基准测试中以 10.7%-27.5% 的误差降低超越 11 个竞争方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理信息神经 PDE 求解器的表示结构在训练中固定不变，难以适应 PDE 解在空间和时空中的强异质复杂性，导致物理困难区域容量不足、简单区域冗余。

Method: 提出 ReCAP（表示能力自适应 PDE 求解器），一种高斯局部化求解器，通过局部丰富、残差导向分裂、门控剪枝、函数感知合并动态重新分配容量；利用责任加权误差指示器和残差能量几何决定细化位置和方式；引入静默子细化以分裂时通过传输父表示并控制瞬时函数扰动初始化新组件；建立条件后验可靠性和结构稳定性保证，将局部物理残差与解误差及稳定细化关联。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的 3D 和 4D PDE 基准测试中，对比 11 个物理信息求解器，ReCAP 在每个问题上均达到最低相对 L^2 误差，相比最强竞争结果降低误差 10.7%-27.5%。

Conclusion: 物理信息求解器不必仅学习参数，还可以学习其表示能力应如何组织，展示了物理信息神经可塑性的潜力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural PDE solvers adapt their parameters to satisfy governing equations, yet their representational structure typically remains fixed throughout training. This rigidity is poorly matched to PDE solutions with strongly heterogeneous complexity across space and space--time, leaving capacity insufficient where the physics is difficult and redundant where it is simple. We introduce physics-informed neural plasticity, a paradigm in which the representation itself reshapes during optimization in response to unresolved physics. We instantiate this principle with Representation Capacity Adaptation for PDEs (ReCAP), a Gaussian-localized solver that dynamically redistributes capacity through local enrichment, residual-directed splitting, gate-based pruning, and function-aware merging. ReCAP uses responsibility-weighted error indicators and the geometry of residual energy to determine where and how to refine. To limit the disturbance introduced by splitting, we introduce quiet-child refinement, which initializes new components by transporting the parent representation while controlling instantaneous functional perturbation. We further establish conditional a posteriori reliability and structural-stability guarantees linking localized physics residuals to solution error and stable refinement. Across five challenging 3D and 4D PDE benchmarks against 11 physics-informed solvers, ReCAP achieves the lowest relative $L^2$ error on every problem, reducing error by $10.7\%$--$27.5\%$ relative to the strongest competing result. These results suggest that physics-informed solvers need not merely learn their parameters---they can learn how their representational capacity should be organized.

</details>


### [267] [Differential Refresh Policies for Models Trained on Lagging Data Snapshots: From a Single-Age Equivalence Limit to an Optimal Per-Segment Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09519)
*Amit Rajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出基于全局加权过期评分的自适应触发器本质等价于统一计时器，无法利用数据段落间的异构性；提出分段差异化刷新策略，最优刷新率与风险平方根成正比，实验证明在相同预算下显著降低加权过期暴露（8-29%），且对噪声鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
