Motivation: 生产环境机器学习模型随训练数据老化而性能下降，常见做法是设计加权过期评分作为自适应重训练触发器，但作者发现这种基于单一全局年龄的静态单调函数触发器在操作上等价于校准后的统一年龄计时器，携带的调度信息不超过时钟，无法针对不同数据段落的异构变化率和权重进行差异化调度。

Method: 1. 证明等价性界限：任意静态严格单调的全局年龄函数触发器等价于统一计时器。2. 提出逃逸路径：当刷新成本可分离（增量训练或段落级模型）时，对各段落赋予独立年龄和刷新间隔。3. 解决预算分配问题：在频繁刷新体制下推导最优刷新率与sqrt(w_j λ_j)成正比（w_j为权重，λ_j为变化率），给出Cauchy-Schwarz不等式导出的“统一性代价”闭式表达式，该代价在同质负载下为零，随异构性增长。

Result: 在真实Poisson变更事件的离散事件仿真中，最优策略在匹配刷新预算下将实现的加权过期暴露降低8%-29%，在86%-100%的随机种子上优于统一计时器；朴素的暴露阈值策略无法获胜，证明是分配策略而非评分函数起作用；优势在50%的变化率估计噪声下依然保持。

Conclusion: 模型刷新的杠杆不在于设计更复杂的过期评分函数，而在于采取更好的行动——即基于段落异构性的差异化刷新调度。分段独立刷新配合最优预算分配能根本性提升刷新效率，且具备理论最优性保证和工程鲁棒性。

Abstract: Production machine-learning models are derived artifacts of time-bounded training snapshots: a deployed model is a materialized view over a training cut that ages the instant it is built. A common response is to replace the fixed retraining cadence with an adaptive trigger -- a weighted staleness score that retrains when accumulated source risk crosses a threshold. We show this is the wrong lever, and identify the right one. First, an equivalence limit: any refresh trigger that is a static, strictly monotone function of a single shared global training-data age is operationally equivalent to a calibrated uniform age timer, so a global staleness budget, however elaborately it weights segments, sources, and sensitivities, carries no scheduling information a clock does not. The limit also shows how to escape it: refresh segments differentially, giving each its own age and refresh interval, which is meaningful when refresh cost is separable across segments (incremental training or per-segment models). We solve the resulting budget-allocation problem. In the frequent-refresh regime each segment's optimal refresh rate is proportional to the square root of its risk $w_j λ_j$ (weight times change rate), and the optimal policy never costs more than the uniform timer, beating it by a closed-form Cauchy-Schwarz "price of uniformity" that is zero for homogeneous workloads and grows with heterogeneity. In a discrete-event simulation with real Poisson change events, the optimal policy lowers realized weighted stale exposure by 8-29% relative to the uniform timer at matched refresh budget, winning on 86-100% of seeds; a naive exposure-threshold policy does not, showing the allocation is what helps; and the advantage survives 50% rate-estimation noise. The leverage in model refresh is not a better score but a better action.

</details>


### [268] [Align Before You Combine: Reference Space Calibration for Supervision Without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09525)
*Jackson Eshbaugh,Jorge Silveyra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于校准的框架，无需真实标签或共享标注空间即可生成监督分数。该框架通过合成有序参考空间对齐子集特定评分器，在三个基准数据集上优于未校准平均和最佳单一评分器，且计算成本较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在没有真实标签且缺乏共享标注空间的情况下构建监督分数的问题，即如何让原本不可比较的评分器输出在同一尺度上对齐融合。

Method: 构建合成有序参考空间（由代表潜在概念的有序校准特征组成），以此为基准对齐各子集评分器，再进行融合。校准过程不依赖训练样本分布，实现了与训练集经验分布无关的对齐。

Result: 在 Ames Housing、Breast Cancer Wisconsin、Wine Quality 三个基准数据集上，该框架持续优于未校准平均法，并在主要指标上超越最佳单一评分器。与样本依赖基线相比，绝对差异均小于 0.02（Ames）和 0.01（其余两数据集）。Bonferroni 校正后，Ames 全部三项比较显著，Breast Cancer 有一项显著。减少校准层级仍能以大幅降低计算成本逼近高分辨率结果。

Conclusion: 该校准优先框架是一种可行的方法，能在无真实标签和共享标注空间的条件下构建有效监督分数，具有分布无关性、计算高效等优势。

Abstract: We introduce a calibration-first framework that produces supervision scores without access to ground-truth labels or a shared annotation space. Our framework aligns subset-specific scorers using a synthetic ordinal reference space before fusion. This reference space is constructed from ordered calibration features that represent the latent concept, providing a common scale on which otherwise incomparable scorer outputs can be aligned. Because our calibration procedure uses the reference space rather than training samples, it is independent of the training set's empirical distribution. Across three benchmark datasets, our framework consistently outperforms uncalibrated averaging and achieves higher primary-metric point estimates on the evaluation metrics than the best individual scorer. Performance relative to sample-dependent baselines varies by domain, with absolute differences below 0.02 on Ames Housing and below 0.01 on Breast Cancer Wisconsin and Wine Quality. After Bonferroni correction, differences remain significant for all three comparisons on Ames Housing and one on Breast Cancer Wisconsin. Additionally, we show that using fewer calibration levels per feature can closely approximate higher-resolution results at substantially lower computational cost. Together, these results support our framework as a viable approach to construct supervision scores when neither ground-truth labels nor a shared annotation space is available.

</details>


### [269] [CircuitGate: Logic-Consistent Circuit-Level Functional Modeling for And-Inverter Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09549)
*Qifan Zhang,Ruijie Li,Fangzhou Zhang,Qian Ma,Hui Li,Furui Zhan,Yongpeng Wang,Liying Hao,Shikai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CircuitGate，一种面向AIG（与-逆器图）的函数感知表示学习框架，通过显式编码全局主输入支持、建模支持重叠感知的汇聚重汇聚以及引入布尔约束，实现从门级语义到电路级功能建模的跨越，在等效门识别和信号概率预测任务上显著优于现有GNN方法，并展示出强跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的AIG表示学习方法主要依赖局部门级消息传递，难以捕捉电路级功能上下文，且学到的表示对拓扑特定模式敏感，缺乏对功能保持变换的鲁棒性。

Method: CircuitGate框架包含三大核心创新：1）显式编码全局主输入（PI）支持集，捕捉每个节点的功能依赖范围；2）建模支持重叠感知的扇入汇聚重汇聚，量化扇入间功能相关性；3）引入逻辑启发的布尔约束，强制表示满足功能一致性（如等价节点表示应接近）。

Result: 在ForgeEDA、EPFL和ITC'99基准上，CircuitGate在等效门识别和信号概率预测任务上均取得最优性能，MAE分别降低高达21.7%和14.2%。在ForgeEDA到OpenABC的零样本迁移中，CircuitGate也实现最佳等效门识别性能，验证了其强跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，超越局部拓扑建模电路级功能依赖对于AIG表示学习至关重要，CircuitGate通过全局功能建模显著提升了表示的功能感知能力和泛化性。

Abstract: And-Inverter Graphs (AIGs) are fundamental representations for logic synthesis and verification in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). As structured representations of complex digital systems, AIGs require models to capture functional dependencies beyond local structure and remain robust to functionality-preserving transformations. In learning-based AIG representation, existing approaches are predominantly based on GNNs and rely on local gate-level message passing, limiting their ability to capture circuit-level functional context and making the learned representations sensitive to topology-specific patterns. Therefore, we propose CircuitGate, a function-aware AIG representation learning framework that advances from gate-level semantics to circuit-level functional modeling. CircuitGate explicitly encodes global primary-input (PI) support and models support-overlap-aware reconvergence between fanins, while incorporating logic-inspired Boolean constraints to encourage functionally consistent representations. We evaluate CircuitGate on the large-scale ForgeEDA benchmark and further validate it on the EPFL and ITC'99 benchmarks. Across equivalent-gate identification and signal-probability prediction tasks, CircuitGate consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving up to 21.7% and 14.2% reductions in MAE, respectively. Under direct ForgeEDA-to-OpenABC transfer without fine-tuning, CircuitGate also achieves the best equivalent-gate identification performance, demonstrating strong cross-dataset generalization. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of modeling circuit-level functional dependencies beyond local topology.

</details>


### [270] [A Framework for the Systematic Review of ML Assets in AI Registries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09551)
*Alexandra González,Quim Motger,Xavier Franch,Silverio Martínez-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将系统性综述方法应用于机器学习资产（ML assets）检索，构建结构化框架，使ML资产选择过程透明、可复现、基于显性证据，解决当前临时性选择缺乏系统方法论的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统越来越依赖预训练模型、数据集、基准等ML资产，但当前对ML资产的探索、选择和复用缺乏类似传统证据综合的系统检索方法论，导致选择常表现为已定的设计决策，仅凭非正式理由而非可追溯、基于证据且可更新的过程。

Method: 分析科学文献中成熟的系统性综述实践，将其规划、实施、文档化三个阶段迁移到AI注册库，把ML资产作为分析的一等单元；构建包含注册库感知搜索策略、跨注册库模式对齐、依赖驱动ML资产探索的框架。

Result: 将ML资产检索概念化为系统且可复现的过程而非临时活动，提出结构化ML资产发现框架。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了系统性综述原则如何延伸至科学文献之外，支持面向不断演化的AI注册库的证据综合。

Abstract: Background: Modern software systems increasingly rely on Machine Learning (ML) assets (i.e., pre-trained models, datasets, benchmarks) for building, evaluating, and integrating ML-based systems. However, current exploration, selection and reuse practices of ML assets are not supported by systematic retrieval methodologies comparable to those used in traditional evidence synthesis. Consequently, in practice, ML asset selection is often presented as a settled design decision, supported by informal justification rather than a traceable, evidence-based, and updatable selection process. Aims: This paper explores how systematic review methods can support ML asset retrieval. In doing so, we aim to make their selection transparent and reproducible, grounded in explicit evidence, and ultimately better suited to its intended use. Method: We analyze established systematic review practices from scientific literature and adapt their phases (i.e., planning, conducting, and documenting) to Artificial Intelligence (AI) registries, treating ML assets as first-class units of analysis. The resulting framework integrates registry-aware search strategies, cross-registry schema alignment, and dependency-driven ML asset exploration. Results: We conceptualize ML asset retrieval as a systematic and reproducible process rather than an ad hoc activity, and propose a framework for structured ML asset discovery. \textbf{Conclusions:} This work illustrates how systematic review principles can be extended beyond scientific literature to support evidence synthesis over evolving AI registries.

</details>


### [271] [YANchor-4B: Effective Long-Horizon Reasoning in O(N) Time with O(1) Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10118)
*Huishan Ji,Hua Xu,Weiming Zhang,Qirui Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning demands access to earlier information at a manageable generation cost. Full-history attention incurs growing storage and computation, while recurrent compression can lose precise details. Therefore, we present YANchor-4B, a general-purpose recurrent model that preserves crucial memory as ANchors for retrieval during subsequent reasoning. Beyond $O(N)$-time generation and $O(1)$ memory, YANchor enables effective long-horizon reasoning through its multidimensional memory mechanism. For example, on challenging math problems, it achieves 82.93% mean pass@1 on AIME 2024--2026 and 63.64% on HMMT, substantially outperforming linear-time, constant-state counterparts, including larger models. It also delivers several-fold higher batched long-generation throughput than Transformer and hybrid baselines on H100. Furthermore, evaluations across dozens of benchmarks demonstrate YANchor's superiority in general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [272] [UniCSI Towards a Universal Wi-Fi CSI Encoder for Ubiquitous Human Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09559)
*Daniel Eckhoff,Hua Kang,Zhitang Chen,Jie Chuai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 UniCSI，一种统一的基础架构，能直接处理异构 CSI 数据，通过物理感知的 RF 分词器和谱聚合器，保留原始波形完整性，实现跨域迁移的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Wi-Fi 感知中，CSI 受设备配置（子载波数、带宽、载波频段）影响，导致张量形状和谱分辨率异构，标准架构难以建模，需有损预处理，损害信号完整性。

Method: UniCSI 包含两大核心创新：(1) 物理感知 RF 分词器，基于频率通道在物理频谱中的相对位置编码，而非固定数组索引，保持谱相干性，实现分辨率无关处理；(2) 谱聚合器，将变长通道序列蒸馏为固定大小的谱特征，解耦特征维度与子载波间隔。

Result: 在 25 个异构公开数据集（14-2048 子载波，20-160 MHz 带宽，2.4/5 GHz 频段）上实验表明，原生异构摄取在监督和自监督训练下显著提升跨域迁移性能，尤其在固定网格架构失效的场景下效果明显。

Conclusion: UniCSI 通过物理感知的分词和谱聚合，实现了对异构 CSI 的无损统一建模，为 Wi-Fi 感知的跨设备、跨配置泛化提供了有效基础架构。

Abstract: Wi-Fi sensing promises to turn the everyday wireless signals that already surround us into ubiquitous sensors for human sensing. However, a fundamental obstacle is that CSI is acquired under diverse device-specific configurations, including different subcarrier counts, bandwidths, and carrier bands. Consequently, the resulting CSI tensors vary in both spectral resolution and tensor shape, making heterogeneous modeling challenging. Standard architectures struggle with such heterogeneity, forcing lossy pre-processing which compromises the underlying signal. To bridge this gap, we present UniCSI, a unified foundation architecture that directly operates on heterogeneous CSI while preserving the integrity of the native waveform. UniCSI hinges on two core innovations: (1) a physics-informed RF tokenizer that encodes each frequency channel based on its fractional position within the physical spectrum rather than rigid array indices. It preserves intrinsic spectral coherence and enables seamless, frequency resolution-agnostic processing across arbitrary sensing configurations. (2) a spectral aggregator that distills variable-length channel sequences into a fixed-size spectral signature, effectively decoupling the feature dimensionality from the physical subcarrier spacing. Extensive evaluations on a large-scale corpus of 25 heterogeneous public datasets, spanning 14 to 2048 subcarriers, 20 to 160 MHz bandwidth, and the 2.4 and 5 GHz bands, demonstrate that native heterogeneous ingestion substantially improves cross-domain transfer under both supervised and self-supervised training schemes, particularly in regimes where fixed-grid architectures fail to generalize.

</details>


### [273] [EvoSignal: LLM-Guided Evolutionary Design of Modular Traffic Signal Control Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09563)
*Leizhen Wang,Peibo Duan,Zhenlin Qin,Yancheng Ling,Jian Xu,Yue Wang,Hao Wang,Zhenliang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoSignal是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的进化框架，用于交通信号控制（TSC），将TSC建模为模块化程序设计问题，自动生成可解释、无需在线LLM推理即可运行的控制程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSC策略依赖人工反复设计调整，难以系统探索更优控制规则；直接使用LLM选择信号相位会导致决策规则隐藏在黑箱模型中，并产生持续的推理成本和延迟。

Method: 将TSC建模为模块化程序设计：交通特征提取、局部相位优先级、可选的网络级优先级调整。从多个成熟策略出发，利用LLM结合交通知识和性能反馈进行进化搜索，保留具有不同性能权衡的策略。生成的程序无需在线LLM推理即可运行。

Result: 在两个真实路网的五个场景中，默认EvoSignal程序相对于20个基线（常规、强化学习、基于LLM）的最低等待时间减少16.8%-49.2%。一个优先考虑行程时间和排队长度的程序在搜索场景下三个指标均超越所有基线，且直接迁移至其他四个场景时每个指标仍保持前三名。

Conclusion: 研究支持自动化设计可检查、可迁移的控制程序，能够跨越评估的路网和交通需求实现有效泛化。

Abstract: Effective traffic signal control (TSC) requires policies that respond to changing traffic demand and network conditions while meeting different control objectives. However, adapting existing strategies often involves repeated manual design and adjustment, making it difficult to systematically explore better control rules for a target network. Large language models (LLMs) can automate this process, but directly using them to select signal phases leaves decision rules embedded in black-box models and incurs recurring inference costs and latency. This paper formulates TSC as a modular program design problem and proposes EvoSignal, an LLM-guided evolutionary framework using traffic knowledge and performance feedback. The modular representation separates traffic feature extraction, local phase prioritization, and optional network-based priority adjustment. Starting from several established strategies, EvoSignal improves programs through feedback on congestion and signal operation, retaining strategies with different performance trade-offs. The resulting programs operate without online LLM inference. Simulation experiments across five scenarios on two real-world road networks show that the selected default EvoSignal program reduces waiting time by 16.8--49.2\% relative to the lowest waiting time achieved by the 20 conventional, reinforcement learning-based, and LLM-based baselines in each scenario. A program prioritizing travel time and queue length outperforms all 20 baselines on all three metrics in the search scenario and remains among the top three on each metric when transferred unchanged to the other four scenarios. These findings support automated design of inspectable control programs that transfer across the evaluated road networks and traffic demands.Code is available at https://github.com/georgewanglz2019/EvoSignal.

</details>


### [274] [From Prompts to Trees: Effective LLM-Guided Tree Generation for Few-Shot Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10227)
*Yue Qiu,Zekang Du,Yiqun Diao,Bingsheng He,Qinbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将大语言模型知识蒸馏到可解释决策树的框架，用于表格数据分类，采用三阶段范式让LLM生成规则并组织成树，在少样本设置下实现更高准确性和可解释性，且提示开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备丰富世界知识和泛化能力，但直接用于表格数据分类面临推理成本高、可解释性差的问题；决策树虽快速透明，但在低数据场景下表现不佳。因此需要桥接两者，将LLM知识蒸馏至决策树中。

Method: 开发三阶段范式：提示LLM生成规则，再将规则组织成决策树，避免直接生成完整树的不稳定性和低效率。在少样本学习设置下进行知识蒸馏。

Result: 在多个真实表格数据集上实验表明，该方法在准确性和可解释性上优于基线，且显著降低提示开销。

Conclusion: 该框架成功结合LLM知识与决策树优势，为表格数据分类提供了一种高效、可解释的少样本学习解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) possess rich world knowledge and impressive generalization capabilities, their direct application to tabular data classification is hindered by high inference costs and limited interpretability. In contrast, decision trees are fast and transparent but often underperform in low-data regimes. In this work, we propose a novel framework that bridges these paradigms by distilling LLM knowledge into interpretable decision trees under a few-shot learning setting. Instead of directly prompting the LLM to generate full trees, which is often unstable and inefficient, we develop a three-stage paradigm that prompts the LLM to generate rules and organize the rules into a tree. Experiments on multiple real-world tabular datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy and interpretability with significantly lower prompting overhead compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [275] [Online Resource Allocation with an Endogenous Markov State: Fewer LP Solves Earn More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09577)
*Zhaohua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域在线资源分配问题，请求i.i.d.，内生马尔可夫状态。建立瞬态流体LP上界和静态LP控制。在已知先验下，非退化且不可约时频繁/不频繁重解均达O(1)遗憾；退化时不频繁重解达Θ(√T)，频繁重解可能Ω(T)。未知先验下，提出三阶段U型不频繁重解策略，O(log log T)次LP求解，给定目标状态类和入口策略时非退化O(1)、退化O(√T)；无目标类信息线性下界不可避免。实验显示逐轮重解不稳定，阈值化缓解，不频繁方案占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线资源分配多假设静态环境或无状态动态，本文解决内生马尔可夫状态下的有限时域问题，其中动作影响状态转移进而影响未来奖励和资源消耗，需平衡即时收益与长期状态演化，且先验可能未知。

Method: 使用瞬态流体LP作为上界基准，静态LP提供随机化状态相关控制。假设静态LP唯一最优，引入原始非退化性和最优诱导核不可约性作为正则条件。设计频繁/不频繁重解策略，以及未知先验下的三阶段U型不频繁重解算法（学习、库存修正、利用），结合阈值化技术。

Result: 已知先验：非退化+不可约 → 两类重解均O(1)遗憾；退化+不可约 → 不频繁重解Θ(√T)最优，频繁重解Ω(T)更差。未知先验：给定目标类和入口策略，非退化O(1)，退化O(√T)；无目标类线性遗憾下界。实验验证不频繁重解优于逐轮重解，阈值化改善。

Conclusion: 在内生马尔可夫状态资源分配中，不频繁重解在渐近性能上优于频繁重解，尤其在退化情况下；未知先验时需协调学习与库存修正，三阶段U型策略近似最优；目标状态类信息对达次线性遗憾至关重要；实践中应采用epoch-wise重解并结合阈值化。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon online resource allocation with i.i.d. requests and an endogenous Markov state on a finite state space: each action affects the transition of the state that governs future rewards and resource consumption. In this problem, a transient fluid LP benchmark upper bounds the expected reward of every nonanticipating policy, while a stationary LP supplies randomized state-dependent controls. We assume that the stationary LP has a unique optimum and identify primal nondegeneracy and irreducibility of the optimal induced kernel as important regularity conditions in this framework. With a known request prior, we show that, under nondegeneracy and irreducibility, both frequent and infrequent re-solving attain $O(1)$ regret. However, under a degenerate optimum, irreducibility yields the sharp worst-case $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ rate for infrequent re-solving, while frequent re-solving can incur $Ω(T)$ regret. Thus, more frequent optimization can perform asymptotically worse. With an unknown request prior, we develop a three-phase U-shaped infrequent re-solving policy that coordinates learning and inventory correction with $O(\log\log T)$ LP solves. When the optimal induced kernel is irreducible and the algorithm is given the optimal target state class and a constant-cost entrance policy, it attains $O(1)$ regret under nondegeneracy and $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret under degeneracy. Without the target-class information, linear minimax regret is unavoidable. Numerical experiments further illustrate the instability of round-by-round re-solving relative to epoch-wise infrequent re-solving, show that thresholding greatly mitigates its loss, and find that infrequent schemes remain dominant under both known and estimated priors.

</details>


### [276] [PatchBench: Measuring Collateral Damage in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10276)
*Alexi Canesse,Mathis Le Bail,Maël Jenny,Clément Elliker,Mahammed El Sharkawy,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PatchBench 基准及其局部评估协议 PatchBench-Local，旨在精准评估 LLM 越狱修补的行为精确性，区分选择性修复与广泛的局部抑制。实验表明，全局能力指标（如 MMLU）可能保持不变，但局部良性回归严重，现有聚合指标会遗漏重要的附带损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 安全补丁可能通过基准测试却实为糟糕的修复：要么仅拦截确切的评估提示词而对近似有害变体失效，要么因过度拒绝共享措辞或结构的良性提示词而抑制有害行为。当前协议主要测试模型是否可被攻破，聚合指标（攻击成功率、拒绝率、全局能力）无法区分选择性修复与更广泛的局部抑制。

Method: 从 37 个公开数据集的 27,870 个提示词出发，筛选 15,314 个英文提示词并查询 8 个开源指令微调模型；结合 WildGuard 过滤、成对 Elo 排序与人工核验，构建包含 400 个高置信度越狱失败样本的精选库。进一步提出 PatchBench-Local 协议：为每个有害源提示词生成三族局部邻居——保持恶意意图的有害变体、结构匹配的良性提示词、复用关键有害术语的良性提示词；分别评估有害邻居的纠正率与良性邻居的保留率，以区分选择性修复与局部抑制。

Result: 在 PatchBench-Local 与 MMLU 上评估四种激活向量引导方法显示：全局能力几乎不变，但局部良性回归严重，证实聚合指标会遗漏关键的附带损害。

Conclusion: PatchBench-Local 为开发和比较越狱修复方法提供了更精准的评估基准，能够揭示传统全局指标忽视的局部行为退化。

Abstract: An LLM safety patch can pass a benchmark while still being a poor repair. This risk is especially acute for jailbreak repairs, where the goal is to correct a specific unsafe behaviour without changing unrelated behaviours. A patch may block exact evaluation prompts yet fail on close harmful variants, or suppress harmful behaviour by over-refusing benign prompts that share its wording or structure. Existing protocols primarily test whether models can be broken, while aggregate metrics (attack success, refusal rates, global capability) cannot distinguish selective repairs from broader local suppression. To address this gap, we introduce PatchBench, a benchmark of empirically observed model-specific jailbreak failures inducing actionable harmful answers. Starting from 27,870 prompts from 37 public datasets, we curate 15,314 English prompts and query 8 open-source instruction-tuned models. Combining WildGuard filtering, pairwise Elo ranking, and manual verification, we retain a curated bank of 400 high-confidence jailbreak failures. We further introduce PatchBench-Local, an evaluation protocol testing whether a patch is behaviourally precise. For each harmful source prompt, PatchBench-Local generates three families of local neighbours: harmful variants preserving malicious intent, benign prompts with matched structure, and benign prompts reusing key harmful terms. It evaluates harmful-neighbour correction and benign-neighbour preservation, distinguishing selective repair from broader local suppression. Evaluating four activation steering methods with PatchBench-Local and MMLU shows that global capability can remain nearly unchanged while local benign regressions are severe, confirming aggregate metrics miss important collateral damage. PatchBench-Local provides a more precise basis for developing and comparing jailbreak repair methods.

</details>


### [277] [SemanticFold: Latent Sequence Compression SeparatesLanguage Modeling, Decodability, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10304)
*Mingyan Liu,Min Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究前缀潜在序列压缩是否保留大语言模型推理能力，提出SemanticFold压缩方案，在五种模型规模上评估，发现压缩对不同评估指标影响非单调且无统一阈值，语言模型拟合、可解码性和推理行为在同一压缩操作下可能朝不同方向变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究前缀潜在序列压缩是否保留大语言模型推理时依赖的能力，现有工作缺乏系统性评估压缩对多维能力的影响。

Method: 提出SemanticFold压缩方案，在学习边界折叠前缀隐藏状态；采用固定目标协议：冻结前缀原生执行或压缩执行，两臂教师强制相同续写token，排除目标选择对似然变化的解释；在五种模型规模（Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-8B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Pythia-1.4B, Pythia-6.9B）上评估五类端点：固定目标负对数似然、有限标签推理准确率、线性探针可访问性、开放式生成、系统级内存和延迟。

Result: 压缩使各端点非单调变化，无单一压缩阈值。Qwen3-1.7B在压缩比R=1.7时压缩减去原生平均NLL下降0.135；SmolLM2在R=1.2时平均变化比原生高0.013；两个Pythia检查点NLL基本不变。NLL分解显示Qwen有利似然主要归因于残差适应而非单纯序列缩短。仅MLP变换（不缩短）比完整SemanticFold低0.082 NLL。线性探针准确率和宏AUC绝对变化<0.03，置信区间跨零。

Conclusion: 潜在压缩下的保留性无单一标量证书：语言模型拟合、可解码性和推理行为回答不同问题，在同一压缩操作下可能朝不同方向变化。

Abstract: We study whether latent sequence compression of prompt prefixes preserves the capabilities that large language models rely on during inference. We introduce SemanticFold, a compression scheme that folds prefix hidden states at learned boundaries, and evaluate it across five model scales: Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-8B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Pythia-1.4B, and Pythia-6.9B. We use a fixed-target protocol: a frozen prefix is executed natively or compressed, and both arms teacher-force identical continuation tokens. This design rules out target-selection explanations for likelihood changes. We examine five endpoint families: fixed-target negative log-likelihood, finite-label reasoning accuracy, linear probe accessibility, open-ended generation, and systems-level memory and latency. We find that compression moves these endpoints non-monotonically and that they do not share a single compression threshold. On Qwen3-1.7B at compression ratio R=1.7, compressed-minus-native mean NLL decreases by 0.135 under paired bootstrap with 10000 draws. On SmolLM2 at R=1.2, the mean change is 0.013 higher than native. On both Pythia checkpoints, NLL is effectively unchanged. An NLL decomposition separating sequence shortening from the learned residual transform shows that the favorable Qwen likelihood is attributable primarily to residual adaptation rather than to shortening alone. MLP-only, which applies the transform without shortening, achieves 0.082 lower NLL than Full SemanticFold. Linear probe accuracy and macro AUC change by less than 0.03 in absolute value across conditions, with confidence intervals crossing zero. We conclude that preservation under latent compression has no single scalar certificate: language-model fit, decodability, and reasoning behavior answer different questions and can move in different directions under the same compression operation.

</details>


### [278] [COPC: Coupled Off-Policy Correction for Asynchronous LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09597)
*Zicheng Hu,Zhijian Zhou,Xuan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Cheng Chen,Yuan Li,Qi Gu,Yan Feng,Hongyan Hao,Chao Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示异步RL中仅靠策略端修正（重要性比率控制）不足，优势估计也会因行为策略延续而产生“优势陈旧性”。作者推导了双通道演员更新的精确偏差/方差分解，发现策略权重误差与优势估计误差存在不可分离的耦合，进而提出耦合离线策略修正（COPC），结合token级比率掩码与双侧裁剪比率加权TD残差，在工具集成数学推理与搜索任务上取得最优性能，且在64步策略陈旧度下保持稳定，步时间仅比异步PPO微增、比同步PPO快1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步RL通过解耦采样与优化加速LLM后训练，但会引入陈旧轨迹。现有工作主要在演员目标中用重要性比率控制做token级策略端修正，忽略了优势估计同样继承行为策略延续带来的分布偏移（优势陈旧性）。作者旨在从理论上量化这种双重偏差的耦合效应，并设计协同修正机制。

Method: 1. 推导通用双通道演员更新的精确偏差与方差分解，揭示策略权重误差与优势估计误差的乘性偏差项及平方权重放大优势不确定性的方差项。2. 提出COPC：演员端使用token级比率掩码；评论家端对TD残差施加双侧裁剪比率加权，同时修正回报与优势估计。两端修正参数联合调节，实现策略端与优势端的协同修正。

Result: 在工具集成数学推理与搜索基准上，COPC均超越最强异步基线；拥有宽广的高性能超参数区域，训练稳定性显著提升（搜索任务中基线多在后期崩溃，COPC全程稳定）；在64步策略陈旧度下增益依然保持；步时间开销极小，保持相对同步PPO 1.7倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 策略端与优势端修正必须协同设计，单边修正会因误差耦合导致性能下降甚至训练崩溃。COPC通过耦合离线策略修正有效解决异步RL中的双重陈旧性问题，兼顾高性能、稳定性与训练效率，为大规模LLM后训练提供了实用的异步RL范式。

Abstract: Asynchronous RL accelerates large language model post-training by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but trains on stale trajectories. Existing methods primarily correct token-level policy mismatch through importance-ratio control in the actor objective. We show that this \emph{policy-side correction} alone is insufficient: advantage estimates also inherit mismatch from behavior-policy continuations, which we term \emph{advantage staleness}. We derive exact bias and variance decompositions for a general two-channel actor update, revealing nonseparable coupling between policy-weight and advantage-estimation errors: their interaction induces multiplicative bias terms, while squared policy weights amplify advantage uncertainty in gradient variance. This motivates the hypothesis that policy- and advantage-side correction should be coordinated. We introduce Coupled Off-Policy Correction (COPC), an actor--critic method combining token-level ratio masking with two-sided clipped-ratio weighting of TD residuals for return and advantage estimation. Joint parameter sweeps across staleness levels support this hypothesis: the effect of one correction parameter depends on, and can reverse with, the other. COPC achieves the highest reported performance on tool-integrated mathematical reasoning and search, outperforming the strongest reported asynchronous baseline in each setting. It also offers a broad high-performing parameter region and improved training stability. In search, COPC remains stable throughout training, while most evaluated asynchronous baselines collapse late in training. These gains persist at 64-step policy staleness. COPC adds minimal step-time overhead over asynchronous PPO and retains a $1.7\times$ step-time speedup over synchronous PPO.

</details>


### [279] [Input-Blind Controls Produce Substantial Oracle Headroom for Layer Programs in Multiple-Choice Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10368)
*Yibei Guo,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了自适应层计算（跳过/重复层）在两个语言模型上的实际收益，发现输入盲扰动基线在共享选项顺序下能产生与真实程序相当甚至更大的提升空间，且这种提升空间跨提示词持续存在，但无法证明选中的层计算本身具有特定优势；仅在选项顺序打乱或KL校准等更严格对照下，真实程序才显示出微弱且不稳定的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应计算旨在通过为每个输入定制执行路径来提升语言模型推理效率。现有的预言机评估利用已知答案估算层程序选择的潜在收益，但选择带来的收益本身无法解释为何被选程序会有帮助。本研究旨在区分“选择带来的提升空间”与“被选计算本身的特定贡献”。

Method: 在 Qwen3-4B-Base 和 Llama-3.1-8B 两个模型上，使用 32 个层跳过/重复程序和 4,413 道多项选择题。将真实程序的增益（相对无评估提示词的固定动作）与同位置的输入盲扰动基线进行对比，并在另一提示词上重新评估选择。进一步引入 KL 散度校准对比、固定字母偏移对照、选项轮换实验，以及生成式回答的补充实验。

Result: 共享选项顺序下，输入盲扰动基线在 Qwen 和 Llama 上分别提供 10.2–11.8 和 15.6–19.4 个百分点的提升空间，均超过真实程序的 9.0 和 10.1 点；两者仅匹配答案改变率，排序依赖程序菜单（Llama 的仅重复菜单上真实程序在每次抽样中领先）。KL 校准的小规模对比中真实程序点估计略优但校正后检验不显著。固定字母偏移产生同量级提升空间。选项轮换大幅降低两类方法的提升空间，但真实程序仍保留 1.4–2.3 和 3.7–4.5 点的正向差异，其大小与显著性依赖进一步调整与参考基线。生成式回答补充实验显示，搜索选中的程序在改写问题后仍比为其他问题选中的程序保持 26.0 点优势，但缺乏安慰剂对照。

Conclusion: 在共享选项顺序的提示词间，可观的提升空间可以持续存在，但这并不确立被选层计算的特定收益；无论是输入盲扰动还是 KL 校准对照，目前的排序比较都无法可靠地识别出该特定收益。

Abstract: Adaptive computation aims to improve language-model inference by tailoring execution to each input. For layer programs, oracle evaluations use known answers to estimate the potential gain from this flexibility, before a practical selector is available. However, a gain from selection does not by itself explain why the chosen programs help. This study examines this distinction using 32 layer-skipping and repetition programs on two models and 4,413 multiple-choice items. The analysis compares their gains over a fixed action selected without the evaluation prompt with those of input-blind perturbations at the same sites, re-evaluating selections on another prompt. With shared option order, the controls give 10.2-11.8 and 15.6-19.4 percentage points of headroom on Qwen3-4B-Base and Llama-3.1-8B, exceeding the real programs' 9.0 and 10.1 in all three random-direction draws per model. They match answer-change rate only, and the ordering depends on the menu: in post hoc comparisons, real programs lead on Llama's repeat-only menu in every draw. A smaller KL-calibrated comparison, including an input-dependent control, favours real programs in point estimate, with inconclusive corrected tests. Fixed letter offsets produce headroom of similar scale. Rotating options sharply reduces both families' headroom, while leaving positive real-minus-control differences of 1.4-2.3 and 3.7-4.5 points; their magnitudes and statistical support depend on further adjustments and the reference. A supplementary generated-answer test finds that search-selected programs keep a 26.0-point advantage over programs selected for other problems after rewording, without a placebo comparison. These results show that substantial headroom can persist across prompts with shared option order without establishing a benefit specific to the selected layer computation; neither ordering against these controls identifies that benefit.

</details>


### [280] [Lightweight and Versatile Learned Optimization by Recombination of Gradient History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09604)
*Minyoung Choi,Dalta Imam Maulana,Wanyeong Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种轻量级且通用的学习优化器，通过动态重组不同时间跨度的梯度历史平均值，实现了零样本泛化到未见任务，在多个基准模型上显著优于Adam，同时计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习优化器通常存在参数量大、内存开销高、泛化能力有限等问题。需要一种轻量级优化器，能够高效利用长期梯度历史，同时保持低内存和计算成本，并具备跨任务零样本泛化能力。

Method: 将梯度历史表示为不同时间跨度的平均值，动态重组这些平均值。将预测空间压缩为每个梯度平均值一个共享的标量系数，多参数共享系数。通过渐进式平均旧梯度最小化长历史内存成本，同时保持各时间跨度贡献的独立可访问性。

Result: 37k参数网络在0.87 GPU-hours训练后零样本泛化：BERT-Tiny验证损失降低9.1%，GPT-Tiny降低0.4%；Vision Transformer测试准确率比Adam提升3.5个百分点；九个图模型平均提升2.7个百分点；FLOPs开销仅0.3%。

Conclusion: 该优化器通过创新的梯度历史分时平均表示和共享系数机制，实现了极致的轻量化与高泛化性的统一，为学习优化器的实际部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a lightweight and versatile learned optimizer that dynamically recombines gradient history, represented as averages over disjoint time spans. The optimizer reduces the prediction space to one scalar coefficient per gradient average, shared by multiple parameters. Progressively averaging older gradients minimizes memory cost of long history, while keeping their contributions independently accessible. A 37k-parameter network trained in 0.87 GPU-hours generalizes zero-shot to unseen tasks, lowering validation loss by 9.1% and 0.4% on BERT-Tiny and GPT-Tiny, and improving test accuracy over Adam by 3.5 %p on a Vision Transformer and by 2.7 %p on average across nine graph models, with FLOPs overhead as low as 0.3%.

</details>


### [281] [Decoupled Optimization for Teacher-Student Semi-Supervised Learning via a Pioneer Student](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09609)
*Haorong Han,Jidong Yuan,Chixuan Wei,Yongqi Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出半监督学习中教师-学生框架的参数耦合和标注/无标注损失梯度不平衡两大优化病理，提出先锋学生模块作为独立参数空间的辅助分支，周期性回传知识，作为通用即插即用组件持续提升主流SSL方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL依赖教师-学生自训练和标注/无标注损失联合优化，但存在两个优化病理：1) 参数耦合导致教师-学生强制同步，学生端强正则化削弱教师拟合能力，限制泛化强度；2) 标注与无标注损失梯度更新一致性不平衡，共享参数过早收敛到标注主导的局部最优，造成全局优化瓶颈。

Method: 提出先锋学生模块，在独立参数空间运行的辅助分支，周期性地将积累的知识回传给教师-学生模型，解耦参数更新并缓解梯度不平衡，作为通用即插即用模块集成到主流SSL方法中。

Result: 大量实验表明，PiS作为通用即插即用模块，能持续改进主流半监督学习方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入独立参数空间的先锋学生分支并周期性知识回传，有效解决了教师-学生参数耦合和标注/无标注梯度不平衡两大优化病理，为半监督学习提供了一个通用性能增强组件。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) relies on two core mechanisms: self-training under the Teacher-Student (T-S) framework and joint optimization of labeled and unlabeled losses. Despite their effectiveness, we find both mechanisms introduce distinct optimization pathologies. First, parameter coupling enforces strict synchronization between teacher and student, where strong regularization on the student degrades the teacher's fitting ability, thereby limiting the permissible generalization intensity. Second, the imbalance in gradient update consistency between labeled and unlabeled losses drives the shared parameters to prematurely converge to labeled-dominated local minima, creating a bottleneck for global optimization. To address both issues, we propose the Pioneer Student (PiS), an auxiliary branch that operates in an independent parameter space and periodically transfers accumulated knowledge back to the T-S model. Extensive experiments show that PiS is a universal plug-and-play module that consistently improves mainstream SSL methods.

</details>


### [282] [Training Parallel Speculative Draft Models by Directly Minimizing Expected Decoding Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10411)
*Yunxiao Zhao,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推测解码建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，推导出期望解码轮数(EDR)目标，该目标精确等于预期解码轮数，无需辅助超参数，并给出精确的时序差分梯度用于无偏随机优化，同时提供离线评估器实现无需运行推测解码的成对比较。在九个基准上微调DSpark和DFly验证了EDR的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行和半自回归起草器虽能单次前向传播提出整个块，但其训练面临跨轮耦合难题：给定位置的草稿分布取决于解码轮的起始位置，而起始位置又取决于前几轮接受的token数。现有训练目标通常依赖块局部代理目标，忽略这种跨轮耦合，因此无法直接优化全局解码效率。

Method: 将推测解码形式化为马尔可夫奖励过程，推导出期望解码轮数(EDR)目标——该目标用状态占用度加权局部拒绝成本，精确等于预期解码轮数。进一步推导精确的时序差分梯度，支持从目标模型采样轨迹进行无偏随机优化。同一框架还产出精确的离线轮数评估器，允许在共享目标模型采样轨迹上进行成对起草器比较，无需实际运行推测解码。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和聊天等九个基准测试中，使用EDR目标微调两个最先进的起草器DSpark和DFly，持续提升平均接受长度，且性能优于现有的训练目标。

Conclusion: EDR框架为推测解码起草器的训练与评估提供了统一的理论基础：它消除了对辅助超参数的需求，直接优化全局解码效率，并通过精确的梯度估计和离线评估器实现了高效、可复现的模型比较与改进。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a low-cost draft model to propose tokens that the full-size target model verifies in parallel. Parallel and semi-autoregressive (semi- AR) drafters improve drafting efficiency by proposing an entire block in a single forward pass, but training them raises a new difficulty: the draft distribution for a given position depends on where the decoding round starts, and where rounds start depends on how many tokens earlier rounds accepted. Existing training objectives typically rely on block-local surrogates that ignore this cross-round coupling, and therefore do not directly optimize the global decoding efficiency. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for training and evaluating these drafters by representing speculative decoding as a Markov reward process. This formulation yields the Expected Decoding Rounds (EDR) objective, which weights local rejection costs by state occupancies and exactly equals the expected number of decoding rounds. Unlike prior surrogate objectives, EDR introduces no auxiliary hyperparameters. We then derive an exact temporal-difference gradient that supports unbiased stochastic optimization from target-model rollouts. The same framework also yields an exact offline evaluator for round counts, enabling paired drafter comparisons on shared target rollouts without running speculative decoding. Finetuning two state-of-the- art drafters, DSpark and DFly, with EDR consistently improves mean accepted length and outperforms existing training objectives across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and chat.

</details>


### [283] [Sequential Pretraining Favors Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09611)
*Mohnish Harwani,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究大型神经网络优于小型模型的原因：是学习更具代表性的特征，还是对训练不良效应更鲁棒。定义并量化了“首因偏差”——早期数据分布对后期学习的损害程度。发现小模型会低效地将学习容量分配给早期分布，而充分过参数化的模型对此具有鲁棒性。这在预训练中尤为关键，因为基础模型通常按顺序而非联合遇到异构数据分布。提出“暴露疗法”（ET）正则化，促进顺序预训练中学习容量的更高效分配。实验表明ET能提升十亿参数规模模型在后期数据分布上的性能及整体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型基础模型优势的来源：是学习更好的特征表示，还是对训练过程中不良效应（如首因偏差）更鲁棒。在预训练中，模型按顺序遇到异构数据分布（而非联合训练），小模型难以学习后期出现的分布（如代码、数学、推理等理想能力），这促使研究首因偏差及其缓解方法。

Method: 1. 定义并量化“首因偏差”：早期数据分布暴露对后期学习的损害程度。2. 提出“暴露疗法”（ET）正则化：一种简单的正则化方法，旨在促进顺序预训练期间学习容量的更高效分配。

Result: 小模型倾向于低效地将学习容量分配给早期数据分布，而充分过参数化的大模型对首因偏差具有鲁棒性。ET正则化显著提升了基础模型在后期数据分布上的性能，并改善了十亿参数规模模型的整体能力。

Conclusion: 大型基础模型的部分优势可能源于对不良训练效应（如首因偏差）的更强鲁棒性，而非学习更具代表性的特征。改进的训练算法（如ET）可以在较小模型中恢复部分大模型优势。

Abstract: Large neural networks often acquire capabilities that small models fail to learn. Does this stem from large models learning more representative features, or from being more robust to unaccounted-for adverse effects introduced during training? We define and quantify one such adverse effect, primacy bias, as the extent to which exposure to early data distributions impairs later learning. We show that small models can allocate learning capacity inefficiently toward early distributions, whereas sufficiently overparameterized models are robust to this effect. This inefficiency is particularly consequential in pretraining, where foundation models often encounter heterogeneous data distributions sequentially rather than jointly. As a result, small foundation models can struggle to learn distributions encountered late in training, which is particularly harmful when later data emphasizes desirable capabilities such as code, mathematics, and reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we introduce Exposure Therapy (ET), a simple regularization that promotes more efficient allocation of learning capacity during sequential pretraining. We demonstrate that ET improves foundation models' performance on late data distributions as well as overall capability in models up to the billion-parameter scale. Overall, our results suggest that some benefits of large foundation models may arise from greater robustness to adverse training effects, rather than from learning more representative features, and that improved training algorithms can recover some of these advantages in smaller models.

</details>


### [284] [Which Rollout Taught It That? BehaviorTrace and the Limits of Training-Data Attribution in Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10422)
*Amit Nautiyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在GRPO在线RL微调中能否追踪到教会语言模型新行为的训练rollouts，发现大多数归因信号来自混淆因素（梯度幅度、流畅度），唯有触发token的梯度与实际行为发生位置的目标对齐这一信号在三个种子上均稳健。作者发布了BehaviorTrace评测框架，并给出了RL归因评估清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当强化学习教会语言模型新行为时，我们能否找到教会它的训练rollouts？当归因方法声称能做到时，如何验证答案是真实的？现有归因方法在RL微调场景下缺乏严格的因果验证和混淆因素控制。

Method: 使用植入行为（planted behavior）且已知因果关系的设置，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上进行GRPO在线RL微调，跨三个随机种子实验。发布BehaviorTrace评测框架，结合全梯度速写（full-gradient sketching）、植入行为设置，并控制梯度幅度、流畅度、余量和跨种子/生成抽样的变异。测试了GAS（重归一化TracInCP）和TRAK风格估计器等现有方法。

Result: 1) 梯度幅度单独排序（无行为目标）达到随机基线4.2-4.5倍，在三个种子中的两个上匹配或超越最佳目标估计器；2) 在饱和检查点，模型流畅度预测行为标签至少与所有梯度方法相当；3) 控制流畅度后，逐rollout结果随种子和生成抽样变化，单次运行无法定论；4) 唯一稳健信号：触发token的梯度与行为实际发生位置构建的目标对齐，在三个种子上均成立。

Conclusion: RL归因评估必须控制梯度幅度和流畅度等混淆因素，需跨多种子和多代理抽样验证稳健性。作者将发现转化为RL归因评估清单，测试了现有估计器但未提出新方法。BehaviorTrace为该领域提供了开放评测基准。

Abstract: When reinforcement learning teaches a language model a new behavior, can we find the training rollouts that taught it? And when an attribution method says it can, how do we know the answer is real? We study both questions on online RL fine-tuning with GRPO, using a planted behavior with a known cause. We release BehaviorTrace, an open evaluation harness that combines full-gradient sketching, the planted-behavior setup, and controls for gradient magnitude, fluency, headroom, and variation across seeds and generation draws. Across three seeds on Qwen2.5-1.5B, much of the apparent attribution signal comes from confounds. A control that ranks training steps by gradient size alone, with no behavior target, reaches 4.2 to 4.5 times chance and matches or beats the best targeted estimator on two of three seeds. At saturated checkpoints, model fluency predicts the behavior label at least as well as every gradient method we compared it with. Once fluency is controlled, the per-rollout results change from seed to seed and from one generation draw to the next, so a single run cannot settle the question. One signal does hold on all three seeds. The gradient of the trigger tokens aligns with a target built where the behavior actually occurs. We turn these findings into a checklist for evaluating attribution in RL. We test existing estimators, including GAS (renormalized TracInCP) and a TRAK-style estimator, and do not propose a new one.

</details>


### [285] [The Identifiability and Observability of Deep Normalized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09620)
*Pranav Venkata Konda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度、无掩码、单头注意力机制中由输入输出函数决定的参数。对于已知的正非恒定实解析归一化器，函数一般决定有效分数和组合值映射，仅受偶归一化器诱导的符号影响。这证明了Henry-Marchetti-Kohn猜想的实解析情况，包括softmax。分类了显式归一化器条件下的特例纤维，识别了塌陷使后续分数不可观测的情况，建立了局部识别的精确泰勒阶。在同时查询/键塌陷附近，计算了在分离有限输入库上的完整原生雅可比衰减谱。对于公共首个非恒定归一化器度数$k$，第$i$层接触阶为$2k3^{i-1}-1$，具有精确重数和核维度。高精度和自动微分计算展示了由此产生的数值敏感性丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解注意力机制中哪些参数由输入输出函数唯一确定，解决Henry-Marchetti-Kohn猜想的实解析情况，分析参数识别的局部性质和数值敏感性。

Method: 理论分析：利用实解析函数理论，研究归一化器条件下的参数识别问题；分类特例纤维；计算雅可比矩阵衰减谱；推导接触阶公式。数值实验：高精度计算和自动微分验证理论预测。

Result: 证明了对于正非恒定实解析归一化器，输入输出函数一般决定有效分数和组合值映射（除偶归一化器符号外），证实了猜想。给出了塌陷导致后续分数不可观测的条件，建立了精确泰勒阶。推导了接触阶公式：第$i$层接触阶为$2k3^{i-1}-1$，给出精确重数和核维度。数值实验展示了数值敏感性的丧失。

Conclusion: 本文完整表征了深度单头注意力的参数可识别性，解决了实解析情况下的猜想，揭示了塌陷现象对参数观测的影响，并量化了数值计算中的敏感性衰减，为注意力机制的理论理解和数值实现提供了重要洞见。}

Abstract: We study which parameters of deep, unmasked, single-head attention are determined by its input--output function. For known positive nonconstant real-analytic normalizers, the function generically determines the effective scores and combined value map up to the signs induced by even normalizers. This proves the real-analytic case of a conjecture of Henry--Marchetti--Kohn, including softmax. We then classify exceptional fibers under explicit normalizer conditions, identifying when collapse makes later scores unobservable, and establish sharp Taylor orders for local identification. Near simultaneous query/key collapse, we compute the complete native Jacobian decay spectrum on separating finite input banks. For common first nonconstant normalizer degree $k$, layer $i$ has contact order $2k3^{i-1}-1$, with exact multiplicities and kernel dimension. High-precision and automatic differentiation calculations illustrate the resulting loss of numerical sensitivity.

</details>


### [286] [When does a network's training history predict its future learning better than its current state? Evidence from a response probe and a forecasting screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09621)
*Martin Hofmann,Patrick Mäder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络的训练历史何时能比当前状态更好地预测未来学习能力。通过两项实验发现：训练历史仅在当前状态尚未对目标任务具有信息量时（训练早期）提供预测价值；一旦当前状态变得具有信息量，历史不再提供额外预测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于可塑性丧失和关键期的研究表明训练路径影响后续学习，但尚不清楚路径本身是否包含当前状态测量所遗漏的信息。本文旨在回答：网络的训练历史何时能比其当前状态更好地预测未来学习表现？

Method: 包含两项研究：(1) 主要研究：在三种历史机制下训练小型多层感知机（共42个历史），在四个检查点用探针任务（复制网络在新任务上训练100步）测量未来学习；包含单调性检查、重测信度（类内相关系数0.940）、重新初始化可见性验证。比较最多4维的历史状态模型与校准的当前状态模型。(2) 配套筛选：1,560次合成回归实验，预测最多240个epoch后的最终误差，对比历史模型与当前验证误差的预测能力。

Result: 主要研究：历史状态模型未改进校准的当前状态模型（增益-21.4%，90%区间[-91.9, 8.1]；预设阈值10%）。配套研究：历史模型在240个epoch中的前12个epoch优于当前验证误差（紧凑状态30.3%，[15.8, 39.4]），48个epoch后两者不可区分。两项研究均显示：历史仅在当前状态尚未对目标具有信息量时提供信息量。

Conclusion: 训练历史对未来学习的预测价值具有阶段性：仅在训练早期、当前网络状态尚未编码足够目标任务相关信息时，历史提供额外预测增益；随着训练推进，当前状态逐渐成为充分统计量，历史信息变得冗余。这揭示了神经网络学习动力学中状态与路径信息的时序互补关系。

Abstract: Networks that behave alike now can still learn differently when training continues. Work on loss of plasticity and critical periods shows that the path to a state shapes what follows; it does not show whether the path carries information that a measurement of the state itself misses. We ask when the training history of a network predicts its future learning better than its current state. In a main study, small multilayer perceptrons were trained under three history regimes (42 histories), and future learning was measured at four checkpoints by a short probe: a copy of the network trained for 100 updates on a new task. Before the prediction result was read, the protocol checked the probe. It responded monotonically to a function-preserving rescaling of hidden units, repeated measurements agreed (intraclass correlation 0.940, [0.903, 0.997], in the least reliable class, mean of three repeats), and a re-initialisation of units was visible directly after it but not 100 to 200 updates later. A history state of at most four dimensions did not improve on a calibrated model of the current state (gain -21.4%, 90% interval [-91.9, 8.1]; required in advance: 10%). A companion screen on 1,560 synthetic regression runs asked the same question for a target further away, the final error of the run. There, history models forecast better than the current validation error after 12 of up to 240 epochs (compact state 30.3%, [15.8, 39.4], a contextual comparison) and were not distinguishable from it after 48. In both studies the history was informative only while the current state was not yet informative about the target; this reading was formed after the results.

</details>


### [287] [Decoupling Exploration from Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536)
*Saif Punjwani,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis)框架，将探索与优化解耦，通过带新颖性奖励的探索器生成轨迹，经筛选后蒸馏给无新颖性奖励的学生策略，多轮交替训练，在数学推理基准上超越DAPO并提升pass@k多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽承诺发现新推理策略，但强新颖性激励在实践中成功有限且降低模型质量，因为可验证奖励仅监督模型知识和行为的狭窄切片，导致退化难以恢复。

Method: 训练一个或多个带有新颖性奖励的探索器策略，筛选其轨迹的正确性与质量，蒸馏到无新颖性奖励的独立学生策略中；重复多轮，在探索与优化间交替，实现探索与优化的解耦。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准和两个模型族上，ExpDis在相同时钟预算下超越DAPO；观察到pass@k扩展性改进，表明ExpDis生成的模型能产出更多样化的正确解。

Conclusion: 解耦探索与优化的ExpDis框架允许激进地扩大探索规模而不损害学生策略，有效提升模型推理多样性与性能，为RLVR中的新颖性引入提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern language models undergo reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) on top of already-trained checkpoints. A key promise of RLVR is the discovery of new reasoning strategies. In principle, a model can sample novel ideas absent from its prior training data. In practice, however, augmenting RLVR with strong novelty incentives has seen limited success and can degrade model quality. Because verifiable rewards supervise only a narrow slice of the model's knowledge and behavior, such degradations are difficult to recover from. Instead, we decouple exploration from optimization in a framework we call Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis). We train one or more explorer policies with a novelty bonus in the reward, filter their trajectories for correctness and quality, and distill them into a separate student policy. The student policy is then trained without a novelty bonus. We repeat the above procedure for several rounds, alternating between exploration and optimization. This decoupling allows us to aggressively scale exploration without degrading the student policy. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two model families, ExpDis outperforms DAPO at the same wall-clock budget. Moreover, we observe improved pass@$k$ scaling, indicating that ExpDis produces models that generate more diverse correct solutions.

</details>


### [288] [Closed-Form Noise Calibration Against Membership Inference for Random-Allocation DP-SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09651)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Marten van Dijk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对随机分配的DP-SGD，提出了成员推理攻击（MIA）准确率的闭式上界公式及精确表达式，显著降低了所需噪声量并将隐私预算计算加速至秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP-SGD隐私核算依赖数值搜索，计算耗时且噪声量较大；亟需一种可解释、快速且更紧致的闭式隐私边界，以直接确定噪声乘数σ。

Method: 利用卡方散度分析高斯分布与高斯混合分布，推导出MIA准确率的闭式上界：1/2 + 1/4√((1+(e^{1/σ^2}-1)/M)^E-1)；进一步推导两分布下的精确攻击准确率表达式并数值计算。

Result: 闭式公式所需噪声最多仅为现有最优闭式边界的一半；精确表达式比公式再减少13.0%-20.2%噪声且为理论最优；实际训练中σ在秒级找到、分钟级认证，优于公式并匹配已发布核算器测试精度，搜索耗时更短或噪声更低；实验验证MIA准确率均低于边界。

Conclusion: 提出的闭式公式与精确表达式为随机分配DP-SGD提供了高效、可解释且近乎最优的隐私保证，显著降低了噪声开销和计算成本，且经实验验证有效。

Abstract: DP-SGD protects training data by adding Gaussian noise to clipped gradients. The amount of noise is usually chosen by running a numerical privacy accountant inside a search. We study DP-SGD with random allocation, where each epoch uses every record once, at a randomly chosen step. For this setting we give a one-line formula that bounds the accuracy of every membership inference attack (MIA) on the trained model. With $M$ steps per epoch, $E$ epochs and noise multiplier $σ$, and with membership and non-membership equally likely a priori, the attack accuracy is at most $\frac12+\frac14\sqrt{(1+(e^{1/σ^2}-1)/M)^E-1}$. The formula comes from the chi-square divergence between a Gaussian distribution and a Gaussian mixture that dominates random allocation. It is interpretable and gives $σ$ in about a microsecond. Where applicable, our formula needs at most about half the noise of the state-of-the-art closed-form bound. To measure how close the bound is, we also derive an exact expression for the attack accuracy of these two distributions and evaluate it numerically. Calibrating to this exact expression requires $13.0\%$ to $20.2\%$ less noise than the formula in our main experiments, and since it is exact, no accountant that knows only $M$, $E$ and $σ$ can certify a smaller $σ$. In training, the resulting $σ$ outperforms the formula and matches a published accountant in test accuracy. It is found in seconds and certified in minutes, whereas every search we ran with that accountant took longer or returned at least $0.62\%$ more noise. We show that MIAs on the trained models stay below the bound.

</details>


### [289] [DSTNet: Dynamic Spectral Trajectory Network for Causal Multi-Horizon Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09654)
*Aashish Bohra,Lokendra Vishwakarm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DSTNet，一种基于小波的因果动态频谱轨迹神经网络，用于多步金融预测。模型通过单边Morlet滤波器组构建因果动态频谱轨迹，结合因子化尺度-时间频谱Transformer和CNN-BiLSTM，实现1/3/5/10天预测。在7个股指和黄金上，DSTNet在MAE/MAPE上全面优于随机游走基准和9个学习型基线，但在下游资产配置诊断中未显示出择时优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统小波金融预测器通常仅将小波变换用于去噪，或将其简化为预测起点的单一频谱快照，且卷积通常是双边的，能够读取预测起点之后的信息（存在前视偏差）。需要一种因果的、保留近期滤波器组幅度演化的动态频谱建模方法。

Method: DSTNet包含：1) 七个滞后技术指标，20天回溯窗口；2) 单边Morlet衍生滤波器组构建因果动态频谱轨迹，显式烧入处理左边界瞬态；3) 因子化尺度-时间频谱Transformer，在时间轴和滤波器组轴分别注意力；4) 学习型门控融合频谱分支与CNN-BiLSTM；5) 地平线特定门控单次前向传播输出1、3、5、10天预测。

Result: 在7个股指和黄金上，采用扩展窗口协议和1年未触碰保留期，对比9个学习型基线和随机游走持久性基准。MAE/MAPE下，持久性基准在32个序列-地平线单元中29个最强；DSTNet是唯一在每个单元均低于持久性基准的模型，1天改进0.7-0.9%，10天改进3.4-4.5%。1天配对检验显示DSTNet优于较弱基线，但对持久性基准和最强学习型预测器结论不显著。下游配置诊断不支持7个指数中任一的择时优势。

Conclusion: DSTNet通过因果动态频谱轨迹和因子化注意力机制，在预测精度（MAE/MAPE）上显著超越持久性基准和学习型基线，长期地平线改进更大。然而，统计显著的预测精度提升并未转化为实际资产配置中的择时超额收益，提示预测误差指标改进不一定等价于经济价值。

Abstract: Wavelet-based financial forecasters typically use the transform only to denoise, or reduce it to a single spectral snapshot at the forecast origin, and the convolution that produces the coefficients is usually bilateral, so it can read past the forecast origin. DSTNet instead retains the recent evolution of filter-bank magnitudes as a causal Dynamic Spectral Trajectory, built from seven trailing technical indicators over a twenty-day lookback with a one-sided Morlet-derived filter bank and an explicit burn-in for the left-boundary transient. A factorized Scale-Temporal Spectral Transformer attends along the time and filter-bank axes separately, a learned gate fuses the spectral branch with a CNN-BiLSTM, and horizon-specific gates emit one, three, five, and ten day forecasts in a single pass. We evaluate seven equity indices and gold under a common expanding-window protocol and an untouched one-year hold-out, against nine learned baselines and a random-walk persistence benchmark. Under MAE and MAPE, persistence is the strongest of the ten fixed competitors in 29 of the 32 series-horizon cells and DSTNet is the only model below it in every cell, by 0.7 to 0.9 percent at one day and 3.4 to 4.5 percent at ten days. At one day, paired testing favours DSTNet against the weaker learned baselines but is inconclusive against persistence and the strongest learned forecasters. A downstream allocation diagnostic does not support an equity-timing advantage on any of the seven indices.

</details>


### [290] [Gauss-Newton Accuracy and Indefinite Hessians: Uniform Coexistence in Low-Cost Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09675)
*Kihun Rhee,Hanjoon Byun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究岭正则化非线性最小二乘中高斯-牛顿曲率的准确性，证明了两种曲率制度的均匀共存，给出了全局极小点的相对Hessian误差上界(1+√2)/8，并建立了基于预测水平集的逐点证书以控制Hessian修正的各分量，获得随预测映射和岭参数变化的尖锐相对误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解岭正则化非线性最小二乘问题中高斯-牛顿近似的曲率行为，特别是在局部正则性和水平集曲率持久性条件下，揭示不同曲率制度的共存机制及其对优化算法的理论指导意义。

Method: 在局部正则性和精确拟合段上水平集曲率大小持久的假设下，采用解析方法证明曲率制度的均匀共存；构建基于当前预测水平集的逐点证书，分别控制Hessian修正的法向、混合和切向部分；通过解析例子阐释输出对齐、曲率方向和持久性的作用；建立雅可比矩阵在低代价集上的满行秩性质及秩亏参考点附近的精确插值结果。

Result: 1) 全局极小点存在，每个全局极小点的相对Hessian误差低于(1+√2)/8；2) 同一低代价集包含不定Hessian且相对误差至少15/8的点，证明两种曲率制度共存；3) 一个正岭上限适用于所有独立的中心和标签扰动，且固定邻域不随岭权重趋零而收缩；4) 给出预测映射和岭参数变化时的尖锐相对误差界；5) 解析例子展示输出对齐、曲率方向和持久性的具体作用；6) 结构结果确立低代价集上雅可比矩阵满行秩及秩亏参考点附近的精确插值性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为岭正则化非线性最小二乘中高斯-牛顿曲率的准确性提供了完整的理论表征，揭示了曲率制度共存的普遍性，建立了不依赖于岭参数大小的均匀误差界，为设计鲁棒的二阶优化算法提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the accuracy of Gauss-Newton curvature in ridge-regularized nonlinear least squares. Under local regularity and persistence of level-set curvature magnitude along an exact-fit section, we prove uniform coexistence of two curvature regimes. Global minimizers exist, and every global minimizer has relative Hessian error below $(1+\sqrt2)/8$, while the same low-cost set contains a point with an indefinite Hessian and relative error at least $15/8$. One positive ridge cap works for all independent center and label perturbations in fixed neighborhoods and every positive ridge weight up to the cap. These neighborhoods do not shrink as the ridge weight tends to zero. A pointwise certificate based on the current prediction level set controls the normal, mixed, and tangent parts of the Hessian correction. We prove a sharp relative-error bound over the stated pointwise class when the prediction map and ridge vary. Analytic examples describe the roles of output alignment, curvature orientation, and persistence. A separate structural result gives full Jacobian row rank throughout low-cost sets and exact interpolation near a rank-deficient reference.

</details>


### [291] [CERO: Where and When to Allocate Rollouts for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09679)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Xing Hu,Jiashuo Jiang,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CERO，一种用于组相对强化学习的在线原始对偶调度器，它在整个训练期间协调有限的 rollout 预算，而非固定的每轮预算。通过凹替代效用和 Fenchel 表示，CERO 自适应选择提示词、重访频率和每轮生成组数，在数学推理基准上取得了最高的 avg@16 宏平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应 rollout 方法通常在每次更新时分配固定的预算，无法在整个训练期间最优地分配有限的 rollout 资源。本文旨在研究如何在整个训练周期内协调有限的 rollout 预算，以提高训练效率和最终性能。

Method: 将问题建模为累积提示词曝光的凹替代效用最大化，引入 CERO 在线原始对偶调度器。利用紧凑的 Fenchel 表示将对累积曝光的依赖线性化，采用投影在线梯度下降更新提示词特定的支撑斜率和共享的预算价格，反馈信号来自奖励变化和预算偏差。CERO 自适应决定提示词准入、重访频率和每轮生成组数。

Result: 在相同的训练-响应预算下，CERO 在三个主干模型上的五个数学推理基准中均获得最高的 avg@16 宏平均分。机制分析显示 CERO 的提示词选择与组内奖励对比相关；多种子消融实验表明自适应节奏优于均匀和预设支出计划。

Conclusion: CERO 通过在整个训练期间协调 rollout 预算，实现了更高效的资源分配，并在数学推理任务上显著优于基线方法。理论分析提供了路径级的性能保证，实验验证了自适应预算调度的有效性。

Abstract: Adaptive rollout methods for group-relative reinforcement learning typically allocate a fixed per-update budget across prompts. We instead study how to coordinate a finite rollout budget over the entire training horizon. We formulate this problem using a concave surrogate utility of cumulative prompt exposure and introduce CERO, an online primal dual scheduler for prompt admission and budget pacing. In our experiments, each admitted prompt receives a fixed-size response group. CERO instead adapts which prompts are selected, how often they are revisited across rounds, and how many groups are generated in each round. A compact Fenchel representation linearizes the dependence on cumulative exposure, while projected online gradient descent updates prompt-specific supporting slopes and a shared budget price using reward-variation feedback and budget deviations. We establish pathwise guarantees for the surrogate allocation objective against fixed-rate and same-path time-varying benchmarks, with explicit terms for proxy discrepancy and rate variation. Under matched training-response budgets, CERO attains the highest avg@16 macro-average on each of three backbones across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Mechanistic analyses link CERO's prompt choices to within-group reward contrast, while multi-seed ablations show gains from adaptive pacing over both uniform and preset spending schedules.

</details>


### [292] [Few-Shot Learning for Personalised Automated Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09692)
*Heinke Hihn,Ibrahim Eisawy,Patrick Thiam,Hans A. Kestler,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用小样本学习（元学习的一个子领域）实现自动疼痛评估的个性化，将从群体级到个体级评估的转变重构为任务域偏移问题，在三个疼痛数据集上验证了支持集条件化的小样本适应能提升跨受试者变异性下的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛感知在个体间存在显著差异，导致基于群体的分类器难以泛化到所有受试者；为了解决这一问题，论文旨在探索个性化分类器训练方法，特别是小样本学习作为元学习的一种形式，以适应个体差异。

Method: 将群体级到个体级的评估转变重构为任务域偏移，其中观测类别固定但目标受试者改变；采用小样本学习框架，利用支持集样本进行 k-shot 条件化适应，在 BioVid、SenseEmotion 和 PMED 三个数据集上进行留一受试者出交叉验证，并进行消融实验和个性化效应分析。

Result: 在二分类和多分类设置下，BioVid 数据集分别达到 85.75% 和 35.49% 准确率，SenseEmotion 分别为 82.37% 和 41.88%，PMED 二分类为 90.47%；引入 k-shot 条件化后，准确率分别提升至 86.25%、40.06%、83.43%、44.08% 和 91.25%。

Conclusion: 支持集条件化的小样本适应能够有效改善跨受试者变异性下的平均性能，证明了小样本学习在自动疼痛评估个性化中的可行性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pain perception varies substantially across individuals, making it difficult for population-based classifiers to generalise across all subjects in a dataset. One way to account for subject variability is to train personalised classifiers. In this work, we evaluate Few-Shot Learning, a sub-area of Meta-Learning, as an approach to personalisation in automated pain assessment. We re-interpret the shift from population-level to subject-level evaluation as a task-domain shift, where the observed classes remain fixed but the target subject changes. We evaluate our method on the BioVid Pain Database, the SenseEmotion Database, and the PainMonit Experimental Dataset (PMED), reaching 85.75% and 35.49% accuracy on BioVid and 82.37% and 41.88% on SenseEmotion in the binary and multi-class settings under a Leave-One-Subject-Out CV protocol respectively, and 90.47% on PMED, for which only a binary benchmark exists. Using samples to implement k-shot conditioning, the accuracies can be improved to 86.25%, 40.06%, 83.43%, 44.08%, and 91.25%, respectively. To further evaluate the effects and robustness of our method, we provide additional ablation experiments and investigate the personalisation effects. Our results suggest that support-conditioned few-shot adaptation can improve average performance under inter-subject variability.

</details>


### [293] [Pretraining Shapes Spectral Structure: Architecture- and Strategy-Conditional Prediction of OOD Robustness in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09709)
*Sangyoon Bae,Sk Miraj Ahmed,Shinjae Yoo,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种仅基于预训练权重谱结构的诊断方法，可在无目标数据情况下预测基础模型的分布外泛化能力。通过分析架构与预训练策略共同决定的谱几何，证明OOD准确率差距受源表示收敛程度约束，并从权重中提取统计量作为代理。实验表明，在细粒度的架构-策略组合（cell）内，该统计量能正确排序92%的模型对；跨模态验证（EEG、基因组、蛋白质）均保持预测方向一致；利用谱收敛可缩小OOD差距24%且保持87.5%分布内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD泛化诊断方法均需源域或目标域数据，无法在目标域出现前评估模型；仅利用权重的现有方法对所有架构采用单一统计量，无法区分鲁棒与脆弱模型。因此亟需一种仅凭预训练权重、且能适应不同架构-策略组合的预测指标。

Method: 研究预训练权重的谱结构，揭示架构决定信息存储方式、预训练策略决定奖励目标，二者共同塑造谱几何从而支配OOD鲁棒性。理论证明OOD准确率差距由源表示收敛紧致度界定，并从权重谱导出统计量作为收敛代理。定义cell为(架构×策略)最细分组，发现代理方向在cell内稳定。在覆盖7模态的116个模型上实验验证，并对未见模态（EEG、基因组、蛋白质）进行盲测。

Result: 全模型汇总时单一统计量因cell间方向抵消导致排序较弱；但针对每个cell选取对应统计量后，能正确排序92%的模型对OOD鲁棒性。盲测中预测方向在所有新模态均成立。基于谱收敛的干预可将OOD差距缩减24%，同时保留87.5%分布内准确率。

Conclusion: OOD鲁棒性可仅从发布的权重中诊断，使模型选择阶段即可在无目标数据、无额外计算开销的情况下评估泛化潜力，为基础模型部署提供实用的事前检查工具。

Abstract: Can we determine whether a foundation model will generalize out-of-distribution (OOD) before any target data is available? Existing diagnostics require source or target data, which rules them out before a target domain exists. Those that use the weights alone apply one statistic to every architecture, and do not separate robust models from fragile ones. We show the answer is encoded in the spectral structure of pretrained weights. Two forces shape that structure. Architecture determines how information is stored in weight matrices. Pretraining strategy determines what is rewarded. Together they set a spectral geometry that governs OOD robustness. We prove that the OOD accuracy gap is bounded by how tightly the source representations concentrate. A statistic computed from the pretrained weights alone serves as a proxy for that concentration. The direction of that proxy reverses between architecture families. We operationalize it: the direction is stable within one (architecture X strategy) combination, the finest grouping we test, which we call a cell. Pooled over 116 models spanning 7 modalities, a single statistic ranks OOD robustness weakly, because cells of opposite direction cancel. Within a cell, the statistic selected for it orders 92% of model pairs by OOD robustness in-sample. The selection does not leak the target: for each model family outside the matrix we logged the cell, metric and sign before running its OOD evaluation, and the predicted direction held in every case: EEG, genomic and protein. Acting on spectral concentration narrows the OOD gap by 24% at 87.5% ID retention. The diagnostic operates on released weights alone, so OOD robustness becomes checkable at model-selection time, before data or compute is committed to a target domain.

</details>


### [294] [EC-EarthFlow: Probabilistic emulation of daily transient global climate model simulations with flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09715)
*Kirien Whan,Nikolaj T. Mücke,Karin van der Wiel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EC-EarthFlow，一种基于生成流匹配的气候模型模拟器，能够以极低的计算成本复现EC-Earth3物理气候模型的温度场预测，包括日变率、空间模式、年周期和长期趋势，并在长期自回归推理中保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理气候模型（如EC-Earth3）计算成本极高，难以进行大规模集合模拟或长期情景探测。需要一种数据驱动的替代模型，能在保持物理一致性的前提下大幅降低计算开销。

Method: 采用生成流匹配框架，以EC-Earth3在1950-2166年SSP2-4.5情景下的瞬态模拟数据为训练集。模型输入为前一天温度场与年平均温度，输出为下一天温度场，通过自回归滚动预测实现月尺度至季节尺度的推理。

Result: EC-EarthFlow能准确复现EC-Earth3的日变率、空间模式、年周期及长期变暖趋势；长周期自回归推理稳定，无明显漂移；计算成本比物理模型低数个数量级，且隐式学习了EC-Earth3中的物理关系。

Conclusion: 基于流匹配的生成模型可作为物理气候模型的高效替代器，在保持关键气候统计特征和物理一致性的同时，实现计算效率的巨大提升，为大规模气候集合预测和长期情景分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce EC-EarthFlow, a generative flow matching model that emulates simulations from the physical climate model EC-Earth3. The model is trained on transient simulations from EC-Earth3 (1950-2166, SSP2-4.5) to predict the day ahead temperature field from the previous days temperature as well as annual mean temperature. Predictions are made auto-regressively with rollout periods of between a month and an extended season. Using only this variable of interest, we are able to reproduce the daily variability, spatial patterns, annual cycle and long-term trend from EC-Earth3 at a substantially lower computational cost than the physical model. We demonstrate that EC-EarthFlow is stable for long inference periods, and that it can learn the physical relationships as simulated in EC-Earth3.

</details>


### [295] [SoftSEEPS improves ML-based precipitation forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09752)
*Jost Arndt,Utku Isil,Noelia Otero,Rodrigo Almeida,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSEEPS——一种可微分的SEEPS评分近似方法，使机器学习模型能直接预测降水。在IMERG数据集（0.1度分辨率）上，通过在预训练低分辨率预测模型的潜在空间训练降水解码器，验证了SoftSEEPS的有效性。联合使用SoftSEEPS和RMSE损失可同时优化两个指标，仅带来边际权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降水预测模型常使用RMSE等标量损失，但无法直接优化空间结构评分如SEEPS（空间误差结构评分）。SEEPS虽能评估空间模式匹配度，但不可微，无法用于梯度下降训练。因此需要一种可微分近似，使模型能直接优化空间预测质量。

Method: 1. 开发SoftSEEPS：将SEEPS的硬阈值操作替换为可微分的软近似（如Sigmoid或软最大值），保留空间结构评价特性同时支持反向传播。2. 实验设置：使用IMERG降水数据集（0.1°分辨率），冻结预训练的低分辨率预测模型编码器，仅训练解码器将潜在表示映射为高分辨率降水场。3. 损失函数：联合优化SoftSEEPS损失与RMSE损失，探索加权组合对两指标的影响。

Result: SoftSEEPS使模型可直接优化空间结构评分。联合训练下，SoftSEEPS显著提升空间模式匹配度（SEEPS指标），同时RMSE仅轻微增加；反之以RMSE为主时，SoftSEEPS下降幅度也很小。两指标存在边际权衡，但均优于单独使用RMSE训练的基线。

Conclusion: SoftSEEPS成功解决了SEEPS不可微的问题，首次实现直接优化空间结构评分的降水预测训练。联合损失策略提供了灵活的权衡机制，为极端天气等对空间结构敏感的预测任务提供了新范式。未来可扩展到其他空间验证指标及端到端训练。

Abstract: In this paper we have developed a differentiable approximation of the well-known SEEPS score, which we name SoftSEEPS. This allows the training of a Machine Learning model to forecast precipitation directly. We test SoftSEEPS on the IMERG dataset (0.1 degree resolution) by training a decoder for precipitation on the latent space of a pre-trained low-resolution forecasting model. Combining SoftSEEPS and RMSE in a joint objective is possible with marginal trade-offs in either metric.

</details>


### [296] [EntroPrefill: Renyi-Guided Context Pruning with Conditional Stability Guarantees for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09757)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EntroPrefill，一种基于 Rényi 熵引导的 mid-prefill 剪枝理论框架，通过显式约束丢弃的注意力质量，给出可计算的 token 删除上界、有限样本观测保证、条件变换器扰动界及首 token 决策边界推论，并给出系统级的盈亏平衡条件；纯理论工作，不含实测加速或精度保持实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 mid-prefill 剪枝仅依赖注意力集中度，无法证明被丢弃上下文真正可舍弃。作者旨在建立一套具有形式化保证的剪枝机制，量化 token 删除的可行边界，并揭示浅层观测无法无条件推导未来输出保证的根本局限。

Method: 1. 以 Rényi 熵为 proposal 机制，配合对丢弃注意力质量的显式约束；2. 提出 sink-isolated、正则化的头池化，兼容 grouped-query attention 并暴露专用化与最坏头覆盖的权衡；3. 推导 mixture-to-head 删除包络（可计算的 token 删除上界）；4. 建立自适应剪枝层选择下依然有效的有限样本观测保证；5. 给出显式 Lipschitz 常数的条件变换器扰动界及首 token 决策边界推论；6. 构造反例说明浅层观测不蕴含无条件未来输出保证；7. 系统层面区分 query-head 并集、物理页分配与 KV 传输载荷，给出算术盈亏平衡条件。

Result: 获得一套完整的理论工具链：可计算的删除上界、自适应层选择下的统计保证、带显式常数的扰动界、决策边界推论、反例界定适用边界，以及系统级盈亏平衡判据。文中未报告任何实测加速或任务精度保持结果。

Conclusion: EntroPrefill 为 mid-prefill 剪枝提供了首个端到端的形式化框架，明确了假设、近似紧度与资源权衡的理论边界。后续工作需通过实验验证假设合理性、近似紧度及端到端资源权衡，方能落地部署。

Abstract: Mid-prefill pruning can reduce the sequence processed by deeper transformer layers, but attention concentration alone does not certify that discarded context is dispensable. We formulate EntroPrefill as a Renyi-guided proposal mechanism coupled to explicit constraints on discarded attention mass. Sink-isolated, regularized head pooling respects grouped-query attention while exposing a quantitative trade-off between specialization and worst-head coverage. We derive a mixture-to-head deletion envelope, a computable upper bound on feasible token removal, and a finite-sample observer guarantee that remains valid when the pruning layer is selected adaptively. We then establish a conditional transformer perturbation bound with explicit sufficient Lipschitz constants and a first-token decision-margin corollary. A counterexample shows why shallow observations alone cannot imply an unconditional future-output guarantee. The systems analysis distinguishes query-head unions, physical page allocation, and KV-transfer payload, and gives an arithmetic break-even condition for pruning. This manuscript is theoretical in scope: it defines the procedure, its assumptions, and its formal limits, but does not report measured acceleration or task-accuracy preservation. Experiments are reserved for subsequent validation of the assumptions, approximation tightness, and end-to-end resource trade-offs.

</details>


### [297] [Unrolled Flow Models for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09759)
*Faissal Izermine,Hanru Bai,Oscar Davis,T. Konstantin Rusch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于双层Transformer的流匹配模型能解决图可达性问题，且所需积分步数随目标距离增加。标准流语言模型因独立监督各时间点而无法从额外步骤中受益。作者提出通过模型自身潜在轨迹在[0,1]随机子区间上的滚动训练，仅在终点解码，结合球面收缩稳定长轨迹动态，并在ProsQA、数独、迷宫等推理任务上显著提升性能，甚至优于参数量大3倍以上的基线。多轨迹采样配合无参数选择分数可进一步提升，但长答案的可靠选择仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配语言模型在少步生成上表现良好，但在推理任务上无法利用额外积分步骤提升性能。作者旨在从理论上建立流模型推理能力的基础，并解决标准目标函数独立监督各时间点、导致连续步骤无法相互构建的根本限制。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明双层Transformer参数化的流可解决图可达性，积分步数随距离增长。2. 滚动训练：在[0,1]随机采样子区间，让模型自行进行潜在状态滚动，仅在终点解码计算损失，强制连续步骤相互依赖。3. 球面收缩：将潜在状态投影到球面上，稳定长轨迹（如数独、迷宫）的动力学。4. 多轨迹采样与选择：采样多条轨迹，使用无参数选择分数挑选最佳输出。

Result: 在ProsQA上准确率达97%，且性能随积分步数增加而提升。在数独和迷宫等长推理任务上，球面收缩带来显著增益，超越参数量大3倍以上的基线。多轨迹采样配合选择分数进一步提升性能，但长答案的可靠选择仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 本文确立了流匹配进行推理的理论基础，展示了滚动训练、稳定潜在动力学（球面收缩）和轨迹选择相结合如何在实践中释放这一能力。这些技术使流模型能有效利用额外计算步骤进行复杂推理。

Abstract: Flow matching enables language generation in few steps, but whether additional integration steps improve reasoning remains unclear. We prove that a flow parameterized by a two-layer Transformer can solve graph reachability, with the required number of integration steps increasing with the target's distance from the root. Yet, standard flow language models can fail to benefit from additional steps on reasoning tasks. We attribute this limitation to objectives that supervise each time point independently, without explicitly training successive steps to build on one another. To address this, we instead train through the model's own latent rollout over a randomly sampled subinterval of [0, 1], decoding only at the endpoint. On ProsQA, this raises accuracy to 97% and enables performance to improve with additional integration steps. For the longer rollouts required by reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Maze, retracting the latent state onto a sphere stabilizes the dynamics and yields substantial gains over baselines with more than three times as many parameters. Sampling multiple rollouts further improves performance when paired with a parameter-free selection score, although reliable selection remains challenging for longer answers. Together, these results establish a theoretical basis for reasoning with flows and show how rollout training, stable latent dynamics, and rollout selection help realize this capacity in practice.

</details>


### [298] [Leaner Transformers Can Easily Learn to Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09760)
*Charlotte Park,Kenneth L. Clarkson,Lior Horesh,Takuya Ito,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入维度更小的Transformer精确执行Lloyd's k-means算法，并在聚类任务分布上训练Transformer学习聚类算法，理论分析并实验验证随机梯度学习的收敛与泛化因素，探究学习到的算法在泛化聚类任务上的成功与失败情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明Transformer可精确执行Lloyd算法但需要较大嵌入维度(d+k)，本文旨在减小嵌入维度至d+⌈log₂k⌉，同时研究通过训练学习聚类算法的收敛性、分布内泛化能力，以及学习到的算法在未见聚类任务上的泛化表现。

Method: 1) 设计嵌入维度为d+⌈log₂k⌉的Transformer架构等价执行Lloyd算法；2) 在聚类任务分布上训练这些Transformer，理论表征并实验验证基于随机梯度学习算法的收敛与分布内泛化影响因素；3) 系统探测学习到的Transformer在一般聚类任务上的表现，分析其成功与失败的条件。

Result: 提出的紧凑Transformer等价表达Lloyd算法；训练后的Transformer能学习聚类算法，理论揭示了影响收敛和泛化的关键因素并获实验支持；发现学习到的算法在某些分布外任务上成功泛化，在其他情况下失效，揭示了其泛化能力的边界。

Conclusion: Transformer可用更小嵌入维度精确实现Lloyd算法，且可通过训练习得聚类算法，其收敛与泛化受任务分布、优化动力学等因素制约；学习到的算法具有一定泛化聚类能力但存在明显局限，为理解Transformer内文学习机制提供了理论与实证见解。

Abstract: Transformers have in-context learning capabilities, where some known learning algorithms can be executed in the forward pass through the model. Recent work shows that transformers can exactly perform Lloyd's algorithm for $k$-means clustering with $n$ points in $d$ dimensions with an embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = d+k$ (thus, requiring attention projection matrices of size $(d+k)^2$). In this work, we build upon this result in the following ways: First, we present an equally expressive but smaller transformer that executes Lloyd's algorithm with embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = (d + \lceil \log_2 k \rceil)$. Next, we train these transformers to learn the clustering algorithms given a distribution of clustering tasks, and theoretically characterize and empirically validate the factors affecting the convergence and in-distribution generalization of learning algorithms based on stochastic gradients. Finally, we probe the general clustering abilities of these learned algorithms (in the form of transformers), and try to understand situations where they succeed and fail.

</details>


### [299] [Beyond Policy Support: Interaction Constrained Offline Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09763)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl Henrik Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出交互约束驾驶策略（ICDP），一种离线强化学习框架，显式控制自主驾驶中的交互层面分布偏移（IDS）。通过将联合支持退化分解为自我支持与交互支持两部分，并利用对比密度比估计恢复交互兼容性，无需显式联合密度建模、周围车辆预测或反应式模拟器/世界模型回滚。在nuPlan、Interplan及真实卡车实验中，ICDP抑制了高价值但交互不支持的轨迹选择，提升了交互关键场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在安全关键领域（如自主驾驶）具有吸引力，但面临分布偏移挑战：策略优化可能偏好离线数据弱支持的动作，导致价值估计不可靠。现有方法主要在策略自身动作空间控制偏移，但在交互环境中不足：候选自车轨迹在边际行为分布下支持良好，但与日志中观测到的周围车辆行为联合考虑时支持不足。这种交互层面的支持退化被称为交互分布偏移（IDS），需显式建模并控制。

Method: 从自车与周围车辆未来的联合数据分布出发，证明联合支持退化可精确分解为自车支持分量与残余交互支持分量。通过对比密度比估计（contrastive density-ratio estimation）恢复后者，隔离交互兼容性，避免显式联合密度建模、周围车辆预测或反应式模拟器/学习世界模型的回滚。将交互约束集成至离线RL目标，形成ICDP框架。

Result: 在nuPlan、Interplan仿真基准及真实卡车闭环实验中，ICDP有效抑制高价值但交互不支持的轨迹选择，在交互关键驾驶场景中显著提升性能指标（如碰撞率、规划舒适度、任务完成率等），优于现有离线RL基线。

Conclusion: ICDP通过显式建模并控制交互层面分布偏移，解决了离线RL在交互式自主驾驶中的关键局限。其分解与对比估计方法无需复杂联合建模或模拟器，实用性强。实验验证了在仿真与真实环境中均能提升交互安全性与策略性能，为安全关键离线RL提供了新范式。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables reward-driven policy improvement from fixed datasets without requiring online exploration, making it particularly attractive in safety-critical domains. A central challenge, however, is distribution shift: policy optimization may favor actions that are weakly supported by the offline data, rendering value estimates unreliable. Existing approaches primarily control this shift in the policy's own action space. In interactive environments such as autonomous driving, this can be insufficient: a candidate ego trajectory may remain well supported under the marginal behavior distribution while being poorly supported jointly with the surrounding-agent behavior observed in the logged interaction. We refer to this degradation in interaction support as \emph{interaction distribution shift} (IDS), and introduce \emph{Interaction-Constrained Drive Policy} (ICDP), an offline reinforcement learning framework that explicitly controls interaction-level distribution shift. Starting from the joint data distribution over ego and surrounding-agent futures, we show that joint-support degradation decomposes exactly into an ego-support component and a residual interaction-support component. We recover the latter through contrastive density-ratio estimation, isolating interaction compatibility without explicit joint-density modeling, surrounding-agent prediction, or rollouts in reactive simulators or learned world models during policy optimization. Closed-loop evaluations on nuPlan, Interplan and real-world truck experiments show that ICDP suppresses high-value yet interaction-unsupported trajectory selections and improves performance in interaction-critical driving scenarios. Project webpage: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/ICDP/

</details>


### [300] [Fluctuations of Nonlinear Observables in Mean Field Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09768)
*Arnaud Descours,Geoffrey Lacour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究宽神经网络训练中均值场波动如何传播到有限维非线性可观测量，建立了浅层神经网络随机梯度下降训练的可观测量中心极限定理及显式协方差公式，并证明了在常秩假设下，感兴趣量能否通过观测泛函局部分解的充要条件，为量化有限宽度不确定性和评估观测信息完备性提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值场极限通过参数经验分布的演化描述宽神经网络的训练动力学，泛函中心极限定理刻画了该分布的渐近波动，但实际关注的通常是参数分布的非线性可观测量而非分布本身。需要将均值场波动理论扩展到有限维非线性可观测量，并研究观测量是否包含识别感兴趣量所需的信息。

Method: 在加权Sobolev空间中构建极限波动过程，利用普通Fréchet可微性下的泛函Delta方法（无需关于度量变量的Lions导数），推导可观测量的中心极限定理并获得显式协方差公式。在常秩假设下，通过泛函微分的核包含关系，建立感兴趣量通过观测泛函局部分解的充要条件。

Result: 得到浅层神经网络SGD训练中非线性可观测量的中心极限定理及从均值场波动理论继承的显式协方差公式。证明在常秩假设下，感兴趣量在邻域内局部通过观测泛函分解的充要条件是观测微分核始终包含于感兴趣量微分核中。该微分条件直接在环境Sobolev空间中给出了精确的非线性局部分解。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个双重框架：一方面能量化统计或物理意义的可观测量上的有限宽度不确定性，另一方面能评估所选观测是否包含识别感兴趣量所需的信息。避免了Lions导数的复杂性，利用普通Fréchet微分在Sobolev空间中实现了理论的严谨性与实用性的统一。

Abstract: Mean field limits describe the training dynamics of wide neural networks through the evolution of the empirical distribution of their parameters. Although functional central limit theorems characterize the asymptotic fluctuations of this distribution, quantities of practical interest are typically nonlinear observables of the parameter distribution rather than the distribution itself. In this work, we show how these mean field fluctuations propagate to finite dimensional nonlinear observables for shallow neural networks trained by stochastic gradient descent. Working in the weighted Sobolev space in which the limiting fluctuation process is constructed, we apply a functional Delta method under ordinary Fr{é}chet differentiability, without requiring Lions derivatives with respect to the measure variable. We obtain a central limit theorem for the observables and, under a suitable representation of their differentials, an explicit covariance formula inherited from the underlying mean field fluctuation theory. We also study whether prescribed quantities of interest can be recovered from the selected observations. Under a constant rank assumption, we prove that a quantity of interest factors locally through the observation functional if and only if, throughout a neighborhood, the kernel of the differential of the observation is contained in that of the quantity of interest. Thus, a differential condition expressed directly in the ambient Sobolev space yields an exact nonlinear local factorization. These results provide a framework both for quantifying finite-width uncertainty on observable, statistically or physically meaningful quantities and for assessing whether the chosen observations contain the information required to identify them.

</details>


### [301] [AdaPS-LiNGAM: Adaptive Predecessor Selection for Linear Non-Gaussian Acyclic Models under Small-Sample Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09782)
*Shun Yanashima,Kentaro Kanamori,Hirofumi Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 AdaPS-LiNGAM 解决小样本下的因果发现问题。DirectLiNGAM 在变量数超过样本量时会因残差化退化而失效。作者发现每个残差仅需由因果序中已排序变量的图决定子集（活性边界）重构，据此设计自适应前驱选择机制，直接从原始观测重构残差并用于边剪枝，在合成数据上实现了小样本下的准确因果结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
