We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient. Our analysis suggests that the teacher should not only perform well on the task, but also provide guidance suited to the student's current capabilities. This characterization motivates a practical teacher-training surrogate that combines outcome rewards with token-level Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the student. Based on this result, we propose Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT), which jointly trains a single policy in two roles: a privileged teacher using a KL-regularized objective, and an unprivileged student using dense on-policy distillation. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, tool use, and terminal use, JOLT improves training efficiency and performance, with further gains from student rewards.

</details>


### [372] [Composing What Each Teacher Learned: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation through Teacher-Relative Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10460)
*Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Xiaomin Li,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Δ-MOPD，在多教师在线策略蒸馏中迁移教师相对于基础模型的 logit 位移而非端点策略，消除继承自基础模型的偏好干扰，在教师信号组合场景下显著提升性能，并证实目标构建是独立于教师选择的设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MOPD 直接蒸馏教师的端点策略，该策略混合了后训练带来的改进与从教师基础模型继承的偏好；继承的基础模型拉力可能超过后训练位移，阻碍有效知识迁移。

Method: 提出 Δ-MOPD：计算教师减去基础模型的 logit 位移，并将其重新锚定在学生的冻结初始化上作为蒸馏目标。在 common-domain composition（多教师对同一领域打分组合）和 routed-domain distillation（不同领域分配专家教师）两种设置下，固定教师选择，对比 Δ-MOPD 与端点监督。

Result: 当教师信号在状态层面组合时，位移目标优势明显。三教师组合时 Δ-MOPD 超越端点组合 4.11 Math 与 1.95 五基准分；两教师时持平。分阶段路由下，两种阶段顺序均提升平均性能，顺序差距从 10.50 降至 6.42。交错路由下两者相当。分阶段结果暗示收益可延伸至跨训练阶段累积信号。

Conclusion: 目标构建（端点 vs. 位移）是 MOPD 中独立的设计轴，与教师选择互补，为多教师蒸馏提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) is used in two settings. In common-domain composition, several teachers score each student rollout from one prompt domain and their signals form a single target; in routed-domain distillation, prompts from different domains are assigned to the corresponding specialist. Both settings usually transfer each teacher's endpoint policy, which mixes what post-training changed with preferences inherited from the teacher's base. We introduce $Δ$-MOPD, which transfers each teacher's teacher-minus-base logit shift re-anchored at the student's frozen initialization, and compare it with endpoint supervision in both settings while holding teacher selection fixed. We first expose the mechanism that impedes endpoint transfer: inherited base pull can exceed the post-training shift. Removing it reduces the teacher-term norm ratio and target--student KL.
  Across our experiments, the results suggest that shift targets are particularly useful when teacher signals are combined at a state. With three composed teachers, $Δ$-MOPD exceeds endpoint composition by $4.11$ Math and $1.95$ five-benchmark points; with two, it matches endpoint accuracy. Under phased routing, it achieves higher mean performance in both phase orders and reduces the observed order gap from $10.50$ to $6.42$ points. Under interleaved routing, where each update involves one teacher, the two targets perform comparably. The phased results provide supporting evidence that the benefit may extend to signals accumulated across training phases. Target construction is thus an independent design axis in MOPD, complementary to teacher selection.

</details>


### [373] [Seq-Flow: Efficient Probabilistic Forecasting with Self-Rollout Error Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10440)
*Yinan Huang,Shitij Govil,Bo Dai,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Seq-Flow 是一种条件流模型，通过将样本从先前预测分布传输到更新后的分布，实现了少步采样下的高精度序列预测更新，并通过自回滚训练解决了误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流模型在每次预测时都从高斯噪声生成，需要许多采样步骤；热启动方法虽复用早期预测但模型未针对预测更新本身训练，导致少步采样时质量下降。需要一种能高效更新预测分布且抑制误差累积的方法。

Method: Seq-Flow 设计为条件流模型，其 ODE 将样本从上一预测分布传输到新预测分布，利用连续预测分布差异小的特性实现少步高精度更新。提出自回滚训练：使用模型的移动平均副本生成预测作为后续流的初始状态，而非像 self-forcing 那样作为条件上下文，从而缓解递归复用带来的误差传播。

Result: 在粒子加速器束流预测任务上，少 NFE 预算下 CRPS 降低 65%；在流体动力学预测任务上与强基线持平。尽管训练时最多仅回滚 4 次更新，模型在超过 400 次连续更新中仍保持准确。

Conclusion: Seq-Flow 通过分布传输而非从噪声生成，结合自回滚训练，在极少采样步数下实现了高质量的序列预测更新，并展现出极强的长期稳定性，为实时科学预测提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Many scientific forecasting tasks require updating a distribution over future trajectories as new observations arrive. Conventional diffusion and flow models generate each forecast from Gaussian noise, often at the cost of many sampling steps. Warm-start methods reuse earlier predictions to reduce this cost, but their models are not trained to perform the forecast update itself, which can compromise quality under few-step sampling. In this work, we introduce Seq-Flow, a conditional flow model whose ODE transports samples from the previous forecast distribution to the updated one. Because successive forecasts often differ only modestly, this transport starts from an informative distribution and can produce accurate updates with few flow evaluations. Recursive reuse also creates a challenge: errors in one forecast become errors in the initial states of subsequent flows. We address this with self-rollout training, in which a moving average copy of the model generates forecasts that initialize later training updates. Unlike self-forcing methods, which reuse generated outputs as conditioning context, Seq-Flow reuses them as the source of the next flow. Experiments On particle-accelerator beam spill forecasting show Seq-Flow reduces CRPS by 65% under a few-NFE sampling budget, while remaining competitive with strong baselines on fluid-dynamics forecasting tasks. Although trained on self-rollouts of at most four updates, Seq-Flow remains accurate over more than 400 consecutive updates. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/Seq-Flow.

</details>


### [374] [NeuralBES: A Differentiable, Control-Aware Emulator for Scalable Building Energy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10459)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Takuya Kurihana,Wing Yee Au,Hon Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuralBES 是一种可微分的建筑能耗模拟器，通过将建筑静态元数据映射为物理约束的 RC 热力学参数，并利用对数空间并行扫描求解线性递推，结合预测-修正循环处理恒温器非线性，实现了物理一致性与数据驱动泛化能力的统一，在 ResStock 数据集上以极少参数量达到最优精度与物理有效性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑能耗模拟工具面临两难：高保真物理模拟器（如 EnergyPlus）虽准确但串行且需逐栋标定，难以规模化；纯数据驱动序列模型虽可扩展但丢弃物理结构，导致预测不可信。需要一种既能保持物理可解释性、又能跨异质建筑泛化、同时支持高效并行计算与端到端梯度流的模型。

Method: NeuralBES 采用共享神经编码器将楼层面积、建造年代、暖通空调类型等静态元数据映射为物理约束的电容、电导及设备系数，构建标量线性递推方程，通过对数空间并行扫描高效求解；引入预测-修正循环闭合恒温器-温度非线性，保持全时域梯度流；在 ResStock 数据集（三个气候区）上端到端训练单一编码器，覆盖多种建筑原型、年代与气候区。

Result: 在全年滚动预测中，NeuralBES 是唯一同时满足物理有效性且误差在最强基线 4 MAPE 点以内的数据条件模型；参数量比 Transformer 与 RNN 基线少约一个数量级；在参数量相当的物理有效基线（灰箱 RC 模型）中，MAPE 减半以上。黑箱基线虽统计合理但轨迹物理不一致。

Conclusion: NeuralBES 成功解决了建筑能耗模拟中物理保真度与数据驱动规模化的矛盾，通过可微分物理建模与神经参数化的结合，实现了跨异质建筑的零样本泛化、高效并行推理与全程梯度回传，为大规模需求侧灵活性管理提供了可信、轻量的仿真工具。

Abstract: Demand-side flexibility i.e. forecasting, shifting, and curtailing residential energy loads, depends on thermal models trusted across millions of heterogeneous buildings. Existing tools force a hard tradeoff: high-fidelity physics simulators such as EnergyPlus are accurate but sequential and require per-building calibration, while purely data-driven sequence models scale but abandon the physical structure that makes their predictions trustworthy.
  We introduce NeuralBES (Building Energy Simulation), a differentiable emulator that resolves this tradeoff by parameterizing a resistance--capacitance (RC) based thermal model with a shared neural encoder: static building metadata such as floor area, vintage, and HVAC type is mapped to physically bounded capacitances, conductances, and equipment coefficients, which become the coefficients of a scalar linear recurrence solved via a log-space parallel scan, and a predictor--corrector loop closes the thermostat--temperature nonlinearity while preserving full-horizon gradient flow. Trained on the ResStock dataset across three climate zones, NeuralBES handles heterogeneous building archetypes, vintages, and climate zones within a single trained encoder, while black-box baselines produce statistically plausible but physically inconsistent trajectories. On the annual full-year rollout, NeuralBES is the only data-conditioned model that is simultaneously physics-valid and accurate to within 4 MAPE points of the strongest raw-error baseline, while operating at roughly an order of magnitude fewer parameters than the transformer and recurrent baselines; among physics-valid baselines at parameter parity it more than halves the MAPE of the grey-box RC alternative.

</details>


### [375] [Oracle-Efficient and Parameter-Free Agnostic Smoothed Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10499)
*Sasha Voitovych,Adam Block,Alexander Rakhlin,Abhishek Shetty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在平滑在线学习框架下，无需知道基础测度 μ 即可在非实现性设定中实现次线性遗憾的预言机高效算法。该算法基于高斯 Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader，无参数（不需要知道 μ、平滑参数 σ 或时间范围 T），每轮仅需一次 ERM 预言机调用，对于 VC 维为 d 的二分类类，达到 Õ(d√(T/σ)) 的遗憾界，最优至多相差 √d 因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预言机高效平滑在线学习算法要么需要对基础测度 μ 的采样访问，要么要求标签能被固定假设完美预测。这两个假设都限制了算法的适用性，与统计学习中经验风险最小化（ERM）在非实现性设定下无需知道数据分布即可高效学习形成对比。

Method: 基于高斯 Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) 的算法，完全无参数：不需要知道 μ、平滑参数 σ 或时间范围 T。每轮进行一次 ERM 预言机调用。引入了多项新技术来建立遗憾界。

Result: 对于 VC 维为 d 的二分类类，算法实现遗憾 Õ(d√(T/σ))，最优至多相差 √d 因子。这是首个在非实现性设定下无需知道 μ 即可达到次线性遗憾的预言机高效算法。

Conclusion: 平滑在线学习中既不需要 μ 的采样访问，也不需要完美可预测标签的假设。该算法填补了平滑在线学习与统计学习之间的鸿沟，在保持在线学习灵活性的同时，达到了与经典学习相当的统计和计算保证。

Abstract: Online learning is an attractive framework in many domains because it permits well-defined learning even when data are dependent or chosen adversarially. This generality, however, comes at a steep price, introducing significant statistical and computational barriers. Recently, smoothed online learning has emerged as a promising framework that interpolates between the fully adversarial and fully stochastic settings by assuming that the conditional law of each covariate has density at most $1/σ$ with respect to some fixed base measure $μ$, and it is known to match the statistical and computational guarantees of classical learning while still allowing for much of the flexibility of online learning. However, existing oracle-efficient algorithms require either (i) sampling access to the base measure $μ$ or (ii) labels that are perfectly predicted by a fixed hypothesis. Both assumptions limit the applicability of these algorithms, in contrast to statistical learning, where empirical risk minimization (ERM) learns efficiently in the agnostic setting without any knowledge of the data distribution. We show that neither assumption is necessary, giving the first oracle-efficient algorithm that achieves sublinear regret in the agnostic setting without knowledge of $μ$. Our algorithm, based on Gaussian Follow-The-Perturbed-Leader, is parameter-free: it requires no knowledge of $μ$, the smoothing parameter $σ$, or the horizon $T$, and it achieves regret $\widetilde O(d\sqrt{T/σ})$ for binary classes of VC dimension $d$ with a single call to an ERM oracle per round, which is optimal up to a $\sqrt{d}$ factor. En route to establishing the regret bound, we introduce several new techniques that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [376] [Why Forget-Only Unlearning Needs Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10519)
*Luka Radić,Vikrant Singhal,Amartya Sanyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究'仅遗忘'机器遗忘场景：删除算法仅接收训练好的模型和需遗忘的样本，无保留数据或额外训练信息。论文证明此类遗忘能否成功取决于学习方法，推导了遗忘精度匹配重训练的下界，并发现为应对任意删除请求，算法必须记忆的信息量可能达到整个数据集规模，远超普通训练仅保留的边界点信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究'仅遗忘'无学习是否总是可行，以及成功遗忘需要满足什么条件。现有机器遗忘研究多假设有保留数据或训练轨迹，但实际场景中往往只有最终模型和需删除样本，因此需要从理论上界定这一设定的基本极限。

Method: 1. 形式化定义'仅遗忘'无学习设定；2. 证明不同数据集可产生相同模型但遗忘后需不同输出，确立依赖学习方法的不可行性；3. 推导遗忘算法输出分布与重训练分布差异的下界，并在标准学习算法（如阈值学习、线性回归等）上实例化；4. 从信息论角度分析为应对任意删除请求，算法必须在训练阶段保留的数据信息量下界。

Result: • '仅遗忘'无学习的可行性取决于具体学习算法，并非普遍可能。• 给出多个标准学习算法的遗忘精度下界，量化了无法完美匹配重训练的根本原因。• 对于简单的阈值学习器，处理任意删除请求所需记忆的信息量可达整个数据集大小，而普通训练仅保留一个边界点。• 普通训练丢弃的信息在后续删除时可能是必需的。

Conclusion: 面向'仅遗忘'无学习设计的模型，在训练阶段必须保留比标准训练更多的信息。这一结果揭示了标准学习范式与无学习遗忘需求之间的根本张力：为了支持未来的任意样本删除，模型不能像普通训练那样极度压缩数据信息，而需显式存储足以重构重训练行为的充分统计量。

Abstract: Machine unlearning asks for a deletion algorithm whose output is close to retraining from scratch without the selected forget examples. In this work, we study forget-only unlearning, where the deletion algorithm receives only the trained model and the examples to forget, with no retained data or extra training information. We ask whether forget-only unlearning is always possible. We first show that this depends on the learning method: different datasets can produce the same trained model but require very different outputs after the same examples are removed. Using this observation, we derive lower bounds on how accurately unlearning can match retraining and instantiate them for several standard learning algorithms. We then ask what must be true when forget-only unlearning succeeds. To this end, we derive lower bounds on what an algorithm must memorize about the training data to handle arbitrary deletion requests. For simple threshold learners, the required information can be as large as the entire dataset, even though ordinary training keeps only one boundary point. Overall, our results show that information discarded during ordinary learning may be needed later for deletion, so models designed for forget-only unlearning may need to retain more information than standard training does.

</details>


### [377] [Distilling Graph Geometry: Knowledge Gap from GNNs to MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10520)
*Zhewei Chen,Hao Zhu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Ahad N. Zehmakan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Ollivier-Ricci曲率的图几何感知MLP（G^2MLP）蒸馏框架，解决了现有GNN-to-MLP蒸馏方法在稀疏图上出现的谱欠拟合和稠密图上的谱过拟合问题，通过曲率引导的预测级和表示级对齐监督分配，在推理阶段保持无图访问的标准MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-to-MLP蒸馏方法主要迁移节点级预测或使用基于置信度的重加权，未指定学生模型应在何处保持教师的图诱导几何结构，导致两种谱失效模式：稀疏图上学生出现谱欠拟合，遗漏边界区域附近的高能量教师方向；稠密图上出现谱过拟合，保留了教师通过聚合已坍缩的虚假方向。

Method: 提出基于能量加权的教师-学生对齐目标，利用Ollivier-Ricci曲率识别两种谱错误的集中区域，据此在预测级对齐和表示级对齐之间分配监督。G^2MLP是一个训练时的蒸馏框架，部署模型仍为标准MLP，推理无需图访问。

Result: 在节点分类基准上，G^2MLP持续优于无图蒸馏基线，在稀疏和稠密图两种机制下均缩小了教师-学生秩差距，且无需架构修改即可迁移到Graph Transformer教师和链路预测任务。

Conclusion: 通过引入图几何感知的曲率引导监督分配机制，G^2MLP有效解决了GNN-to-MLP蒸馏中的谱欠拟合和过拟合问题，实现了在保持推理高效性的同时更好地保留教师模型的图结构知识。

Abstract: GNN-to-MLP distillation aims to retain the predictive accuracy of a message-passing teacher while deploying a graph-free MLP at inference. Existing methods mainly transfer node-wise predictions or use confidence-based reweighting, but they do not specify where the student should preserve the teacher's graph-induced geometry. We show that this omission leads to two spectral failure modes in the student's representation space. On sparse graphs, the student suffers from spectral underfit, missing high-energy teacher directions concentrated near boundary regions. On dense graphs, it suffers from spectral overfit, retaining spurious directions that the teacher has collapsed through aggregation. Motivated by an energy-weighted teacher-student alignment objective, we propose Graph Geometry-aware MLP (G^2MLP), a training-time distillation framework guided by Ollivier-Ricci curvature. Curvature identifies where the two spectral errors concentrate and is used to allocate supervision between prediction-level and representation-level alignment. The deployed model remains a standard MLP and requires no graph access at inference. Across node-classification benchmarks, G^2MLP consistently improves over graph-free distillation baselines, reduces the teacher-student rank gap in both regimes, and transfers without architectural changes to Graph Transformer teachers and link prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [378] [Trustworthy Domain-Specific AI for Structured Knowledge Retrieval and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08894)
*Ryan C. Barron*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展架构，将非结构化领域文本转化为结构化知识以支持检索与推理，集成了半自动语料库筛选、语义结构化、检索与推理的可解释管道。核心贡献包括：Binary Bleed（降低NMF低秩搜索复杂度的二分搜索变体）、HNMFk（自动潜在特征选择的层级NMF，生成专家指导的可解释分类学）、类型化知识图谱与语义对齐向量存储、张量结构检索增强生成（T-SRAG）、对比对齐映射嵌入到层级主题结构、张量链接预测补全知识图谱。在网络安全、法律、材料科学、医疗等领域验证了检索精度、早期趋势检测、假设生成和幻觉缓解的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将非结构化、领域特定的文本大规模转化为可用于可靠检索和推理的结构化知识。主要挑战包括：NMF等主题模型的低秩搜索复杂度高、缺乏自动潜在特征选择和层级可解释性、知识图谱与向量存储难以同步、RAG系统存在幻觉且缺乏语义保真度、知识图谱链接不完整阻碍推理。需要一个统一、模块化、可部署的管道来解决这些问题。

Method: 1. Binary Bleed：改进的二分搜索方法，降低NMF的低秩搜索复杂度。2. HNMFk：带自动潜在特征选择的层级非负矩阵分解，深度自适应生成可解释分类学，领域专家指导。3. 类型化知识图谱与语义对齐向量存储，通过事件驱动基质同步。4. T-SRAG（张量结构检索增强生成）：动态路由查询至多检索路径。5. 对比对齐：将文档和查询嵌入映射到层级主题结构，提升语义保真度、减少幻觉。6. 基于张量的链接预测：识别并补全知识图谱缺失链接，支持基于引用结构的推理。

Result: 在网络安全、法律、材料科学、医疗四个领域的应用中，系统显著提升了检索精度、早期趋势检测能力、假设生成质量，并有效缓解了幻觉问题。Binary Bleed降低了NMF计算复杂度；HNMFk生成了专家可解释的层级分类学；T-SRAG与对比对齐提升了检索语义保真度；张量链接预测补全了知识图谱，增强了推理能力。整体架构模块化、可部署，为可信领域特定AI系统提供了基础。

Conclusion: 该论文构建了一个完整的、可扩展的管道，将非结构化领域文本转化为结构化知识，支持高保真检索和可靠推理。通过Binary Bleed、HNMFk、T-SRAG、对比对齐和张量链接预测等技术创新，解决了主题建模复杂度、可解释性、知识表示同步、检索幻觉和图谱不完整等核心问题。多领域实验验证了方法的有效性和通用性，为构建可信赖的领域特定AI系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: This dissertation presents a scalable architecture for transforming unstructured, domain-specific text into structured knowledge for retrieval and reasoning. It integrates semi-automatic corpus curation, semantic structuring, retrieval, and inference into an interpretable pipeline.
  The research introduces Binary Bleed, an adapted binary search method that reduces low-rank search complexity for Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF), and Hierarchical NMF with automatic latent feature selection (HNMFk), a depth-adaptive topic modeling method that produces interpretable taxonomies guided by subject matter experts. These representations populate a typed Knowledge Graph and a semantically aligned Vector Store containing extracted latent features, synchronized through an event-driven substrate.
  Tensor-Structured Retrieval-Augmented Generation (T-SRAG) dynamically routes queries across retrieval paths. Contrastive alignment maps document and query embeddings to hierarchical topic structures to improve semantic fidelity and reduce hallucinations. Beyond retrieval, tensor-based link prediction identifies and completes missing links in the Knowledge Graph, supporting inference grounded in citation structure.
  Applications across cybersecurity, law, materials science, and healthcare demonstrate improvements in retrieval precision, early trend detection, hypothesis generation, and hallucination mitigation. The dissertation provides a deployable, modular foundation for trustworthy, domain-specific AI systems that retrieve and reason over structured knowledge.

</details>


### [379] [What Transfers from a VLM Teacher? Comparing Supervision Signals for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09177)
*Saba Sturua,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉文档检索器中教师模型的信号利用方式，发现教师模型判断负样本（硬负例）并进行分数蒸馏比丰富正样本更有效，显著提升检索性能，且教师模型的判断与人类标注者一致性高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索器采用对比学习，仅有一个标注的正样本，其余视为负样本。近期方法尝试用视觉语言模型（VLM）教师丰富正样本（如注意力迁移或描述对齐），但标签不完整：许多相关页面被当作负样本推离。本文探究教师模型是否更适合用于判断检索器挖掘出的候选负样本。

Method: 固定学生模型、数据、优化器和评估，对比教师模型在正样本侧（描述对齐、注意力 grounding）与负样本侧（教师判断硬负例、分数蒸馏）的信号利用方式。在 ViDoRe v2/v3 基准上实验，并与无教师规则（如正样本感知阈值）对比。另进行人工审计评估教师判断质量。

Result: 教师判断硬负例+分数蒸馏将 ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5 从 55.2 提升至 62.6 和 63.0；描述对齐增益 2.6 分，注意力 grounding 无可测增益。教师判断比无教师规则（最佳为正样本感知阈值）在 v2 上多增 4.1 分，v3 上多增 1.7 分。标签不完整：标注者判定查询前四个挖掘候选中约两个相关，但均无标签。教师评分粗粒度：82% 评分集中在 0-100 两端，二分相关/不相关保留大部分蒸馏增益。十人审计显示教师判断在人类标注者差异范围内，且在单一确定答案查询上可靠。

Conclusion: 教师模型用于判断负样本（硬负例挖掘与分数蒸馏）比丰富正样本更有效，能显著缓解标签不完整导致的误推问题。教师判断与人类标注一致性高，且粗粒度评分即可保留大部分增益。释出代码、3.3M 教师判断、页面描述、挖掘池、人工审计及训练适配器。

Abstract: Visual document retrievers are trained contrastively: each query is matched to one page labelled relevant - the positive - and pushed away from negatives, pages presumed irrelevant. Recent methods distil a vision-language model (VLM) teacher into the retriever by enriching that positive, transferring the teacher's attention over it or a description of it. We ask whether the teacher is better spent on the other side, judging the candidates the retriever mines as negatives, which the label says nothing about. With student, data, optimizer and evaluation fixed, teacher-judged hard negatives and score distillation raise ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5 from 55.2 to 62.6 and 63.0; description alignment, as adapted here, gains 2.6 points and attention grounding nothing measurable. Against teacher-free rules that select four candidates from the same mined pool at identical training compute, the best of which is the positive-aware threshold current systems use, the teacher's judgement adds 4.1 points on v2 and 1.7 on v3. This is consistent with how incomplete the labels are. Annotators judge about two of a query's four top-ranked mined candidates relevant, none of them labelled, so training pushes the retriever away from relevant pages treated as negatives. What reaches the student is coarse: under a greedily decoded 0-100 rating prompt, 82% of the teacher's ratings come back at one end of the scale or the other, and a relevant/irrelevant partition keeps most of the distillation gain. A ten-annotator audit places the teacher within the range of variation among human annotators, and finds it reliable where a query has a single determinate answer. We release the code, the teacher's 3.3M judgements and page descriptions, the mined pools, the human audit and the trained adapters at https://github.com/elastic/vdr-teacher-signals.

</details>


### [380] [Quantize by Drift: Label-Free Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Text Embedders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09227)
*Hyojung Han,Jongmin Kim,Seung-Hun Jeon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需标签的混合精度训练后量化方法，针对文本嵌入模型，利用量化诱导的表示漂移作为敏感度信号。通过量化单个模块、重新编码语料库并测量输出嵌入向量与全精度位置的偏移，无需相关性标签即可指导混合精度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度训练后量化需要逐模块的敏感度信号；对于文本嵌入器，最直观的信号——量化某模块时检索质量的损失——需要相关性标签，但实际部署中很少有标签。因此需要一种无标签的替代敏感度度量。

Method: 测量量化诱导的表示漂移：量化一个模块，重新编码语料库，记录输出嵌入向量偏离全精度位置的距离。将这种漂移作为可观测量（部署中稠密检索器用于排序的输出表示），在硬字节预算约束下进行加性精度分配，无需标签也无需搜索。

Result: 在五个开发嵌入器上，配置级漂移对采样的混合精度方案与保留的检索质量排序，宏观 Spearman 相关系数为 0.911；敏感度可跨校准语料库和检索域迁移；模块漂移按秩一致组合但非数值一致；相关性导出的敏感度无额外价值。预注册假设在三个未见嵌入器上对先前 LieQ 准则成立（3/3 在主预算下，无崩溃），漂移在 2/3 上优于 LieQ 钢人论证；但在主预算下，漂移法数值上均低于同预算均匀精度（-0.99, -0.85, -1.01 分），按设计降低了模块和整模型漂移。

Conclusion: 输出漂移是一种稳健的粗粒度敏感度信号，而非普适最优分配目标：它避免了迁移符号几何适应的灾难性失败，在均匀量化崩溃的紧张预算下仍可用，但在强均匀操作点周围的细粒度重新分配问题仍未解决。

Abstract: Mixed-precision post-training quantization needs a per-module sensitivity signal; for a text embedder the obvious one -- the retrieval quality a module costs when quantized -- needs relevance labels that deployments rarely have. We measure a label-free substitute: quantization-induced representation drift, obtained by quantizing one module, re-encoding the corpus, and recording how far the output embeddings moved from their full-precision positions. What is specific is the observable: the deployed output representation a dense retriever ranks with. Across five development embedders, configuration-level drift orders sampled mixed-precision plans against held-out retrieval quality at a macro Spearman of 0.911, the sensitivity transports across calibration corpora and retrieval domains in the usable regime, module drifts compose rank-consistently but not numerically, and relevance-derived sensitivity adds no consistent value. The method is one additive allocation under a hard packed-byte budget, with no labels and no search. On three embedders held untouched until method, baselines and hypotheses were frozen and sealed, the pre-registered directional hypothesis against the prior LieQ criterion holds (3/3 at the main budget, no collapse) and drift scores above a two-sided LieQ steelman in 2/3; but at the main budget drift is numerically lower than same-budget uniform precision on all three (-0.99, -0.85, -1.01 points), having reduced module and whole-model drift as designed. Output drift is thus a robust coarse sensitivity signal, not a universally optimal allocation objective: it avoids the catastrophic failures of the transferred signed-geometry adaptation and can remain usable at stressed budgets where uniform collapses, but fine-grained redistribution around a strong uniform operating point remains unresolved.

</details>


### [381] [Reading Position Is the Baseline to Beat: A Time-Ordered Evaluation of Personalised Highlight Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09262)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究在社交高亮平台上，仅凭读者的第一个高亮预测其下一个高亮位置。发现基于阅读位置（首个高亮下方句子）的简单基线在初期预测中表现最优（Top-5 命中率 47%），但综合所有后续高亮时，折扣流行度方法（结合距离最新高亮的距离）整体表现优于流行度基线及两种相似度方法。强调个性化文档推荐需采用时间序评估并以阅读位置为基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阅读产品常利用相似读者的高亮数据进行推荐，但缺乏系统性基线对比。作者主张最基础的基线应为读者自身的阅读位置（即首个高亮之后的文本位置），旨在探究在无其他读者数据时，仅凭首个高亮能否有效预测后续高亮，并验证个性化方法是否真正优于简单基线。

Method: 在一个社交高亮平台上收集 1,511 个页面、7,343 个读者-页面对（每个读者至少有一个高亮）。采用时间序评估：按时间顺序预测读者的下一个高亮。对比方法包括：1) 阅读位置基线（对首个高亮下方句子排序）；2) 流行度基线（所有读者高亮频次）；3) 两种相似度方法（基于相似读者的高亮）；4) 折扣流行度（流行度按距最新高亮距离折扣，尝试三种尺度）。评估指标为平均精度（Average Precision）及 Top-5 命中率。

Result: 1) 仅凭首个高亮，阅读位置基线将下一个高亮排入 Top-5 的概率为 47%，显著高于流行度 (26%) 和两种相似度方法 (29%)。2) 针对所有后续高亮，阅读位置基线不敌流行度；但最佳折扣流行度方法（三种尺度中平均精度最高者）同时击败流行度与两种相似度方法。3) 预先设定的对比中，两种相似度方法在平均精度上均未显著优于流行度（增益 +0.01 被排除）。4) 合成实验显示，相似度方法的增益不等于捕捉到读者偏好；非时间序评估会高估方法表现。

Conclusion: 阅读位置是文档内个性化推荐的强基线，尤其在冷启动（仅有一个高亮）时极具竞争力。综合长期预测需结合折扣流行度。评估必须采用时间序设计，并以阅读位置为基线，否则易高估个性化方法效果。相似度方法在实测中未显示出超越流行度的稳健优势。

Abstract: A reader's first highlights on a page are the cheapest personal signal a reading product has. The natural plan is to suggest what similar earlier readers marked, and to judge the result against popularity. We argue that the baseline to beat is reading position. In a time-ordered evaluation on one social highlighting platform (7,343 reader-page pairs on 1,511 pages after one highlight), ranking the sentences just below a reader's first highlight, with no other reader's data, puts the next highlight in the top five 47% of the time, against 26% for popularity and 29% for the better of two similarity methods. The baseline depends on the target: over all later highlights that ranking loses to popularity, while popularity discounted by distance from the latest highlight, at the scale with the best average precision of three tried, beats popularity and both similarity methods on both targets. In a comparison specified in advance, neither similarity method shows a gain over popularity in average precision over all later highlights, from one to five highlights, and a gain of +0.01 is excluded. Nor would a gain by itself show that a method has found a reader's preferences: synthetic readers who share one set of preferences produce one, and an evaluation out of time order shows a method where the reader went. The position results are exploratory and unconfirmed. Personalisation inside a document should be evaluated in time order and against reading position.

</details>


### [382] [From Chunks to Functional Evidence: Function-Aware Retrieval for EDA Documentation QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09361)
*Xiaotian Qiu,Kairui Liu,Shi Chenyi,Jinyuan Deng,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向EDA文档的RAG新方法，将异构文档组织为功能单元（超边），结合单元检索与块检索，经重排后生成答案，在EDADocEval-QA和ORD-MMBench上显著提升ROUGE-L。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA文档中知识分散于异构且紧密耦合的制品中，查询与检索组织方式不匹配是主要瓶颈，传统块级或图谱检索难以捕捉跨制品的功能关联。

Method: 将类型化制品聚合为EDA功能单元，每个单元表示为连接源块的超边；训练编码器将查询与功能单元对齐，融合单元检索与直接块检索；将选中单元映射回源块，再经统一重排器选取证据送生成器。

Result: 在新构建的EDADocEval-QA数据集上，ROUGE-L较Chunk RAG提升37.1%，较最强图基线提升55.6%；在公开ORD-MMBench基准上，ROUGE-L较最强基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在EDA文档场景中采用功能感知的证据组织方式，通过超边建模功能单元可有效缓解查询-知识组织不匹配问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to ground answers in documents. For complex technical documentation, however, the primary bottleneck is often not model reasoning but a mismatch between a query and the way knowledge is organized for retrieval. This mismatch is pronounced in Electronic Design Automation (EDA) documentation, where the information needed for an answer is scattered across heterogeneous yet tightly coupled artifacts. We therefore redesign the basic retrieval unit of RAG. Instead of operating on isolated chunks or binary relations, we collect typed artifacts into EDA functional units. Each unit is recorded as a hyperedge with links to its source chunks. We then train an encoder to align queries with functional units and combine unit retrieval with direct chunk retrieval. After mapping the selected units back to their sources, a unified reranker chooses the evidence given to the generator. On the newly constructed EDADocEval-QA dataset, our method improves ROUGE-L by 37.1% over Chunk RAG and 55.6% over the strongest graph baseline. On the public ORD-MMBench benchmark, it improves ROUGE-L by 30.0% over the strongest baseline. These results support function-aware evidence organization in the evaluated EDA documentation settings.

</details>


### [383] [Finding the Right Balance: Relevance and Diversity in LLM Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09412)
*Guillaume Brouillette,Faustin Kagabo,Usef Faghihi,Nadia Ghazzali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究检索增强生成（RAG）框架中的检索多样化策略，发现其效果取决于候选池的冗余程度和查询所需的证据数量。作者提出了一种查询自适应规则，仅在最近邻检索的前k个结果中有效不同文档数低于查询证据需求时才进行多样化，并引入了RNG-Score几何重排序器。结论是多样化应基于可观测的冗余度和证据需求选择性使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对检索多样化是否能提升检索和答案质量存在分歧。作者旨在明确多样化生效的条件，并提出一种自适应机制，避免在不需要时引入多样化带来的负面影响。

Method: 通过受控近似重复注入和生产风格的重叠分块实验，分析多样化在不同冗余度下的表现。提出查询自适应规则：利用现有嵌入计算最近邻前k个结果中的有效不同文档数，仅当其低于查询证据需求时才启用多样化。同时引入RNG-Score几何重排序器，具有精确最近邻回退机制，其边界指示重复结构。

Result: 在干净候选池上，多样化损害相关性、证据覆盖和答案质量；但在多证据任务且冗余导致最近邻检索选择重复段落时，多样化变得有益。自适应规则捕获了大部分可实现的增益，跨数据集和编码器泛化良好，且对单证据查询自动退化为最近邻检索。RNG-Score能有效指示重复结构。

Conclusion: 检索多样化不应盲目应用，而应根据可观测的候选池冗余度和查询证据需求选择性使用。提出的自适应规则和RNG-Score为实际RAG系统提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval diversification is widely available in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frameworks, yet prior studies disagree on whether it improves retrieval and answer quality. We show that its effectiveness varies primarily with candidate-pool redundancy, in a pattern consistent with the number of distinct evidence pieces a query requires. Using controlled near-duplicate injection and production-style overlapping chunking, we find that diversification harms relevance, evidence coverage and answer quality on clean pools, but becomes beneficial on multi-evidence tasks when redundancy causes nearest-neighbor retrieval to select repeated passages. We therefore introduce a query-adaptive rule that diversifies only when the effective number of distinct documents in the nearest-neighbor top-$k$ selection falls below the query's evidence requirement. Computed from existing embeddings, the rule captures most of the achievable gain, transfers across datasets and encoders and automatically reduces to nearest-neighbor retrieval for single-evidence queries. We also introduce RNG-Score, a geometric reranker with an exact nearest-neighbor fallback whose margin indicates duplicate structure. Overall, we conclude that diversification should be used selectively, based on observable redundancy and evidence requirements.

</details>


### [384] [Towards Explaining Query Expansion Performance in Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09724)
*Sourav Saha,Aditya Dutta,Soumajit Pramanik,Mandar Mitra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个互补视角——理想扩展查询（IEQ）和基于Cohen's d的可分离性度量——来解释查询扩展（QE）性能的差异，实验表明更接近IEQ的扩展查询检索效果更好，且可分离性提供了理解QE性能的补充视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询扩展技术虽广泛用于解决词汇不匹配问题，且在基于大语言模型的现代检索系统中仍具相关性，但没有单一QE方法能在所有查询上持续优于其他方法，因此需要从理论层面解释QE性能变异的原因。

Method: 提出两个互补视角：1）理想扩展查询（IEQ）——假设的能最大化下游BM25检索效果的查询；2）可分离性视角——利用Cohen's d量化扩展查询下相关与非相关文档得分的区分度。开发了可分离性度量及近似IEQ的实用公式，并在TREC Robust、TREC DL 2019-2022段落集及TREC DL 2019-2020文档集上进行大量实验验证。

Result: 实验发现：更接近理想扩展查询（IEQ）的扩展查询倾向于获得更高的检索效果；相关与非相关文档的可分离性为理解QE性能提供了互补的解释视角。

Conclusion: IEQ与可分离性度量共同构成了分析QE性能变异的理论框架，揭示了扩展查询质量与检索效果的内在联系，为未来设计更鲁棒的查询扩展方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Query Expansion (QE) techniques have long been widely used in Information Retrieval (IR) to address the vocabulary mismatch problem. They remain relevant in modern retrieval systems, including those based on large language models (LLMs). However, no single QE method consistently outperforms others across all queries. This work seeks to explain the variation in QE performance through two complementary perspectives. The first is the concept of an Ideal Expanded Query (IEQ)--a hypothetical query that maximizes retrieval effectiveness with a downstream BM25 retrieval model. The second is a separability perspective, which quantifies how distinctly relevant and non-relevant documents are scored for a given expanded query using Cohen's (d). We develop a separability measure and practical formulations to approximate the IEQ and investigate how these factors relate to retrieval effectiveness. Extensive experiments on the TREC Robust collection, TREC DL 2019-2022 passage collections, and TREC DL 2019-2020 document collections reveal several interesting patterns. In particular, we find that expanded queries that are closer to the ideal expanded query tend to achieve higher retrieval effectiveness. We further show that the separability of relevant and non-relevant documents provides a complementary perspective for understanding QE performance.

</details>


### [385] [The Impact of Backbone Evolution on LLM-Based Relevance Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09820)
*Chuting Yu,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM作为相关性判断器时，模型版本迭代对判断一致性的影响。实验发现：新版本模型并不总能带来更好的判断效果，且判断结果在版本间存在不稳定性，正确判断可能在新版本中丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM快速迭代，普遍假设更强能力的模型在相同提示下会获得更高的人类判断一致性。论文旨在挑战这一假设，系统性探究骨干模型演进对相关性判断稳定性的影响。

Method: 保持提示固定，评估单提示方法（UMBRELA）和基于评分细则的方法（EXAM），跨商业模型（Gemini、GPT）和开放权重模型（Qwen、Llama）的连续版本进行实验对比。

Result: 未发现新版本持续提升判断性能的证据；汇总性能相似或提升不代表判断稳定性——早期版本的正确判断在新版本中不一定保留；识别出导致回归的潜在驱动因素。

Conclusion: 不应假设为特定骨干模型版本设计和验证的判断提示，在模型更新后仍能保持同等或更好表现，即使在同一模型家族内也存在风险。

Abstract: LLMs are evolving rapidly, with newer models offering stronger capabilities. This suggests that in LLM-based relevance judging, more capable models will achieve higher agreement with human judgements under the same prompt. We challenge this understanding by investigating the behavior of LLM-based relevance judges under backbone evolution. Keeping the prompts fixed, we evaluate a representative single-prompt (UMBRELA) and a rubric-based prompt (EXAM) across sequential model versions of commercial (Gemini, GPT) and open-weight (Qwen, Llama) models. Overall, we find no consistent evidence that newer versions lead to better relevance judges. Crucially, similar or improved aggregate performance does not imply judgment stability: correct judgements made by an earlier version of an LLM backbone are not necessarily preserved by later versions. We investigate the potential drivers of these regressions. Our findings caution against the assumption that judging prompts designed and validated for one backbone version will perform equivalently or better when the model is updated, even within the same family.

</details>


### [386] [Inverting Multi-Vector Visual Document Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09920)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 多向量视觉文档检索器存储的向量索引可被逆向还原为原始页面，泄露敏感信息，需像保护文档一样保护索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多向量视觉文档检索器将每页存储为约千个补丁向量，普遍认为索引不如页面敏感，但因索引保持光栅顺序且向量由预训练视觉语言模型生成，假设索引可被用于还原页面。

Method: 将逆向攻击建模为条件文档图像生成，利用编码器、页面形状及向量顺序（若打乱则需恢复顺序）从索引重建页面；在ViDoRe v3基准测试，评估词召回率、敏感token召回率及源页面排序；测试token池化和打乱两种廉价防御；并迁移攻击至另一检索器验证泛化性。

Result: 原始索引逆向页面恢复47%词和45%敏感token，作为查询时源页面排首位98.4%；池化和打乱将词召回降至~8%；打乱顺序恢复模型使源页面排首位从3.8%升至93.5%，池化索引逆向仍可行；迁移攻击中源页面排首位70.2%，但词召回低于最近邻基线。

Conclusion: 多向量视觉文档检索器的存储索引存在逆向漏洞，应按文档同等级别保护，单纯打乱或池化不足以完全防御。

Abstract: Prevailing multi-vector visual document retrievers store each page as about a thousand patch vectors, often in vector databases run by a third party. Since no one can read a page from its vectors, this index is easily treated as less sensitive than the page. However, because the index keeps one vector per patch in raster order, and each vector is computed by a vision-language model pre-trained to read documents, we hypothesize that whoever runs or breaches the store can reproduce a page from its index alone. We frame inversion as conditional document image generation and infer from the vectors what the attack needs: the encoder, the page shape and, for shuffled vectors, their order. On the ViDoRe v3 benchmark, pages inverted from raw indices recover 47% of the words and 45% of the sensitive tokens. Used as queries against the stored indices, they rank their source page first 98.4% of the time. We test two cheap protections, token pooling and shuffling, which both cut word recall to about 8%. A model that restores the order of a shuffled index raises the share of source pages ranked first from 3.8% to 93.5%, while inverting a pooled index remains open. To test generalisation, we apply the same attack unchanged to another multi-vector retriever: its inverted pages still rank their source page first 70.2% of the time, though its word recall stays below a nearest-neighbour baseline. Multi-vector visual document retrievers are therefore vulnerable to inversion through their stored index, which should be protected like the documents it encodes.

</details>


### [387] [Training with Missed Targets in Generative Recommendation: Separating Supervision from Probability Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10124)
*Xuesi Wang,Yangbin Shi,Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究生成式推荐系统中重排序训练策略：将推理时遗漏的目标追加到训练列表中。作者指出这种追加操作同时改变了检索目标权重、增加了追加目标的监督、并使两组目标竞争概率质量，导致简单的追加/不追加对比无法解释排序变化。他们设计了三个匹配损失函数，分离监督与竞争效应，发现组间竞争会损害返回项目的排序质量。实验表明移除竞争可显著提升FT-NDCG，且开发集规则应逐生成器评估候补完成策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器返回有限候选集，重排序前可能遗漏观测目标。常用训练策略将遗漏目标追加到训练列表，但推理时仍仅对原始候选排序。这种操作同时改变检索目标权重、增加追加目标监督、引入组间概率竞争，三者耦合导致无法通过简单追加/不追加对比解释排序变化，需分离各因素效应。

Method: 构建三个匹配损失函数：固定检索目标权重，分别引入追加目标监督、组间竞争、以及两者结合。中间损失在两组内训练但分别归一化，防止仅训练的目标与推理候选竞争。使用发布的OneRec模型和本地训练的Amazon生成器实验，在四个预设的Amazon Video Games类别对比中评估FT-NDCG，并采用保守开发集规则选择策略。

Result: 移除组间竞争在四个对比中将FT-NDCG提升7.8%–22.2%；用户层面95%置信区间及四分之三训练运行区间均不包含零。开发集规则在一个举出类别中为三个生成器中的两个选择追加目标训练，在另一类别中拒绝所有三个，避免了1.7%的损失。表明候补完成策略需逐生成器评估而非自动应用。

Conclusion: 重排序训练中追加遗漏目标引入的组间概率竞争会损害返回项目排序质量。应将监督信号与竞争机制解耦，并针对每个生成器在开发集上评估候补完成策略，而非盲目应用追加训练。

Abstract: Generative recommenders return a limited candidate set and may omit observed targets before reranking. A training strategy appends these missed targets to reranker training lists, although inference still ranks only original candidates. This operation simultaneously changes retrieved-target weight, adds supervision over appended targets, and makes the two groups compete for probability. An append/no-append comparison therefore cannot explain changes in returned-item rankings. We construct three matched losses that hold retrieved-target weight fixed while introducing appended-target supervision and group competition separately. The intermediate loss trains within both groups but normalizes them separately, preventing training-only targets from competing with inference candidates. Experiments with a released OneRec model and locally trained Amazon generators show that this competition can harm returned-item ranking. In four prespecified Amazon Video Games comparisons, removing it improved full-target normalized discounted cumulative gain (FT-NDCG) by 7.8--22.2\%; 95\% intervals over users and three of four intervals over training runs excluded zero. A conservative development-set rule selected appended-target training for two of three generators in one held-out category and rejected it for all three in another, avoiding a 1.7\% loss. Candidate completion should therefore be evaluated for each generator rather than applied automatically.

</details>


### [388] [Does Document Structure Help Dense Retrieval? A Placebo-Controlled Ablation of Four Mechanisms Across Two Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10170)
*Andrey Kuehlkamp,Priscila Correa Saboia Moreira,Samuel Rund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文通过统一协议对四种文档结构处理方法进行消融实验，发现结构对齐分块结合真实标题路径优于固定窗口和安慰剂，两阶段层级检索反而有害，金标准结构在维基百科优于LLM生成结构但在QASPER上无差异，效应虽小但显著且跨语料库一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别在不同语料库、嵌入模型和指标上验证各种文档结构处理方法，且未控制共同混淆因素：添加到分块前的任何文本都会扰动其嵌入。需要机制隔离的消融实验来厘清各方法的真实效果。

Method: 在统一协议下测试四种处理（结构对齐分块、LLM生成分块上下文、标题路径元数据、两阶段层级检索），匹配分块大小，引入语义空安慰剂（跨文档打乱的标题路径）。使用覆盖感知nDCG评分，通过文档聚类引导重采样和Holm校正检验四个预注册对比。在两个远程语料库上实验：200篇维基百科精选文章（951查询）和1,585篇QASPER论文（4,303问题）。

Result: 结构组织有帮助，原因是内容而非标记：带真实标题路径的结构对齐分块优于上下文化固定窗口（+0.022/+0.012 cov-nDCG@10）和安慰剂（+0.010/+0.016）。朴素两阶段层级检索有害（-0.033/-0.015），归因于第一阶段段落召回。金标准结构在维基百科优于LLM诱导结构，但在QASPER上无差异。效应量小（dz 0.06-0.11）但经Holm校正显著且跨语料库一致。

Conclusion: 文档结构对RAG检索有益，但关键在于结构对齐分块和真实标题路径提供的语义内容，而非单纯的标记增加。两阶段层级检索需谨慎设计。LLM生成结构在某些领域可替代金标准结构。效应虽小但稳健，为RAG系统设计提供实证指导。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems increasingly rely on document-structure treatments: structure-aligned chunking, LLM-generated chunk contexts, heading-path metadata, and hierarchical two-stage retrieval. Separate studies support each on different corpora, embedders, and metrics, and none control for a shared confound: any text prepended to a chunk perturbs its embedding. We present a mechanism-isolating ablation testing all four treatments under one protocol, matching chunk sizes across conditions and adding a semantically null placebo---heading paths that are structurally valid but shuffled across documents. We score retrieval with a coverage-aware nDCG and test four pre-registered contrasts via document-clustered bootstrap with Holm correction, on two distant corpora: 200 Wikipedia Featured Articles (951 queries) and 1,585 QASPER papers (4,303 questions). Organization helps, and the cause is content, not tokens: structure-aligned chunks with real heading paths beat contextualized fixed windows (+0.022 / +0.012 cov-nDCG@10) and the placebo (+0.010 / +0.016). Naive two-stage hierarchical retrieval hurts (-0.033 / -0.015), traceable to first-stage section recall. Gold structure beats LLM-induced structure on Wikipedia but not on QASPER. Effects are small ($dz$ 0.06-0.11) but Holm-significant and consistent across corpora.

</details>
